# hls-est vs. Vitis HLS matching — change log

Newest entry first. One entry per logical change, including changes that were
tried and reverted. Branch: `hls-est-vitis-match` in `~/circt`.

## 2026-10-14 — an array passed to a call before a loop is still hoisted from inside the loop (N38-impl 3/5)
- **Commit:** this entry's commit (branch `hls-est-vitis-match`)
- **Files:** `lib/Dialect/Resource/Passes/AffineToLoopScheduleModified.cpp` (`isMemrefWrittenInLoop` takes `callsOnlyInLoop`: a memref passed to some call counts as written in the loop only when the loop contains a call; option `hoist-past-calls`, default on), new lit `test/hls-est/Passes/bram_repl_hoist_past_calls.mlir`.
- **What:** the invariant-load hoist refused every memref that any call in the function receives (the conservative rule O47-fix kept for hoisting). A callee can write an array it received only while a call runs (during that call or, keeping the address, during a later one), so only a call inside the loop can write it while the loop runs; the same reasoning as O47-fix's position-wise barrier for the redundant-access cleanup.
- **Why:** class_ece8893_2023_spring Analog-CIM on-board: `v_ref_buf` is filled by `load_v_ref_from_DRAM` and read 32 times per iteration under a break-guarded compare; Vitis 4 BRAM (plain RAM, the loads hoisted as in `w9r_b9condinv`), hls-est 60 (15 copies). Corpus evidence only, no kernel with a call (the hoist itself is the kernel-backed R-hoist-cond).
- **Effect:** see the previous entry (Analog-CIM on-board 258 -> 202 = Vitis needs both options; Analog-CIM optimized 76 -> 114). No other design changes with this option alone.
- **Reverted?:** no

## 2026-10-14 — loop-invariant loads under an if are hoisted (N38-impl 2/5, VITIS_NOTES N38 R-hoist-cond)
- **Commit:** this entry's commit (branch `hls-est-vitis-match`)
- **Files:** `lib/Dialect/Resource/Passes/AffineToLoopScheduleModified.cpp` (`hoistInvariantLoads`: an affine.load nested only in scf.if / affine.if regions of the loop body, with loop-invariant address and memref, is hoisted like a top-level one and marked `hls.hoisted_conditional`; option `hoist-conditional-invariant`, default on), `BRAMAnalysis.cpp` (access record `hoisted_conditional`, debug text and JSON, additive), new lit `test/hls-est/Passes/bram_repl_hoist_conditional.mlir`.
- **What:** the LICM before scheduling only hoisted top-level loads of the pipelined loop's body; a load guarded by an if (unrolled compares with an early `break`, a guarded read) stayed in the pipeline and counted toward R. It is now hoisted when its memref is not written in the loop.
- **Why:** VITIS_NOTES N38 R-hoist-cond (kernel `w9r_b9condinv`: 32 `rb[l]` loads under a break-guarded compare at II 2: the pipeline takes 32 scalar `rb_load_*` arguments, `rb` a plain RAM, total 4; hls-est 32); OPEN_ISSUES O51 (d).
- **Effect:** measured together with the other N38-impl mechanisms (see the "user II target" entry). Kernel `w9r_b9condinv` 32 -> **4 = Vitis**. Catalogue (with `hoist-past-calls`, the next entry; each one alone leaves these designs unchanged): class_ece8893_2023_spring Analog-CIM on-board 258 -> **202 = Vitis** (`v_ref_buf` RAM_1WNR 60 -> 4 = Vitis; newly exact); Analog-CIM hls/optimized 76 -> 114 (Vitis 32; not in band before or after): its pipeline goes from II 16 to II 10 once `v_ref_buf` leaves it, and `weight2d_cond_buf`, which Vitis partitions completely into 168 LUTRAM banks (0 BRAM) and hls-est keeps whole (OPEN_ISSUES O53), needs 16 copies instead of 10; `input_buf` 2 -> 4 (Vitis 20).
- **Reverted?:** no

## 2026-10-14 — same-address accesses in the two arms of an if/else count once: hoisted load, sunk store (N38-impl 1/5, VITIS_NOTES N38 R-ifelse)
- **Commit:** this entry's commit (branch `hls-est-vitis-match`)
- **Files:** `lib/Dialect/Resource/Passes/AffineToLoopScheduleModified.cpp` (new `mergeIfElseAccesses` with `movableAccesses`, `mayConflict`, `pairSameAddress`, `addIfResults`; option `merge-if-else-accesses`, default on; runs before `cleanupRedundantAccesses`), `BRAMAnalysis.cpp` (access record `merged_if_else_stores`, debug text and JSON, additive), new lit `test/hls-est/Passes/bram_repl_if_else.mlir`.
- **What:** for every scf.if / affine.if with an else block (innermost first), a load both arms make of one address of a tracked memref (a local array or a function argument that has not escaped), with nothing before it in its arm that may write that address, becomes one load above the if; a store both arms make to one address, with nothing after it in its arm that may touch that address, becomes one store below the if of a value the if yields. The address operands must be defined outside the if; accesses at a disjoint constant offset do not block the move. The store forwarding of the redundant-access cleanup then turns an unrolled read-modify-write repeated in both arms into one load and one store per iteration.
- **Why:** VITIS_NOTES N38 R-ifelse (kernels `w9r_e1ifelse`, `w9v_r_e1ifelse`: `if (in > 0) A[i] = in; else A[i] = 0;` + 8 reads at II 1 -> RAM_1WNR 7 copies, 14 BRAM; hls-est counted 2 stores, RAM_T2P 2); OPEN_ISSUES O51 (b).
- **Effect:** measured together with the other four N38-impl mechanisms (catalogue numbers in the "user II target" entry); attributed by re-running each changed design with `merge-if-else-accesses=false`: kernels `w9r_e1ifelse` 2 -> 14 = Vitis, `w9v_r_e1ifelse` 2 -> 14 = Vitis. Catalogue: YOLOv4-Tiny-Accelerator Conv2D_Layer1Layer2 33 -> **54 = Vitis** (`conv_in_buf` RAM_T2P 2 -> RAM_1WNR 16, `conv_wt_buf` 1 -> 8, both = Vitis; newly exact); vitis_examples static_memory 2 -> **1 = Vitis** (newly exact). Further from Vitis, none leaving exact or the band: Yolo-v3-tiny 24Layers 112 -> 433 (Vitis 257) and 4Layers 63 -> 285 (Vitis 229), ECG_Classifier Optimized 191 -> 201 (Vitis 188, still in band): their arrays (`conv_in_buf*`, `conv_wt_buf*`, `temp_input`) are now replicable, as in Vitis, but hls-est counts each read of a cyclic partition at a runtime index against every bank (72 reads per bank per iteration in `conv_3x3_id16`, 5 in `conv1d_1`), where Vitis steers the factor-many consecutive offsets to one read per bank: the affine-load case of OPEN_ISSUES O50 (b); the two-store count used to hide it. Not fixed here: ECG_Classifier Baseline (5 arrays: the reading loop runs at II 10 / 12 because hls-est flattens the enclosing loop, as on `w9r_e4rmwflt`) and polybench_pragmas k3mm `tmp1` (no if/else in its body: its two stores per iteration come from `unroll factor=2` of the pipelined loop, so N38's attribution of `tmp1` to R-ifelse needs a kernel).
- **Reverted?:** no

## 2026-10-13 — dataflow process merges: the writers of a region-local array or stream are one process, with every process on a dependence path between them (N36-impl, VITIS_NOTES N36)
- **Commit:** this entry's commit (branch `hls-est-vitis-match`)
- **Files:** `lib/Dialect/Resource/Passes/DataflowChannels.cpp` (`ProcessMerges::compute` now runs `mergeOnParameters` (the former body: processes reading one parameter of a non-top region function) and new `mergeWritersAndPaths` in every region, the top included: it collects the region-local storages (allocas of the region function and globals only it uses, not one-word scalars, every access inside the region; stream handles through new `streamAccesses`, which follows views, pointer casts and callee parameters to `hls::stream` read / write calls and anchors each at the outermost call), unites all writer processes of each storage ("writes on '<var>'"), builds the dependence edges (P -> Q for P before Q in program order touching one array that one of them writes; writer -> reader for a stream) and unites every process that lies on a path from a merged group back to itself, repeating until nothing changes ("on a dependence path from one process of the merged group to another"); new `unite`, `groupLeader`, `isMerged`, `bodyPositions`, `storageName`, `streamMethodWrites`; `analyzeDataflowChannel` groups the accessors by merge group: the writers must form one group, the array is a channel when a process outside that group reads it, and an ordinary memory of the merged process ("every process that accesses it is merged into one process (<group>: <reason>)") when the group holds every reader), `include/circt/Dialect/Resource/Passes/DataflowChannels.h` (`DataflowProcess::group` / `mergeReason`, the new `ProcessMerges` members), `lib/Dialect/Resource/Passes/BRAMAnalysis.cpp` (debug text prints "merged into the process of <leader> (<reason>)" under each merged process; debug JSON additive `pipo.processes[].merge_group` / `merge_reason`, version stays 2), new lit `test/hls-est/Passes/bram_dataflow_merges.mlir` (7 regions mirroring the kernels `w9m_ll2`, `w9m_rwr`, `w9m_cyc`, `w9v_m_half`, `w9m_trans`, `w9m_inner`, `w9m_strm`, each with its Vitis total; 6 of 7 totals differ on the previous binary), `test/hls-est/Passes/bram_dataflow_channels.mlir` (the reason text of the consumer-writes and parameter-merge arrays; same prices).
- **What:** an array written by two processes of a dataflow region was always an ordinary memory ("2 processes write it"). Now the writers are merged with the dependence-path closure and the merged group counts as one process for the N33 channel rule; arrays and streams wholly inside a group stay ordinary memories / FIFOs (one port per sub-loop, as before). Not modelled, because no kernel decides them: a one-word scalar as merge or path carrier (`w9v_m_scal` does not decide it), the region's own arguments as path carriers.
- **Why:** VITIS_NOTES N36 (11 `w9m_*` kernels + 4 review kernels `w9v_m_*`, 36 of 36 corpus arrays with `[HLS 214-475] ... due to writes on`); W9r2 diagnosis class C1 (+5,376 BRAM, 21 designs); OPEN_ISSUES O50 (e).
- **Effect:** the 15 N36 kernels (installed cgeist 8f27f3c0, exactclass, the run pipeline below): totals equal Vitis 2 -> **15 of 15** (betw 4, cond 2, cyc 4, ff2 2, inner 3, ll2 2, rmw 4, rwr 2, strm 6, sub 1, trans 4, `w9v_m_betw` 4, `half` 4, `rmw` 4, `scal` 2). Full v2 catalogue (frozen `hls-est.n36b` md5 44a7aff6, MLIR reused from `w9rfix/after_merged.json`, pipeline `--lower-polygeist-subindex --inline --split-struct-memrefs --mark-constant-fills --canonicalize --sccp --vitis-fold --dead-memory-elimination --affine-loop-normalize --auto-pipeline-unroll --narrow-ram-widths --memory-banking-bram --convert-affine-to-loopschedule --bram-analysis`, `-j 4 --timeout 120`; `~/hls-est-work/coord/scratch/n36impl/after.json`), against the W9r2 review-fix run `w9r2fix/after.json`: compared 671 -> 671, exact **549 -> 570**, in band **564 -> 585**, under **85 -> 64**, over > 15 % 22 -> 22, \|err\| **36,118 -> 30,743**. 22 estimates change: **21 newly exact**, all stream_hls DepthwiseSeparableConvBlock opt1 / opt3 / opt4 / opt5 (1792 -> 2048, 1696 -> 1952) and ResidualBlock opt1 / opt3 / opt5 (1024 -> 1280, 992 -> 1248) in their three catalogue copies (`stream_hls/`, `WIP/stream_hls__`, `WIP/stream_hls______processed_dataset__`): `v4` 103968x32 256 -> 512 and `v4` / `v38` / `v53` 53824x32 128 -> 256 are now the single PIPOs Vitis builds (zero-fill loop + copy loop merged, read by the next process). pp4fpgas huffman_encoding 24 -> 25 (Vitis 23, stays in band): `length_histogram` 64x10 0 -> 1 = Vitis (a PIPO between the inlined compute_bit_length and truncate_tree loops); the over-count is elsewhere (`sorting_1` RAM_1WNR copies, O51). 98 memrefs in 23 designs change kind or BRAM; the other 64 are class_ece8893_spring_2025 mizanur_Lab3_2025 `local_C` banks (RAM_1P 1 -> PIPO single 1, same BRAM; no Vitis result). No design leaves exact or the band; one status change, forgebench/mlp ok -> timeout under `-j 4` load (no dataflow region; alone 98 s, 1030 = the previous total). Harness subset unchanged: 108 compared / 90 exact / 93 in band / \|err\| 461; gates bicg 8, gesummv 16, mvt 8, syrk 32. stream_hls ResidualBlock opt4 still times out (as before). Lit 130 pass / 1 unresolved (sha) / 1 xfail (+1 new test); `check_debug_invariance.sh` 41 pass / 0 fail / 1 tool error (= baseline); the 15 kernels give identical output with and without `--bram-debug --bram-debug-json`. The committed binary differs from `n36b` only in comments.
- **Reverted?:** no

## 2026-10-13 — W9 round 2 review fixes: `&&` / `||` guards bound the stored range; the widening cutoff counts fixpoint rounds, not stores
- **Commit:** this entry's commit (branch `hls-est-vitis-match`)
- **Files:** `lib/Dialect/Resource/Passes/RamWidthNarrowing.cpp` (`conditionBound` now reads `arith.andi` / `arith.ori` of i1s and the `scf.if` forms the front end gives `p && q` (`yield q` / `yield false`) and `p || q` (`yield true` / `yield q`), meeting both bounds when the conjunction holds or the disjunction fails and joining them otherwise; the comparison itself moved to `compareBound`; new `meetBounds` also tightens the unsigned view from a non-negative signed one and back, so the remark prints `[0, 299]`, not `[0, 2147483647]`; `forwardOp` only marks `grewThisRound`, new `endRound` counts rounds in which an array's (or a callee parameter's) range grew and pins after `kWidenAfterRounds` = 8 rounds (was `kWidenAfterChanges`, 8 growing stores); new `pinReason` gives the remark "never written", "a stored value has no known range" or "still growing after 8 rounds" instead of always "still growing: a recurrence"); `test/hls-est/Passes/narrow_ram_widths.mlir` (new `@guards`: `&&` scf.if, `||` scf.if else arm, `andi`; new `@unrolled_stores`: 16 stores; both fail on the previous binary); `VITIS_NOTES.md` N35 item (f); record fixes in `MEMOS.md` and the previous entry (below).
- **What:** the two defects of the W9 round 2 adversarial review. (1) A store under `x >= 0 && x < 300` kept 32 bits because the condition bound read only a single `arith.cmpi`. (2) An array written by more than 8 unrolled stores with growing ranges was pinned to the full range within one pass over straight-line code; a memory recurrence already reads full range through its cyclic class, so the cutoff only bounds the work and now counts fixpoint rounds. Record fixes: the narrowing decision row was the second MEMOS row numbered 57 and is now #58 (`common.py` comment too); the previous entry and MEMOS #58 claimed both catalogue runs used one binary (`run8`), the control actually ran on `hls-est.run` (md5 dc7cc1e8); corrected in place (the review's control re-run on `run8` gives identical totals on all 813 designs).
- **Why:** VITIS_NOTES N35 (f), review kernels td=off: `w9rv_ifg` (`b` under `x >= 0 && x < 300` -> 1024x9, `a` under `(unsigned)x < 16` -> 4; 2 BRAM), `w9rv_wid` (16 unrolled stores `(x & 0xF) + 16k` -> 1024x8, 1 BRAM); the 8-store variant `w9rx_wid8` (7 bits) narrowed before and after.
- **Effect:** kernels (cgeist exactclass + default pipeline): `w9rv_ifg` b 32 -> 9 bits, total 3 -> 2 = Vitis; `w9rv_wid` 32 -> 8 bits, 2 -> 1 = Vitis; `w9rx_wid8` 7 / 1 unchanged. Full v2 catalogue (frozen `hls-est.fix` md5 11cd50e9, MLIR reused from `w9rfix/after_merged.json`, `-j 4 --timeout 120`, compared with `w9r2i/after8.json`): no change: 671 compared, exact 549, in band 564, under 85, over > 15 % 22, \|err\| 36,118; harness subset 108 / 90 exact / 93 in band / 9 under / 6 over / \|err\| 461; 0 status changes; per-memref debug JSON identical on all 708 ok designs (the one array the old cutoff pinned, `16_basic_examples_vhls_double_pointer`, now gets its real range, which is full 32 bits). Gates bicg 8, gesummv 16, mvt 8, syrk 32 hold. Lit 129 pass / 1 unresolved / 1 xfail (= baseline); debug invariance 41 pass / 0 fail / 1 tool error (= baseline).
- **Reverted?:** no

## 2026-10-13 — RAM element-width narrowing: `--narrow-ram-widths` prices each array at the width Vitis stores (W9r2-impl)
- **Commit:** this entry's commit (branch `hls-est-vitis-match`)
- **Files:** new `lib/Dialect/Resource/Passes/RamWidthNarrowing.cpp` (`--narrow-ram-widths`, options `stored-ranges`, `load-demand`, `remarks`; class `Narrowing`: `buildClasses` (arrays, their views and the callee memref parameters they reach as one class), `solveRanges` / `forwardOp` / `operandRange` / `conditionBound` (stored-value ranges), `markCyclicClasses` / `feedsBackIntoSameBank` (cycles of arrays), `solveDemand` / `useDemand` / `subscriptBits` (demanded low bits), `annotate`); `MemoryDS.h` (`kNarrowBitsAttrName`, `kNarrowReasonAttrName`); `VitisCleanup.h`, `DeadMemoryElimination.cpp` (registration), `CMakeLists.txt`; `MemoryBankModified.cpp` (`bankAttrs` copies `hls.narrow_bits` / `hls.narrow_reason` to the banks); `BRAMAnalysis.cpp` (`applyNarrowWidth`; the LUTRAM test, the RAM / RAM_1WNR formula and `estimateChannel` (PIPO) use the narrowed width; ROMs keep N26's rule; debug text line `narrowed width:` and JSON key `narrow` {bits, declared_bits, reason}, additive); `common.py` (`NO_FRONT_PIPELINE`, so `DEFAULT_PIPELINE`, gets `--narrow-ram-widths` between `--auto-pipeline-unroll` and `--memory-banking-bram`); `check_debug_invariance.sh` (same default); new lit tests `test/hls-est/Passes/narrow_ram_widths.mlir` (3 RUN lines: index use 1-D / 2-D, masks, shifts, compares, ReLU / select / clamp / counter ranges, callee scalar parameter and shared memref parameter, recurrence / ROM / escape not narrowed, cyclic and complete partitions, cycles of arrays, histogram, copy between complete-partition banks) and `bram_narrow_width.mlir` (LUTRAM 64x6 = 0, 1024x6 = 1, cyclic banks 1024x6 = 1 each; debug text and JSON; debug-JSON invariance `diff`; without the pass 8).
- **What:** an array's element width for pricing is min(Wr, Wd), capped by its type. Wr: the bits of the union of the integer ranges of every stored value (MLIR's `InferIntRangeInterface` on the arith ops, loop IVs from their bounds, `scf.if` / `select` arms refined by a compare with a constant, a load having its array's range, a private callee's scalar parameter the union of its call sites, an initial value counted; a store whose value has no range at the fixpoint makes the array full range). Wd: 1 + the highest bit the loads' users demand (a subscript needs the bits of the flattened address minus a power-of-two stride, complete-partition dimensions removed; add / sub / mul / and / or / xor / select pass the demand, `& m` caps it at m's top bit, shifts and power-of-two `remui` / `divui` move it, casts cap it, a store into another array needs that array's Wd, everything else every bit). Arrays reaching one callee memref parameter share one class. Not narrowed: interfaces, escaping arrays, ROMs, non-integer elements, vector accesses. Vitis does not iterate around a cycle of arrays, so a load of an array whose stores depend on its own loads is full range and a value stored back into its own array keeps every bit, unless the load and the store reach different memories of a complete partition.
- **Why:** VITIS_NOTES N35 (46 `w9n_*` kernels) and its new item (e) (4 `w9i_*` kernels proved here, td=off: `w9i_self` 1024x64 kept, `w9i_noself` 32 / 32, `w9i_selfadd` 8, `w9i_hist` 32). The W9r2 diagnosis put width narrowing at 230 arrays in 41 designs (O18, O50 (a)). Kernels: the 50 compiled `w9n_*` / `w9i_*` kernels through cgeist (exactclass) + the default pipeline equal Vitis in total and in every memory row's width (50 of 50 with the pass, 12 of 50 without; `w9n_h3_apf` does not compile under exactclass).
- **Effect:** full v2 catalogue with and without the pass (*corrected by the W9 round 2 review:* the control `before.json` ran on the earlier frozen binary `hls-est.run`, md5 dc7cc1e8, and only `after8.json` on `hls-est.run8`, md5 fdcabaf6; a control re-run on `run8` with the pass off, `w9r2rev/off.json`, has identical totals and status on all 813 designs and identical debug JSON per memref, so the numbers stand), MLIR reused from `w9rfix/after_merged.json`, `-j 4 --timeout 120`, no status change, summed hls-est time 4762 s -> 4791 s: compared 671 -> 671, exact **543 -> 549**, in band **560 -> 564**, under 78 -> 85, over > 15 % **33 -> 22**, \|err\| **37,141 -> 36,118**. Harness subset (polybench, polybench_pragmas, forgebench, machsuite, spector_hls, vitis_examples) unchanged: 108 compared, 90 exact, 93 in band, \|err\| 461. Gates: bicg 8, gesummv 16, mvt 8, syrk 32. Newly exact (7): Analog-CIM hls baseline 154 -> 94, lab3_hanchen 22 -> 16, nayan_nair lab3 36 -> 25, nikhil_s lab3 36 -> 25, pranay_kamineny lab3 32 -> 26, vishnu_varma_venkata lab3 516 -> 340 (every narrowed index array = Vitis per array), DGNN_Booster_convLSTM over -> in band (1886 -> 1550, Vitis 1543). No exact design left exact. Left the band (3), each narrowed array now = Vitis and the design was in band by cancellation: DGNN_Booster_EvolveGCN 1950 -> 1614 / 1723 (`neighbor_table` 512 -> 176 = Vitis; `weight_buffer_1/2` 16 vs 67, pragma partition class 9 of the W9r2 diagnosis, O17 item 3 / N24), Apurba lab3 69 -> 57 / 64 (`local_column_indices_A` 16 -> 4 = Vitis; `local_row_ptr_A` 0 vs 8, O50 (b)), Ayush lab3 249 -> 243 / 247 (`B_row_indices_local` 8 -> 2 = Vitis; `row_a_col_indices` / `row_a_values` 2 + 2 have no hls-est array, the struct-element class of the diagnosis). Over -> under (narrowed arrays = Vitis, other arrays under): lab3_sushkrutha 60 -> 38 / 46, pengzhao lab3 43 -> 30 / 31, sahith lab3 460 -> 276 / 284 (`C_l` 8 vs 16, O50 (c)), shreesahitya lab3 68 -> 46 / 54 (`local_B_colptrs` 0 vs 8, O50 (b)). Arrays further from Vitis (9): gnnbuilder `neighbors` (3 instances where Vitis has 6) and `neighbor_table` (one 4200-word memory where Vitis has 7 banks) had matched by the 32-bit width, the width is now Vitis's 10; Analog-CIM optimized `input_buf` 8 bits = Vitis, 2 vs 20 BRAM (partition, as before); Dehazing `Median_Tile` 4 -> 1 / 4 because the IR stores a constant 0 into it after `--auto-pipeline-unroll` forwards a store across a call that writes the scalar through a `memref.cast` (new OPEN_ISSUES O52, wrong code before this pass).
- **Reverted?:** no

## 2026-10-13 — W9 streams review fixes: one helper serving two dataflow processes, stream arrays inside structs, unused stream-array elements, two unsound O47 cases
- **Commit:** this entry's commit (branch `hls-est-vitis-match`)
- **Files:** `BRAMAnalysis.cpp` (`sameCycleAccesses` groups by (outermost call, scope); fifo group `count_source` in the debug text and JSON, additive), `StreamFifos.{h,cpp}` (`traceHandles` carries the constant element path, `constantSubindex`, `ElementPath`, `handlesPerMember`; `analyzeStreamHandle` counts reached elements and member-array handles; `FifoGroup::countSource`), `AffineToLoopScheduleModified.cpp` (`collectMemrefAliases`: a call returning an `!llvm.ptr` escapes the memref; `cleanupRedundantAccesses`: every call is a barrier for a memref some call received); lit `bram_dataflow_channels.mlir` (new RUN `COPY`, `@top_copy`), `bram_stream_fifos.mlir` (dynamic-index, constant-index and struct-member-array cases; total 22 -> 34), `access_call_barrier.mlir` (`@captured`, `@ptr_result`); VITIS_NOTES N31 / N33 review bullets, OPEN_ISSUES O47 review bullet.
- **What:** the four defects of the adversarial review of `92544ff24` / `3453a3201` / `fead55997`. D1: a channel whose producer and consumer are two calls of one function had both accesses in one pipelined scope and was priced as one process driving two ports (separate); accesses are now grouped per process (the call they were reached through) and scope. D2: a struct member that is an array of streams counted as one FIFO; now one per element of the member array. D3: every element of an array of streams was priced; when every read / write reaches it through constant indices, only the distinct elements reached count. D4: a call returning a pointer derived from the array, and a callee that keeps the address and writes through it during a later call, could let store forwarding cross a write.
- **Why:** review kernels (Vitis 2025.1 td=off, `experiments/w9rev3_*`): `rv3p_copy` Vitis single 2 (was 4, now 2); `rv3f_arr` Vitis 12 (was 6, now 12); `rv3f_top` Vitis 2 (was 6, now 2); `o47_ptr2.mlir` / `o47_cap.mlir` (the load is kept now; the O47 binary forwarded 7).
- **Effect:** full v2 catalogue (frozen `hls-est.fix` md5 09c90e7f, MLIR reused from the O47-fix run, `-j 6 --timeout 120`; forgebench mlp and hlsyn adi timed out at `-j 6` and were re-run at `-j 2`, same totals as before): compared 671, exact 543 -> 543, in band 560 -> 560, under 78 -> 78, over > 15 % 33 -> 33, \|err\| 37,141 -> 37,141; **no estimate and no per-memref kind / BRAM changes** (as the review predicted). Harness subset 108 / 90 exact / 93 in band / \|err\| 461, unchanged; gates bicg 8, gesummv 16, mvt 8, syrk 32. The 82 PIPO and the FIFO kernels of W9-PIPO / W9-FIFO: totals unchanged (123 kernel MLIRs, 0 differ). Lit 127 pass / 1 unresolved (sha) / 1 xfail (tests extended, none added). `check_debug_invariance.sh` 41 pass / 0 fail / 1 tool timeout; the 8 review kernels identical with and without the debug flags.
- **Reverted?:** no

## 2026-10-13 — a call taking a memref is a barrier for load CSE and store forwarding only at its own position (O47-fix)
- **Commit:** this entry's commit (branch `hls-est-vitis-match`)
- **Files:** `lib/Dialect/Resource/Passes/AffineToLoopScheduleModified.cpp` (`MemrefAliasInfo::passedToCall`; `collectMemrefAliases` records a call operand there instead of in `escaped` when the call returns no memref; `isMemrefWrittenInLoop` treats `passedToCall` like `escaped`, so invariant-load hoisting is unchanged; the redundant-access cleanup comment); new lit `test/hls-est/Passes/access_call_barrier.mlir`; `OPEN_ISSUES.md` O47 resolved.
- **What:** `cleanupRedundantAccesses` (load CSE, store-to-load forwarding, dead-store elimination within a block) gave up on a memref for the whole function as soon as any use of it was a call. A callee can read and write the array only while it runs and cannot hand it back (no memref result), so the call is now an opaque use at its own position: the known values of the memref and of its views are forgotten there (the existing rule for every non-affine use), and accesses that do not span the call keep CSE and forwarding. A call that returns a memref still counts as an escape.
- **Why:** OPEN_ISSUES O47: machsuite backprop left exact when cgeist (W10) kept `update_weights` as a call (Vitis keeps it too, module `update_weights_1`); Vitis still forwards the 3-write / 2-read accumulator of `get_oracle_activations` (`oracle_activations1` Vitis ram_1p 2), whose array is passed to the call after the loop.
- **Effect:** full v2 catalogue (frozen `hls-est.o47` 1963693a, MLIR reused from the W9-PIPO run `after6_merged.json`, `-j 6 --timeout 120`; the 2 runs that timed out at `-j 6`, forgebench mlp and hlsyn adi, re-run at `-j 2` with the same totals as before): compared 671 -> 671, exact **542 -> 543**, in band 560 -> 560, under 78 -> 78, over > 15 % 33 -> 33, \|err\| 37,148 -> 37,141. 3 estimates change, all toward Vitis: machsuite backprop 42 -> **40 = Vitis** (`oracle_activations1` RAM_2P 4 -> RAM_1P 2 = Vitis; newly exact); class_ece8893_2022_fall Dehazing-denoising 26 -> 30 (Vitis 93; `Min_RGB_Tile` RAM_2P 4 -> RAM_1WNR 2 copies 8, Vitis RAM_1WNR 56: same kind now, fewer copies); accelerators DGNN_Booster_convLSTM 1887 -> 1886 (Vitis 1543; `rnn_output_vector` RAM_T2P 2 -> RAM_1P 1, Vitis has no such memory). Harness subset 108 compared / 89 -> **90** exact / 93 in band / \|err\| 463 -> 461. No design leaves exact or the band. Gates bicg 8, gesummv 16, mvt 8, syrk 32. Lit 127 pass / 1 unresolved (sha) / 1 xfail (+1 new test). `check_debug_invariance.sh` 41 pass / 0 fail / 1 tool timeout with identical outputs.
- **Reverted?:** no

## 2026-10-13 — dataflow ping-pong channels (PIPO) priced the Vitis way; channels are no longer priced as ordinary memories or replicated (W9-PIPO-impl)
- **Commit:** this entry's commit (branch `hls-est-vitis-match`)
- **Files:** new `include/circt/Dialect/Resource/Passes/DataflowChannels.h`, `lib/Dialect/Resource/Passes/DataflowChannels.cpp` (`analyzeDataflowChannel`: region, processes, producer / consumers, blocks, binding; `ProcessMerges`: processes Vitis merges; `priceChannel`: the price of one bank); `BRAMAnalysis.cpp` (option `--bram-dataflow-channels`, on by default; `sameCycleAccesses`: the path-wise second-port test; `ChannelPorts`: per bank and per array; `estimateChannel`, `tryDataflowChannel`; stdout `kind=PIPO`; debug text and JSON `pipo` record, additive: `channel`, `region`, `processes`, `blocks` / `blocks_source`, `bind_*`, `second_port`, `mode` / `mode_reason`, `rule`, or `not_channel_reason`; `status` `pipo`, `ram_type` `PIPO`); `MemoryBankModified.cpp` (`bankAttrs`: banks keep the array's `hls.stream` and `hls.bind_storage`); `CMakeLists.txt`; new lit test `test/hls-est/Passes/bram_dataflow_channels.mlir` (8 RUN lines).
- **What:** an array of a dataflow region (`hls.dataflow` on a function, or on a loop: a dataflow-in-loop body) that one process writes and a later process reads is a ping-pong channel. Processes are the calls and region ops (loops) directly in the region body; a straight-line access of the body (an invariant load hoisted in front of its loop) belongs to the next process. Not channels: function arguments, `stream type=fifo` / other non-pipo stream types, one-word arrays (scalars), completely partitioned arrays (`hls.registers`), constant fills (`hls.const_fill`: ROMs) and constant globals, an array written by two processes, read by a process ahead of its producer, or whose producer and a consumer are merged (two processes that access the same parameter of a non-top region function are one process). Blocks N: `hls.stream {type = "pipo", depth = N}`, else 2, else 1 when the region is a non-top function none of whose direct callers lies in a dataflow region. Mode: separate when some process can access the bank twice in one cycle (a pipelined scope: two accesses not on mutually exclusive `if` arms, physical or `hls.if_arms`, two loads of one address counted once; a sequential scope: two accesses of one state by the existing same-state test), with every bank of one partitioned array taking the answer of all its banks (one RAM module), or when bound to `ram_2p` / `ram_s2p`; a `ram_1p` / `ram_s2p` binding gives a process one port, so a second access waits. Price per bank: single `F(N x D, W)` at x18 (0 when N x D x W < 1024), separate `N x F(D, W)` at x18, x36 allowed when bound `ram_2p` / `ram_s2p` with one port per process (0 when D x W < 1024), N = 1 `F(D, W)` like an ordinary memory (x36 with one port per process), `impl=lutram` 0; never RAM_1WNR. `--bram-dataflow-channels=false` gives the former model.
- **Why:** VITIS_NOTES N33 (72 + 10 kernels, 143 + 46 kernel rows, 3,667 + 6,904 corpus rows), MEMOS decision #55. Two refinements from the corpus, written into N33: the mode is per RAM module, so per partitioned array (class_ece8893_spring_2025 vishnu_varma_venkata lab3 `row_ptr_A_local`: only bank 0 is read twice per iteration, one `200-740` line, all 8 banks 65x32 = 4), and processes that read a parameter of a non-top region function are merged (`[HLS 214-475] ... due to reads on`, gnnbuilder gcn, 12 lines). The S2P clamp is from `pipo_c_bindt2p` (two reads per iteration of a `ram_s2p` channel: 256x32 = 2 = 2 x 1 at x36).
- **Effect:** PIPO kernels (72 `pipo_*`, 9 `rvp_*`, `pipofix_excl2`) through the installed cgeist (exactclass) and the default pipeline: kernel totals equal Vitis 12 -> **71 of 82**; channel modes equal the Vitis log line on 119 of 121 rows (the 2 others: `pipo_m_rmwnp`, `pipo_m_chainnp`, `pipeline off` loops that `--convert-affine-to-loopschedule` pipelines, OPEN_ISSUES O20; same price). The 11 kernels left: value-range narrowing of the channel width (`pipo_p_r1/r2/s1/s2`, `rvp_p_r1/s1`, `rvp_x_wide`; O18), `syn.dataflow.default_channel=fifo` (`pipo_e_cfgfifo`, `_r2`), `stream type=fifo` on an array (`pipo_c_typefifo`) and the automatic FIFO conversion `[HLS 214-453]` (`pipo_c_loops`) (O50). Full v2 catalogue (frozen `hls-est.pipo6` dfd6a9b8, MLIR reused from the baseline, `-j 6 --timeout 120`, the 19 timeouts re-run at `-j 2`, 2 finish; hlsyn trmm-opt finishes alone in 114 s with the same 48 as before), against the W9-FIFO-impl run: compared 671 -> 671, exact **495 -> 542**, in band **507 -> 560**, under 132 -> 78, over > 15 % 32 -> 33, \|err\| **54,567 -> 37,148**; 81 estimates change; 48 newly exact (stream_hls / WIP Autoencoder, FeedForward, MultiHeadSelfAttention1 opt1 / opt2, ResMLP, atax, k3mm, mvt, k15mm*, pp4fpgas 24 multi-stage FFT), 54 newly in band. Per array: 351 hls-est channel arrays equal their Vitis rows (45,633 BRAM); 181 differ only where Vitis narrows the element width (Vitis 26,571 / hls-est 27,948; O18); 48 others (Vitis 1,982 / hls-est 958; O50). **No longer exact / in band:** class_ece8893_spring_2025 `pengzhao_liu_Lab3_2025` 31 -> 43 (Vitis 31): `bVals` 4096x16 is now the single PIPO Vitis builds (8 = 8), `bRowIdx` 4096xi32 is priced at 32 bits where Vitis narrows it to 6 (16 vs 3, O18); the design was exact by cancellation (both arrays priced as plain RAMs). Other designs further from Vitis, each explained per array: `max_beaulieu_Lab_3_2025` 58 -> 70 (Vitis 35; every channel array equal to Vitis, the over-count is the two 8192-deep m_axi adapters, 32 BRAM that Vitis does not show), `sahith_lab3_2025` 236 -> 460, `vishnu_varma_venkata` 246 -> 516, `shibao_su` 37 -> 63 (int index channels narrowed to 7-12 bits by Vitis, O18; sahith `C_l` also O50 (c)), gnnbuilder gcn 10070 -> 10102, gin 10404 -> 10451, sage 10391 -> 10464 (O18 widths +224 each; fewer region copies than Vitis, O50 (h)). The 6 stream_hls MultiHeadSelfAttention1 opt3 / opt5 copies that W9-FIFO-impl left over > 15 % (2768 / 3296) are now 2240 against 2272 / 2688 (under: O50 (g)). Harness subset: 108 compared / 89 exact / 93 in band / \|err\| 463, unchanged; gates bicg 8, gesummv 16, mvt 8, syrk 32 exact. hls-est lit 126 pass / 1 unresolved (sha) / 1 xfail (+1 new test); `check_debug_invariance.sh` 41 pass / 0 fail / 1 tool timeout with identical outputs.
- **Reverted?:** no

## 2026-10-13 — hls::stream objects priced as the FIFOs Vitis builds; stream handle storage no longer priced as a RAM (W9-FIFO-impl)
- **Commit:** this entry's commit (branch `hls-est-vitis-match`)
- **Files:** new `include/circt/Dialect/Resource/Passes/StreamFifos.h`, `lib/Dialect/Resource/Passes/StreamFifos.cpp` (`parseStreamMethod`, `mangledElementBits`, `packedTypeBits`, `priceFifo`, `analyzeStreamHandle`); `BRAMAnalysis.cpp` (option `--bram-stream-fifos`, on by default; `estimateStreamFifos`; the memref walk prices stream storage as FIFOs; debug text and JSON `fifo` record, additive, `status` `fifo`, `ram_type` `FIFO`); `CMakeLists.txt`; new lit test `test/hls-est/Passes/bram_stream_fifos.mlir`.
- **What:** cgeist lowers an `hls::stream` object to an i32 handle (`memref<1xi32>`, `memref<NxMxi32>` for an array of streams, a struct member, a static `memref.global`) that only reaches the external `hls::stream` methods. A storage whose handles reach an `_ZN3hls6streamI<T>Li<D>EE*` method as the object argument (through casts, `polygeist.subindex`, pointer casts, member GEPs and calls into callees' parameters) is now one FIFO per element (per struct member): depth from `hls.stream {depth}`, else the template argument (`Li<D>E`, 0 = default), else 2; width from the mangled element (builtin, `ap_[u]int` / `ap_[u]fixed`, so bool is 1 bit), else the packed bits of the IR type the reads / writes move (a struct is the sum of its members); price 0 when depth <= 2 or depth x width <= 1024 bits, else the depth bands at x18 (`estimateBlocksBand(.., sdp=false)`), `bind_storage type=fifo` impl=srl/lutram 0, impl=bram the bands at any size. A stream that is only constructed costs 0. Stream parameters (top ports, callee parameters) are never priced: the FIFO is the caller's storage. The handle storage is no longer priced as a RAM (w9prep: 173 BRAM on the corpus). `--bram-stream-fifos=false` gives the former model.
- **Why:** VITIS_NOTES N31 (41 kernels, 199 FIFO rows; every one of the 20,754 + 277 corpus FIFO rows), MEMOS decision #53. The 41 N31 kernels through cgeist + the default pipeline: every FIFO row as N31 predicts; kernel totals equal Vitis on 32 of 41, the 9 others are the 3 config/option kernels (`fifo_c_cfg`, `fifo_e_cfgstorage`, `fifo_e_srlbits`), the struct-member pragma (`fifo_d_member`, OPEN_ISSUES O48), bool inside a struct element (`fifo_rvf_struct`, O48) and 1-word wide temporaries priced as RAMs (`fifo_rvf_small_a/c`, `fifo_w9fx_a/bits`, O49).
- **Effect:** full v2 catalogue (frozen `hls-est.fifo1` 9520263a, MLIR reused from the baseline `hlsest-2026-10-13-v2-defaults.json`, `-j 6 --timeout 120`, 3 timeouts re-run at `-j 2`): compared 671 -> 671, exact **342 -> 495**, in band **352 -> 507**, under 293 -> 132, over > 15 % 26 -> 32, \|err\| **179,732 -> 54,567**; 254 estimates change, 153 newly exact, 155 newly in band, none leaves exact or the band. On every design with a Vitis hierarchy walk (w9prep `vhier.json` + `vhier_inband.json`) hls-est's FIFO BRAM equals the Vitis FIFO table per design (53,681 = 53,681). The 6 new over > 15 % are the 3 copies each of stream_hls MultiHeadSelfAttention1 opt3 (1897 -> 2768, Vitis 2272) and opt5 (2267 -> 3296, Vitis 2688), under-counts before: their FIFOs now match (+871 / +1029 as w9prep predicted), the remaining over-count is the dataflow ping-pong / replication class (w9prep "PIPO+repl" -832 / -1248, the W9-PIPO model). Harness subset (6 datasets of the v2 run): 108 compared / 89 exact / 93 in band / \|err\| 463, unchanged (no streams); gates bicg 8, gesummv 16, mvt 8, syrk 32 exact. hls-est lit 125 pass / 1 unresolved (sha) / 1 xfail (+1 new test); `check_debug_invariance.sh` 41 pass / 0 fail / 1 tool timeout with identical outputs.
- **Reverted?:** no

## 2026-10-13 — lit: Estimation CHECKs regenerated under the current defaults (W1b part 1, MEMOS #16)
- **Commit:** this entry's commit (branch `hls-est-vitis-match`)
- **Files:** `tools/hls-est-validate/regen_estimation_checks.py` (rewritten: `split_header`, `top_by_name`, `main`), the 42 `test/hls-est/Estimation/**/*.mlir` headers, `MEMOS.md` row 16
- **What:** every Estimation input is run as the harness runs a design: top from the catalogue (`run_hlsest.find_top`, by name only for inputs without a design), `--symbol-privatize=exclude=<top> --symbol-dce`, `common.DEFAULT_PIPELINE`, `--bram-analysis=top=<top> math-costs=%S/.../math_costs.json`. Header: RUN, `CHECK: Total BRAM: <current hls-est total>{{$}}` (the `{{$}}` stops 13 from matching 130), a `Vitis 2025.1 td=off:` comment (value and results/vitis.json key, or "no ground truth" with the reason). The other comments at the top of a file are kept below it. An input hls-est fails on gets no RUN line, only a comment with the error (no XFAIL). The old script wrote `XFAIL: *` for failures, removed all leading comments, and its RUN lines had no top selection, no top= and no math costs. The 24 inputs that had no RUN line now have one, except chstone_xilinx/sha.mlir: hls-est cannot parse it (`expected non-function type` at `memref<xi32>`, a stale input), so it stays unresolved with that comment.
- **Why:** MEMOS #16: lit checks the current model, and the harness compares with Vitis. The defaults are settled (W1 / W2).
- **Effect:** lit `test/hls-est` 97 pass / 4 fail / 24 unresolved / 1 expected failure -> **124 pass / 0 fail / 1 unresolved / 1 expected failure** (126 tests; this is the new baseline). The 4 forgebench CHECK-BRAM failures (activation_op1, tiled_attn_module, tiled_attn_p1, tiled_attn_p2) had RUN lines with no matching CHECK prefix. Against Vitis, 23 of the 36 inputs that run and have ground truth are exact (machsuite 10, spector_hls 5, polybench 8 of 9); not exact: gesummv 21 vs 135, flowgnn gin 256 vs 1158 and pna 0 vs 20600, forgebench activation_* / attention_* / tiled_attn_* (these MLIR files are older frontend output than the harness's MLIR). No estimator change, so harness numbers are unchanged.
- **Reverted?:** no

## 2026-10-13 — math cores: W2-impl review fixes (instances in sequential code, fabs of double, logic_only check, one warning per unlisted pair, pipeline lit coverage)
- **Commit:** this entry's commit `hls-est: math-function cost configuration review fixes` (branch `hls-est-vitis-match`)
- **Files:** `lib/Dialect/Resource/Passes/MathCores.cpp` (`analyzeMathCores`: sequential instances; new `mayOverlapInSequentialCode`, `accessedRoots`; removed `maxIndependent`, `forwardReach`; `MathCostConfig::load`: logic_only check), `lib/Dialect/Resource/Passes/CallInstances.cpp` / `include/.../CallInstances.h` (`groupConcurrentCalls`: optional `alsoOrdered` callback, default unchanged), `test/hls-est/Passes/math_costs_{rules,config}.mlir`, `math_costs.json` (entry `abs` of double; instancing-rule texts), generator `~/hls-est-work/exp-src/mathcost/make_math_costs.py` (outside the repo), VITIS_NOTES N16 (new R10, (i) resolved), OPEN_ISSUES O46
- **What:**
  - **D1 (over-count):** calls in the two arms of an `if` were two instances. Sequential calls of one core in one function are now grouped by CallInstances' `groupConcurrentCalls`, the rule of non-inlined callees: value and memory dependences and exclusive `if` arms (`hls.if_arms` included) order calls; the instances are a minimum chain cover.
  - **D2 (under-count):** stretches no longer share by a maximum. Calls in different stretches are ordered (share) unless they lie in two different loops of the function body that access no common array; those may run concurrently and the dependence rule decides. Copies from hls-est's automatic unroll of a loop Vitis keeps sequential: one body copy stands for them (as before).
  - **m1:** a `logic_only` entry with `bram_18k` other than 0 is a configuration error; `operator` and `inline_tables` are documented (code and table text) as counted like `per_concurrent_call`.
  - **m2:** new table entry `abs` of double (C `fabs`, logic only, 0 BRAM, measured); the unlisted-pair warning is issued once per (function, type, argument form) per module, not once per calling function.
  - **m3:** lit coverage of scheduled `loopschedule.pipeline` regions (ceil(calls / scheduled II), user and automatic source, a chain live through the stage registers, CORDIC table kept / folded by the pipeline source, a pipelined array fill folded).
- **Why:** W2-impl review D1 / D2 / m1-m3. Vitis kernels (td=off, exp of ap_fixed<32,16>, 5 BRAM per core; VITIS_NOTES N16 R10): `w2rv_excl` 5 (hls-est was 10, now 5); `w2rv_twoloops` two disjoint `pipeline off` loops, outlined `_Outline_L1/_L2`, 10, latency of one loop (was 5, now 10); new `w2fx_twoshared` (both loops read one array) 5, `w2fx_twodep` (second loop reads what the first wrote) 5, `w2fx_twonest` (the disjoint loops inside an outer loop) 5, `w2fx_around` (calls before and after a loop) 5, `w2fx_loopdirect` (call in a loop and after it) 5; hls-est now equals Vitis on all 8 (with `w2rv_scauto` 4). A first version that let every pair of independent calls overlap gave 10 on `w2fx_around` / `w2fx_loopdirect` / `w2fx_twoshared` / `w2fx_twonest` and was replaced before commit. `w2fx_abs_f64_std`: `fabs(double)` 0 BRAM, 0 LUT.
- **Effect:**
  - **Harness subset, exactclass, installed cgeist (m1, md5 8f27f3c0), fresh MLIR, private HOME** (`~/hls-est-work/coord/scratch/w2fix/runs/B2.json` head binary b56f2c87 -> `F2.json` fixed binary): compared 108, exact 89 -> 89, \|err\| 463 -> 463, goal band 93 -> 93; 0 designs change total or any math-core record. (With the W2-impl MLIR `w2impl/runs/H3d.json` / `S2d.json`: exactclass 83 / 107, \|err\| 1562, scalar 60 / 107, \|err\| 22673, both unchanged.)
  - **HLSFactory v2, the 144 designs with math (re-run of `V2merged.json`'s commands with math-costs, head vs fixed binary, `runs/v2math.json`; 107 with v2 ground truth):** exact 24 -> 24, \|err\| 68737 -> 68684. Changed: adithi lab2 2092 -> 2071 (Vitis 2057; exp_33_9 6 -> 3 instances, Vitis 1), whetstone 48 -> 16 (Vitis 0; dead math, O46), and instance counts of 0-BRAM cores (anirudh, zihan_qi exp, backprop sqrt). ## 2026-10-13 — math cores: hls-est prices the hls:: math cores from the cost table (`--bram-analysis math-costs=`, W2-impl, MEMOS #18, VITIS_NOTES N16)
- **Commit:** `4278ab3f1` (hls-est: `MathCores` + `--bram-analysis` option + lit) and this entry's commit (harness: shim markers, cost table, run_hlsest / sweep / compare / check_semantics, notes). Frozen binary `~/hls-est-work/coord/scratch/w2impl/bin/hls-est.w2d` (md5 `b56f2c87...`), cgeist c25 (md5 `555d1d05`, Polygeist ddfa8ee1), private HOME `~/hls-est-work/coord/scratch/w2impl/home`.
- **Files:** `lib/Dialect/Resource/Passes/MathCores.cpp`, `include/circt/Dialect/Resource/Passes/MathCores.h` (new: `MathCostConfig::load` / `lookup`, `analyzeMathCores`, `onlyFillsArrays`, `portBoundII`, `keptSequential`, debug text / JSON / stdout line); `BRAMAnalysis.cpp` (option `math-costs=`, `mathCoreDebugRecord`, `MemrefRecord::math`); `CMakeLists.txt`; lit `test/hls-est/Passes/math_costs_{config,rules}.mlir` (new); `tools/hls-est-validate/shim/exactclass/{hls_math.h,cgeist_flags,README.md}` (markers, `hls::pow` of ap_fixed); `math_costs.json` (regenerated by `~/hls-est-work/exp-src/mathcost/make_math_costs.py`: 47 entries, keys `cordic_loop_unrolled_in`, `shares_instance_with`, `blocks_automatic_pipelining`, the W2-exp review's text fixes); `run_hlsest.py` (`--math-costs` default `math_costs.json`, `--no-math-costs`, `add_math_costs_args`, `resolve_math_costs`, `with_bram_option`, `bram_option_supported`; result `math_costs`, meta `math_costs`, `math_costs_md5`, `math_costs_applied`), `sweep_hlsfactory_v2.py` (same options), `compare.py` (`math_core:<module>` rows from the Vitis instance tables), `check_semantics.py` (drops `-DHLSEST_MATH_MARKERS` from native builds, links marker stubs); VITIS_NOTES N16; OPEN_ISSUES O45, O46; MEMOS #18.
- **What:**
  - **Recognition:** a math dialect op or a call of an external libm function, typed by its MLIR element type (f16 half, f32 float, f64 double); under the exactclass shim, a call of the marker `__hlsest_math_<fn>(args..., W, I, S)`, which the shim's fixed-point functions now call instead of the libm builtin when cgeist defines `HLSEST_MATH_MARKERS` (from `cgeist_flags`): the ap_fixed type survives cgeist. Op forms found (v1 kernels, all three shims): float / double / half math -> `math.exp|log|sqrt|sin|cos|powf` or `call @tanh[f]`; ap_fixed under scalar / exact -> the same ops on f32 / f16 / f64 (type lost); ap_fixed under exactclass -> `call @exp|log|sin|tanh(f64)` (sqrt: `math.sqrt`) inside inlined integer code with `call @floor` -> the marker replaces that call. `hls::pow` of ap_fixed did not compile under exactclass (ambiguous); the shim now has it.
  - **Pricing:** the entry of (function, type, argument form) gives BRAM per instance; folded calls (all-constant arguments, and new: calls that only fill a local array from loop indices and constants) and dead ones cost 0; unlisted pairs warn and cost 0. Instances: ceil(calls / II) per pipelined loop (an unscheduled one gets the requested II raised to two accesses per array per cycle), the largest set of independent calls of a sequential stretch (shared across stretches), sin + cos of one ap_fixed argument share one CORDIC, the CORDIC table folds in a loop that unrolls it (sin / cos: only a pragma-pipelined loop; tanh: any), a core with `blocks_automatic_pipelining` (double sin / cos) in an automatically pipelined loop is sequential with hls-est's unroll copies counted once, and a callee kept as a module multiplies its cores by its instances.
  - **Output:** one `math.core function=... type=... func=@... instances=N bram=B (...)` line per core and function on stdout (counted in the total), a `math_core` pseudo-memref in `--bram-debug` / the debug JSON (additive, version 2: function, type, arguments, config entry, core, kind, instancing, regions, sites).
  - Without `math-costs=` nothing changes.
- **Why:** MEMOS decision #18 (the programmer sets the math costs in a configuration file). Vitis evidence: N16 (W2-exp, 191 kernels) and its W2-exp review fixes; the earlier W2-impl kernels (`w2i_sin_auto128`, `w2i_tanh_auto128`, `w2i_rope_{full,nopow,args,tanh}`, `w2i_cx_*`: automatic pipelining keeps the sin / cos CORDIC loop, unrolls tanh's); new kernels of this step (td=off, `~/hls-est-work/exp-src/mathcost/w2i/`): `w2i_exp33_9` (`hls::exp` of ap_fixed<16,5> minus ap_fixed<32,8>: `exp_33_9_s` 7 BRAM, D41), `w2i_powce_f64` (C `pow(x, 2)` of double: `pow_generic_double_s` 30, D70), `w2i_ivtab64` / `w2i_ivtab1024` (cos fill of a 64 / 1024-entry table: no core, the table a ROM) and control `w2i_ivdata64` (one `sin_or_cos_double_s`, loop not pipelined).
- **Effect:**
  - **Neutrality of the marked shim (no math-costs):** harness subset (112 designs) with the original exactclass shim (`--mlir-from` the coordinator's c25 control run) against fresh MLIR from the marked shim: 0 status or total differences, 0 per-memref differences; 21 MLIR files differ (the forgebench designs with ap_fixed math: callee name and three constants). The no-math baseline equals the control run made with hls-est f1 for both shims (0 differences). HLSFactory v2: the 235 designs with a math op or a cgeist error before, recompiled with the marked shim, against the c25 sweep: 1 difference, a load timeout with byte-identical MLIR (WIP Autoencoder opt3, O43).

## 2026-10-13 — W8b review fixes: two wrong-code bugs in cgeist (for counter after `break`, C++ condition variables), any stray pragma token is a Vitis-style warning, a design whose top is missing is no longer "ok", unmodelled polygeist ops on memrefs are an error
- **Commit:** Polygeist `66a8afca` (wrong code), `ddfa8ee1` (pragma tokens), branch `hls-est-vitis-match` in `/mnt/d/HLS`; circt: this entry's commit. Private cgeist `~/hls-est-work/coord/scratch/w8b-fix/cg/cgeist.c25` (md5 555d1d05, Polygeist `ddfa8ee1`), not installed; frozen hls-est `~/hls-est-work/coord/scratch/w8b-fix/hls-est.f1` (md5 08f8b51e, = `build/clion`).
- **Files:** Polygeist `lib/polygeist/Passes/CanonicalizeFor.cpp` (`carriedOnlyUnderGuard`, `ForBreakAddUpgrade`); `tools/cgeist/Lib/CGStmt.cc` (`storeKeepRunningForCondition`; `VisitForStmt`, `VisitWhileStmt`, `VisitDoStmt`, `VisitIfStmt`, `VisitSwitchStmt`); `tools/cgeist/Lib/HLSPragmaParse.cc` (`PragmaParser::parse`); cgeist lit `Test/HLS/regressions/{for-break-exit-value.c,condition-variable.cpp,pragma-unexpected-number.c}`, `Test/HLS/parse/syntax-errors.c`. circt `lib/Dialect/Resource/Passes/PolygeistCompat.cpp` (`LowerSubIndexPass::checkUnmodelledOps`), lit `test/hls-est/Passes/polygeist_unknown_memref.mlir`; `tools/hls-est-validate/run_hlsest.py` (`process`: status `top not found`), `make_hlsfactory_report.py` (`classify`); VITIS_NOTES N32; OPEN_ISSUES O43; regenerated `hls-doc/hlsfactory-v2.{md,csv}`.
- **What / Why** (the W8b review's defects 1-6):
  - **Wrong code (defect 1):** `for (i = 0; i < n; i++) { if (..) { ..; break; } }` left i one too high. cgeist's own IR was right; Polygeist's `ForBreakAddUpgrade` (canonicalize-scf-for) picked the "continue" value i + 1 of the inner `scf.if` for every user, although only the counter copy whose for result is unused may take it (MoveWhileToFor keeps a second, used copy fed by the same value). It now rewrites only that copy's yield slot, and only when the counter is read nowhere but under the loop's no-break guard (in its other branch only as its own carried value). Same for the select and add forms of the pattern.
  - **Wrong code (defect 2):** `while (int v = f())` built the declaration under the loop's own keepRunning flag, unset before the first test and false after a `continue` (garbage, or a loop that never ends); `for (; int v = f();)` and `switch (int v = ..)` never initialised v; `if (init; c)` / `switch (init; c)` dropped the init statement. The condition variable is now built inside the condition's no-break guard (so a `break` also skips it), keepRunning is reset to the no-break flag before a while / do condition, and the init statements are visited.
  - **Count (defect 3):** the 3 `top-not-in-sources` designs (Dehazing debayer_csynth and script, sreenidhireddy lab3) were status `ok` with BRAM 0 computed on an `ap_detail::bits` helper. `run_hlsest.process` now gives a design whose top is not among the MLIR functions (fallback rule `single` / `last`) the status `top not found` without running hls-est; `make_hlsfactory_report.classify` classes it as before. No design of the harness subset or of the v2 sweep uses the fallback otherwise (h10 and the subset checked).
  - **Load (defect 4):** OPEN_ISSUES O43 records that the v2 "ok" count depends on `-j` and load; every figure below names both.

## 2026-10-13 — cgeist: W10 review fixes to the automatic inlining decision (always_inline before the size count, pipeline-implied unroll, misplaced dataflow pragma, unroll budget; VITIS_NOTES N18)
- **Commit:** Polygeist `baa1f85e` (branch `w10-autoinline`, worktree `/home/peter/w10/HLS`, on `b1067998`); circt: this entry, VITIS_NOTES N18 (W10 review bullet), `hls-doc/frontend-pragmas.md` 3.2. Private binary `/home/peter/w10/cgeist.w10g` (md5 `ef3fe0bd71e70177f5dfa93d575a1544`), not installed.
- **Files:** Polygeist `tools/cgeist/Lib/HLSAutoInline.{h,cc}` (`measure`: always-inliner first, `estimateUnrolled` / `unrollCopies` and `--hls-auto-inline-unroll-budget`; `unrollFactor` / `markUnrollLoops`: pipeline-implied complete unroll; `forEachEffectiveCall`: counting and marking through always_inline bodies; `recordPipelineLoop`, `recordPipelineFunction`), `Lib/HLSPragmaSema.cc` (`FunctionSema::process`: records pipeline loops / functions and `unroll off` loops, only for placed pragmas; `inFunctionOrForBody`: a misplaced `dataflow` is dropped with a warning), `docs/hls-pragmas.md`; lit `Test/HLS/regressions/auto-inline-review.cpp` (new), `auto-inline-vitis.c` (`ai2`), two regenerated hlsfactory tests (`neuro_monarchs`, `siddharth_khatri lab3`).
- **What:** (D2) clang's always-inliner runs before the size count, as in N18's `clang -O0` proxy; the first implementation summed each always_inline callee's separately simplified size - 2, which measured every `ap_fixed` helper far too large. Calls inside an always_inline body count in, and are marked in, the caller it is inlined into (a conflict between two callers of one always_inline function keeps the call: not modelled, no corpus case). (D1) `#pragma HLS pipeline` on a loop or a function unrolls every loop nested in it completely before the count (an explicit `unroll` wins, `unroll off` stays rolled, unconfirmed). (D3) a `dataflow` pragma not directly in a function body or a `for` body gets cgeist's warning and is dropped, so its callees follow the size rule. (Guard) when the unroll for measuring would make a function larger than 65536 instructions, its size and calls are estimated from the trip counts instead of running LLVM's unroller (it did not finish class lab2 `hanchen` in 10 min, a 4 x 100 x 128 nest under a pipelined loop; Vitis's ground-truth run of that design is a `vitis timeout`, 3820 s); such a function is kept by every caller.
- **Why:** W10 adversarial review D1-D3 with Vitis kernels: `rv_apf` (matrix_add on ap_fixed: Vitis 25 at `cmplx 002`, inlined; cgeist 68 kept -> 32 inlined), `rv_ai_pipe_inner`, `w10f_pipe_deep`, `w10f_fnpipe` (214-291, `g` x 4 kept), `rv_df_nested3`, `w10f_df_forblock`, `w10f_df_while` (207-5576, callees inlined by 214-178), `w10f_ai1`, `w10f_ai_h2` (always_inline inlined by 214-131 first, `cmplx 002` shows 2 calls of `g51` in the caller, no 214-178 for `g51`). Runs `~/hls-est-work/experiments/{rv_*,w10f_*}`.
- **Effect:** full default catalogue (787 designs), exactclass shim, default pipeline, frozen hls-est `/home/peter/w10/hls-est.w10c` (md5 `51d600db`), `-j 4`; `/home/peter/w10/runs/r6_F2.json` (cgeist.w10d) -> `r7_G.json` (cgeist.w10g). Against Vitis td=off: compared 104 -> 104, exact 80 -> 80, \|err\| 17920 -> 17920, goal band 84 -> 84; harness subset compared 95, exact 78, \|err\| 470, unchanged. No design with ground truth changes total. The harness changed between the two runs (W8b added `-D__SYNTHESIS__ ... -std=gnu++14` and other cgeist attempts), so pp4fpgas huffman_encoding (`cgeist error` -> 21) and hlsyn aes (a different first `cgeist error`) are harness effects, not this change. Kept-call sets changed in 26 designs: no longer kept (D2, as Vitis's 214-178): gpt_transformer_p1 / llama_transformer_p2 `matmul_*`, `matrix_add`, `relu`; mlp `relu`, `softmax`; GradCAM `cam_core`, `avg_pool_core`, `store_output_tile`; gnnbuilder `mean_incremental_update`, `sum_incremental_update`, `hls::recip` (gcn_qm9 214-178 inlines `recip<32,16>`, `recip<64,32>`, `sum_incremental_update`); lab3 siddharth_khatri `get_A_elements`, `get_B_elements`, `compute_C` (D3); newly kept: neuro_monarchs `conv1x1_downsample` (D1; Vitis `cmplx 002` 1579 instructions, still a function at `004`; cgeist 1313), gabriel_lopez_lugo lab3 `load_indices`, `load_values`, `store_values` (Vitis modules of those names), shibao_su lab2 `hls::exp<31,8>` (an hls:: shim size, W11; Vitis run timed out). ## 2026-10-12 — cgeist: the calls Vitis does not inline automatically stay calls (`--hls-auto-inline`, W10, MEMOS #34, VITIS_NOTES N18)
- **Commit:** Polygeist `b1067998` (branch `w10-autoinline` in the private worktree `/home/peter/w10/HLS`, on top of `989a2035`; the coordinator merges it onto `hls-est-vitis-match`); the exactclass shim change and this entry in the circt commit that adds it. Private binary `/home/peter/w10/cgeist.w10d` (md5 `dfac86b63367338bcca2fda9e562da5e`), not installed.
- **Files:** Polygeist `tools/cgeist/Lib/HLSAutoInline.{h,cc}` (new: `InlineSizeConsumer`, `measure`, `markUnrollLoops`, `MarkAutoInlinePass`), `Lib/HLSPragma.cc` (`HLSFuncInlinerInterface::isLegalToInline` refuses calls marked `hls.inline_auto_off`), `Lib/HLSPragmaSema.cc` (records the loops with `#pragma HLS unroll` for the size measurement), `Lib/clang-mlir.cc` (`MLIRAction::CreateASTConsumer`: the size consumer next to cgeist's), `driver.cc` (the mark pass right before the inliner), `CMakeLists.txt` (LLVM `Passes`), `docs/hls-pragmas.md`; the Vitis stand-in headers `Test/HLS/Inputs/vitis/ap_{int,fixed}.h` and the circt shim `tools/hls-est-validate/shim/exactclass/ap_{int,fixed}.h` (`#pragma clang attribute push(always_inline)`, as Vitis' `AP_INLINE`); lit `Test/HLS/regressions/auto-inline-vitis.c`, `auto-inline-invalid-source.cpp` (new), `stream/stream.c` (KEEP prefix), 159 `Test/HLS/hlsfactory/**.test` (regenerated pragma remarks: a remark now names the kept callee instead of each caller it was inlined into); `hls-doc/frontend-pragmas.md` section 3.2.
- **What:** with `--hls-annotate`, a second AST consumer runs clang's own LLVM codegen (-O0 without optnone, location-only debug info), unrolls the loops that carry `#pragma HLS unroll`, simplifies every function with `sroa, early-cse, instcombine, simplifycfg, dce` and counts its instructions without the integer casts. Right before cgeist's inliner, `hls-mark-auto-inline` walks the call graph bottom-up and applies N18: per caller C and callee F without an inline pragma, all calls of F in C are kept iff size(F) x calls(F in C) > 100; pointer / reference returns, `inline` callees and `always_inline` callees are inlined; dataflow processes (calls at the top level of a `dataflow` function) and callees holding a dataflow region are kept; an inlined callee adds size - 2 per call to its caller. Kept calls get `hls.inline_auto_off = {calls, size}` (the callee `{size}`); hls-est treats them like `inline off` calls (commit `6e3fb02b8`). Explicit pragmas still win. `--hls-auto-inline=false` restores the old MLIR (identical, byte for byte up to locations, on every design of the catalogue, see Effect); `--hls-auto-inline-threshold`, `--hls-auto-inline-dataflow`, `--hls-auto-inline-report` are the knobs. The size consumer stops at the first error of the source (it crashed in clang's codegen on an invalid AST: in an intermediate build 69 designs with a source error, e.g. soda `no viable overloaded '='`, became `cgeist crash` instead of `cgeist error`; fixed, all 69 are `cgeist error` again as with the installed cgeist; lit `auto-inline-invalid-source.cpp`).
- **Why:** N18 (`[HLS 214-178]`, 122 + 21 kernels, 136 corpus logs): the rule reproduces 1057 of 1061 corpus decisions on Vitis's own counts; the size proxy reproduces 146 of 150 kernel decisions. Vitis keeps 123 modules without any pragma in 39 of the 131 designs with a result; cgeist inlined all of them, so their memories were charged once instead of once per instance (N14 R1-R3), or their arrays were merged into the caller's.
- **Effect:** frozen hls-est `/home/peter/w10/hls-est.w10c` (md5 `51d600db`), default pipeline, exactclass shim, full default catalogue (787 designs), `-j 4`; runs `/home/peter/w10/runs/r3_E.json` (installed cgeist c7be40ee, md5 `7d923368`), `r3_C.json` (`cgeist.w10c` with `--hls-auto-inline=false`), `r5_F.json` (`cgeist.w10d`), plus three reruns merged into `r6_F2.json` (below).
  - Control: `--hls-auto-inline=false` (C) and the installed cgeist (E) give the same MLIR (locations aside) for every design both compile; the only status difference is testing_unroll (the merge entry below).
  - Against Vitis td=off, installed (E) -> W10 (F, both W10 commits; the merge entry's testing_unroll is one of the 7 newly exact, the other 6 come from this rule), all compared designs: compared 103 -> 104, exact 73 -> 80, \|err\| 19028 -> 17920, goal band 77 -> 84 (over > 15 %: 9 -> 6, under: 17 -> 14). Harness subset (112 designs): compared 94 -> 95, exact 71 -> 78, \|err\| 1571 -> 470, goal band 74 -> 81.
  - Newly exact: forgebench conv_A 99 -> 594, conv_B 275 -> 586, conv_C 323 -> 610 (the conv2d callee is kept, size 33661 / 34722, 1 call), mult_op_p1 6 -> 2 (`gemm_ijk` kept), mult_op_p2 4 -> 2, mult_op_p3 4 -> 2 (`mmv_ij` kept), testing_unroll error -> 2 (merge entry). polybench_pragmas bicg 8, gesummv 16, mvt 8, syrk 32 exact. No exact design stops being exact.
  - Closer, not exact: chstone sha 32 -> 24 (Vitis 7): `sha_transform` (139 instructions) is kept, as in Vitis (two `sha_transform` instances in the csynth report); its `W[80]` is now charged per instance (2 x 8) instead of per inlined copy (3 x 8). The remaining 16 are the pre-existing classification of `W` (Vitis: no memory in `sha_transform`, 0 BRAM).
  - Away by 1: accelerators DGNN_Booster_EvolveGCN 1927 -> 1928 (Vitis 1723, in band both times): `GRU_cell_sigmoid` (308) is kept with 2 instances, as in Vitis (`GRU_cell_sigmoid`, `GRU_cell_sigmoid_1`); its local `rnn_output_vector[67]` (1 BRAM) is now charged per instance. Vitis has no memory in either instance: the array is written and read back at the same index in the same iteration, which Vitis forwards (O7, `forward-stores` off): a pre-existing per-array over-count, now counted twice.
  - Designs without ground truth: 45 change status or total (E -> F). 18 hls-est timeouts become estimates because the processes / kept callees are now separate functions (12 WIP and 4 stream_hls dataflow designs, class_ece8893_spring_2025 neuro_monarchs 36106 and apurba (cgeist timeout before) 361); 4 more timeouts become estimates with unchanged MLIR (load noise: hlsyn-vitis 3mm, gemm-p, nw; class_ece8893_2022_fall test_systolic_array). 9 class_ece8893_spring_2025 and 3 gnnbuilder totals change (no Vitis result). forgebench mlp 136 -> hls-est timeout at 120 s; with 600 s it finishes in 167 s at 1030 (`runs/r6_mlp.json`; the 4 kept callees `gemm_ijk_bias` x2, `relu_256_128`, `softmax_256_16`). Its td=off Vitis run timed out at 3600 s, so it is not compared, but the record's totals (written before the timeout) say BRAM 1030 (informational). The 14 mismatches are the same, first difference byte for byte, with the `--hls-auto-inline=false` MLIR (C) as candidate: 10 differ only against the real headers (shim equal: header semantics, W11), 4 against both (forgebench gpt_transformer_p1 = O37, fixed by W8b 524525de, not in this branch's base; class_ece8893_spring_2025 apurba_prasad_padhy, davendra_maharaj, mohammad_zain). Keeping calls changes no result. Results `/home/peter/w10/sem/sem{F,C}.json`.

The W10 tests (`auto-inline-vitis.c`, `auto-inline-invalid-source.cpp`, `array-partition-merge.c`, `array_partition/errors.c`, `stream/stream.c`, `inline/`) and every hlsfactory test pass. hls-est is unchanged by this entry (lit not rerun)..

## 2026-10-12 — cgeist: two `array_partition` pragmas on one dimension are merged the Vitis way instead of rejected (W10, MEMOS #33, VITIS_NOTES N17 (c), N28)
- **Commit:** Polygeist `989a2035` (branch `w10-autoinline`, private worktree `/home/peter/w10/HLS`); VITIS_NOTES N28 and this entry in the circt commit that adds it.
- **Files:** Polygeist `tools/cgeist/Lib/HLSPragmaSema.cc` (`FunctionSema::placePartition`, `mergePartition`, `withDim`), `Lib/HLSPragma.cc` / `HLSPragmaInternal.h` (`State::remark`), `docs/hls-pragmas.md`; lit `Test/HLS/regressions/array-partition-merge.c` (new), `Test/HLS/array_partition/errors.c`; `hls-doc/frontend-pragmas.md` section 3.1 (new page, linked from `hls-doc/README.md`).
- **What:** a second `array_partition` on a dimension an earlier one partitions is merged per dimension: the same type keeps the larger factor whatever the order, `complete` wins over cyclic or block in either order, a `dim = 0` entry is split per dimension first; cyclic against block stays an error (Vitis' own `[HLS 214-290]`). The dropped pragma gets a remark naming it and a note at the pragma that wins. `--hls-reject-partition-conflicts` restores the old error.
- **Why:** N17 (c) (`fbh6_conf_up/down`) and N28 (10 W10 kernels `w10k_*`: cyclic 2/4 in both orders, block 2/4, complete against cyclic and block in both orders, `dim = 0` against `dim = 1`, cyclic against block with equal and different factors). OPEN_ISSUES O28 item 3.
- **Effect:** full default catalogue (787 designs), exactclass shim, default pipeline, frozen hls-est `/home/peter/w10/hls-est.w10c` (md5 `51d600db`); installed cgeist c7be40ee (`/home/peter/w10/runs/r3_E.json`) -> W10 cgeist with `--hls-auto-inline=false`, i.e. this commit alone (`r3_C.json`): forgebench testing_unroll `cgeist error: empty module` -> 2 = Vitis (BRAM_2 cyclic 2 and 4 on dim 1 -> factor 4). Every other design that compiled keeps its MLIR. Harness subset: compared 94 -> 95, exact 71 -> 72, \|err\| unchanged. lit: `array-partition-merge.c` and `array_partition/errors.c` pass (full lit run in the auto-inline entry).
- **Reverted?:** no

## 2026-10-13 — cgeist (W8b private build c23): no condition after a `break`, IfAndLazy wrong code, a stray number in a pragma, C++ `--i` as an lvalue, `std::nullptr_t`; the Vitis front-end flags become the harness default (MEMOS #54); 19 more HLSFactory_v2 designs estimated, semantic check 612 equal
- **Commit:** Polygeist `ba032913` (loop conditions after `break`; IfAndLazy), `fe364b78` (a number where a pragma expects an option name), `9c83fd76` (prefix decrement), `715f02c1` (`std::nullptr_t`), branch `hls-est-vitis-match` in `/mnt/d/HLS`; circt: this entry's commit (run_hlsest.py `VITIS_FRONTEND` on by default, MEMOS #54, VITIS_NOTES N32, OPEN_ISSUES O30 / O31 removed and O44 updated, the regenerated `hls-doc/hlsfactory-v2.{md,csv}` and `hls-doc/semantics.md`). Private binary `~/hls-est-work/w8b9/cg/cgeist.c23` (md5 6f5e9c9e), not installed; frozen hls-est `~/hls-est-work/w8b9/hls-est.p10` (md5 93c03a94).
- **Files:** Polygeist `tools/cgeist/Lib/CGStmt.cc` (`beginNoBreakGuard`, `endNoBreakGuard` in `VisitForStmt`, `VisitCXXForRangeStmt`, `VisitDoStmt`, `VisitWhileStmt`); `lib/polygeist/Ops.cpp` (`IfAndLazy`); `tools/cgeist/Lib/HLSPragmaParse.cc` (`PragmaParser::parse`); `tools/cgeist/Lib/clang-mlir.cc` (`VisitUnaryOperator` UO_PreDec, `getMLIRType` for `nullptr_t`, `VisitCXXNullPtrLiteralExpr`); cgeist lit `Test/HLS/regressions/{break-skips-condition.c,pragma-unexpected-number.c,predec-lvalue.cpp,nullptr-t.cpp}`, `Test/HLS/hlsfactory/hlsyn-vitis/hlsyn-vitis__vivado-target__sources__aes.test` (the two pragma diagnostics are warnings now); circt `tools/hls-est-validate/run_hlsest.py`.
- **What / Why:**
  - **Wrong code, found by `check_semantics.py`:** after a `break` cgeist still evaluated the loop condition and only then combined it with the no-break flag, so `do { if (..) break; } while (++i > 0)` incremented i on the break (machsuite aes_tableless `gf_log`: mismatch against both native builds, OPEN_ISSUES O44). The condition is now built inside `scf.if` on the flag (else false); without a break the flag is constant and the IR is as before. Structuring the guard exposed a Polygeist canonicalization bug: `IfAndLazy` gave the else branch of the if it moves into the outer if's then branch the outer if's *else* values, which dropped the final increment of `while (i++ < 100)`; it now maps that branch with the values of the branch it sits in. Reduced kernels `~/hls-est-work/w8b9/aes/{dw,wh,wh1,brk,k_*}.c`.
  - `#pragma HLS unroll 2`: Vitis warns ([HLS 207-5571]) and drops the pragma (VITIS_NOTES N32, hlsyn-vitis aes ground-truth run); cgeist rejected it with an error.
  - In C++ `--i` is an lvalue like `++i`; `for (i = 8; --i;)` asserted in `VisitCastExpr` (hlsyn-vitis aes).
  - `std::nullptr_t` is a null pointer (`!llvm.ptr`), the `nullptr` literal `llvm.mlir.zero`; the type stopped cgeist with "unhandled type" (auto_ntt fwd_load_inv_store_poly_1_limbs, fwd_store_inv_load_poly_1_limbs through the TAPA headers).
  - `VITIS_FRONTEND` (N19's `-D__SYNTHESIS__ -D__VITIS_HLS__ -D__HLS_SYN__ -DAESL_SYN`, `-std=gnu++14` / `-std=gnu99`) is on by default (MEMOS #54, under delegation #36): Vitis compiles every design this way, TAPA's headers and pp4fpgas huffman_encoding need `__SYNTHESIS__`, and hlsyn-vitis aes needs C++14 (`register`). `VITIS_FRONTEND=0` restores the old command and MLIR cache keys.
- **Effect:**
  - HLSFactory_v2 sweeps (`~/hls-est-work/w8b7/runs/`, hls-est p10): h8 (c17) -> h9 (c17, `VITIS_FRONTEND=1`): hls-est ok 678 -> 690; 16 designs compile and are estimated (15 auto_ntt modules, pp4fpgas huffman_encoding 21); no estimate of the 674 designs ok in both changes; the MLIR of 11 designs changes (hls::stream code under `__SYNTHESIS__`), not their estimates. h9 -> h10 (c22, flags on): 692 ok; hlsyn-vitis aes 1 BRAM; the MLIR of 31 designs changes (loops with `break`), no estimate of the 687 designs ok in both changes. c23 adds the two auto_ntt fwd modules (0 BRAM each, checked one by one; its two changes reach only code that aborted before). Timeouts move with machine load (stencil-3d, stream_hls Autoencoder / ResMLP opt3 / opt4 and their WIP copies, near 120 s; OPEN_ISSUES O43). Every transition of the 3 sweeps among designs ok on both sides keeps its total.

## 2026-10-12 — hls-est: fully unrolled bodies get scheduled within the time limit (W8b, class "hls-est timeout")
- **Commit:** `6011eb6e0` (dependence pairs bucketed by the constant indices every access of a memref shares; address comparisons without affine differences when two addresses differ only in constant terms; invariant-access promotion in one loop rewrite), `871f3e291` (`fast-ops-threshold`, the bounded positive-cycle test, the shift-invariant dependence memo; its message says 13 designs, the count is 11), branch `hls-est-vitis-match`.
- **Files:** `lib/Analysis/DependenceAnalysis.cpp` (`dimsConstantInAll`, `stripConstantTerms`, `PairKey::constantDelta`, `checkMemrefDependence`); `lib/Dialect/Resource/Passes/AffineToLoopScheduleModified.cpp` (`compareAddresses`, `FastModuloScheduler::build` / `asapTimes`, `hasParentCycle`, the scheduler choice in `scheduleLoop`); `lib/Dialect/Resource/Passes/AutoPipelineUnroll.cpp` (`provablyDisjoint`, `isPromotableIn`, `promoteInvariantAccesses`); `include/circt/Conversion/Passes.td` (`fast-ops-threshold`, default 16384); lit `test/hls-est/Passes/fast_schedule_recurrence.mlir` (a RUN line with `fast-ops-threshold=1`, same IIs).
- **What:** the HLSFactory_v2 designs that timed out at 120 s spend their time on fully unrolled bodies (hlsyn-vitis design points with full `unroll` pragmas, stream_hls opt3/opt4: 75,000 to 315,000 operations in one pipelined loop). Profiles (gdb samples, `--mlir-timing`, `--debug-only=fast-modulo-size`): (1) the simplex scheduler building its dense tableau for a 143,903-operation problem with fewer than 150 memory operations (hlsyn-vitis adi); it now goes to the greedy scheduler above 16384 operations; (2) the greedy scheduler's Bellman-Ford test ran n rounds per infeasible II (adi: 220,750 edges, 10,092 carried); it now stops after carried-edges + 1 rounds, and as soon as the relaxing predecessors close a cycle (such a cycle has a positive weight, so the II is infeasible; without a positive cycle the rounds run as before and the times are the same); (3) the dependence analysis and the redundant-access and promotion passes compared every pair of addresses of a memref with affine differences; pairs that differ only in constant terms are now compared directly, and the memo key is shift-invariant (`src(x) + cs = dst(y) + cd` has the solutions of `src(x) - dst(y) = cd - cs`).
- **Why:** run time only; no estimate may change. The 16384 threshold is above every problem the simplex scheduler solves today within the time limit (the largest seen on the subset: 2,895 operations, mlp) and is a designer knob for the run time, not a Vitis rule.
- **Effect:** frozen `hls-est.p10` (md5 93c03a94), cgeist c17, exactclass shim, default pipeline.
  - Harness subset (default catalogue, MLIR of `w8b6/runs/sub_n4.json`, c11): compared 107, exact 80, \|err\| 1584, before and after; every design's status, total and per-memref record unchanged; polybench_pragmas bicg 8, gesummv 16, mvt 8, syrk 32. Slowest subset designs: diff_orders_p1 60 s -> 39 s, mult_op_module_mm 56 s -> 34 s, mlp 50 s -> 29 s.
  - HLSFactory_v2 sweeps (`~/hls-est-work/w8b7/runs/`): `6011eb6e0`: n4 -> h6 hls-est ok 650 -> 662 (11 timeouts, plus chstone sha from the polygeist.undef entry below); `871f3e291`: h7 -> h8 another 11 timeouts finish (hlsyn-vitis fdtd-2d, heat-3d, jacobi-2d, stencil-3d; stream_hls Autoencoder opt3, ResMLP opt4 and their four WIP copies, WIP ResMLP opt3). On the 666 designs ok in both h7 and h8 every per-memref record is identical (paths aside).
  - Still over 120 s: 19 designs; with a 900 s limit (`~/hls-est-work/w8b9/prof/long9/summary.txt`, binary p9, which lacks only the shift-invariant memo) 10 of them finish (adi 132 s, trmm-opt 170 s, syr2k 182 s, syrk 190 s, doitgen / doitgen-red 270 s, symm 544 s, trmm 596 s, seidel-2d 741 s, class lab apurba lab2 162 s); 9 do not (OPEN_ISSUES O43).
  - Lit `test/hls-est`: 94 pass, 4 fail, 24 unresolved, 1 expected failure (the baseline). `check_debug_invariance.sh`: 41 pass, 0 fail, 1 tool timeout with identical outputs (as before).
- **Reverted?:** no

## 2026-10-12 — cgeist (W8b private build c17) and hls-est: console stream output dropped, a C++ top selected by its source name, no code for functions clang rejected, a cast pointer to an array passed to an array parameter, polygeist.undef and unmodelled Polygeist ops parse; 16 more HLSFactory_v2 designs estimated
- **Commit:** Polygeist `b49ce6dc` (stream I/O), `a3b201a5` (`--function` by source name), `73bde58b` (no code for functions with semantic errors), `95a7a567` (pointer-to-array argument), branch `hls-est-vitis-match` in `/mnt/d/HLS`; circt `6b84c1080` (polygeist.undef, unregistered polygeist ops, memrefs of `!llvm.func`). Private binary `~/hls-est-work/w8b9/cg/cgeist.c17` (md5 8c52e044), not installed.
- **Files:** `Polygeist/tools/cgeist/Lib/CGCall.cc` (`VisitCallExpr`: calls on a standard stream; `castCallerMemRefArg`: rank + 1); `Polygeist/tools/cgeist/Lib/clang-mlir.cc` (`hasSemanticErrors`, `MLIRASTConsumer::run`, the `--function` match on source and qualified names); cgeist lit `Test/HLS/regressions/{stream-io-dropped,function-by-source-name,no-codegen-after-errors,pointer-to-array}.cpp`; circt `include/circt/Dialect/Resource/Passes/PolygeistCompat.h`, `lib/Dialect/Resource/Passes/PolygeistCompat.cpp` (`UndefOp`, `allowUnknownOperations`, `LLVMFunctionType` as a memref element); lit `test/hls-est/Passes/polygeist_undef_unknown.mlir`.
- **What / Why:**
  - Console output (`std::cout << ... << std::endl`, `os.flush()`) is removed, as Vitis' reflow removes it (VITIS_NOTES N30, kernel `w8b7_cout1`); an argument with a side effect is still evaluated. cgeist used to emit the libstdc++ internals the calls reach, whose `llvm.load` of a memref of `!llvm.struct` hls-est rejected (8 designs).
  - `--function=<name>` also matches a C++ function's source or qualified name, as `set_top` names the top; a design whose `'*'` run stops in a function outside the top's call graph reached an empty module (FocusedACO FACO).
  - A function clang's semantic analysis rejected (an invalid declaration, error-recovery expressions in its body) is not emitted: cgeist reports the errors and fails instead of asserting in `ValueCategory::getValue` (FocusedACO LocalSearchOptimized, whose `uint32_t` needs `<stdint.h>`; its class is W11's standard-include class).
  - A cast `(T (*)[8][8]) p` has cgeist's type for a pointer to an array, the pointee's `memref<8x8xT>`; passed to `T a[4][8][8]` it gave an invalid `func.call`. It is now re-typed through its address, which is how C addresses the parameter (GradCAM src_hls). The type mapping itself is wrong code when such a pointer is indexed past its first row; the fix there broke 22 cgeist lit tests and was reverted (OPEN_ISSUES O42).
  - hls-est: `polygeist.undef` (a variable read before any assignment on some path, chstone sha) is a pure op without storage; Polygeist ops hls-est does not model (`polygeist.get_func`) parse as unregistered ops of the polygeist dialect; `memref<?x!llvm.func<...>>` parses.
- **Effect:** frozen hls-est as in the entry above; exactclass shim, default pipeline.
  - HLSFactory_v2 sweeps: h6 (c11) -> h7 (c15): 9 designs fail -> ok (the 8 stream-output designs: class_2022 Explainable-ML 79, Yolo-v3-tiny 24-layer synth 241 and 4-layer synth 63; class_2023 DSC Golden_C 116 and HLS optimized 120, YOLOv4-Tiny CSP_Layer1Layer2 133, Conv2D_Layer1Layer2 33, Maxpool_Layer 15; FocusedACO FACO 0); h7 -> h8 (c17): GradCAM src_hls 122. The MLIR text of every design that had MLIR before is unchanged (h6 -> h7 -> h8, 695 designs). With chstone sha (`6b84c1080`), 16 designs gained an estimate.
  - Harness subset: no design's MLIR changes (each fix applies only to code these designs do not contain; checked on their v2 copies).
  - cgeist lit (`Test/HLS`, hlsfactory excluded, private lit config `~/hls-est-work/w8b8/cglit`): 112 pass, 3 unsupported.
  - Semantic check (`check_semantics.py --ref real,shim` over h7, `~/hls-est-work/w8b9/sem/sem_h7.json`): 626 designs checked, 609 equal, 17 mismatch (14 only against the real headers, shim equal: header semantics, W11; machsuite aes_tableless and class lab davendra_maharaj lab2 differ from both references: OPEN_ISSUES O44; apurba lab2's candidate divides by zero, now counted as no valid run). Before (2026-10-11 run): 292 checked, 279 equal, 13 mismatch.
- **Reverted?:** no (the getMLIRType part of the pointer-to-array fix was reverted before the commit, see O42)
