## 2026-10-12 — hls-est: a callee cgeist keeps because Vitis would not inline it automatically (`hls.inline_auto_off`) is treated like an `inline off` callee (W10, MEMOS #34, VITIS_NOTES N18)
- **Commit:** the commit that adds this entry (branch `hls-est-vitis-match`).
- **Files:** `include/circt/Dialect/Resource/Passes/CallInstances.h` (`kInlineAutoOffAttr`, `isKeptCallee`); `lib/Dialect/Resource/Passes/HlsInliner.cpp` (`HlsFuncInlinerInterface::isLegalToInline`); `lib/Dialect/Resource/Passes/MemrefBankClassification.cpp` (`BRAMClassification::enumerateBanks`: a kept callee's parameters are a callee's); `lib/Dialect/Resource/Passes/BRAMAnalysis.cpp` (`InstanceRecord::inlineAutoOff`, debug text "(not inlined automatically)", JSON `"inline_auto_off"`, additive); tests `test/hls-est/Passes/inline_auto_off_kept.mlir` (new), `call_instances_bram.mlir` (JSON key).
- **What:** W10's cgeist (`--hls-auto-inline`, Polygeist branch `w10-autoinline`) keeps every call Vitis would not inline automatically and marks the call and the callee `hls.inline_auto_off`. hls-est now keys its kept-callee handling on either attribute: `--inline` keeps such calls, the classification treats the callee's array parameters as a callee's (untracked, two ports, N14 R6), and the instance model already counts every remaining `func.call` (N14 R1-R3), so a kept callee's memories are charged once per instance. The instance model needed no change: it keys on the call, not on the attribute.
- **Why:** N18 (`[HLS 214-178]`): Vitis keeps a callee without a pragma when size x calls > 100; a kept callee is its own module with its own memories per instance, exactly as for `inline off` (N14).
- **Effect:** none on MLIR from the installed cgeist (c7be40ee), which never writes the attribute: hls-est lit unchanged (93 pass, 4 pre-existing forgebench failures, 24 unresolved, 1 expected failure); `check_debug_invariance.sh` 41 pass, 0 fail, 1 tool timeout with identical outputs. The harness effect with W10's cgeist is in the W10 cgeist entry.
- **Reverted?:** no

## 2026-10-12 — hls-est: a sequential loop whose bound reads an affine.apply keeps a valid bound in --convert-affine-to-loopschedule (W8b, class "hls-est error")
- **Commit:** the commit that adds this entry (branch `hls-est-vitis-match`).
- **Files:** `lib/Dialect/Resource/Passes/AffineToLoopScheduleModified.cpp` (new `composeBoundApplies`, called at the start of `lowerAffineStructures`); new test `test/hls-est/Passes/loop_bound_apply.mlir`.
- **What:** before the affine structures are lowered, every `affine.apply` that feeds a bound of an `affine.for` is folded into the bound map (`fullyComposeAffineMapAndOperands` + `canonicalizeMapAndOperands`). The lowering expands each `affine.apply` into arith ops, and an arith value computed inside a loop is no valid affine dimension, so a loop left in place (not pipelined) whose bound read an apply stopped with "'affine.for' op operand cannot be used as a dimension id". Loops whose bounds read no apply are untouched.
- **Why:** plain C semantics, no Vitis behaviour: polybench nussinov's `for (k = i + 1; k < j; k++)`, whose `j` cgeist computes as an apply of the enclosing counters. All 7 nussinov designs of polybench__reproducible failed with this error (HLSFactory_v2 sweep).
- **Effect:** harness subset (default catalogue, exactclass, cgeist c7be40ee, MLIR reused from `~/hls-est-work/w8b5/runs/subset-h2.json`; hls-est `w8b6/bin/hls-est.h3`, md5 780d176f): every one of the 112 designs keeps its status and total (`subset-h3.json` vs `subset-h2.json`); compared 94, exact 71, \|err\| 1571, unchanged. polybench_pragmas bicg 8, gesummv 16, mvt 8, syrk 32 exact. The 14 cached nussinov MLIR files (7 designs, two cgeist builds): error -> Total BRAM 0. lit: 4 pre-existing forgebench failures, 24 unresolved, 1 expected failure (the new test passes).
- **Reverted?:** no

## 2026-10-12 — hls-est: cgeist's pointer casts and arrays of structs parse; a local array of structs is split into one memref per member (W8b, class "hls-est cannot parse the MLIR"; VITIS_NOTES N29)
- **Commit:** the commit that adds this entry (branch `hls-est-vitis-match`).
- **Files:** `include/circt/Dialect/Resource/Passes/PolygeistCompat.h`, `lib/Dialect/Resource/Passes/PolygeistCompat.cpp` (ops `Pointer2MemrefOp`, `Memref2PointerOp`, the dialect's inliner interface, `registerPolygeistMemRefElementTypes`, `aggregateElementBits`); new `lib/Dialect/Resource/Passes/SplitStructMemrefs.cpp` (`--split-struct-memrefs`: `StructSplitter`, `inlineStructCall`); `tools/hls-est/main.cpp` (registration); `BRAMAnalysis.cpp` (`MemrefRecord::structMember`, debug text "struct array split", JSON `struct_member`, additive); `tools/hls-est-validate/common.py` and `check_debug_invariance.sh` (`--split-struct-memrefs` after `--inline` in the default pipeline); tests `test/hls-est/Passes/polygeist_pointer_casts.mlir`, `split_struct_memrefs.mlir`.
- **What:**
  - `polygeist.pointer2memref` / `polygeist.memref2pointer` (cgeist's casts between `!llvm.ptr` and memrefs: string literal arguments such as `hls::stream<T> s("name")`, member accesses, pointer comparisons) are registered as pure casts that own no storage. Before, hls-est stopped at the parser on 141 v2 designs (W8b base sweep: pointer2memref 97, memref2pointer 44).
  - `memref<... x !llvm.struct<...>>` / `!llvm.array` parse: the LLVM aggregate types get MLIR's `MemRefElementTypeInterface`, as Polygeist registers it (26 v2 designs stopped with "invalid memref element type").
  - `--split-struct-memrefs` (default pipeline, after `--inline`) replaces a local `memref.alloca` / `memref.alloc` of structs whose every use it understands by one memref per scalar leaf (an array member adds its dimensions), and turns the `memref2pointer` + `llvm.getelementptr` + `llvm.load` / `llvm.store` accesses into memref accesses; the member functions that receive an element view are inlined first. Anything else keeps the struct memref, with a warning (its accesses stay invisible, as before). An aggregate element that stays is reported as not estimated.
- **Why:** Vitis disaggregates a local array of structs into one memory per member, each priced on its own (VITIS_NOTES N29, kernels w8b3_st1 / st2 / sp1); the casts are what cgeist emits for plain C/C++ (O25).
- **Effect** (harness subset, default catalogue, default shim exactclass, installed cgeist c7be40ee (copy `~/hls-est-work/w8b3/bin/cgeist.c7be40ee`), private HOME; before: hls-est of HEAD 0643d7730's C++ (`w8b3/bin/hls-est.base`) with the previous default pipeline, after: this commit's build (`w8b5/bin/hls-est.h2`, md5 a2a7151e) with the new pipeline; runs `~/hls-est-work/w8b5/runs/subset-{base,h2}.json`, `compare.py` against `results/vitis.json` (md5 5f7616d5)): hls-est ok 91 -> 96 of 112; compared 89 -> 94; exact 68 -> 71; \|err\| 1567 -> 1571. Every design keeps its status and total except the five that now run: spector_hls/matrix_multiplication 0 (Vitis 0, exact), vitis_examples/12 accs_w_asserts 0 (exact), 15 pointer_casting 0 (exact), 02 fir_filter 1 (Vitis 3: the class member `fir1[84]` lives in a global of struct type, which the pass does not split (N29 unconfirmed case), so it is "not estimated"; Vitis prices it 2), 17 axi_master 2 (Vitis 4: `buff` 2 = Vitis; the other 2 are not in a memory row of hls-est). No design that was exact stops being exact. polybench_pragmas bicg 8, gesummv 16, mvt 8, syrk 32 exact. lit: 4 pre-existing forgebench failures, 24 unresolved, 1 expected failure (as before; the two new tests pass). `check_debug_invariance.sh`: 41 pass, 0 fail, 1 tool error with identical outputs. The v2 sweep result is in the W8b sweep entry.
- **Reverted?:** no

## 2026-10-11 — cgeist: W5 review fixes (D1: a marked op or wide cast read unsigned is raised only when provably non-negative; D2: no termination assumption for loops that access volatile objects)
- **Commit:** Polygeist c7be40ee (branch `hls-est-vitis-match` in `/mnt/d/HLS`); circt: the commit that adds this entry. **Not installed.** Private binary: `/home/peter/tmp/w5fix3/cgeist.d15`, md5 7d9233685c50fe57cdc36997d6650271 (W5's `cgeist.i2b`, md5 95b960fc, is superseded; `build/bin/cgeist` is untouched).
- **Files (Polygeist):** lib/polygeist/Passes/AffineCFG.cpp (`unsignedReadKeepsValue`; `exactArith`, `peelExactCasts`, `roundTripKeepsValue` use it; `IntRangeOracle` ranges for `scf.parallel` / `affine.parallel` induction variables and the gpu thread / block ids and dims); lib/polygeist/Passes/CanonicalizeFor.cpp (`mayDoObservableWork`, `WhileToForHelper::terminationImpliesNoWrap`); include/polygeist/Passes/Passes.h (`kVolatileAccessAttrName` = `polygeist.volatile_access`); tools/cgeist/Lib/clang-mlir.cc (`VolatileAccessFinder`, `accessesVolatile`, set in `MLIRScanner::init`); tools/cgeist/Lib/HLSPragma.cc (`HLSFuncInlinerInterface::processInlinedCallBlocks` carries the mark to the caller); tools/cgeist/driver.cc (`stripFrontendAssumptions` removes the mark); Test/HLS/regressions/nowrap-unsigned-read.c (new), Test/HLS/regressions/narrow-counter-assume-termination.c (4 volatile cases).
- **What:**
  - D1 (wrong code, older than W5): AffineCFG raises a `polygeist.nsw` add / sub / mul and a cast at least 32 bits wide without a range proof; both are exact only as a value read signed. Read unsigned (through an `extui` / `index_castui`) and narrower than 64 bits, a negative value is 2^N more, so such a read is now raised (and an index -> i32 -> index round trip read unsigned is folded) only when the range oracle proves the signed value non-negative. A truncation marked `polygeist.nuw` needs no proof; a 64-bit value needs none (it reaches an index only through `index_castui`, which keeps its bits). `-affine-cfg-assume-nowrap-width=N` keeps the old behaviour. The tighter rule made 2 Verification tests (CUDA kernels reading `threadIdx` etc. through unsigned casts) stop raising; the oracle now knows that gpu thread / block ids and dims lie in [0, 2^31 - 1] and gives `scf.parallel` / `affine.parallel` induction variables their bound ranges, which restores both.
  - D2 (W5 item 2): cgeist does not represent C `volatile` on memref accesses, so the termination assumption (`-canonicalize-for-assume-termination`) raised `for (uint8_t k = 0; k < n; k++) *p = k;` with `volatile int *p` although C11 6.8.5p6 does not allow assuming that such a loop terminates. The frontend now marks every function whose body (or constructor initialisers) contains a glvalue of volatile-qualified type `polygeist.volatile_access`; inlining passes the mark to the caller; CanonicalizeFor does not apply the assumption to a loop in a marked function or calling one. The mark is removed from the output unless `-keep-frontend-assumptions`.
- **Why:** the W5 adversarial review (D1: reproducer `scratchpad/w5rv2/q1.cpp` `q08`, ref -232039 vs cgeist 51355; D2: `scratchpad/w5rv2/v1.cpp`), N15's own recommendation ("keep `scf.while` when the body has a volatile access").
- **Effect:**
  - Harness subset (default pipeline, frozen hls-est md5 071f97ba, the review's harness copy and vitis.json md5 5f7616d5, private HOMEs, `--force-cgeist`; `/home/peter/tmp/w5fix5/h/h_{x,s}_d15.json` vs W5's `/home/peter/tmp/w5rev/h2/h_{x,s}_w5.json`): the MLIR of all 112 designs is byte-identical to W5's under exactclass and under scalar. exactclass 55 / 89 exact, \|err\| 5243; scalar 45 / 89, \|err\| 28718; both unchanged. Gates exact (bicg 8, gesummv 16, mvt 8, syrk 32). The only status difference, forgebench/mlp under scalar (`hls-est timeout 120s`), is load: re-running its recorded command on the identical MLIR gives 270 (as W5) in 53.5 s.
  - Differential execution against native clang 18 (`scratchpad/w5fix/batt5.sh`, 16 probe sets, the fix binary and W5's i2b side by side): fix binary 430 OK, 1 BAD; i2b 413 OK, 18 BAD. The 17 fixed are all D1 shapes (q08 of the review, `rf_d1` f01-f06 / f08 / f09 / f14, `rf_rt` g01, `rr_nsw` r01 / r02 / r11, `rv_nsw` p01 / p02 / p20). The remaining BAD, `rv_nsw` p16_u32_counter_wrap (a 32-bit unsigned counter that wraps three times), fails identically with i2b and 2ec1956c: the counters CanonicalizeFor creates for loops as wide as int are assumed not to wrap (`counterStaysInRange`), a pre-existing rule outside this fix. 4 probes crash or time out in the lowered program identically under both binaries (lit_d1, rf_sym, f4_exit segfault; n1_exact, n2_countdown time out under load).
  - Identity: 136 inputs (the 100 Test/HLS lit sources other than hlsfactory, the 35 N15 narrow-counter kernels, the review probe q1.cpp) x {exact, exactclass}. With `-canonicalize-for-assume-termination=false -affine-cfg-assume-nowrap-width=32` the output equals 2ec1956c's except in narrow-counter-assume-termination.c (its new volatile functions); with default options it equals W5 i2b's except in that test and q1.cpp (q08 now correct). Measured on the previous fix build d13 (`/home/peter/tmp/w5fix3/offid13`); the final build d15 (adds the null-type guard of `VolatileAccessFinder` and the parallel / gpu ranges) gives byte-identical output to d13 on all 136 x 2 x 2 compiles (`/home/peter/tmp/w5fix5/id`).
  - d13 crashed on 4 hlsfactory tests (GradCAM linear_fc, gnnbuilder fpga_{sage,gin,gcn}_qm9): `VolatileAccessFinder` asked a type-less expression of a template for its qualifiers; d15 skips such expressions.
  - Polygeist lit (private lit tree, fix binary): HLS without hlsfactory 96 pass, 2 fail, 3 unsupported (the new nowrap-unsigned-read.c passes; the 2 failures `array_partition/lowering.c` and `pipeline/lowering.c` fail with 2ec1956c too); Verification 135 pass, 8 expected failures; hlsfactory 692 pass, 84 expected failures, 0 fail (as W5).
- **Reverted?:** no

## 2026-10-11 — hls-est: W7 review fixes: conditional constant stores stay RAMs, a filler's constant is taken per call, unrolled runtime-indexed reads that hit distinct banks read each bank once (VITIS_NOTES N26 additions, N27)
- **Commit:** the commit that adds this entry (branch `hls-est-vitis-match`).
- **Files:**
  - `lib/Dialect/Resource/Passes/ConstantFills.cpp` (review D1, D2): `collectFill` carries the array's scope and the chain of calls it followed; a store or a call on the way that sits under an `scf.if` / `affine.if` inside the scope makes the array a RAM (`isConditionalIn`); `evaluate` takes a callee parameter as the operand of the call on that chain, not the union over every call (the old union stays for a parameter of the function the array itself lives in).
  - `lib/Dialect/Resource/Passes/MemoryBankModified.cpp` (review D3): `steerLoadGroups` (with `residueBase`, `hoistBefore`), run by `rewriteAccesses` before the per-access rewrite. Runtime-indexed `memref.load`s of one block on a one-dimension cyclic / complete partition whose indices are one base plus constants distinct modulo the factor become one load per bank (address = the offset of the copy that hits that bank) and one select per copy; groups whose offsets collide, block partitions, several split dimensions and affine loads keep the per-copy rewrite.
  - Lit: `test/hls-est/Passes/constant_fills_path.mlir` (new: conditional store, call under `if`, array declared inside the `if`, one filler with 1 and 300), `test/hls-est/Passes/banking_steered_loads.mlir` (new: four distinct offsets -> 4 loads, RAM_1P; two colliding offsets -> unchanged, RAM_2P).
  - Notes: VITIS_NOTES N26 (review additions), N27 (new); OPEN_ISSUES O18 (cond0), O41 (new: runtime).
- **Why:** the W7 adversarial review, Vitis 2025.1 td=off kernels `~/hls-est-work/exp-src/w7rv/` (runs `experiments/w7rv_*`): `w7rv_cond0` `buf 4096x1 RAM_AUTO_1R1W` 1 BRAM (hls-est HEAD: `zero_rom`, 0); `w7rv_twoconst` A `4096x1 ROM_AUTO_1R` 1 + B `4096x9 ROM_AUTO_1R` 2 = 3 (HEAD 4); `w7rv_rt4` 4 banks `ram_1p`, II 1, four sparsemuxes, 8 (HEAD and pre-W7: 24, RAM_1WNR x3 per bank).
- **Effect** (frozen `~/hls-est-work/w7f/bin/hls-est.f1` md5 94b1bd37..., the commit's code; x_g3 MLIR, exactclass, default pipeline, whole catalogue, `--timeout 600`; run `~/hls-est-work/w7f/r/f1.json`, against W7 HEAD run `w7/r/a6.json`):
  - subset exact 68 -> **68** / 89, \|err\| 1567 -> **1567**; whole catalogue 70 -> **70** / 95, \|err\| 1952 -> **1952**; forgebench 23 / 1420 unchanged. No design's total changed; the only differences are 9 designs without Vitis ground truth (stream_hls Autoencoder_opt3, ResMLP_opt3 / opt4 and their WIP copies) that timed out at a6's 120 s and finish under 600 s; 4 time out at 600 s in both (ResidualBlock_opt4 x3, class_ece8893_spring_2025 lab2_hanchen).
  - Kernels (default pipeline, `w7f_k.sh`): w7rv_cond0 0 -> 8 (Vitis 1: the RAM is narrowed to 1 bit, O18; was right by accident before), w7rv_twoconst 4 -> **3** = Vitis, w7rv_rt4 24 -> **8** = Vitis; w7rv_rt2 8, once 8, twice 8, w7_dl_local 4, w7_dl_toplocal 8, every w7_rf* / w7_rtbl* ROM kernel unchanged (w7_rf_iota still 8 vs 3). dead_globals: dg_c_const0_cond 0 -> 1 (Vitis 0: `buf 512x1` RAM in LUTs; the 1-bit narrowing is O18, like dg_c_const01 / const0_x2, which give 1 too); the other dead_globals kernels unchanged. The 13 T2 replication kernels (`t2/mlir_vitis`, T2 pipeline) unchanged: 136, 16, 24, 32, 64, 128, 136, 72, 128, 15, 0, 32, 16.
  - Gates: bicg 8, gesummv 16, mvt 8, syrk 32 exact. Lit `test/hls-est`: 89 pass, 4 fail (the baseline activation_op1, tiled_attn_module / p1 / p2), 24 unresolved, 1 expected failure, of 118. Debug invariance (build/clion = f1): 38 / 42 pass, 0 fail, 4 ERR as before.
- **Reverted?:** no

## 2026-10-11 — hls-est: an array filled by one constant store is a ROM of that constant, and every ROM is priced at the width its values need; `--mark-constant-fills` joins the default pipeline (W7 item 3, forgebench-gap C5 / O18; VITIS_NOTES N26; MEMOS #45)
- **Commit:** the commit that adds this entry (branch `hls-est-vitis-match`).
- **Files:**
  - `lib/Dialect/Resource/Passes/ConstantFills.cpp` (new), `VitisCleanup.h`, `DeadMemoryElimination.cpp` (registration), `CMakeLists.txt`: `--mark-constant-fills`. An alloca / global written by exactly one store, reached once (directly, or in a private callee through the parameter it is passed to), whose stored value evaluates to an integer constant (constants; side-effect-free ops on them, folded by the op's own folder; a private function's parameter every call gives a constant), gets `hls.const_fill = <width>` (N26's rule over the values, a dense initial value included; 0 when all are 0).
  - `tools/hls-est-validate/common.py`: `DME_FRONT` = `--lower-polygeist-subindex --inline --mark-constant-fills --canonicalize --sccp --vitis-fold --dead-memory-elimination` (the mark must see the values before the folds: forgebench attention's `scores` becomes `store 0` only through `--vitis-fold`, and Vitis keeps it a 16-bit RAM). `check_debug_invariance.sh`: the same default.
  - `lib/Dialect/Resource/Passes/BRAMAnalysis.cpp`: `narrowRomWidth`; `estimateBRAM` gets every get_global site of a global (`allSites`) so that a global's reads are its ports also without `--bram-attribute-globals`. A marked array that is read and written is a ROM of its read ports at the marked width; a constant-initialised global that nothing writes is a ROM at the narrowed width, now also without `--bram-attribute-globals`. Width 0: status `zero_rom`, 0 BRAM; narrowed under 1024 bits: LUTRAM. The ROM reason names the width ("element width W of D bits from its values (N26)"). `MemoryBankModified.cpp`: banks inherit `hls.const_fill` (and the other marks of their array) through one list.
  - Lit: `test/hls-est/Passes/rom_narrowing.mlir` (new: a fill of 300 -> 9 bits, a constant table of 6 -> 2 bits, an all-zero fill -> no memory). Updated because a ROM is now priced at the width its values need: `bram_formula_rom_ir`, `bram_rom_initialised_global`, `bram_attribute_globals` (the first table word becomes 0x9adf1b1e, the first word of the Vitis kernels' random 32-bit tables, so the tables stay 32-bit as in those kernels; without the flag the constant table is now a ROM), `bram_top_scope`, `call_chain_depth` (splat tables of 3 / 7, which would narrow to 1 bit, become full-width splats), `bram_debug_text` (its unattributed global is uninitialised now, so it still shows the unattributed path).
  - Notes: VITIS_NOTES N26 (new); OPEN_ISSUES O18 narrowed; MEMOS #45.
- **Why:** VITIS_NOTES N26: 16 kernels (`exp-src/{w7rom,w7rom2,w7rom3}`) and forgebench activation_op1 (`FM_buffer_3_U 50176x12 ROM_AUTO_1R`; an `internal constant` global at a.o.1, `i12` at a.o.2), with the dead_globals kernels for which arrays stay RAMs (`dg_c_const01`, `dg_c_const0_x2`, `dg_c_const0_cond`) and `dg_d_apfixed16` / attention_op_p1 for the ordering against the folds.
- **Effect** (default pipeline with the mark, exactclass, x_g3 MLIR, 787 designs; before = W7 item 2, run `w7/r/a3.json`; after = run `w7/r/a6.json`, frozen `w7/bin/hls-est.a6` md5 24137668..., the commit's code):
  - subset: exact 67 -> **68** / 89, \|err\| 1583 -> **1567**, goal band 71; whole catalogue: exact 69 -> **70** / 95, \|err\| 1968 -> **1952**, goal band 73.
  - Newly exact: forgebench activation_op1 194 -> **178** (`FM_buffer_3` 64 (50176x16 RAM_1P) -> 48 (50176x12 ROM_1P) = Vitis). No other compared design moved; none lost. (An intermediate version that marked every array whose stores were all constant at --bram-analysis time gave the same harness numbers, `w7/r/a4.json`, but priced the test's attention `scores` at 0, against Vitis's 1.)
  - Kernels (cgeist 2ec1956c, exactclass, default pipeline; `~/hls-est-work/w7/romk/`): every fill kernel = Vitis: w7_rf6 1, rf7 1, rf9u 2, rf12u 3, rf17u 4, rf_apf 3, rfneg 3, rfneg1 1, rf_part 2, rf_stride 2 (before, item-2 binary: 8 each, 4 for the 16-bit rf12u / rf_apf / rfneg1); w7_rf_iota 8 vs 3 (a computed fill, not modelled). Every constant table = Vitis: rtbl9 2, rtblneg 3, rtbl_16 1, rtbl_gap 2, rtbl_hi 4 (before 8 each). dead_globals (`~/hls-est-work/w7/dgk/`): dg_c_const0 0, const5 1, const0_cond 0, d_int0 0, d_int0_single 0, overwrite 1, d_apfixed16 2 = Vitis; dg_c_const01 1 and const0_x2 1 vs Vitis 0 (several constant stores: a narrowed RAM, O18).
  - Gates: bicg 8, gesummv 16, mvt 8, syrk 32 exact. Lit `test/hls-est`: 87 pass, 4 fail (the baseline activation_op1, tiled_attn_module / p1 / p2), 24 unresolved, 1 expected failure, of 116. Debug invariance (`hls-est.a6`, the new default pipeline): 38 / 42 pass, 0 fail, 4 ERR as before (sha, gin, pna, matrix_multiplication).
- **Reverted?:** no

## 2026-10-11 — hls-est: `--dead-memory-elimination` deletes a kept call whose written arrays nothing reads when no call of the same form is live, and marks the arrays Vitis keeps at 0R0W (W7 item 2, forgebench-gap C4 / O34; VITIS_NOTES N25)
- **Commit:** the commit that adds this entry (branch `hls-est-vitis-match`).
- **Files:**
  - `lib/Dialect/Resource/Passes/DeadMemoryElimination.cpp`: `CalleeEffectTable` (per function: which array parameters it reads / writes, also through further calls, and whether it touches anything else: a global write, an op with unknown effects, a memref returned or given to a function without a body) and `eraseDeadCalls`, run each round before the liveness under the new option `dead-calls` (default on). Calls are grouped by callee and argument partition (`PartitionRoots`, N24 (g)); a call is dead when its callee is pure in that sense, its results are unused and every array it writes is an alloca / global nothing reads (no load, no call whose callee reads the parameter, no other use). A group whose calls are all dead is deleted, and the liveness then removes the arrays, whose `hls.dead_memrefs` reason names the deleted call. In a group with a live call the dead calls stay and the arrays they write get `hls.kept_reason` ("kept: never read, written by a call of @f, which has another live call site of the same form; Vitis keeps the array as RAM_AUTO_0R0W at its full cost (VITIS_NOTES.md N17 (a), N25)").
  - `lib/Dialect/Resource/Passes/BRAMAnalysis.cpp`: the reason in `--bram-debug` (one line under the memref) and `--bram-debug-json` (`kept_reason`, additive, only when present). `MemoryBankModified.cpp`: banks carry `hls.kept_reason` over.
  - Lit: `test/hls-est/Passes/dme_dead_calls.mlir` (new: a single-site filler deleted with its array, a filler with a live call keeping its array and the reason, two forms of one filler).
  - Notes: VITIS_NOTES N25 (new); OPEN_ISSUES O34 removed (fixed; its guess that Vitis deletes such locals was wrong, N25).
- **Why:** VITIS_NOTES N25: kernels `exp-src/w7dead/w7_dl_local`, `w7_dl_global`, `w7_dl_toplocal` (a local or global written only by a call of a filler that has another live call site stays as `RAM_AUTO_0R0W` with its full BRAM, 2 each) with N17 (a) / (d); forgebench diff_orders_module (`BRAM_A3` / `BRAM_B3`: single-site fillers; `BRAM_C3`: the only call of its unpartitioned form of `load_64_128`, N24 (g); Vitis has no row for any of them) and mult_op_module_dot `BRAM_A3` (both calls of `load_16_32` write arrays nothing reads).
- **Effect** (default pipeline, exactclass, x_g3 MLIR, 787 designs; before = W7 item 1, run `w7/r/a2.json`; after = run `w7/r/a3.json`, frozen `w7/bin/hls-est.a3` md5 a7279676...; the commit's tree (`hls-est.c2`, the a3 code plus the debug fields) gives the same totals on diff_orders_module, mult_op_module_dot and tiled_attn_module):
  - subset: exact 66 -> **67** / 89, \|err\| 1608 -> **1583**, goal band 71 -> 71.
  - whole catalogue: exact 68 -> **69** / 95, \|err\| 1993 -> **1968**.
  - Newly exact: forgebench diff_orders_module 1562 -> **1538** (`BRAM_A3` / `B3` / `C3` 8 -> 0 each; every other row already = Vitis). mult_op_module_dot 101 -> 100 (`BRAM_A3` 1 -> 0; Vitis 58, the rest is O40). tiled_attn_module stays 268 (Vitis 270): `BRAM_A2` / `A3` (64 each) and `BRAM_B2` / `B3` (16 each) are the kept 0R0W arrays and now say so in the debug output.
  - No exact design lost; no other compared design moved. Designs without ground truth that differ between the runs are 120 s timeouts under load (13 -> 17).
  - Gates: bicg 8, gesummv 16, mvt 8, syrk 32 exact. Lit `test/hls-est` (commit tree, `hls-est.c2` md5 a0d4f9ef...): 86 pass, 4 fail (the baseline activation_op1, tiled_attn_module / p1 / p2), 24 unresolved, 1 expected failure, of 115.
- **Reverted?:** no

## 2026-10-11 — hls-est: `--memory-banking-bram` partitions the way Vitis does: every dimension of a spec, `dim = 0`, the larger factor, callee-parameter pragmas on the caller's arrays across kept calls, a callee form per argument partition, globals banked in place (W7 item 1, forgebench-gap C1 / C2 / O17 item 2; VITIS_NOTES N24)
- **Commit:** the commit that adds this entry (branch `hls-est-vitis-match`).
- **Files:**
  - `include/circt/Dialect/Resource/Passes/PartitionLayout.h`, `lib/Dialect/Resource/Passes/PartitionLayout.cpp` (new): `parsePartitionEntries`, `buildBankLayout` (N17 (c), N20, N21, N24 (a)-(c)), `bankAndOffset`, `partitionFormKey`, `PartitionRoots` (the partition of every alloca / global: its own pragma merged with the pragmas of every callee parameter it reaches through calls, transitively, N24 (g)).
  - `lib/Dialect/Resource/Passes/MemoryBankModified.cpp` (rewritten): a module pass (`Passes.td` anchor `ModuleOp`; the checked-in `Passes.h.inc` regenerated). `BankingPlan::assignCalleeForms` walks top-down from the functions no call reaches and gives a callee one form per argument partition (`<callee>_form<N>` clones, N14 R4 / N24 (g)); `bank()` splits allocas, globals (bank globals `<name>_bank_<i>`, every `get_global` site rewritten, a dense initial value sliced per bank) and callee parameters (one parameter per bank, every call passes the banks, `hls.port_bound_args` renumbered). Accesses: a constant or rotating bank per split dimension; the candidates of a rotating access are every combination of its rotating dimensions (one dimension: the same loads / selects / store_enables as before). An array that reaches a view, a cast, a function without a body, a returned or stored memref, or a parameter of another shape stays one memory with "array_partition not applied: ..." (it used to stay unbanked whenever it reached a kept call, O33). The interface ports of the top are never split.
  - `lib/Dialect/Resource/Passes/PolygeistCompat.cpp`: a row parameter's pragma keeps its dimension when the whole array has a single row (N24 (d)).
  - `lib/Dialect/Resource/Passes/BRAMAnalysis.cpp`: debug only, additive: `hls.partition`'s `dims` (every split dimension) and `from` (the parameter whose pragma it is) in `--bram-debug` (" dims=[...] from ...") and `--bram-debug-json` (`partition.dims`, `partition.from`, only when present).
  - Lit: `test/hls-est/Passes/banking_multidim_calls.mlir`, `banking_callee_forms.mlir`, `lower_subindex_single_row.mlir` (new); `bram_debug_provenance.mlir` (`dim = 0` now splits both dimensions: `d` has 4 banks).
  - Notes: VITIS_NOTES N24 (new); OPEN_ISSUES O33 removed (fixed), O17 / O4 status, O39 / O40 (new); MEMOS #44.
- **Why:** VITIS_NOTES N24 from the W7 kernels (`exp-src/{w7rom,w7rom2,w7prop,w7}`: w7_p2d, w7_prop_2d, w7_dim0c, w7_prop_conflict / conflict8, w7_prop_row0only / row1only / row1, w7_prop_two, w7_prop_inline) and N17 (b) / (c); hls-doc/forgebench-gap.md C1 (callee-parameter pragmas), C2 (globals not banked), O17 item 2 (first spec entry only). The directed propagation and the forms come from forgebench diff_orders_module (N24 (g)): Vitis does not partition `BRAM_C3`, filled by the same `load_64_128` as the partitioned `BRAM_A1` / `BRAM_C1`; a first version that made every array passed to one callee parameter one partition (union-find) partitioned it (128 banks).
- **Effect** (default pipeline, exactclass, MLIR of `w3g/r/x_g3.json` (installed cgeist 2ec1956c), the whole Polygeist catalogue of 787 designs; baseline `x_g3` = hls-est bcfb9bb3a; this change = run `~/hls-est-work/w7/r/a2.json`, frozen binary `w7/bin/hls-est.a2` md5 f83aca43...; the commit's tree, built as `hls-est.c1` md5 d75e881b..., gives the same totals on the designs spot-checked (diff_orders_module 1562, mult_op_module_dot 101, diff_dims_p1 210, conv_module 1042, diff_orders_p3 514, GradCAM conv_ds 0); the shared-layout refactor is otherwise the a2 code):
  - harness subset (89 compared): exact 55 -> **66**, \|err\| 5243 -> **1608**, goal band 61 -> 71.
  - whole catalogue (95 compared): exact 56 -> **68**, \|err\| 5631 -> **1993**, goal band 62 -> 73.
  - No exact design lost. Newly exact (11 forgebench + 1): conv_module 1042, diff_dims_module_large 1922, diff_dims_p1 210, diff_dims_p2 114, diff_dims_p3 354, diff_orders_p1 130, diff_orders_p2 2, diff_orders_p3 514, testing_impl 2, vec_mtx_module 2, vec_mtx_p1 2, class_ece8893_2023_spring GradCAM conv_ds 0 (its static buffers reach `conv_small`'s completely partitioned parameters).

## 2026-10-11 — hls-est: non-inlined calls review fixes (two W3 reviews: call ordering, instance count = most concurrent calls, exclusive branches, call depth chains with three-input adders, port-bound arrays of shared modules, written statics charged once)
- **Commit:** the commit that adds this entry (branch `hls-est-vitis-match`), "hls-est: non-inlined calls review fixes". It contains the first review's fixes, which an earlier attempt of this step made but did not commit, and the second review's fixes.
- **Files:**
  - `CallInstances.{h,cpp}`:
    - `groupConcurrentCalls` orders a write after a read only when the write can come first: later in program order, or in the next iteration of a common loop.
    - A tainted terminator forwards each operand to its own result or iter_arg.
    - A callee that writes a global orders all of its calls (`calleeWritesGlobal`).
    - New: calls in different branches of an `scf.if` / `affine.if` / `scf.index_switch`, or carrying different arms of one `hls.if_arms` id, are ordered (`mutuallyExclusive`).
    - New: the order is closed transitively and covered by a minimum set of chains, one instance per chain (`chainCover`). This replaces the union-find components.
    - New `InstanceModel::{instanceSites, singleInstanceOwner, argReachesSharedModule}`.
    - The debug rule line adds ", N by exclusive branches" when there are any.
  - `AutoPipelineUnroll.cpp`:
    - `call_depth::Model` is an ASAP schedule of a loop-free callee: two ports per memory, delays from the verbose schedule reports, a 7.3 ns budget. New: `ternaryInner` / `ternaryAdderNs` group add(add(x, y), z) into one three-input adder.
    - `hls.call_depth` on calls in pipelined loops.
    - `markPortBoundArgs` writes `hls.port_bound_args`.
  - `AffineToLoopScheduleModified.cpp`: `chainSharedCalleeInstances` chains the calls of one instance at their depth (`hls.call_depth`) instead of their occupancy, with a distance-1 back edge. `hoistIfBodies` also records `hls.if_arms` on calls.
  - `BRAMAnalysis.cpp`: accesses reached through a module instantiated at more than one place are port-bound and never replicate the bank (debug text "port-bound", JSON `port_bound`, additive). A written global is charged once, in the nearest single-instance owner (N14 R9).
  - `MemrefBankClassification.cpp`: skips port-bound arguments.
  - `MemoryDS.h`: `portBound`.
  - `LoopPragmas.h`: `hls.call_depth`, and `hls.if_arms` now also on calls.
  - Lit:
    - New: `test/hls-est/Passes/call_instances_width.mlir`, `call_instances_review.mlir`, `call_chain_depth.mlir`.
    - Updated: `auto_pipeline_call_boundary`, `call_args_owner`, `call_in_pipeline_sched`, `call_instances_bram`.
  - Notes: VITIS_NOTES N14 "W3 review addendum".
- **Why:** VITIS_NOTES N14 W3 review addendum, from 25 kernels in `~/hls-est-work/exp-src/{w3rev,w3fx,w3r2}`.
  - Review 1:
    - D1(a) `w3rev_k6`: a write after a read.
    - D1(b) `w3rev_k2`: per-operand forwarding.
    - D2 `w3rev_k3` / `k3b`, `w3fx_d2_conc`: port-bound.
    - D3 `w3rev_k5`, `w3fx_d3_mix`, `w3fx_d3_samepar`: written statics.
    - D4 `inl_e3`, `w3rev_k2` / `k4`, `w3fx_d4_*`: II = sum of the call depths.
  - Review 2:
    - D1 `w3r2_k5_branches`: if/else calls share one instance, Vitis 4 BRAM, hls-est gave 8.
    - D2 `w3r2_k6_join`: two concurrent calls joined by a third keep 2 instances, Vitis 12, hls-est gave 8.
    - D3 `w3r2_k8_unrolled_calls`: Vitis II 16 = 4 calls x depth 4. `get3`'s two adds form one 4.37 ns TernaryAdder after the third load (`db/get3.verbose.sched.rpt`). hls-est at HEAD gave II 8 (occupancy); the first fix gave 12 (depth 3, two plain adds).
  - D1 and D2 of review 2 rest on one kernel each; their variants are unconfirmed (N14).
- **Effect:**
  - **Kernels** (exactclass, installed cgeist 2ec1956c, default pipeline, BRAM = Vitis; `~/hls-est-work/w3g/kern/{head,fix1,g3}/summary.txt`). These are the 88 kernels: 60 N14 (`inl_*`), 3 w3h, 9 w3rev, 8 w3fx and 8 w3r2.
    - HEAD (21a959f47, md5 071f97ba…): 73 / 88.
    - First fix attempt: 82 / 88.
    - This commit (frozen `~/hls-est-work/w3g/bin/hls-est.g3`, md5 6b0d4609b1f30917ee8328a00508b8be): **84 / 88**.
    - Newly equal: w3rev_k2, k3, k3b, k4, k5, k6, w3fx_d2_conc, d3_mix, d3_samepar, w3r2_k5_branches, w3r2_k6_join.
    - Still different (unchanged, recorded): inl_c5_unrolled_loop (R4), inl_g_loopbig_dep2 (N18 / W10), inl_o5_f8 / i8 (O5).
    - Caller-loop II (Vitis / HEAD / this commit):
      - k8: 16 / 8 / 16
      - inl_e3: 4 / 2 / 4
      - d4_rd4: 8 / 4 / 8
      - d4_three: 6 / 3 / 6
      - d4_add3: 6 / 2 / 6
      - d4_mul: 8 / 2 / 8
      - w3rev_k2 p1: 6 / 2 / 6
      - w3rev_k4: 4 / 2 / 4
  - **Harness** (catalogue polygeist, which now has 787 designs; 95 compared with `results/vitis.json`; MLIR from `w3h/r3/{xinst,sinst}.json` plus the shared cache; runs `~/hls-est-work/w3g/r/{x,s}_{head,g3}.json`):
    - exactclass: 56 / 95 → 56 / 95, \|err\| 5631 → 5631, 62 → 62 in the goal band.
    - scalar: 45 / 95 → 45 / 95, \|err\| 29209 → 29209.
    - Every compared design has the same total before and after, under both shims.
    - Gates bicg 8, gesummv 16, mvt 8 and syrk 32 are exact.
    - The counts differ from the W3 entry's 55 / 89 because the catalogue and the ground truth grew (W8), not because of this change.
  - **Lit** `test/hls-est`: 82 pass, 4 fail (the baseline activation_op1 and tiled_attn_module / p1 / p2), 24 unresolved, 1 expected failure, out of 111.
  - **Debug invariance:**
    - The 42 Estimation inputs: 38 pass, 0 fail, 4 ERR as before (sha, gin, pna, matrix_multiplication).
    - The 25 w3rev / w3fx / w3r2 kernels: 25 / 25 pass.
- **Reverted?:** no

## 2026-10-11 — check_semantics.py: each design's cgeist MLIR run against its native build, bit for bit, with the real Vitis headers in C simulation as the reference; first run over the HLSFactory_v2 sweep; cause classes for decision #39 (W8b part 1)
- **Commit:** the commit that adds this entry (branch `hls-est-vitis-match`). It changes only the harness, notes and docs: no C++, cgeist or shim code, no estimate, no MLIR cache key, no harness command. The new cgeist flags are opt-in.
- **Files:**
  - `tools/hls-est-validate/check_semantics.py` (new; the first W8b attempt started it, this one finishes it).
  - `run_hlsest.py`: `VITIS_FRONTEND=1`, which is opt-in. It adds N19's `-D__SYNTHESIS__ -D__VITIS_HLS__ -D__HLS_SYN__ -DAESL_SYN` and `-std=gnu++14` / `-std=gnu99` to the cgeist command, and names them in the MLIR cache key only when on.
  - `make_hlsfactory_report.py`:
    - The shim-gap classes now name track W11 (#39).
    - A cgeist crash after a clang error takes the error's class.
    - New class `absolute-include`.
    - An ambiguous call involving hls:: or ap_ types is `shim-hls-api`.
    - `--semantics` adds the semantic-check columns.
  - Notes: VITIS_NOTES N20, N21, N22 (new); OPEN_ISSUES O36, O37, O38 (new); MEMOS #28 status.
  - Docs: `hls-doc/semantics.md` (new, generated), `hls-doc/harness.md`, `hls-doc/README.md`.
- **What:**
  - **Candidate:** the MLIR file hls-est reads, plus a wrapper `cs_entry`. CIRCT's MLIR (`mlir-opt` / `mlir-translate`, the parser hls-est uses) lowers it, and `llc -O0` compiles it. The shim only declares the `hls::stream` methods, so the script generates definitions for them (LLVM IR) on token FIFOs keyed by the object's address.
  - **Reference `real` (#39):** the design's sources built with `g++-13 -O1` against an ext4 mirror of the Vitis 2025.1 headers, without `__SYNTHESIS__`. This is Vitis' own C simulation.
    - A generated thunk converts every argument between the MLIR layout and the native objects.
    - `ap_int` / `ap_fixed` values go through `ap_int_base<W,false>`. The C-simulation `ap_private` keeps signed values sign-extended, and from 65 bits it is larger than `iN`.
    - `hls::stream` arguments go through a real `hls::stream`, filled from the driver's FIFO before the call and drained back into it afterwards.
  - **Reference `shim`:** clang 18 against the headers cgeist read, with no conversion.
  - **Which reference decides:** the first one that builds. The next one runs after a mismatch or an n/a. A mismatch under both references is a frontend bug. A mismatch under `real` only is a header-semantics difference (W11).
  - **Driver (shared by both sides):**
    - Every argument gets guard zones and the same seeded values: modes small / wide / pos, seeds 1 and 2, two calls per run.
    - It compares every buffer, the guards and the tokens left in each stream, bit for bit. A NaN counts as one value.
    - A run counts only if the reference passes AddressSanitizer.
    - On a mismatch it rebuilds with `-ftrivial-auto-var-init=pattern|zero`; outputs that then change mean uninitialised reads, which is reported as n/a. It also runs UBSan; the design's own reports come first, ahead of the headers'.
  - **Robustness:**
    - The standard headers come before the design's sources.
    - The thunk's identifiers are prefixed, and the library names it uses are shielded from design macros with `push_macro`. Two designs broke without this: spector_hls dct (`#define size ...`) and vitis_examples 06-13 (`#define N`).
    - Overloaded tops are resolved.
    - There is a reference-build cache.
  - **Modes:** `--probe a.cpp` (small kernels through the harness cgeist command), `--top-fn` (bisect one function of a design), `--md`, `--self-test`.
- **Why:**
  - Definition of done item 1 ("semantically correct (checked by differential execution ...)").
  - MEMOS #39 ("validate it with tests on real xilinx headers").
  - The lessons skill's rule 5: every frontend change gets a runtime check.
  - The cause-class changes apply #39: no new shim headers, so the shim-gap classes belong to W11.
- **Effect:** run on the W8a sweep's MLIR (installed cgeist c70ba2b0, md5 6ff60ea1, exactclass shim). The first pass used `--ref real` on all 801 designs (47 min at -j 10). A second pass used `--ref real,shim --all-refs` on the 22 designs that mismatched or were n/a under `real`, and a third re-ran 6 designs after the thunk fixes. The merged result is `results/semantics.json` (gitignored) and the page is `hls-doc/semantics.md`.
  - **292 designs checked:** 291 decided by the real headers, 1 by the shim.
    - **279 equal.** Per dataset: polybench 9/9, polybench__reproducible 183/183, machsuite 11/11, vitis_examples 15/15, spector_hls 7/7, forgebench 19 of 27, class 2023 6/6, class 2025 24 of 29, hlsyn-vitis 1, pp4fpgas 3, rosetta 1.
    - **13 mismatches.** 12 are "real only (shim equal)": the shim's `hls::exp` / `tanh` / `sqrt` on `ap_fixed` is not Vitis' C simulation (O38, W11). These are forgebench activation_module / op1-3, attention_op_p2/p3, mlp, and the five class_ece8893_spring_2025 lab2 softmax designs. **1 is a frontend wrong-code bug:** forgebench gpt_transformer_p1 (O37). The bisection with `--top-fn` narrowed it to `dropout`, and the plain-C++ probe `q3.cpp` reproduces it. It is still present in W5's cgeist (md5 95b960fc).
  - **Not checked:**
    - 332 have `polygeist.*` ops; they need a polygeist-opt build after the W8b gate.
    - 146 have no MLIR (cgeist failed; W8a classes).
    - The reference itself has undefined behaviour on 16: 9 out-of-bounds accesses (forgebench conv_block_* locals, GradCAM, spector normals, ...) and 7 divisions by zero (forgebench attention_op_p1 / tiled_attn_*, which divide by zero by construction, N1; polybench fixed cholesky).
    - 6 need Vitis' C-simulation math library, which ships only as a Windows binary (cordic tables, `hls::powf`, `hls::atan(double)`). Their shim reference is n/a as well (FPE / OOB).
    - 3 reference timeouts (Analog-CIM).
    - 2 have argument buffers over 1 GiB (Yolo-v3 synth).
    - 2 read uninitialised memory.
    - 1 candidate does not link: cgeist emits `__builtin_inff()` as an external call (a frontend gap, reproduced by a probe).
    - 1 top is not a function of the sources (sreenidhireddy lab3, W8a `top-not-in-sources`).
  - **Probes (all equal under both references):**
    - 4 `hls::stream` kernels: argument streams, a local stream, `ap_fixed` tokens, `read_nb` / `size`.
    - The q4 / q5 controls for O37.
    - Polybench and machsuite re-run through the CIRCT-MLIR path give the first attempt's 20 of 20 equal again.
  - **Cause classes** (reclassifying the W8a sweep): 8 of the 26 `cgeist-crash` designs crashed after a clang error and move to that error's class:
    - edgemoe: `missing-vendor-header` `gmp.h` (W11).
    - GNN-virtual-node bitstream / pipelined: new `absolute-include` (`/tools/software/xilinx/Vitis_HLS/2021.1/include/gmp.h`, UNSUPPORTED).
    - gramschmidt x3, streaming_vadd: `shim-hls-api`.
    - flowgnn gat: `shim-ap-feature`.
    The page will be regenerated from the W8b re-sweep.
  - **Vitis evidence for the W8b fixes (new notes):**
    - N20: `unroll` outside a loop and a partition `dim` beyond the rank are warnings that Vitis ignores; a partition naming an undeclared variable is an error.
    - N21: a cyclic / block factor above the dimension clamps silently to a complete partition.
    - N22: two `interface` pragmas on one port are accepted, and the later one decides the bundle.
    - Each rests on `vitis_adhoc` kernels `w8b_pragma_*`, `w8b_factor_*`, `w8b_iface_*`.
- **Reverted?:** no

## 2026-10-11 — cgeist lit: `Test/HLS/Inputs/vitis` becomes a copy of the exactclass shim, every `%vitis_include` RUN line passes `--scalar-wrapper-abi`, the HLSFactory tests are regenerated (MEMOS decision #10; W5 item 3)
- **Commit:** Polygeist 95a5ce16 (branch `hls-est-vitis-match` in `/mnt/d/HLS`; test headers, tests and docs only, no cgeist code); circt: the commit that adds this entry. W5's private binary is unchanged by this item: `/home/peter/tmp/w5g/cgeist.i2b`, md5 95b960fc13d458da755563eb3a80cecb, the code of Polygeist 5432780b (items 1 + 2). **Not installed** (the coordinator installs it).
- **Files (Polygeist):** tools/cgeist/Test/HLS/Inputs/vitis/{ap_fixed.h, ap_int.h, hls_half.h, hls_math.h} (now byte-identical to `shim/exactclass` of circt 25060c328; ap_axi_sdata.h, ap_cint.h, hls_stream.h and hls_vector.h were identical already); Inputs/vitis/README.md (new: where the headers come from, the flag, what is missing); Inputs/vitis/cgeist_flags (new, the shim's flag file: `run_hlsest.py --shim polygeist` now passes `--scalar-wrapper-abi` too, checked on bicg 8 and attention_op_p1 73); Test/HLS/lit.local.cfg (comment); HLSAllocation.cpp and regressions/hlsfactory-subscripted-variable.cpp (RUN lines); hlsfactory/generate.py (runs and writes the flag); hlsfactory/*/*.test (776 RUN lines, 287 tests regenerated); hlsfactory/SUMMARY.md; docs/hls-pragmas.md.
- **What:** the old stand-ins kept a `long long` behind every `ap_[u]int<W>` and a `double` behind every `ap_fixed`. The lit tests now use the harness's bit-exact exactclass headers (classes around a `_BitInt(W)`); decision #9 keeps `--scalar-wrapper-abi` opt-in, so each RUN line passes it. The hlsfactory tests were regenerated with the W5 binary the way generate.py writes them, from the tests' own design list (generate.py itself needs the harvested tree and rewrites Inputs). With the old headers the same procedure reproduces 768 of the 776 committed tests and SUMMARY.md byte for byte. The other 8 differ because of earlier cgeist commits (below).
- **Why:** MEMOS decisions #10 (exactclass replaces the lit shim, after W3) and #9. The old headers hid every width and stopped 220 designs in cgeist with struct-operand errors.
- **Effect:**
  - **Polygeist lit** (private lit tree with the W5 binary, private HOME):
    - hlsfactory: 714 pass / 61 XFAIL / 1 fail -> **692 pass / 84 XFAIL / 0 fail**. With the new headers and the old CHECK lines it was 662 / 60 / 53 fail / 1 XPASS.
    - HLS without hlsfactory: 95 pass / 2 fail / 3 unsupported, before and after. The 2 failures (array_partition/lowering.c, pipeline/lowering.c) also fail with 2ec1956c.
    - Verification: 135 pass + 8 XFAIL, before and after.
  - **What the new headers change in the hlsfactory tests** (old headers vs new, same binary):
    - 220 designs go from check-only to full: they now compile end to end. With the old headers, 204 stopped at a `func.call` operand type mismatch between `llvm.struct` memrefs, 15 at a `math.exp` / `cos` / `log` / `ceil` on a struct or integer operand, and 1 at the 180 s limit. Every pragma the check-only test placed is still checked, now with its target op.
    - 28 full designs change:
      - Mangled names: functions with `ap_fixed` parameters change, because the Q / O template parameters are the `ap_q_mode` / `ap_o_mode` enums of the Vitis headers, not `int`.
      - Loops: in 6 class_ece8893_spring_2025 designs, loops with `ap_int` counters or bounds are `scf.for` / `affine.for` instead of `scf.while`.
      - Inlined copies: callees whose `ap_fixed` member calls now fold to integer ops have no calls left, and cgeist inlines them into their callers. Their pragmas are therefore also reported for the inlined copy (forgebench conv_A/B/C, mult_op_p1/p2/p3, testing_impl, and 3 class designs). Under exactclass the harness already saw this.
      - GNN-virtual-node: the write-only `psum` buffer is removed because its stores now forward to the loads, so its `array_partition` is reported "not applied: ... removed during optimization".
    - **24 designs become XFAIL**, because the headers lack what they use. Each is a compile error quoted in its test:
      - 20 assign to a range select (O15): the 11 soda designs, Mask-Net, SkyNet, 6 class labs and vitis_examples fixed_point_sqrt.
      - 3 polybench fixed gramschmidt call `hls::nextafter` on `ap_fixed`. Only the `float` / `double` overloads exist, so the call is ambiguous.
      - FLAT-acceleration calls an unqualified `exp` on an `ap_fixed`. The call is ambiguous between the `<cmath>` overloads (shim-gap class `shim-hls-api` in hls-doc/hlsfactory-v2.md).
      - Under decision #39 these gaps are W11's (real headers), not new shim work.
    - 1 XFAIL design now compiles (mohammad_zain_lab_2: the old `ap_fixed` had an ambiguous `operator^`).
    - 8 quoted reasons change (4 check-only, 4 XFAIL).
    - flowgnn/pna and gnnbuilder fpga_gin_qm9 now compile within generate.py's 180 s limit (171 s / 151 s, re-measured at low load because the -j 10 regeneration timed them out) and become full.
  - **Changes from cgeist commits made after the tests were generated** (the same with W3's 2ec1956c):
    - chstone gsm and machsuite aes_table / aes_tableless / bfs_bulk / viterbi compile end to end (check-only -> full).
    - accelerators GP_selection_kernel_float_dataset reports 3 copies of each `array_partition` instead of 12 (inline off, 62c40544 / 2ec1956c). It failed with 2ec1956c before this item.
  - **SUMMARY.md:** full 454 -> 666, check-only 254 -> 19, xfail 61 -> 84. Unchanged: errors 5, errors-check-only 2, skip 3.
  - **Harness:** no harness input changes. `shim/exactclass` and `shim/scalar` are untouched, and the Polygeist copy is read only by `--shim polygeist`. As a check of the final W5 binary against the installed cgeist, both were run with the current hls-est and the default pipeline:
    - Setup: committed harness of circt 974acde4b, hls-est `build/clion` of 02:26 (sources = HEAD; md5 071f97ba67586eee0c7d66d505a4423a, frozen copy `/home/peter/tmp/w5h/hls-est`), private HOMEs, `--force-cgeist`, runs `/home/peter/tmp/w5h/h_{x,s}_{inst,w5}.json`. Installed = 2ec1956c (md5 947214ff...).
    - exactclass: exact 55 / 89, |err| 5243, both sides.
    - scalar: 45 / 89, |err| 28718, both sides.

This includes the verify3 n3-n11 probes. n1_exact / n2_countdown hold intentionally infinite loops and are checked structurally: identical loop forms, except `e_ap8_lt_ap9` / `e_ap8_le_ap8` (runtime bounds), which are now raised by the termination assumption (item 2).

## 2026-10-11 — hls-est: calls to `inline off` callees are kept, priced per instance and scheduled at the call boundary; `--lower-polygeist-subindex` leads the default pipeline; the W3 cgeist (Polygeist 2ec1956c) is installed (MEMOS decisions #11 / #12, VITIS_NOTES N14; W3-hlsest)
- **Commit:** 21a959f47 (hls-est, lit tests, harness pipeline); the notes and the install record are in the commit that adds this entry (branch `hls-est-vitis-match`). **Installed:** Polygeist 2ec1956c (the W3-cgeist private binary `/home/peter/tmp/w3cg/cgeist.2ec1956c`, md5 947214ff4640d47c32387d20df58493e) is `build/bin/cgeist` since 2026-10-11 04:24. The binary it replaced is `cgeist.prev-c70ba2b0` (md5 6ff60ea1c05f11f99420dd672a5ce457). Install log: `~/hls-est-work/w3h/install.log`.
- **Files:** include/circt/Dialect/Resource/Passes/CallInstances.h + lib/.../CallInstances.cpp (new: `computeInstances`, `groupConcurrentCalls`, `storageRoot`, `allocationLimit`, `InstanceModel`); HlsInliner.{h,cpp} (new: hls-est's func inliner interface, `--inline-ignore-inline-off`); PolygeistCompat.{h,cpp} (new: a minimal `polygeist` dialect with `subindex`, pass `--lower-polygeist-subindex`); LoopPragmas.h (`hls.call_boundary`, `hls.call_occupancy`); MemoryDS.h; BRAMAnalysis.cpp (per-instance charge, accesses of a callee to an argument priced on the caller's array, debug text / JSON); AutoPipelineUnroll.cpp (`markCallBoundaries`, `occupancy`, `hasLoops`, callers decided before callees); AffineToLoopScheduleModified.cpp (`chainSharedCalleeInstances`, call latency, `--loopschedule-kept-call-latency`); DeadMemoryElimination.cpp (unused memref parameters of kept callees); MemoryBankModified.cpp (`unbankableUser`); MemrefBankClassification.cpp; CMakeLists.txt; tools/hls-est/main.cpp; tools/hls-est-validate/common.py (`DME_FRONT`), check_debug_invariance.sh; lit tests test/hls-est/Passes/{inline_off_kept, call_instances_bram, call_args_owner, auto_pipeline_call_boundary, call_in_pipeline_sched, call_symbol_dce_top, dme_unused_callee_param, lower_subindex}.mlir (new) and bram_debug_json.mlir. Notes: VITIS_NOTES.md N14 (R7 addendum, hls-est bullet), OPEN_ISSUES.md (O5, O10, O25 updated; O33, O34, O35 new), MEMOS.md (rows #11, #12, #43).
- **What:**
  - `--inline` keeps every call whose callee carries `hls.inline_off` and inlines the rest as before. `--inline-ignore-inline-off` inlines everything (the old behaviour).
  - `--bram-analysis` charges a function's memories once per instance (N14 R1). Within one parent, the calls of a callee that can run concurrently get one instance each. Ordered calls share one: a common array (any memref operand with the same storage root), a value of one call reaching the other, or `allocation function instances=<f> limit=N` (R2). Each parent module gets its own instances, and the counts multiply down the call tree (R3). A callee's accesses to an argument array are priced on the caller's array, so replication crosses the call (R6). The debug text and JSON name, for every such access, the call and the function it is made in. Every callee lists its instance count and its call sites. The JSON keys are additive (`instances`, `bram_per_instance`, `owner_func`, `via_call`, `in_func`, `call_sites`, `parents`, `allocation_limit`, ...), so `version` stays.
  - `--auto-pipeline-unroll` decides callers before callees, so a callee's loops count in the caller loop's trip test as written (R7 and its addendum). A loop around a call whose callee keeps loops is marked `hls.call_boundary` and left sequential. A call inside a pipelined loop gets `hls.call_occupancy`: the distinct accesses of its busiest memory at two per cycle (inl_f2 II 8, w3h_cb_flat64 II 32, w3h_cb_nest64 II 8).
  - `--convert-affine-to-loopschedule` schedules a call with the occupancy as its latency (`--loopschedule-kept-call-latency` when it has none), bounds the II by it, and serializes calls that share an instance (inl_e3). Inside a pipelined loop the latency is the occupancy, which the measured IIs support (inl_f2, w3h_cb_flat64, w3h_cb_nest64). Elsewhere the call keeps the existing call-latency mechanism (`--loopschedule-kept-call-latency`, default 1). The callee's own schedule is not used as the call's latency, since no kernel has measured it.
  - `--lower-polygeist-subindex` parses cgeist's `polygeist.subindex` (a row argument `f(a[k])`, O25) and removes it. Accesses through the view index the whole array. A call that passes a view is redirected to a clone of the callee per argument form, which matches the clones R4 observes; R4's trigger itself is unconfirmed. It is the first pass of `DEFAULT_PIPELINE` (decision #43 under #36).
  - `--dead-memory-elimination` drops a memref parameter of a private kept callee that is no longer accessed, so the caller's array can die (N17 (a), fbh3_l_callee_rd_dead; tiled_attn_module `split_wq` / `split_wk`).
  - `--memory-banking-bram` leaves an array that reaches a kept call unbanked, with a warning (O33). It used to leave a use of an erased value.
  - `--symbol-dce` and `top=` keep the callees the top reaches (lit `call_symbol_dce_top.mlir`).
- **Why:** VITIS_NOTES N14 R1-R3, R6, R7 (60 kernels + 27 corpus designs, W3-exp) and the R7 addendum (3 kernels `exp-src/w3h/w3h_cb_*.cpp`, W3-hlsest: the callee's loop nest counts in the 64-trip test, and a pipelined caller's II is the callee's port occupancy). Also N17 (a), O25, decision #11 (honour `inline off`), and #36 (delegation) for the pipeline order and the install.
- **Effect** (all measurements in this step). The harness ran on all 143 designs of the Polygeist catalogue (11 datasets, 89 compared) with the default pipeline and a frozen hls-est. That hls-est is `~/hls-est-work/w3h/bin/hls-est.final`, md5 071f97ba67586eee0c7d66d505a4423a, the build/clion of 21a959f47. The runs are `~/hls-est-work/w3h/r1/{xi2,si2,xw,sw}.json` (+ `.cmp.json`, `.csv`). The W3 runs used the private HOME `~/hls-est-work/w3h/home_w3`.

Nine designs change, all of them forgebench. The per-array table for exactclass (installed → W3, Vitis) follows:

## 2026-10-11 — hls-est-validate: W8a review fixes (unreadable directories are load problems and `--merge` no longer drops unseen designs; a both-ways file that defines the top stays a design source; class `design-std-include`; compare.py compares only designs with the ground truth's sources)
- **Commit:** the commit that adds this entry (branch `hls-est-vitis-match`); fixes the independent review of W8a (5ff9321ce, c926618f5), defects D1-D5.
- **Files:** tools/hls-est-validate/hlsfactory_v2.py (`_scan` on `os.scandir`, `_is_dir_entry`, `IO_ERRORS` / `_SCAN_ERRORS`, `_drain_io_errors` at the end of `discover()` and `load()`, `_walk` reports cached listing errors again; read errors of Tcl candidates and in `_file_set`; `_sources_from_tcl` keeps a both-ways file that defines the top, `_text_defines_function` / `_defines_function`, self-test cases; module doc), sweep_hlsfactory_v2.py (`--drop-stale`; a catalogue problem stops the sweep), make_hlsfactory_report.py (class `design-std-include`, `_includes_vitis_header`; the page header names the ground-truth file), run_vitis.py (`extra_cfg_for`, `cache_key(d, extra_cfg, files)`, `cache_keys`; every summary entry records `catalog`), run_hlsest.py (`vitis_keys` per design record, `_meta.catalog` for every run), compare.py (status `sources differ`, summary count `sources_differ`, the catalogue in the report header), make_hlsdoc.py (`design_status` names the new status), designs.py (mode 100755 restored), hls-doc/harness.md, hls-doc/hlsfactory-v2.md and .csv (regenerated), the W8a entry below (corrected in place).
- **What:**
  - D1 (medium): `_scan` replaced `os.walk` (which drops a directory it cannot list and takes an entry whose `is_dir()` fails for a file) by its own `os.scandir` walk with the same listing (top-down, sorted, symlinked directories listed but not entered, `__pycache__` / hidden directories left out). A directory that cannot be listed, an entry that cannot be stat'ed, an unreadable Tcl candidate and an unreadable file of a source comparison are now problems (`load_hlsfactory_v2` raises on them; with a problems list they are listed); a listing error is cached with the listing and reported again by a second load in the same process. `sweep_hlsfactory_v2.py` stops before sweeping when the load reports a problem, and `--merge` drops designs of the old file that the catalogue lacks only with the new `--drop-stale` (otherwise it stops and names them), because a design can also be missing when the walk did not see its directory without an error (the review saw a plain walk of `/mnt/d` under load miss forgebench/diff_orders_p2 once).
  - D2 (low): a file the script adds both as a design file and with `-tb` stays a design source when it defines the top (a definition: the name after a type-like token, a parameter list without parentheses, then `{`; comments removed; a function-pointer parameter makes it a testbench file as before); the other both-ways files are testbench files as before. The docstring's claim that dropping such a file "cannot change what Vitis builds" was wrong and is replaced; what Vitis does with both-ways files stays unconfirmed.
  - D3 (low): an unknown standard-library name (`stderr`, `uint32_t`, ...) is the shim gap `shim-std-include` only when the design's include closure (sources, then every `#include` found in the including file's directory or the design's include dirs, transitively) names a Vitis header; otherwise the new class `design-std-include` (fix track: check per design, a missing `#include` in the design).
  - D4 (low, latent): `run_vitis.cache_keys(d)` gives a record's Vitis cache keys for td off and auto; run_hlsest.py stores them per design (`vitis_keys`) and writes `_meta.catalog` for every run (it wrote it for v2 runs only); run_vitis.py writes `catalog` into every summary entry; compare.py compares a design only when the ground-truth entry's `key` is one of the record's keys, else the row is `sources differ` (not compared, listed with both keys). Older prediction files without the keys compare as before. The environment switch (`HLSFACTORY_ROOT` makes v2 the default, also for run_vitis.py) is unchanged: W8c changes the default catalogue.
  - D5 (trivial): designs.py is 100755 again (5ff9321ce had dropped the bit).
- **Why:** the W8a review. D1: the reviewer's reproducer (`scratchpad/rv_walkerr.sh`, a design directory with mode 000) gave 1 of 2 designs, no problem and no skipped entry; `--merge` then deleted the missing design's record. D2: accelerators/DGNN_Booster_convLSTM and DGNN_Booster_EvolveGCN add their only design file (top and `main`) both ways and got their sources only from the fallback; `rv_bothways.sh` (`add_files k.cpp; add_files helper.cpp; add_files -tb k.cpp; set_top k`) lost the top. D3: class_ece8893_2022_fall/Ray-tracing golden_c includes no Vitis header (`<cmath>`, `<algorithm>`, `<limits>`, `<math.h>`, `<stdint.h>` only) and calls `fprintf(stderr, ...)` without `<stdio.h>`. D4: 6 rosetta designs have a v2 record with other sources (other Vitis cache key, e.g. spam-filter `718710d971e0e3a5` against `cbd5c3e034ec3eab` in vitis.json) under the same dataset/benchmark key, and nothing recorded which catalogue a result came from. Not a Vitis behaviour change: harness only.
- **Effect:** no estimate, status or catalogue record of the default catalogue changes; no C++, cgeist or shim file is touched (lit not run).
  - Reproducers: `rv_walkerr.sh` now reports `.../ds/b: cannot list directory (Permission denied)`; a second load in the same process reports it again, and the load without a list raises `DesignParseError`; an unreadable Tcl candidate is reported too. `rv_bothways.sh`: sources `['k.cpp', 'helper.cpp']`. `--merge` with a stale design stops and names it, `--drop-stale` drops it (`_meta.resweeps[-1].dropped`). Self test (`python3 hlsfactory_v2.py`) passes with 14 new definition cases (calls inside conditions among them). The full reload below ran before the matcher was narrowed to parameter lists without parentheses; the matcher only decides both-ways files, and the 61 both-ways designs were checked again with the final matcher: same result.
  - Full v2 catalogue reload with the fixed loader (Windows Python 3.13 reading D: directly, 254.8 s under load) against the review's catalogue (committed loader, `~/hls-est-work/w8a-review/cat.json`): 801 records, 0 problems, the same 801 keys, 3 skipped, 1 vanished; dir, sources, includes, defines, top, mode and source_match are equal in all 801; only the 2 DGNN records differ, in `v2.source_rule` (`fallback` -> `tcl`) and `v2.source_notes`. Fallback-rule designs 90 -> 88 (85 of them WIP).
  - Both-ways designs (61 in the catalogue): the 2 DGNN designs keep their file, the other 59 drop it as before, and their source lists are unchanged; in none of the 59 does a dropped file define a function that a kept source calls.
  - Cause classes: `--reclassify` of the W8a sweep (results/hlsfactory-v2-sweep.json) with the committed classifier changes nothing and reproduces the committed CSV byte for byte; with the fix exactly one of 801 records changes: Ray-tracing golden_c `shim-std-include` -> `design-std-include`. shim-std-include 6 -> 5 (the 5 left include `hls_math.h` or `ap_fixed.h`). hls-doc/hlsfactory-v2.md and .csv regenerated against the ground truth W8a used (the 143-key snapshot `~/hls-est-work/w8a/snap/tools/hls-est-validate/results/vitis.json`; results/vitis.json is rewritten by the running v2 ground-truth work); no other line changes. The two DGNN sweep records keep their old `v2.source_rule` (`fallback`); their dir, sources, includes, defines and top are unchanged, so they were not re-swept.
  - compare.py on W5's subset run (`/home/peter/tmp/w5g/h_x_i2b.json`, results/vitis.json): the committed compare.py, the new one without keys and the new one with the keys of today's records injected all give 87 compared, 56 exact, \|err\| 4932, 0 `sources differ`. The keys of all 110 ok td=off (vitis.json) and 111 ok td=auto (vitis-tdauto.json) subset entries equal the current records' keys; a truth entry given another key reads `sources differ`. Computing both keys for the 112 subset designs takes 13-15 s in all (run_hlsest does it in its worker threads).
  - Default catalogue unchanged: the review's 5-design check repeated with the new run_hlsest.py (no `--catalog`, `HLSFACTORY_ROOT` unset, fresh private HOME, the W8a binaries cgeist md5 6ff60ea1... and hls-est sha256 f1b8040586298de2, the review run's pipeline; `~/hls-est-work/w8a-fix/d5/new5.json`): Llama_GPT_module 33, fft_transpose 12, atax 13, fir 4, static_memory 2, the same status, total and per-memref BRAM as the review's `default5.json`, MLIR byte-identical; the records now carry `vitis_keys` and `_meta.catalog` is `polygeist`. compare.py gives compared 5, exact 2, \|err\| 20 on both runs (0 `sources differ`).
- **Reverted?:** no

## 2026-10-11 — cgeist raises narrow-counter loops against runtime bounds by assuming termination and marks them `hls.uncounted`; `--auto-pipeline-unroll` does not collapse an `hls.uncounted` loop with its perfect inner nest (MEMOS decision #14, option (C) of VITIS_NOTES N15; W5 item 2)
- **Commit:** Polygeist 5432780b (branch `hls-est-vitis-match` in `/mnt/d/HLS`); circt: the commit that adds this entry (hls-est side + notes). **Not installed.** Private binary of 5432780b (items 1 + 2): `/home/peter/tmp/w5g/cgeist.i2b`, md5 95b960fc13d458da755563eb3a80cecb.
- **Files (Polygeist):** lib/polygeist/Passes/CanonicalizeFor.cpp (`-canonicalize-for-assume-termination`, default on; `mayDoObservableWork`; `WhileToForHelper::terminationImpliesNoWrap` / `counterProvablyInRange` / `counterStaysInRange`; `MoveWhileToFor` sets `polygeist.counter_nowrap` and `hls.uncounted`; `ForOpInductionReplacement::assumedOwnCastOf` marks the counter's casts; `RemoveUnusedArgs` keeps both marks); lib/polygeist/Passes/AffineCFG.cpp (`truncationMarkedExact`; `roundTripKeepsValue` and the truncation step of the range proof accept the marked casts); lib/polygeist/Passes/RaiseToAffine.cpp (moves `hls.uncounted` to the affine.for); include/polygeist/Passes/Passes.h (`kNoUnsignedWrapAttrName`, `kCounterNoWrapAttrName`, `kUncountedLoopAttrName`); tools/cgeist/driver.cc (`LowerAffineKeepingLoopMarksPass` / `MarkedAffineForLowering` replace `mlir::createLowerAffinePass()` before cgeist's inliner; strips `polygeist.nuw` / `polygeist.counter_nowrap` too); tests regressions/narrow-counter-assume-termination.c (new), narrow-counter-wrap.c (`u8_int_bound` is raised now). **Files (circt):** include/circt/Dialect/Resource/Passes/LoopPragmas.h (`kLoopUncountedAttr`), lib/Dialect/Resource/Passes/AutoPipelineUnroll.cpp (`Node::uncountedPerfect`; the chain collapse stops at an `hls.uncounted` loop; step 3 flattens such a perfect parent into its pipelined child), test/hls-est/Passes/auto_pipeline_uncounted.mlir (new), VITIS_NOTES.md (N15 addendum; it went in with the item 1 notes, a44c6d985), MEMOS.md (row #14).
- **What:**
  - cgeist: a while loop over a counter narrower than `int` whose range check cannot keep the counter in range, because its bound is a runtime value (`for (uint8_t i = 0; i < n; i++)`, `int n`) or a narrow bound lets a power-of-two step overshoot (`uint8_t i < uint8_t m; i += 2`), is raised to a for loop by assuming that it terminates (C11 6.8.5p6, C++ [intro.progress]). Counting by +-2^k with a test that reads the counter with its own signedness, a wrap returns the counter to values the test accepted, so a terminating loop never wraps. Required: counter narrower than int, runtime bound, constant step +-2^k (2^k <= 2^(W-1)), not a sign-extended counter compared unsigned, no call to a function without a body / atomic / fence / barrier / volatile LLVM access in the loop or its visible callees. Constant bounds and other steps keep the exact check. The counter's casts are marked so that AffineCFG reads them as exact; a loop whose bound has a wider range than the counter is marked `hls.uncounted` (kept in the output). The lowering of the raised loops before cgeist's inliner keeps that mark (the upstream lowering builds a new `scf.for` and dropped it; the second raising then lost it: found on the first build, fixed before the commit).
  - hls-est: in `--auto-pipeline-unroll` step 1 an `hls.uncounted` loop is never the outer loop of a collapsed perfect pair, so it is pipelined with a <= 64-trip perfect inner loop unrolled into it; when the inner loop is pipelined instead (> 64 trips), step 3 flattens the marked loop into it as the collapse would have. Loops without the mark are untouched.
- **Why:** VITIS_NOTES N15 (33 kernels, W5-exp: Vitis pipelines such loops as the `int` ones, trip `?`, and does not collapse them with a perfect inner nest) and its W5 addendum (3 new kernels: a `uint8_t` countdown to an `int` bound is not collapsed either, a same-width step-2 counter is collapsed like `int`, trip `16 ~ 2048`); decision #14 (Peter: "do whatever you feel will make the result closer"; option (C) was the closest).
- **Effect:**
  - Harness subset (default pipeline, same frozen hls-est and snapshot as item 1; `/home/peter/tmp/w5g/h_{x,s}_i2b.json`): the MLIR of all 112 designs is byte-identical to item 1's (new3) under exactclass and scalar, so exact 56 / 87, \|err\| 4932 (exactclass) and 45 / 87, \|err\| 24709 (scalar) are unchanged; gates exact. No subset design compares a narrow counter with a wider runtime bound (N15 corpus scan).
  - The 32 N15 kernels (`/home/peter/tmp/w5g/nc/{base,i2b}_{s,x}/`, 2ec1956c vs the W5 binary, hls-est = the frozen binary above, Vitis td=off results from `exp-src/narrowctr/out/`): exact BRAM 19 / 32 -> 27 / 32 (scalar), 18 / 32 -> 25 / 32 (exactclass); on the 16 narrow-counter kernels 3 -> 11 (scalar) and 3 -> 10 (exactclass), the N15 prediction for (C). Still off: the five `nc_q_*` kernels (8 vs Vitis 120, the `array_partition` on the argument `out` does not reach the port model: hls-est schedules the outer loop at II 8) and, under exactclass only, `nc_s_apu8` (2 vs 6: an `ap_uint<8>` class counter in a one-element memref is never raised, N15 "Related"). w5c_pdn_u8 / w5c_pdn_int / w5c_ps2_u8: 8 = Vitis 8 before and after; the loop decisions now equal Vitis's in all three (before: 1 of 3).
  - Differential execution against native clang 18 on terminating inputs (`scratchpad/w5g/probes/nc_term.cpp`, 16 functions with per-function value lists: uint8_t / int8_t / uint16_t counters against `int` / `long` bounds, step 4, countdowns, `<=`, same-width step 2, the exit value, `(int8_t)i` views, a second counter that wraps, an inlined callee reading the counter signed, an accumulation, `ap_uint<8>` with the exact shim): 16 of 16 OK, 15 of them raised to `affine.for` (14 marked `hls.uncounted`); the 2ec1956c binary keeps all 16 as `scf.while`.
  - Polygeist lit (private lit tree, private HOME): HLS without hlsfactory 95 pass / 2 fail / 3 unsupported (the 2 failures as before); Verification 135 pass + 8 expected failures.
  - hls-est lit (`build/clion` hls-est of 01:02, md5 ada0a27071a036dd479629745140c80a, which also holds other agents' uncommitted changes): 78 pass, 4 fail (the forgebench CHECK-BRAM tests tiled_attn_p1 / _p2 / _module, activation_op1), 24 unresolved, 1 expected failure: the baseline; auto_pipeline_uncounted.mlir passes. hls-est's output changes only for loops marked `hls.uncounted`, which no older input has.
- **Reverted?:** no

## 2026-10-11 — cgeist: C signed int arithmetic marked `polygeist.nsw`; AffineCFG proves every unmarked add / sub / mul (MEMOS M9, decision #13; W5 item 1)
- **Commit:** Polygeist b3a248a8 (branch `hls-est-vitis-match` in `/mnt/d/HLS`, on top of W3's 2ec1956c); this entry: the commit that adds it. **Not installed.** Private binary of b3a248a8: `/home/peter/tmp/w5f/cgeist.new3` (md5 9f8ac20715c9e42b4291a4fb4fe24cf6); W5's final binary (items 1 + 2) is listed in the item 2 entry.
- **Files (Polygeist):** tools/cgeist/Lib/clang-mlir.cc (`markNoSignedWrap`, `operandTypeAsWritten`; called for `+ - *`, unary minus, `+= -= *=`, `++ --`); include/polygeist/Passes/Passes.h (`kNoSignedWrapAttrName`, `populateNoSignedWrapPatterns`); lib/polygeist/Passes/AffineCFG.cpp (`needsNoWrapProof(Operation *)`, `castNoWrapWidth`, `raisableRange`, `constantRepresentative`, the range oracle, `operandsUnsigned`, `exactArith`, `AffineApplyNormalizer`; `-affine-cfg-assume-nowrap-width` default 32 -> 0); lib/polygeist/Passes/CanonicalizeFor.cpp (`SubToAdd` keeps the mark; `NoSignedWrapAddSubChain`, `NoSignedWrapMulChain`); lib/polygeist/Passes/PolygeistCanonicalize.cpp (registers them); tools/cgeist/driver.cc (`-keep-frontend-assumptions`, `stripFrontendAssumptions`); tools/cgeist/Test/HLS/regressions/signed-nowrap-mark.c (new).
- **What:** this LLVM (18) has no overflow flags on arith ops (no `IntegerOverflowFlags` in ArithOps.td), so cgeist marks with the discardable unit attribute `polygeist.nsw` every `arith.addi / subi / muli` it builds for a C integer `+`, `-`, `*`, unary minus, `+=`, `-=`, `*=`, `++` or `--` computed in a signed type at least as wide as `int`, in that type's width, unless the program is compiled with `-fwrapv`. Unsigned arithmetic, an operand promoted from a type narrower than `int` (char, short, bool, enum, narrow `_BitInt`) and an op built narrower than its computation type (the `+=` of a narrow LHS) stay unmarked. AffineCFG raises a marked op of at least 32 bits without a range proof (as it raised every op of at least 32 bits before) and reads its signed value as exact in its range oracle; every unmarked add / sub / mul, i32 and i64 included, needs the proof that narrow arithmetic needs (ranges capped at 2^63 - 1, constant representatives within int64). Casts keep the 32-bit rule. The canonicalizer keeps the mark where the folded op provably has the same exact value: `x - C` -> `x + (-C)` (unless -C overflows) and folds of two marked add / sub with constants or two marked mul by constants into one marked op (polygeist-canonicalize, benefit 10, ahead of the arith DRR folds). The mark is stripped from the output unless `-keep-frontend-assumptions`, so unchanged raisings give byte-identical MLIR. `-affine-cfg-assume-nowrap-width=N` restores the old width rule for comparison.
- **Why:** MEMOS M9 (the round-4 recheck reproducer `for (unsigned i = 0; i < 4; i++) O[i] = A[(i * 0x7FFFFFFFu) / 0x7FFFFFFFu]` read `A[3]` at i = 3 with every earlier cgeist, C reads `A[0]`); decision #13 (mark signed arithmetic in the frontend, AffineCFG trusts only the marked ops). Not a Vitis behaviour: C semantics.
- **Effect:** harness subset, default pipeline, frozen hls-est `/home/peter/tmp/w5f/hls-est` (md5 7c79043201cfc9a303dfe7ccc2aad2da) and the w5f harness snapshot for both sides, private HOMEs, `--force-cgeist`; control = W3's 2ec1956c binary (`/home/peter/tmp/w3cg/cgeist.2ec1956c`, md5 947214ff4640d47c32387d20df58493e). Runs `/home/peter/tmp/w5g/h_{x,s}_base.json` vs `/home/peter/tmp/w5f/h_{x,s}_new3.json`.
  - exactclass: exact 56 / 87 -> 56 / 87, \|err\| 4932 -> 4932; scalar: 45 / 87 -> 45 / 87, \|err\| 24709 -> 24709. All 112 statuses and totals unchanged; gates bicg 8, gesummv 16, mvt 8, syrk 32 exact.
  - The MLIR changes in 2 of 112 designs under both shims, both unsigned index arithmetic on a runtime argument, which wraps in C and is no longer raised without a proof: spector_hls/fir (`2 * tid * totalInputLength`, `unsigned int`: affine loads 24 -> 20, stores 56 -> 52, memref loads 4 -> 8, stores 0 -> 4) and spector_hls/histogram (`data[i + offset]`, `unsigned long offset`: affine loads 17 -> 9, memref loads 8 -> 16). The other 110 designs' MLIR is byte-identical, among them all 19 designs of the M9 width-64 experiment except fir (forgebench attention / attn_breakdown / conv / gpt / llama / tiled_attn / diff_dims_module_small, spector_hls dct): their `int` index arithmetic (`h * 4 + j`) stays affine.
  - Differential execution against native clang 18 (lowered MLIR vs the natively compiled probe, every output buffer compared bit for bit): the W5 probes (`scratchpad/w5f/probes`: m1_nsw with the M9 reproducer and unsigned 32 / 64-bit wraps, signed, provably ranged unsigned and promoted narrow cases; lit_nsw = the lit test's functions) 31 of 31 kernels OK (the installed c70ba2b0 fails t1 and u32_mul_div, the reproducer); round 4's probe set `v4/probes` 76 of 76 OK, the same as the installed c70ba2b0; `v4/orig` 81 kernels OK, none wrong (c70ba2b0: 75 OK, one cgeist timeout under load); f4_exit's driver crashes and n1_exact / n2_countdown time out with both binaries (the latter two hold intentionally infinite C loops). The W5 final binary repeats these sets in the item 2 entry.
  - Polygeist lit (private lit trees, private HOME): HLS without hlsfactory: 2ec1956c 93 pass / 2 fail / 3 unsupported on the 98 older tests (and fails both new W5 tests); b3a248a8 93 pass / 3 fail / 3 unsupported, the third failure being a CHECK of the new test (`-h` is hoisted as a symbol), fixed before the commit and passing alone: 94 / 2 / 3. The 2 failures (array_partition/lowering.c, pipeline/lowering.c) fail with 2ec1956c too. Verification 135 pass + 8 expected failures, unchanged.
- **Reverted?:** no

One Polygeist design has no v2 counterpart: hp_fft_hls/hp_fft_hls (the shared common.tcl).
