## 2026-10-10 — hls-est-validate: W1c exactclass defaults review fixes (experiment run cache keyed on its inputs; a damaged cgeist md5 memo is recomputed; no MLIR cache key from an empty md5; stale W1a scalar numbers corrected in place)
- **Commit:** the commit that adds this entry, "hls-est: W1c exactclass defaults review fixes" (branch `hls-est-vitis-match`)
- **Files:** tools/hls-est-validate/common.py (`dump_json`; new `cgeist_md5`, `_read_cgeist_md5_memo`, `_md5_stable`, `CGEIST_MD5_MEMO`), run_hlsest.py (`cgeist_md5` replaced by `cgeist_md5_or_raise` / `CgeistUnhashable`; `source_hash`; new `select_designs`, `inputs_digest`, `mlir_from_record`, `needs_cgeist`; `process`, `main`), experiment_pipeline.py (new `inputs_key`; `run_config`), shim/exactclass/README.md, CHANGELOG.md (in-place corrections), MEMOS.md (M6 addendum W1c), OPEN_ISSUES.md (O6, O7, O13 titles)
- **What:**
  - *Review defect 1, experiment_pipeline.py's run cache.* `run_config` keyed `experiments/runs/<cid>.json` on the flags, the hls-est identity and the shim only, so a run after a cgeist reinstall (or with another `CGEIST_BIN`), or a full-set run after a `--datasets` run, reused the old result. The key is now: flags | hls-est git | hls-est sha256 (always; before only with `HLS_EST_BIN`) | shim | timeout | `run_hlsest.inputs_digest` (sha256 over every selected design's key and its MLIR cache key: sources, headers, the shim's files and cgeist flags, the cgeist md5; an mlir-only design's MLIR file hash). It is computed once per invocation; the script exits when cgeist cannot be hashed. Old `experiments/runs/*.json` are no longer reused (one-time miss).
  - *Review defect 2, the md5 memo.* `common.cgeist_md5` (moved from run_hlsest.py) treats a memo that is not valid JSON, not an object, or holds a value that is not an md5 as missing: it rehashes and rewrites it. Hash and memo key come from one open file; a binary that changes while it is read (a copy in progress) gives `''`, and `''` is never memoized. `common.dump_json` writes through a temporary file unique per call (`mkstemp` in the target's directory, mode 0644 as before) and `os.replace`, so concurrent writers of one file (several agents after a reinstall) no longer share `<file>.tmp`.
  - *Related, an empty md5.* `run_hlsest.source_hash` raises `CgeistUnhashable` instead of building a key from `''`; `main` checks once, before any work, when a selected design would run cgeist (not mlir-only, has sources, no usable `--mlir-from` record) and exits 1 with a message. Runs that call no cgeist proceed and record `_meta.cgeist_md5 ''` as before.
  - *Docs (review defect 3).* W1a's scalar numbers, measured on stale 2026-10-09 MLIR (pre-W1 36 / 89, \|err\| 27361; item 1 42 / 31567; items 2-4 and the W1a review fixes 45 / 31570), are corrected in place to the fresh-MLIR values (37 / 27297; 43 / 31329; 46 / 31332), with the replay runs named; mult_op_module_dot's stale per-array line in W1a item 1 too. W1c item 2: 84 adapter rows, not 86. W1c item 4 and MEMOS M6: `scores` RAM_T2P -> RAM_2P (1 BRAM) in all five designs that change, not only llama_transformer_p2. O6 / O7 / O13 titles say "until W1c". `shim/exactclass/README.md` names the shim the default; this re-keys every exactclass MLIR cache entry once (the run below paid it). Not edited: CLAUDE.md "Current default pipeline" / "Current numbers" (the coordinator's file; text in the W1c implementer report).
- **Why:** the W1c review (defects 1-3).
- **Effect:** no estimator change; no C++ change (the rebuild left `build/clion/bin/hls-est` at sha256 f1b80405...). Harness subset, default pipeline and shim, installed cgeist c70ba2b0 (md5 6ff60ea1), every design re-run through cgeist because of the README re-key (`~/hls-est-work/rvf-w1c/runs/def.json`, frozen `rvf-w1c/bin/hls-est` = f1b80405): **55 / 89, \|err\| 8425**, design for design equal to W1c item 4 (`w1c/runs/x_i4`), MLIR byte-identical on all 96 subset designs with sources, no timeouts; gates polybench_pragmas bicg 8, gesummv 16, mvt 8, syrk 32 exact; no exact design lost. W1a stages replayed on fresh scalar MLIR (`~/hls-est-work/rvf-w1c/runs/s_a1`-`s_a5`, frozen W1a binaries, `--mlir-from w1c/runs/s_prew1`, `--timeout 900`, no timeouts): 43 / 31329, 46 / 31332, 46 / 31332, 46 / 31332, 46 / 31332; per stage the same designs change as on the stale MLIR. Reproducers (throwaway HOME `~/hls-est-work/fx-w1c/home`): the review's `printf '{' > cgeist-md5.json; run_hlsest.py --benchmarks atax --mlir-from ...` now exits 0 (atax 13) and leaves a valid memo; a missing `CGEIST_BIN` with cgeist needed exits 1 before writing anything, with `--mlir-from` it runs; `run_config`'s cache id differs between `--datasets polybench` and all designs, between cgeist binaries, shims and timeouts, and is stable when repeated; 8 processes x 150 concurrent `dump_json` of one file: no reader error, no temp file left. Lit `test/hls-est`: 71 pass, 1 expected failure, 24 unresolved, 4 failing pre-existing (activation_op1, tiled_attn_module / p1 / p2; the same sets as W1c item 4). `check_debug_invariance.sh`: 38 / 0 / 4 (sha, gin, pna, matrix_multiplication). `check_pipeline_unroll.py`: 81 / 86 decisions, 70 / 86 BRAM.
- **Reverted?:** no

## 2026-10-10 — cgeist: honour `#pragma HLS inline off`; opt-in `--hls-keep-invariant-loops` (MEMOS decisions #11, #12; W3-cgeist)
- **Commit:** Polygeist 62c40544 and its follow-up 2ec1956c (branch `hls-est-vitis-match` in `/mnt/d/HLS`); this entry: the commit that adds it. **Not installed:** `build/bin/cgeist` stays c70ba2b0 until hls-est handles the kept calls (N14). Private binary: `/home/peter/tmp/w3cg/cgeist.2ec1956c` (md5 947214ff4640d47c32387d20df58493e).
- **Files (Polygeist):** tools/cgeist/Lib/HLSPragma.{h,cc} (`cgeist::hls::registerInlinerExtension`, `HLSFuncInlinerInterface`, `--hls-ignore-inline-off`); tools/cgeist/driver.cc (registered in place of `mlir::func::registerInlinerExtension`; `--hls-keep-invariant-loops`); lib/polygeist/Passes/ParallelLICM.cpp, include/polygeist/Passes/Passes.h (`createParallelLICMPass(bool keepLoops)`); tools/cgeist/Test/HLS/regressions/inline-off-kept-call.c and keep-invariant-loops.c (new); Test/HLS/inline/inline.cpp (comment); docs/hls-pragmas.md (4.2, 7, 8, 10).
- **What:**
  - `inline off` (every spelling `--hls-annotate` parses) is honoured by default. cgeist's func-dialect inliner interface refuses to inline a call whose callee carries `hls.inline_off`, so the call stays a `func.call` (inside loops it is raised to affine like any other op) and the callee stays a function with `hls.inline_off`. Functions without the pragma, or with `inline` on, are inlined as before. `--hls-ignore-inline-off` restores the old behaviour for comparisons.
  - Exception (2ec1956c): an array argument that does not fit the shape the parameter declares (C ignores the first dimension, e.g. `memref<32x32xi16>` passed to `p[8][32]`) gives a `memref.cast` that MLIR rejects and that only inlining removes. Such a call is inlined as before, with a warning naming the callee and both types. A row argument (`f(a[k])`) is passed as `polygeist.subindex` (O25).
  - `--hls-keep-invariant-loops` (default off): ParallelLICM, the LICM before raise-scf-to-affine, no longer hoists an op that is or contains a loop; other invariant ops are still hoisted. Bisected with `--pm-enable-printing` on inl_o5_f8. It needs `--parallel-licm` (the default); otherwise cgeist reports an error.
- **Why:** VITIS_NOTES N14 (R1-R3: Vitis keeps an `inline off` callee as one module with instances by concurrency; "O5": Vitis decides pipeline/flatten on the unhoisted loop, 3 kernels); OPEN_ISSUES O10, O5; MEMOS decisions #11 (honour by default, escape option) and #12 (opt-in flag).
- **Effect** (harness subset, exactclass, default pipeline, private HOMEs). Both sides use the same frozen hls-est, `hls-est.cur` = build/clion at aa8c68eba plus another agent's uncommitted LoopPragmas.h / AutoPipelineUnroll.cpp (built 21:45). The MLIR comes from cgeist c70ba2b0 (installed) vs 2ec1956c (private). Runs: `/home/peter/tmp/w3cg/r_{base,new2}.json`, replays of `h_{base,new2}.json`.
  - Exact 55 / 89 → 56 / 87 compared, \|err\| 8425 → 4932. On the 87 designs compared in both: 55 → 56, \|err\| 8338 → 4932. Only forgebench changes (10 / 40 → 11 / 38). No exact design is lost; the gates bicg 8, gesummv 16, mvt 8, syrk 32 stay exact.
  - The MLIR changes in 19 designs, all forgebench and all with `inline off`. The other two subset designs with the pragma, mult_op_module_mm and _mmv, stop in cgeist both before and after (empty module).
  - hls-est's default pipeline starts with `--inline`, so hls-est still inlines every kept call per call site. Per-instance pricing (N14) is not implemented. The totals that change do so because cgeist no longer specialises and raises the callee per call site before hls-est sees it:
    - tiled_attn_module 110 → **270 = Vitis, by cancellation**. BRAM_A2 / BRAM_A3 go 0 → 64 each (= Vitis, RAM_AUTO_0R0W in top). split_wq / split_wk go 0 → 16 each (Vitis has no such rows). Still off: BRAM_B2 / BRAM_B3 0 (Vitis 16 each), V / scores 2 each (Vitis 1), and Vitis' 4 non-array BRAM.
    - conv_module 1663 → 767 (Vitis 1042): localW 1088 → 192 (Vitis 512 in its one `conv_via_tiling_3x3` instance). Before, one of the three inlined copies had localW as RAM_1WNR with 15 copies (960). Now each inlined copy is RAM_1P 64. Neither is right per array.
    - conv_block_module 7257 → 5465 (Vitis 5139) and conv_block_op2 6246 → 4454 (Vitis 4796): localW 2248 → 456 and 1920 → 128 (Vitis 512), by the same mechanism.
    - The other 13 MLIR changes leave the total as it was: activation_module / _op1 / _op2 / _op3, attn_breakdown_module / _op1 / _op2, conv_block_op1 / _op3, diff_dims_module_large / _small, diff_orders_module, vec_mtx_module.
  - **New hls-est failures (not fixed here):** Llama_GPT_module (was 33, Vitis 30) and mult_op_module_dot (was 142, Vitis 58) fail with "operation being parsed with an unregistered dialect". The cause is `polygeist.subindex` from row arguments of kept calls (O25). Neither design was exact.
  - The 62c40544 build alone also stopped attn_breakdown_module / _op1 / _op2 in cgeist (empty module: the invalid `memref.cast` of a kept call). 2ec1956c fixes this.
  - Polygeist lit, in private lit trees with the private binary:
    - HLS without hlsfactory: 93 pass, 5 fail. The 5 (array_partition/lowering.c, consumer/hls-est-banking.c, consumer/hls-est-pipeline.c, pipeline/lowering.c, polybench/hls-est-e2e.c) also fail with c70ba2b0, which in addition fails the 2 new tests.
    - Verification: 135 pass and 8 expected failures, the same as c70ba2b0.
    - hls-est is unchanged, so `test/hls-est` is not affected.
- **Reverted?:** no

## 2026-10-10 — hls-doc/forgebench-gap.md: why forgebench is far off and which passes would fix it (FB-diag); VITIS_NOTES N17; OPEN_ISSUES O28
- **Commit:** 0845d6dc0 (branch `hls-est-vitis-match`)
- **Files:** hls-doc/forgebench-gap.md (new: cause table, hypothesis verdicts H1-H6, per-design table, ranked pass list); tools/hls-est-validate/VITIS_NOTES.md (N17); OPEN_ISSUES.md (O28 new; O17 item 3: cause confirmed); CHANGELOG.md.
- **What:** Diagnosis of the forgebench gap in `~/hls-est-work/w1c/runs/x_i4.json` (hls-est 5b9418cb8, exactclass, default pipeline): 40 compared, 10 exact, \|err\| 8278 of the subset's 8425. Seven causes, each with per-array evidence (`--bram-debug-json` of the run, the Vitis memory and loop tables, `hls_compile.log`, the `a.o.*.ll` stages), 12 Vitis kernels (`~/hls-est-work/exp-src/fbh{1,3,6}/`) and 3 cgeist probes.
- **Why:** Peter asked why forgebench is so far off and which passes would close the gap (MEMOS decision #27, track W7).
- **Effect:** none on any estimate (documentation and notes). Findings (\|err\| explained per dominant cause): C1 callee-parameter `array_partition` lost at inlining 1284; C2 `memref.global` unbanked and unattributed (O4) 1970; C3 one copy per call site instead of one instance per `inline off` callee (O10) 4831 together with C1; C4 dead arrays written through a shared callee instance kept by Vitis as `RAM_AUTO_0R0W` 160; C5 constant-fill ROM width (O18) 19; C6 math-core tables (N16, W2) 13; C7 the callee's accumulator recurrence sets the II (N14 R7) 42; residual 4. Hypotheses: H1 refuted as stated (the arrays are not partitioned in hls-est at all; the 15 copies are N4 working as designed on an unbanked array), H2 confirmed for the global designs (diff_orders_module / diff_dims_module_large are C1 locals), H3 confirmed with a different rule (N17 (a): not "never accessed" but "written through a shared instance"), H4 16, H5 5 per affected design, H6 three cgeist defects plus a harness blind spot (O28). Opt-in options on forgebench, same binary, `--mlir-from x_i4.json`: `--bram-attribute-globals` 10 / 40, 8278 (nothing moves); with `--promote-globals-to-allocas` 7 / 40, 9343 (O17). Ranked passes: propagate callee-parameter pragmas before inlining (~1250 alone), bank globals in place (~1970), one instance per non-inlined callee (~4800 with the first), dead arrays behind shared instances (160), ROM narrowing (19), math tables (13-20). *Corrected in place by the FB-diag review fixes (entry above): C1 alone does not gain about 1250; it needs two-dimensional banking first (O17 item 2), and measured with first-entry banking it gains 0 on diff_dims_module_large and loses 672 on diff_orders_module. At design level C3 is 4834 (with Llama_GPT_module), C5 17, C6 13, C7 0 (28 over arrays, BRAM_C1/C2 only) and there is no residual (tiled_attn_module's is a `compare.py` artefact, O29). mult_op_module_dot's BRAM_B1/B2 are a new cause C8 (N17 (d)). C4's condition is "written through a callee with another live call site", not "a shared instance". The two-dimensional banking plus the C2 own-pragma part (859) comes first in the ranked list.*
- **Reverted?:** no

## 2026-10-10 — hls-est-validate: compare.py groups Vitis memory rows by their own variable (FB-diag harness fix)
- **Commit:** a892b780c (branch `hls-est-vitis-match`)
- **Files:** tools/hls-est-validate/compare.py: new `BANK_TAIL_RE`, `known_root`, `vitis_array_name`; `vitis_arrays(v, known=None)`; `compare_one` passes the hls-est array names.
- **What:** `vitis_arrays()` grouped the Vitis `* Memory:` rows by `vitis_parse`'s bank base (`array`), which folded the real arrays `FM_buffer_1` / `FM_buffer_2` / `FM_buffer_3` into one fake `FM_buffer` row (and `BRAM_1_0_15` into `BRAM_1_0`). With the hls-est names known, a row is now grouped by its own variable (csynth.xml BindNode VARIABLE, else the instance without `_U`), and a name that extends a known array by bank / inline suffixes only is folded into it: `align_arrays.py`'s T1 `report_key` / `known_root`, ported.
- **Why:** the per-array tables showed spurious gaps (`FM_buffer: None/3072` next to `FM_buffer_1: 1024/None`; 64 separate `BRAM_conv_weight_<k>` rows in conv_A), which misled the forgebench diagnosis.
- **Effect:** totals, exact counts and \|err\| unchanged on all 143 designs (checked against the previous module on `x_i4.json` and `coord/x_i4_all.json`; subset 55 / 89, 8425; forgebench 10 / 40, 8278). The per-array rows change in 28 designs, e.g. conv_A `BRAM_conv_weight: 64/512`, diff_orders_p3 `BRAM_1: 8/128; BRAM_2: 16/256; BRAM_4: 8/128`.
- **Reverted?:** no

## 2026-10-10 — hls-est-validate: the cgeist md5 is memoized by path, size and mtime (W1c item 1 follow-up)
- **Commit:** 8257adddd (branch `hls-est-vitis-match`); this hash line: the W1c changelog-only commit
- **Files:** tools/hls-est-validate/run_hlsest.py: `cgeist_md5` memoizes its result in `~/hls-est-work/cgeist-md5.json`, keyed by the binary's real path, size and mtime; new `MD5_MEMO`.
- **What:** Every `run_hlsest.py` run hashed the 4 GB cgeist binary for `_meta.cgeist_md5`, even with `--mlir-from`. Since W1c item 1 the same md5 is also part of the MLIR cache key. Read through `/mnt/d`, the hash took 5-15 minutes per run on a loaded machine: `x_i4fs` spent 15 minutes at about 4 MB/s before its first design. The md5 is now looked up by (real path, size, mtime) and computed only when that triple is new. A reinstall renames the old binary aside and copies the new one, which gives a new mtime, so a new binary is always hashed. A failed memo write is ignored, because the memo is only an optimization.
- **Why:** harness turnaround; no estimate or key changes (the same md5).
- **Effect:** none on any result: the key and `_meta` carry the same md5. The first run after this commit writes the memo, and later runs skip the hash.
- **Reverted?:** no

## 2026-10-10 — hls-est: `--convert-affine-to-loopschedule=forward-stores` on by default (MEMOS M6 recommendation 2, decision #5; W1c item 4)
- **Commit:** d4210fc36 (branch `hls-est-vitis-match`); this hash line: the W1c changelog-only commit
- **Files:**
  - include/circt/Conversion/Passes.td: option `forwardStores` defaults to `"true"`; description updated.
  - lib/Dialect/Resource/Passes/AffineToLoopScheduleModified.cpp: comment at `cleanupRedundantAccesses`' call.
  - test/hls-est/Passes/access_forwarding.mlir: new RUN line, where the default gives the forwarded report (REPORT). The NOFWD line passes `forward-stores=false`. Comment updated.
  - test/hls-est/Passes/dme_attention_int.mlir: its two BRAM RUN lines pin `forward-stores=false`, with a comment explaining why (see Checks).
  - tools/hls-est-validate/experiment_pipeline.py: `ABLATION` row `no-forward-stores`.
  - MEMOS.md: M6 addendum with the Llama_GPT_module analysis; decision row #5.
  - OPEN_ISSUES.md: O7 status and a Llama_GPT_module note under O10.
- **What:** Store-to-load forwarding and dead-store elimination on the unrolled bodies are now on by default: an unrolled `acc[i] +=` becomes one store per iteration. `=false` keeps every access. Decision #5 allowed this only after #4 (the dead-memory front end, item 2) and only if working-loop rule 4 holds after a look at Llama_GPT_module's Q / K. Both conditions are met, as below.
- **Why:** VITIS_NOTES N4: R and W per iteration are counted after unrolling, CSE and forwarding. OPEN_ISSUES O7, MEMOS M6.
- **Effect** (harness subset, exactclass; `--mlir-from` the item-1 baseline; run `~/hls-est-work/w1c/runs/x_i4fs.json`, made with the item-3 binary `hls-est.flp` and `--convert-affine-to-loopschedule=forward-stores=true` on the item-3 default pipeline, vs `x_i3.json`; replayed with this commit's binary `hls-est.fwd` without the option, `x_i4.json`: identical to `x_i4fs` on all 91 designs, every memref and every pipeline record):
  - 52 / 89 → **55 / 89** exact, \|err\| 8435 → **8425**. Only forgebench changes (7 → 10 / 40, \|err\| 8288 → 8278).
  - *Newly exact:* attention_op_p2 and attention_op_p3 20 → 24 (Vitis 24), and gpt_transformer_p1 24 → 28 (Vitis 28). The unrolled `Q[s][d] +=` / `K[s][d] +=` of the QKV loop is one store per iteration. The scores loop then runs at II 1 with 4 reads of Q and of K per cycle, so Q and K become RAM_1WNR with 3 copies each, = Vitis (RAM_1WNR_AUTO_1R1W 3; Vitis pipelines that loop at II 1 in both designs). scores goes RAM_T2P → RAM_2P at the same 1 BRAM.
  - *No exact design lost.* Gates polybench_pragmas bicg 8, gesummv 16, mvt 8, syrk 32 are exact.
  - *Farther (the only one):* forgebench/Llama_GPT_module 29 → 33 (Vitis 30). Per array it is explained by O10 and by the math tables (decision #18). It is not a forwarding error:
    - The source calls `grouped_multihead_attention_..._rope` twice, with `(groups, is_rope)` = (8, 0) and (4, 1). The function has `#pragma HLS inline off`, and the top has `#pragma HLS allocation function instances=... limit=1`.
    - Vitis builds one shared instance in which `groups` and `is_rope` are runtime values. Its scores loop (`VITIS_LOOP_207_9_VITIS_LOOP_208_10`, trip 64) runs at II 2, and its QKV loop at II 16. Q, K and scores are ram_t2p and V is RAM_1WNR, 1 BRAM each.
    - cgeist inlines both calls with constant arguments (O10), so hls-est has two instances.
      - The (4, 1) copy matches Vitis' instance: scores loop at II 2, Q / K RAM_2P 1 each.
      - The (8, 0) copy is the one Vitis does not have. With forwarding it behaves like the single-call designs above (scores loop II 1, Q / K RAM_1WNR 3 each); without forwarding it was RAM_2P 1 each (scores loop II 3).
    - So the extra instance costs Q 3 + K 3 + V 1 + scores 1 = 8 instead of 4.
    - The other side of the total is −5: hls-est does not cost the hls:: math tables Vitis instantiates in this design (`cordic_apfixed_circ_table_arctan_128` 4, the `pow_reduce` table 1; VITIS_NOTES N16, decision #18, W2).
    - So 33 = 30 + 8 − 5, and the former 29 = 30 + 4 − 5. Neither is right per array.
  - *Kernels* (cgeist c70ba2b0 + exactclass + the default pipeline; `~/hls-est-work/w1c/dgk/`):
    - dg_d_apfixed4: 8 with forwarding = Vitis 8 per array (Q 3 and K 3 RAM_1WNR, V 1, scores 1); 4 without (Q / K 1 each).
    - dg_d_apfixed16: 2 = Vitis either way (V 1, scores 1; Q / K dead).
  - Rule 4 holds: net +3 exact with lower error, no exact design lost, and the one design that gets farther is explained per array by named issues (O10, decision #18 / N16).
  - Debug JSONs of `x_i3` vs `x_i4fs`: memrefs change only in these 4 designs and in llama_transformer_p2, whose scores goes RAM_T2P → RAM_2P at the same 1 BRAM. (The same relabel happens in the 4 designs too, both instances in Llama_GPT_module; no other memref changes status, RAM type or BRAM.) Pipeline II records change in 10 designs; the changes are lower IIs, for example attention_op_p1's context loop 5 → 4 and vitis_examples imperfect_loops 2 → 1, with no BRAM change.
- **Checks:**
  - Lit `test/hls-est`: 71 pass, 1 expected failure, 24 unresolved, 4 failing pre-existing (activation_op1, tiled_attn_module / p1 / p2). dme_attention_int.mlir failed under the new default and now pins `forward-stores=false` on its two BRAM RUN lines. Its reduced kernel has no softmax, so scores becomes replicable and the context loop runs at II 1: V / scores RAM_1WNR, 21 BRAM (81 for the control). Real attention keeps the context loop at II 4 (attention_op_p1, = Vitis), and the real kernels above are exact. access_forwarding.mlir has the new default RUN line.
  - `check_debug_invariance.sh` (default pipeline): 38 / 0 / 4; `check_pipeline_unroll.py` unchanged (81 / 86 decisions, 70 / 86 BRAM).
  - The frozen binary also contains another agent's uncommitted `hls.uncounted` edit, inert as in item 2.
- **Reverted?:** no

## 2026-10-10 — hls-est: `--auto-pipeline-unroll=float-loads-perfect` on by default (MEMOS M8, decision #3; W1c item 3)
- **Commit:** 5b9418cb8 (branch `hls-est-vitis-match`); this hash line: the W1c changelog-only commit
- **Files:**
  - lib/Dialect/Resource/Passes/AutoPipelineUnroll.cpp: option `floatLoadsPerfect` now `cl::init(true)`; description; the two header comments on the rule.
  - test/hls-est/Passes/auto_pipeline_float_loads.mlir: the default RUN line now checks the float rule (FLT), a new explicit `=float-loads-perfect` line does too, and the former default prefix `DEF` is `OFF` on a `=float-loads-perfect=false` line.
  - tools/hls-est-validate/experiment_pipeline.py: `ABLATION` row `no-float-loads-perfect`.
  - MEMOS.md: decision row #3.
  - OPEN_ISSUES.md: O13 status.
- **What:** A read-only inner-invariant load of a floating-point element no longer makes a loop nest imperfect, so such a nest is flattened and its inner loop pipelined instead of unrolled. This is VITIS_NOTES N3 "Floating-point invariant loads" (18 kernels; polybench atax lp3/lp4, bicg lp1/lp2). The option stays: `=false` gives the original T3 rule. The rule keys on the MLIR element type. With exactclass, now the harness default (item 1), that is the type Vitis sees: an `ap_fixed` is an integer. That was M8's condition for the default.
- **Why:** decision #3 (approved together with exactclass); N3 / M8 / O13.
- **Effect** (harness subset, exactclass; `--mlir-from` the item-1 baseline; frozen `~/hls-est-work/w1c/bin/hls-est.flp` = this commit's code on fcf9a59b2; run `~/hls-est-work/w1c/runs/x_i3.json` vs `x_i2.json`):
  - **No total and no memref changes:** 52 / 89, \|err\| 8435 before and after. Every memref's BRAM, RAM type and status is identical on all 91 designs (debug JSONs compared).
  - Pipeline decisions change in 3 designs:
    - polybench atax and bicg: in the nest whose inner-invariant float load made it imperfect, the outer loop was pipelined at II 32 with the 64-trip inner loop unrolled. It is now flattened into an II-1 pipeline. This is Vitis' decision (N3, M8). The totals stay 13 / 14 = Vitis: buff_y_out / buff_s_out stay 2 = Vitis, now for Vitis' reason (O3 item 3, M6).
    - machsuite gemm_blocked (double): before, k was pipelined (II 5) with j unrolled 8x and 3 parents flattened; now j is pipelined (II 1) with 4 parents flattened. Vitis pipelines `loopk_loopj` (j with k flattened, II 8) and does not flatten loopi. Neither hls-est decision is Vitis'; the BRAM stays 0 = Vitis.
  - The three ap_fixed designs whose decisions changed under the scalar shim in M8 (Llama_GPT_module, gpt_transformer_p1, llama_transformer_p2) do not change: their elements are integers under exactclass. polybench_pragmas/bicg does not change either.
  - Gates exact; no exact design lost.
  - Validation kernels (`check_pipeline_unroll.py`, 86 compared): decisions agree on 74 → 81, BRAM totals equal on 64 → 70.
    - Decisions now agree on pf_fadd, pf_fmac, pf_fmul, pf_srchoist, rv_rmw_hoist_f_m8_n64, rv_rmw_hoist_f_m64_n64 and rv_st_hoist_f_m64_n64.
    - BRAM now equal on pf_fadd / pf_fmac / pf_fmul / pf_srchoist 4 → 2, rv_rmw_hoist_f_m8_n64 26 → 3 and rv_rmw_hoist_f_m64_n64 99 → 11, all = Vitis.
    - Nothing gets worse. The 5 remaining disagreements are O10 (4) and O8 (1), as in M8.
  - Rule 4: the same exact count and the same error on the subset, with per-loop improvements on the subset and on the kernels, and nothing lost.
- **Checks:**
  - Lit `test/hls-est`: 71 pass, 1 expected failure, 24 unresolved, 4 failing pre-existing. No new failures; `auto_pipeline_float_loads.mlir` passes all three lines.
  - `check_debug_invariance.sh` (default pipeline): 38 / 0 / 4.
  - The frozen binary also contains another agent's uncommitted `hls.uncounted` edit, inert as in item 2.
- **Reverted?:** no

## 2026-10-10 — hls-est: the dead-memory front end joins the default pipeline and the m_axi adapter cost is on by default (MEMOS M5 recommendation 1 with T8's `--inline`, decision #4; W1c item 2)
- **Commit:** fcf9a59b2 (branch `hls-est-vitis-match`); this hash line: the W1c changelog-only commit
- **Files:**
  - lib/Dialect/Resource/Passes/BRAMAnalysis.cpp: `bramMaxiAdaptersOpt` now `cl::init(true)`; description updated.
  - tools/hls-est-validate/common.py: new `DME_FRONT` and `NO_FRONT_PIPELINE`; `DEFAULT_PIPELINE` = `DME_FRONT` + `NO_FRONT_PIPELINE`.
  - tools/hls-est-validate/experiment_pipeline.py `ABLATION`:
    - the canonical-relative rows carry the front;
    - the T4 `with-dme*` rows and `DME_PRE` / `DME_FLAGS` are replaced by one-change rows `no-front`, `no-inline`, `no-vitis-fold`, `no-dme`, `no-maxi`, `+globals` and `+call-latency`;
    - rows that became duplicates are dropped: `base` (= `no-auto-unroll`), `+sccp` and `+inline` (now in the front), `pipeline-loops-64` (= canonical);
    - `+canonicalize+cse` becomes `+cse`.
  - tools/hls-est-validate/check_debug_invariance.sh: default `PIPELINE`.
  - tools/hls-est-validate/check_pipeline_unroll.py: its BRAM step runs `common.DME_FRONT` first. The decision step does not, because its loop mapping follows the source order per function.
  - test/hls-est/Passes/{dependence_scope,index_castui,pipeline_yield_late}.mlir: the default-pipeline RUN line gets the front; comments updated.
  - test/hls-est/Passes/maxi_adapter.mlir: the main RUN line relies on the default; the OFF line passes `--bram-maxi-adapters=false`.
  - MEMOS.md: decision row #4.
  - OPEN_ISSUES.md: O16 and O17 status.
- **What:**
  - The default pipeline is now `--inline --canonicalize --sccp --vitis-fold --dead-memory-elimination --affine-loop-normalize --auto-pipeline-unroll --memory-banking-bram --convert-affine-to-loopschedule --bram-analysis`.
    - The front is exactly T8's "prefix B" (`~/hls-est-work/t8/runs/xc2_B_nos.json` `_meta.pipeline`).
    - The harness puts it right after its top selection (`--symbol-privatize=exclude=<top> --symbol-dce`, plus `top=<top>` on `--bram-analysis`).
  - The m_axi adapter cost is a `--bram-analysis` default rather than a pipeline flag. That way it applies wherever `--bram-analysis` runs: every lit RUN line, and the Compiler Explorer integration if one appears (W1a found none). `--bram-maxi-adapters=false` turns it off.
  - Globals stay globals: no `--promote-globals-to-allocas` and no `--bram-attribute-globals`, which wait for O10 / O17 (M5 recommendation 4).
  - `--vitis-fold` keeps its `undef` default (T8).
- **Why:** decision #4 (MEMOS M5 recommendation 1, approved with exactclass, which item 1 made the default). N1 / N1a: Vitis deletes the arrays whose loads reach no output by `a.o.1.ll` and adds a `<bundle>_m_axi_U` adapter per m_axi bundle. N13: Vitis' undefined `x / 0`.
- **Effect** (harness subset, exactclass; `--mlir-from` the item-1 baseline `x_base.json`; frozen `~/hls-est-work/w1c/bin/hls-est.maxi` = this commit's code on b345b36b2; run `~/hls-est-work/w1c/runs/x_i2.json` vs `x_base.json`):

  | dataset | n | before (item 1 baseline) | after |
  |---|---|---|---|
  | polybench | 9 | 8 / 114 | 8 / 114 |
  | polybench_pragmas | 7 | 6 / 16 | 6 / 16 |
  | forgebench | 40 | 3 / 8462 | **7 / 8288** |
  | machsuite | 11 | 10 / 16 | 10 / 16 |
  | spector_hls | 8 | 8 / 0 | 8 / 0 |
  | vitis_examples | 14 | 13 / 1 | 13 / 1 |
  | **subset** | 89 | **48 / 8609** | **52 / 8435** |

  Cells are exact / total \|err\|. Only forgebench changes. Changes per array, from the compare rows:
  - *Newly exact (7):*
    - activation_module / op2 / op3 256 → 258 and diff_dims_module_small 1152 → 1154: the mem1 adapter is 2 and mem2 is 0, both = Vitis.
    - attention_op_p1 and tiled_attn_p1 203 → 73, and tiled_attn_p2 55 → 23. Q, K, BRAM_weights_q and BRAM_weights_k are `eliminated_dead` (64 + 64 + 2 + 2, or 16 + 16 + 1 + 1 in p2), and the adapter adds 2. This equals Vitis per array (N1's a.o.1 list).
  - *No longer exact (3), all O17 cancellations (M5):* mult_op_p2, mult_op_p3 and vec_mtx_p1 go 2 → 4 (Vitis 2). The new mem1 adapter is 2 = Vitis. The remaining 2 are BRAM_1 / BRAM_2 at 1 each, where Vitis partitions the globals into 16 banks of 64x16 or registers (0 BRAM). Their pragmas sit on a callee parameter or on two dimensions, which the "global = one RAM_1P" rule ignores (O17 items 2-3). They matched before only because that error cancelled the missing adapter.
  - *Moved, not exact before or after:*
    - +2 from the adapter alone, = Vitis per bundle: Llama_GPT_module 27 → 29 (30), gpt_transformer_p1 22 → 24 (28), llama_transformer_p2 23 → 25 (30), attention_op_p2 / p3 18 → 20 (24), attn_breakdown_* (op1 9 → 11, Vitis 10), conv_*, diff_dims_*, diff_orders_module, activation_op1, testing_impl, vec_mtx_module, mlp (no Vitis result).
    - mult_op_module_dot 136 → 142: three bundles of 2.
    - diff_orders_p1 24 → 22 (130), p2 10 → 11 (2), p3 40 → 34 (514), and mult_op_p1 5 → 6 (2): BRAM_3 (4 / 1 / 8 / 1) is `eliminated_dead`, which agrees with Vitis (no BRAM_3 memory), plus the adapter.
    - tiled_attn_module 304 → 110 (Vitis 270): Q, K, split_wq and split_wk are eliminated, = Vitis. So are BRAM_A2 / A3 / B2 / B3, which Vitis keeps as `RAM_AUTO_0R0W` (160 BRAM) because its `load_*` callees are not inlined. This is O16 / O10, as in M5.
  - Over the compared designs, all 84 adapter rows equal Vitis per bundle (*corrected in place by the W1c review fixes: 84, not 86; counted from `x_i2.cmp.json`, rows of kind `M_AXI_ADAPTER` / core `m_axi_adapter` in the 89 compared designs; 97 over all 143 rows*). Of the 32 eliminated arrays, 28 have no Vitis BRAM; the 4 others are the tiled_attn_module ones above.
  - Compared with T8's "exactclass + B" run (`t8/runs/xc2_B_nos`, 49 / 89, 8442), design for design the only differences are W1a's `--bram-seq-dual-port` changes: atax, bicg and mvt become exact, and gesummv goes 20 → 21.
  - Gates polybench_pragmas bicg 8, gesummv 16, mvt 8, syrk 32 are exact. No other exact design is lost.
- **Checks:**

The 71 include another agent's uncommitted `auto_pipeline_uncounted.mlir`; W1a's 70 plus that one, so nothing new fails. The three default-pipeline RUN lines pass with the front, and maxi_adapter passes with the default and with `=false`.
  - `check_debug_invariance.sh` with the new default pipeline: 38 pass / 0 fail / 4 err, as before.

## 2026-10-10 — hls-est-validate: `exactclass` is the harness default shim; the MLIR cache key names the cgeist binary (MEMOS M2 / M3, decision #8; W1c item 1)
- **Commit:** b345b36b2 (branch `hls-est-vitis-match`); this hash line: the W1c changelog-only commit
- **Files:** tools/hls-est-validate/common.py (new `DEFAULT_SHIM = "exactclass"`), tools/hls-est-validate/run_hlsest.py (`--shim` default `C.DEFAULT_SHIM` and help text, docstring; new `cgeist_md5` (memoized per process); `source_hash` adds the cgeist md5 to the MLIR cache key; `_meta.cgeist_md5` from the same md5), tools/hls-est-validate/experiment_pipeline.py (`--shim` default `C.DEFAULT_SHIM`; `run_config`'s run-cache key includes the shim), tools/hls-est-validate/results/baseline/hlsest-2026-10-10-exactclass-w1a-defaults.csv (new: the new baseline, compare.py CSV), tools/hls-est-validate/OPEN_ISSUES.md (O6 status), tools/hls-est-validate/MEMOS.md (decision row #8)
- **What:**
  - `run_hlsest.py` and `experiment_pipeline.py` run cgeist with `shim/exactclass` unless `--shim` names another (`scalar`, `exact`, `polygeist` or a directory stay selectable). The shim's `cgeist_flags` file supplies `--scalar-wrapper-abi`; cgeist's own default is unchanged (decision #9).
  - *MLIR cache key, checked:* it already hashed the shim's path, every file in the shim directory and the shim's cgeist flags, so the shims never share an entry. It did not name the cgeist binary, so after a reinstall the previous binary's MLIR was reused silently. This affected W1a: its scalar runs (`~/hls-est-work/w1a/runs/s_*`) read cache entries written 2026-10-09 11:05-17:00 by that day's cgeist. In them machsuite viterbi's uint8 loop is still an `scf.while`, giving 64 instead of 128 (Vitis 128), and mult_op_module_dot predates O23. W1a's exactclass entries came from cbc105e0, the binary installed at the time. The key now includes the md5 of the cgeist binary. A run computed that md5 for `_meta` anyway; it is now computed once per process and shared. After this commit every entry gets a new key once, which costs one cgeist pass per shim (6-12 min at `-j 8`). `--mlir-from` is unaffected. Tested in `~/hls-est-work/w1c/keytest`: a second run hits the cache (`cached`), and both cbc105e0 and the scalar shim get keys of their own.
  - `experiment_pipeline.py`'s run cache (`experiments/runs/<cid>.json`) was keyed on the flags and the binary only. A run with another shim and the same flags would have been reused. The key now includes `--shim`.
  - `shim/exactclass/README.md` still calls the shim "opt-in". It is left alone because every file in a shim directory is part of the cache key, so editing the README would re-key every exactclass entry. *(Fixed by the W1c review fixes, which accept that one re-key.)*
- **Why:** decision #8 (Peter, 2026-10-10: "Yes use exact class"). MEMOS M2 / M3: this gives exact element widths (the N5 LUTRAM threshold; `ap_fixed<16,5>` as `i16`, not `f32`) and the integer ap_fixed that the Vitis folds and DME need (O16).
- **Effect** (harness subset, exact / compared, total \|err\|; frozen `~/hls-est-work/w1c/bin/hls-est.base` = 78899ec78 for the W1a defaults, `~/hls-est-work/w1a/bin/hls-est.base` = e12ea00da for the pre-W1 default; runs `~/hls-est-work/w1c/runs/`):
  - *cgeist during this item.* The installed `build/bin/cgeist` changed from cbc105e0 (md5 23b7ca89) to c70ba2b0 (md5 6ff60ea1, the O23 review-fixes binary) while the first baseline run (`x_w1a`) was running. That run reused the cbc105e0 cache and is not used here. The baseline was re-made with a fresh cgeist pass: c70ba2b0, private HOME `~/hls-est-work/w1c/home` (`x_base`, `s_prew1`). Under exactclass that pass gives MLIR byte-identical to the cbc105e0 cache on all 96 subset designs with sources, with the same cgeist statuses.

    | configuration | shim | cgeist MLIR | exact | \|err\| |
    |---|---|---|---|---|
    | pre-W1 default: split pipeline, e12ea00da (`s_prew1`; conv_block_module / op1 re-run alone at 900 s) | scalar | c70ba2b0, fresh | 37 / 89 | 27297 |
    | W1a defaults, 78899ec78 (`s_w1a`) | scalar | c70ba2b0, fresh | 46 / 89 | 31332 |
    | **W1a defaults, 78899ec78 (`x_base`): the new baseline** | **exactclass** | c70ba2b0, fresh | **48 / 89** | **8609** |
    | W1a's reported numbers (`w1a/runs/s_fix`, stale scalar MLIR) | scalar | 2026-10-09 cache | 45 / 89 | 31570 |

  - *Scalar → exactclass at the W1a defaults* (`s_w1a` → `x_base`, the same cgeist). Only forgebench changes: 41 designs, plus the text of vitis_examples fixed_point_sqrt's cgeist error.
    - Newly exact: mult_op_p2 and vec_mtx_p1, 3 → 2 (Vitis 2). BRAM_1 / BRAM_2 drop from 2 x18 columns at f32 to 1 at i16. They match only by cancellation: Vitis keeps neither global as a memory (O17 items 2-3), and the m_axi adapter is still missing until item 2.
    - No exact design is lost.
    - The forgebench totals roughly halve with the 16-bit elements. Examples: activation_* 512 → 256 (Vitis 258), conv_block_module 13741 → 7255 (5139), attention_op_p1 406 → 203 (73).
    - mult_op_module_dot moves 19 → 136 (Vitis 58). Its six dot-product buffers go from RAM_2P to RAM_1WNR with 15 copies: with integer elements the unrolled 16-read loop has no float recurrence to raise its II, so it replicates (N4). Vitis partitions BRAM_A* instead; this is not diagnosed further, as in W1a item 1.
    - polybench, polybench_pragmas, machsuite, spector_hls and vitis_examples are identical across the two shims.
  - Gates polybench_pragmas bicg 8, gesummv 16, mvt 8, syrk 32 are exact in all four runs.
  - The exactclass baseline equals W1a's exactclass result (`w1arf/runs/x_fix`, 48 / 89, 8609) design for design.
  - The new baseline's compare CSV is saved as `results/baseline/hlsest-2026-10-10-exactclass-w1a-defaults.csv` (the JSON stays git-ignored, `~/hls-est-work/w1c/runs/x_base.json`).
  - No hls-est change, so lit and `check_debug_invariance.sh` are unaffected.
- **Reverted?:** no

## 2026-10-10 — hls-est: W1a defaults review fixes (`top=` reaches every referenced symbol and attributes only reachable get_global sites; scoped debug pipelines and `--vitis-fold` summary; workbook second-run labels; item-1 baseline corrected)
- **Commit:** 0ae005759 (branch `hls-est-vitis-match`); this hash line: the follow-up changelog-only commit
- **Files:** lib/Dialect/Resource/Passes/BRAMAnalysis.cpp (`computeTopScope`, `TopScope::covers` comment, `runOnOperation` get_global sites, `collectPipelineRecords`, `FoldSummary` / `collectFoldSummary` / `printFoldSummary`, `writeDebugJson` fold fields, ROM branch of `estimateBRAM`, new `usedPortWidth`, stdout notes, `top` option text), lib/Dialect/Resource/Passes/VitisFold.cpp (`FoldNote::func`, `recordNotes`), test/hls-est/Passes/bram_top_scope.mlir (new), test/hls-est/Passes/bram_top_fold.mlir (new), test/hls-est/Passes/bram_top.mlir (note line), test/hls-est/Passes/bram_formula_rom_ir.mlir (per-loop ROM copies), test/hls-est/Estimation/run_all.sh (comments), tools/hls-est-validate/make_sheet.py (`--second`, `--second-label`, `second_label`), tools/hls-est-validate/CHANGELOG.md (W1a item 1 Effect corrected in place), tools/hls-est-validate/OPEN_ISSUES.md (O12 status)
- **What:** fixes for the independent review of the four W1a commits (`~/hls-est-work/rv-w1a/`):
  - **D1** `top=` with `--bram-attribute-globals`: a global's get_global sites are attributed only when they lie in a reachable function (`TopScope::covers`), so the port demand, the RAM_1WNR copies and the ROM test (`neverWritten`) no longer count accesses from functions the top never reaches. Before, `top=` alone and the `--symbol-privatize` / `--symbol-dce` prefix disagreed (review kernel `k/top_glob.mlir`: `top=topA` ROM_NP 8 vs prefix ROM_2P 2).
  - **D5** `computeTopScope` follows every SymbolRefAttr of a reachable function's ops (as SymbolTable's use walk: a nested reference is one symbol), the set `--symbol-dce` keeps: the callee of a func.call, the function of a func.constant (an indirect call), the global of a get_global, any other symbol attribute. Before, only CallOpInterface ops with a symbol callee were followed, so a function reached through func.constant + func.call_indirect was silently not costed.
  - **D4** with `top=`, the debug pipeline records cover only the reachable functions, and so does the `--vitis-fold` summary: `--vitis-fold` now records the function of each rewrite (`func`) and the counts per function (`func_counts`) in `hls.vitis_fold` (additive keys), and `--bram-analysis` sums the reachable functions' counts and keeps their records. An `hls.vitis_fold` without `func_counts` (older IR) stays whole-module and the text says so. JSON (additive): `vitis_fold.scope` = `"top"` / `"module"`, `vitis_fold.rewrites[].func`.
  - **Nits:** the stdout note names the aspect mode the formula used (`usedPortWidth`, from the bank math): a 1024x32 RAM_1P is `(x18/port)`, not `(x36/port)`; RAM_1WNR notes likewise (deep copies are not x18). For a ROM the per-loop debug line now gives the ROM_NP copies (`ceil(need/2)` above two reads, else 1) instead of the RAM_1WNR `need - 1`, matching the summary. `run_all.sh` comments no longer speak of "the two passes".
  - **D3** `make_sheet.py`: the second run is `--second` (`--nosplit` stays as an alias) on a sheet named "second pipeline"; its label comes from the run's recorded pipeline ("split pipeline" when it has `--infer-vitis-split-array`, else "second pipeline") unless `--second-label` names it, in the sheet header, the run config rows and the summary line. Before, the split run (the comparison the old help suggested since M1) was labelled "no-split".
  - **D2** the item-1 entry below claimed the three scalar conv_block designs time out "also when re-run alone" and left them out of the before row; corrected in place (before 36 / 89, \|err\| 27361, not 36 / 86, 12225; per-array changes of the three designs added). *(Both from stale 2026-10-09 MLIR; on fresh MLIR the before row is 37 / 89, \|err\| 27297: W1c review fixes, see the item-1 entry.)*
  - CLAUDE.md (coordinator's file) still names "the no-split pipeline's predictions" sheet and the old default pipeline string (review (a), D3); not edited here.
- **Why:** review D1-D5: `top=` alone has to cost exactly what the prefix leaves (MEMOS M7), and the debug output has to describe the same scope as the estimate.
- **Effect** (harness subset, `--mlir-from` the W1a baseline runs `~/hls-est-work/w1a/runs/{s,x}_base.json`, frozen `~/hls-est-work/w1arf/bin/hls-est.fix` = this commit's code on 4a76b5f5c; runs `~/hls-est-work/w1arf/runs/{s,x}_fix.json` vs W1a item 4 `{s,x}_i4.json`): **no change in any number.**

  | shim | before (W1a item 4) | after |
  |---|---|---|
  | scalar | 46 / 89, \|err\| 31332 | 46 / 89, \|err\| 31332 |
  | exactclass (information; the harness default once decision #8 lands) | 48 / 89, \|err\| 8609 | 48 / 89, \|err\| 8609 |

  *Scalar row corrected in place by the W1c review fixes:* it first read 45 / 89, \|err\| 31570 on both sides, measured on stale 2026-10-09 MLIR (CHANGELOG W1c item 1). On fresh c70ba2b0 MLIR with the same frozen binaries (`~/hls-est-work/rvf-w1c/runs/s_a4` / `s_a5`) the totals are again identical on every design. The memref counts below are from the stale runs.
  Statuses, totals and every memref's (location, kind, BRAM) identical (1226 scalar / 1297 exactclass memrefs); the note text changes on 49 designs per shim (aspect mode used). Gates polybench_pragmas bicg 8, gesummv 16, mvt 8, syrk 32 exact on both shims; no exact design lost. The harness passes both the prefix and `top=`, so D1 / D5 cannot show there; checked directly instead (`~/hls-est-work/w1arf/runs/{s,x}_topscope.{json,txt}`): default pipeline + `--bram-attribute-globals` on the 91 ok designs, `top=<top>` alone vs the prefix: equal total and per-memref kind / BRAM on **91 / 91** per shim (debug pipeline records: the same count on 91 / 91; the default pipeline has no `--vitis-fold`, so the fold scoping is covered by lit only); the pre-fix binary (`~/hls-est-work/rv-w1a/bin/hls-est.head`) with `top=` alone differs from the prefix on 3 / 91 per shim (forgebench activation_op1, activation_op2, conv_block_op1: one global each RAM_T2P instead of RAM_1P from the accesses of uncalled functions, same BRAM). Review kernels: `k/top_glob.mlir` `top=topA` 8 -> 2 (= prefix), `top=topB` 8 (= prefix); `k/top_rec.mlir` debug pipelines with `top=topB` 11 -> 4 (= prefix), totals unchanged (16 / 6). Lit `bram_top_scope.mlir` (D1, D5, D4 pipelines: fails on the pre-fix binary with 8 and @topB's pipeline) and `bram_top_fold.mlir` (D4 fold: the pre-fix binary reports both functions' rewrites). Lit `test/hls-est`: 70 pass (2 new), 1 expected failure, 24 unresolved, 4 failing pre-existing (activation_op1, tiled_attn_module / p1 / p2). `check_debug_invariance.sh`: 38 pass / 0 fail / 4 err.
- **Reverted?:** no

## 2026-10-10 — cgeist: O23 review fixes (frontend)
- **Commit:** Polygeist c70ba2b0 (branch `hls-est-vitis-match`, repo root `/mnt/d/HLS`, on 18a550dd); this entry: the following changelog-only commit in `~/circt`. **Not installed:** `build/bin/cgeist` is still cbc105e0 (md5 23b7ca89…). The binary to install is now `/home/peter/tmp/o23/cgeist` (= `cgeist.v4`, md5 6ff60ea1c05f…, built from c70ba2b0); it replaces the 18a550dd binary (`cgeist.v3`) named in the O23 entry below.
- **Files:** Polygeist `lib/polygeist/Passes/AffineScalRep.cpp` (new `holdSameValue`; `accessesDisjointWithin`), `tools/cgeist/Test/HLS/regressions/store-forward-loop-carried.c` (`acc_break` comment; new `fwd_while_sym`, `while_sym_same`, `while_sym_alias`); OPEN_ISSUES.md O23 (status), O27 (new).
- **What:** the independent review of 18a550dd found no wrong forwarding and made three notes. (1) *Precision lost against cbc105e0:* the disjointness exception of `mayReachThroughNonAffineOp` (index operands fixed in the op, access functions that differ by a non-zero constant) only cancelled an index operand when both accesses used the same SSA value. cgeist emits a separate `arith.index_cast` of `s` for every access, and nothing merges them before the first scalrep runs. So `o[s] = v; while (...) { t += o[s]; o[s + 1] = ...; }` (verifier probe `v12_sym_disjoint`) kept a load of `o[s]` that cbc105e0 forwarded, correctly. `accessesDisjointWithin` now first gives every index operand of one access the operand of the other access that holds the same value, then takes the difference. `holdSameValue` treats two values as the same when they are the same SSA value, or the same result of two side-effect-free ops without regions that have equal name, attributes, properties and types and whose operands hold the same values, at most 4 ops deep. These are the ops CSE would merge. An op without operands counts only if it is a constant, because two `llvm.mlir.undef` may differ. Indices that are equal only through different expressions still count as may-alias, which is conservative. (2) The `acc_break` test comment said the loop is never raised. It is an `scf.while` when the forwarding first runs and an `affine.for` with iter_args in the output. (3) The reviewer's other finding, that a backward `goto` is dropped, is older and unrelated to O23. It is filed as O27.
- **Why:** the review of 18a550dd (verifier notes 1-3; probes in the session scratchpad `o23v/probes/`).
- **Effect** (the new binary `cgeist.v4` compared with `cgeist.v3` = 18a550dd):
  - *Probes, differential*, run against the natively compiled source. The implementer's sets (`o23/probes` 38 kernels plus `alias_drv` 6 calls; `v4/orig`, `v4/probes`, `v4/probes_xc`, `v4/probes2`, `v4/probes3`, `r6/probes` 246 kernels) and the new `o23f/probes/t1_sym.cpp` (3 kernels): v4 and v3 are both right on all of them. The cgeist IR is identical in every function except `fwd_while_sym`, which now forwards. On the verifier's sets (`o23v/probes`, `o23v/probes2`), lowered MLIR with and without raising and `-emit-llvm` with and without raising, v4 is right wherever v3 was. Only `v06_goto` is wrong, as with every binary (O27). The IR differs from v3 only in `v12_sym_disjoint`, which is now identical to cbc105e0's output, with and without raising. `v11_sym_alias` and `v20_2d_sym`, which cbc105e0 got wrong, keep their loads.
  - *Lit*, private lit tree (`/home/peter/tmp/o23/lt_v4`; every `build/bin` tool shared, `cgeist` pointing at v4, `HLS_EST` unset):
    - Everything except hlsfactory and polybench: 221 passed, 8 expectedly failed, 2 failed. The 2 failures are the pre-existing `HLS/array_partition/lowering.c` and `HLS/pipeline/lowering.c`.
    - `--filter polybench`: 242 passed, 2 expectedly failed. The source tree is clean afterwards.
    - `--filter hlsfactory`: 720 passed, 61 expectedly failed, 1 failed. The failure is `accelerators__GP_selection_kernel_float_dataset`, the same as v3 and cbc105e0 in the review.
    - The updated `store-forward-loop-carried.c` fails with v3, which does not forward `fwd_while_sym`.
  - *Harness*, scalar shim, 6 datasets, `-j 8 --force-cgeist`, frozen hls-est (`/home/peter/tmp/o23/hls-est`), private HOMEs, the split pipeline of the earlier O23 runs: the cgeist output is identical to v3 on all 112 designs.
    - Under load, conv_block_module and conv_block_op1 hit the 120 s hls-est limit, with identical IR.
    - Rerun alone with the same pipeline given explicitly (`h_cbm5.json`), they give 11832 and 7649, the same as cbc105e0 and v3.
    - Note: the shared `DEFAULT_PIPELINE` changed during this work (919792784, decision #1). An earlier rerun on the new default (`h_cbm4.json`, 13736 and 7265) is not comparable.
    - Against cbc105e0, the only cgeist output changes are still mult_op_module_dot, mult_op_p3 and testing_impl, as in the O23 entry.
  - *Harness `--shim exactclass` on forgebench*: all 45 designs are identical to v3 (cgeist output, status, BRAM).
  - *Build:* objects via ninja (`AffineScalRep.cpp.o`, `libMLIRPolygeistTransforms.a`), linked to `/home/peter/tmp/o23/cgeist.v4` against the ext4 archive copy, then copied to `/home/peter/tmp/o23/cgeist`. `build/bin/cgeist` was not touched.
- **Reverted?:** no.

## 2026-10-10 — hls-est: `--bram-analysis=top=<symbol>` costs only what the top reaches; warning without it (MEMOS M7, decision #7; W1a item 4)
- **Commit:** 433f07113 (branch `hls-est-vitis-match`); this hash line: the W1a changelog-only commit
- **Files:** lib/Dialect/Resource/Passes/BRAMAnalysis.cpp (new `TopScope`, `computeTopScope`, `publicFuncsWithMemrefs`; pass option `top`; `runOnOperation` skips memrefs outside the scope, keeps only the top's m_axi adapters and the dead-memory records of reachable functions, warns without top=; `printDebugText` / `writeDebugJson` record the top), tools/hls-est-validate/run_hlsest.py (`with_top`, `bram_top_supported`, `run_hlsest`: `top=<top>` on the pipeline's `--bram-analysis` next to the unchanged `--symbol-privatize=exclude=<top> --symbol-dce` prefix; `cmd` / `pipeline_full` / the `.err` header shell-quoted with `shlex.join`; `_meta.top_passes`), test/hls-est/Passes/bram_top.mlir (new), tools/hls-est-validate/OPEN_ISSUES.md (O12 status)
- **What:** with `top=<symbol>` the pass costs the memrefs of the func.funcs reachable from that func.func through calls (any CallOpInterface op with a symbol callee; func.call in practice), the memref.globals those functions take with memref.get_global, and only the top's own m_axi adapters (`--bram-maxi-adapters`); everything else is neither printed nor counted. An unknown or body-less top is an error. Without top= the output is unchanged, plus a warning when more than one public func.func with a body holds a memref (an alloca or a get_global): `warning: --bram-analysis: N public functions hold memrefs (@a, @b, ...); all of them are costed. Give the top function with --bram-analysis=top=<symbol> (or --symbol-privatize=exclude=<top> --symbol-dce first) ...`. Debug text: a `top: @x (N function(s) reachable through calls; M memref(s) outside them not costed)` / `top: none` header line; JSON (additive): root `top` = null or {symbol, functions, skipped_memrefs}. run_hlsest.py passes `top=<top>` (merged into an existing `--bram-analysis=<options>` argument) only when the binary's `--help` lists the option, so a frozen binary older than this commit still runs; `--no-privatize` drops both.
- **Why:** O12 / M7: without the prefix hls-est costed every public function (uncalled alternative tops, standalone copies of callees cgeist inlined, O10): 15 of 79 totals changed, up to 2x. Vitis synthesizes only the top and what it calls.
- **Effect:** harness subset with `run_hlsest.py` passing top= (frozen `~/hls-est-work/w1a/bin/hls-est.top`; runs `{s,x}_i4.json` vs `{s,x}_i3.json`): **identical** on both shims, every design and every array (scalar 46 / 89, \|err\| 31332 (*corrected in place by the W1c review fixes: first reported as 45 / 89, \|err\| 31570, measured on stale 2026-10-09 MLIR; fresh c70ba2b0 MLIR, `~/hls-est-work/rvf-w1c/runs/s_a3` / `s_a4`, again identical totals*); exactclass 48 / 89, \|err\| 8609), as expected: the prefix leaves only the top public. Without the prefix (`~/hls-est-work/w1a/bin/hls-est.top` on the baseline MLIR): forgebench tiled_attn_module 606 with the prefix, 1339 + the warning without it, 606 and no warning with `top=` alone; attention_op_p1 406 / 420 + warning / 406; conv_A 193 in all three (single public function with memrefs, no warning; the O12 crash no longer reproduces). Lit `bram_top.mlir`: two public functions and a public helper (no top: 16 and the warning naming @a, @helper, @b; `top=a` 4 = a's alloca + the helper reached through a call + the global the helper reads; `top=b` 12; the privatize/dce prefix without top= gives the same 4 and no warning; JSON `top` record; errors for an unknown and for an external top). Lit `test/hls-est`: 68 pass (1 new), 1 expected failure, 24 unresolved, 4 failing pre-existing. `check_debug_invariance.sh`: 38 / 0 / 4.
- **Reverted?:** no

## 2026-10-10 — hls-est: the 7-series depth-band formula is the default BRAM formula (MEMOS M4, decision #6; W1a item 3)
- **Commit:** 51b9a6434 (branch `hls-est-vitis-match`); this hash line: the W1a changelog-only commit
- **Files:** lib/Dialect/Resource/Passes/BRAMAnalysis.cpp (new `BramFormula`, `formulaName`, `estimateBlocksBand`, dispatcher `estimateBlocks`; the old formula renamed `estimateBlocksN6`; `estimateBRAM` takes the formula; `fifoBlocksNarrow` now calls `estimateBlocksBand`; `isWideKind` comment = the SDP test; `BankMathRecord` `formula` / `band`; `printDebugText` / `writeDebugJson`; pass option `bram-formula=band|n6` on `BRAMAnalysisLoopSchedule`), test/hls-est/Passes/bram_formula_band.mlir (new), test/hls-est/Passes/bram_formula_rom_ir.mlir (new), test/hls-est/Passes/aspect_modes.mlir (RUN line with `bram-formula=n6`), test/hls-est/Passes/bram_debug_json.mlir (the two new bank_math keys), test/hls-est/Passes/replication_1wnr.mlir and replication_two_stores.mlir (4096-word layout text), tools/hls-est-validate/VITIS_NOTES.md (N8: implemented), tools/hls-est-validate/OPEN_ISSUES.md (new O26)
- **What:** per bank, by the address depth P = 2^ceil(log2 words) and the width W: P <= 512 -> ceil(W/36) if the bank is SDP else ceil(W/18); 1024 -> ceil(W/18); 2048 -> ceil(W/9); 4096 -> min(ceil(W/4), 2*ceil(W/9)); 8192 -> ceil(W/2); >= 16384 -> (P/16384) * W. Written from our measurements (N8 (a)-(d): the 23003 memory rows of results/vitis.json and the fr_* kernels), not ported from CoreQuerier.cpp. SDP = one used port, write-only + read-only, or two read-only ports: `isWideKind` on the port set each kind stands for (RAM_1P, RAM_S2P, ROM_1P, ROM_2P are SDP; RAM_2P = read-write + read, which is what Vitis binds for two accesses per cycle of an automatic RAM, RAM_T2P, RAM_1WNR and ROM_NP are not), so x36 / x18 decisions are unchanged. Read-only banks with n > 2 reads per cycle stay ROM_NP = ceil(n/2) x18 ROMs (not n - 1 replicas; the T4 ROM path, now confirmed by fr_rom3r / fr_rom4r). RAM_1WNR replication (N4) unchanged. `--bram-analysis=bram-formula=n6` keeps the N6 minimum over the aspect modes. Debug text: a header line `bram formula: ...` and, per bank, `; formula <f>: <band>` after the bank math (and "simple dual port -> x36 allowed" for ROM_2P / RAM_S2P instead of "one live port"); JSON (additive): root `bram_formula`, bank_math `formula` and `band`. Under band, a 4096-word bank's layout is reported as 4Kx4 columns (8 for W = 32, the same count as N6's 2 x18 columns of 4 blocks).
- **Why:** N8: the band formula matches all 23003 Vitis memory rows (N6 23000) and every one of the 18 fr_* kernels where the two differ: deeper than 4096 words Vitis does not stack 2Kx9 / 1Kx18 / 512x36 blocks.
- **Effect** (`--mlir-from` the baseline runs, default pipeline; frozen `~/hls-est-work/w1a/bin/hls-est.band`; runs `{s,x}_i3.json` vs `{s,x}_i2.json`, and `s_i3n6.json` = band binary with `bram-formula=n6`): **no change**. Scalar 46 / 89, \|err\| 31332 (*corrected in place by the W1c review fixes: first reported as 45 / 89, \|err\| 31570, measured on stale 2026-10-09 MLIR; fresh c70ba2b0 MLIR, `~/hls-est-work/rvf-w1c/runs/s_a2` / `s_a3`, again no change*); exactclass 48 / 89, \|err\| 8609, before and after; every design and every array (915 / 986 array groups) has the same BRAM; `bram-formula=n6` reproduces item 2's run exactly. No harness bank is deeper than 4096 words with a width where the formulas differ (the deep forgebench buffers are 16 / 32 bits, where both give the same count), as M4 predicted. The kernel values are pinned in lit: 8192x9 = 5 (n6 4), 8192x18 = 9 (8), 6000x27 = 14 (12), 16384x18 = 18 (16), 512x72 written in one loop and read in another (write-only + read-only port) = 2, the same with an in-place update (read-write + read port) = 4, 4096x9 = 2; ROM 512x32 read 1 / 2 / 3 / 4 times per cycle = 1 / 1 / 4 / 4 (on hand-lowered IR: from affine input the scheduler gives a global two port slots, so 3 or 4 reads get II 2 and 1 BRAM; O26); every `aspect_modes.mlir` value unchanged under both formulas. bind_storage is not carried into hls-est (`getPragmaStorageKind` is a stub), so "an explicit ram_2p with one read-only + one write-only port" is expressed by the ports used. Lit `test/hls-est`: 67 pass (2 new), 1 expected failure, 24 unresolved, 4 failing pre-existing. `check_debug_invariance.sh`: 38 / 0 / 4.
- **Reverted?:** no

The bracketed exactclass values were current.)* conv_module 1769 -> 2937 (1042) [1141 -> 1661], conv_block_op2 8901 -> 11717 (4796) [5220 -> 6244], conv_block_module [6687 -> 7255]: localW 128-576 banks -> 2-9 banks with RAM_1WNR copies (12 at f32, 15 at 16 bits), localOut fewer banks. diff_orders_module [144 -> 1104] (1538; scalar fails in both): BRAM_A1/B1/C1 (8192x16) and A2/B2/C2 (16384x16) 16 banks -> RAM_1WNR 15 copies (120 / 240 each), A4/B4/C4 16 banks -> one RAM_T2P 8; closer in error terms but not exact. ## 2026-10-10 — hls-est: T8 review fixes (`--vitis-fold` undef rules for shifts right / remui by U, two U operands, selects decided by a compare of U; a use after free in `--dead-memory-elimination`)
- **Commit:** a752b67e5 (branch `hls-est-vitis-match`); this hash line: the following changelog-only commit
- **Files:** lib/Dialect/Resource/Passes/VitisFold.cpp (`producesUndef`, `UndefFolder::run`, new `isSelectToMinMax`, `VitisFoldPass::runOnOperation`, file comment), lib/Dialect/Resource/Passes/DeadMemoryElimination.cpp (`eliminateOnce`), test/hls-est/Passes/vitis_fold_undef.mlir (`@use_zero` changed; new `@review_undef2`, `@select_by_compare`), tools/hls-est-validate/VITIS_NOTES.md (N13)
- **What:** the T8 review (D1-D5) of 90a14d498 / ec2c787a8:
  - *D1:* `x >> U` (shrsi, shrui) and `remui x, U` keep U (they fell through to U := 0). `remui x, <constant 0>` stays 0.
  - *D3:* `U * U2`, `U & U2`, `U | U2` keep U; `U ^ U2` is 0 (was 0, 0, -1 and U: reversed). `U ^ x` with one U operand still keeps U.
  - *D2:* a select whose condition is a constant takes the arm it picks, U or not (rule `select-constant-condition`; also in `producesUndef`). The selects of a round are rewritten after every other use, so a compare of U (directly or through a pair of extensions) that decides the condition is folded first: `(u >= x) ? u : x` and `(u < x) ? x : u` are U and their stores are deleted, `(u > x) ? u : x` stores x. The rules run once before the pass's first greedy rewrite, and with `undef` that rewrite leaves out arith's 16 `select (cmpi a, b), a, b -> min / max` patterns (root `arith.select`, generated op a min / max), which would merge the sge and sgt forms into one `maxsi`. Known difference, documented in N13 and pinned in `@use_zero`: a `--canonicalize` before `--vitis-fold` (prefix B) has already formed the max, which stays an unmeasured use (U := 0).
  - *D4:* `eliminateOnce` read `o.def` of every memory object after the dead allocas were erased (`isa<memref::GlobalOp>(o.def)` on freed memory; from 77e9ca1fa4, T4). The dead globals are now collected before anything is erased.
  - *D5:* the comment on the user order said "in IR order"; it is U-discovery and use-list order, now with the selects last.
- **Why:** Vitis run `~/hls-est-work/experiments/t8review_undef2/e731b57e6aee` (kernel `~/hls-est-work/exp-src/t8review/rv_undef2.cpp`, made by the reviewer): `db/a.o.1.ll` keeps only `out[7] = 0` (U ^ U2) and `out[9] = in[8]` (x >> (U & 31)). Every other slot is deleted (U): x >> U, x >>> U, x % U unsigned, U * U2, U & U2, U | U2, and the two ternaries decided by a compare of U. The frontend IR has the ternaries as a branch and a phi, so Vitis folds the compare (isTrueWhenEqual) before any select or max exists. D4: valgrind "Invalid read of size 8" in `eliminateOnce` on a single write-only alloca (`/home/peter/tmp/rv_t8/dme_min.mlir`, binary `~/hls-est-work/t8/hls-est.t8b`).
- **Effect:**
  - *Kernel (SSA mirror of rv_undef2, `/home/peter/tmp/rv_t8/undef2_ssa.mlir`; lit `@review_undef2`):* `--vitis-fold` now leaves exactly Vitis' two stores (before: 9 stores, among them x for out[0] / out[1], 0 for uout[0], max(x, 0) for out[3] / out[4], 0 for out[5] / out[6], -1 for out[8]). With `canonicalize=false` the result is the same. With `--canonicalize` before the pass, out[3] / out[4] still store `maxsi(x, 0)` (the known difference).
  - *Edge case `@b` of the review (`/home/peter/tmp/rv_t8/edge.mlir`):* `select (cmpi sge (ext h), (ext U)), U, h` is U, and its store is deleted (before: h). The cgeist output of rv_undef2 cannot be read by hls-est: its ternaries are `scf.if` over `polygeist.subindex` views (O25).
  - *Harness:* frozen binary `~/hls-est-work/t8fix/hls-est.t8fix`, runs in `~/hls-est-work/t8fix/runs/`. All runs used `--mlir-from` with the T8 runs' MLIR, compared with T8's runs (`~/hls-est-work/t8/runs/xc2_{A,B}_{nos,def}.json`, `s_{def,nos}.json`). **Every design is identical in status, total and per-array memref rows.** The one exception is lab2_hanchen (no Vitis ground truth), which timed out in both: 120 s here, 900 s on T8's retry.

    | run | designs | no split | default |
    |---|---|---|---|
    | exactclass + prefix B + adapters (8 datasets) | 213 | 49 / 8442 | 41 / 7543 |
    | exactclass + prefix A + adapters (8 datasets) | 213 | 49 / 9473 | 42 / 7771 |
    | scalar, no prefix (subset) | 112 | 43 / 31503 | 37 / 27297 |

    Each cell is exact / \|err\| on the 89 compared designs. Gates bicg 8, gesummv 16, mvt 8, syrk 32 are exact in all six runs. No harness design contains a U, so none of the rule changes can reach one.
  - *Kernels with prefix B + adapters:* forgebench attention_op_p1 gives 73 with no split and 78 with the default pipeline, with prefix A or B. dg_d_fold_divrem0 is 6, dg_d_fold_divrem0b 2, k 1, and k_ctrl 3 (no split) / 1 (default). All are equal to the T8 binary's numbers.
  - *Memory checks:* valgrind is clean on dme_min, edge.mlir (both canonicalize settings), undef2_ssa, `vitis_fold{,_undef}.mlir` (both settings), `dead_memory_elimination.mlir` and `dme_cfg.mlir`. The T8 binary still shows the D4 invalid read on dme_min.
  - *`check_debug_invariance.sh`:* 38 pass / 0 fail / 4 err under both the default pipeline and prefix B, per case identical to the review's runs.
  - *Lit `test/hls-est`:* 94 tests: 65 passed, 1 expectedly failed, 24 unresolved and 4 failed (activation_op1, tiled_attn_module, tiled_attn_p1, tiled_attn_p2; pre-existing). Unchanged. The new CHECK and NOCANON lines fail on the T8 binary.
- **Not adopted / for Peter:** nothing joins the default pipeline: `--vitis-fold` stays opt-in and `DEFAULT_PIPELINE` and the lit RUN lines are unchanged. Running `--vitis-fold` before `--canonicalize` in the M5 prefix would avoid the min / max known difference. That is not proposed, because no harness design has a U and so it is not measured to matter.
- **Reverted?:** no

## 2026-10-10 — hls-est: T8 self-review fixes (`--vitis-fold` store forwarding through a view of unknown storage; notes only on the module)
- **Commit:** ec2c787a8 (branch `hls-est-vitis-match`); this hash line: the following changelog-only commit
- **Files:** lib/Dialect/Resource/Passes/VitisFold.cpp (`provablyDistinct`, `recordNotes`), test/hls-est/Passes/vitis_fold_undef.mlir (`@local_escape`)
- **What:** (1) the forwarding of a store of U into a local (90a14d498) treated a write through any memref whose base is not an allocation as unable to write the local; a view whose base is not known storage (an if / select result that may be the local, a region block argument, `polygeist.subindex`) can, so the following load was forwarded as U and an output store was deleted (`@local_escape`: `c ? loc : other` written, then `loc` read). Two memrefs are now distinct only when both bases are known roots (alloca / alloc, global, function argument) and one is an allocation, or both are different globals. (2) `hls.vitis_fold` is written only when the pass runs on the module (as on the hls-est command line); nested under a function it would have modified the parent from a pass that may run in parallel with its siblings.
- **Why:** self-review of 90a14d498 before the review cycle; (1) is wrong code in a corner of the opt-in pass (cgeist emits such views for C ternaries over lvalues, O25).
- **Effect:** the fixed binary (`~/hls-est-work/t8/hls-est.t8b`) on the T8 runs' MLIR, exactclass and exact, prefixes A and B, both pipelines (8 runs, `~/hls-est-work/t8/runs/{xc4,x4}_*.json`): every design identical in status and total to 90a14d498's runs (lab2_hanchen hits the hls-est time limit in both); dg_d_fold_divrem0 6, dg_d_fold_divrem0b 2, attention_op_p1 73 unchanged. Lit `test/hls-est`: 94 tests, 65 passed, 1 expectedly failed, 24 unresolved, 4 failed (pre-existing).
- **Reverted?:** no

## 2026-10-10 — hls-est: Vitis' undefined value of an integer x / 0 (`--vitis-fold` rule `undef`), `--inline` on cgeist output, route A end to end (T8)
- **Commit:** 90a14d498 (branch `hls-est-vitis-match`); this hash line: the following changelog-only commit
- **Files:** lib/Dialect/Resource/Passes/VitisFold.cpp (`UndefFolder`: `isUndefSource`, `producesUndef`, `forwardStore` / `mayWrite`, the per-use rewrites, `recordNotes`; `FoldIntDivByZero` restricted to what the undef rules do not model; options `undef` and `canonicalize`), lib/Dialect/Resource/Passes/BRAMAnalysis.cpp (`FoldSummary`, `collectFoldSummary`, `printFoldSummary`; `vitis_fold` in the debug JSON), tools/hls-est/{main.cpp,CMakeLists.txt} (`mlir::LLVM::registerInlinerInterface`, MLIRLLVMIRTransforms), include/circt/Dialect/Resource/Passes/VitisCleanup.h (comment), test/hls-est/Passes/vitis_fold_undef.mlir (new, 12 cases), vitis_fold.mlir (T4 behaviour under `undef=false`, new UNDEF prefix), dme_attention_int.mlir (debug text and JSON of the fold); VITIS_NOTES.md N13 (new) and N1a, OPEN_ISSUES.md O16 (C int part fixed), O24, O25 (new), MEMOS.md M2 / M3 / M5 addenda; kernel `~/hls-est-work/exp-src/t8/undef_cint.cpp` (Vitis run `~/hls-est-work/experiments/t8_undef_cint/1edaadb26c7c/`)
- **What:**
  - *`--vitis-fold` models Vitis' undefined value U* (option `undef`, default on; the pass is opt-in, not in `DEFAULT_PIPELINE`; `undef=false` is the T4 rule x / 0 -> 0). Integer types only. Sources: `divsi / divui / remsi %x, <constant 0>`. U is kept by trunci, addi, subi, xori, `x / U`, `x % U`, `x << U` and `U * <odd constant>`; extsi / extui / sitofp / uitofp of U, muli, andi, `U << c`, `U >> c`, `U / y`, `U % y` fold to 0, ori to -1, cmpi to `isTrueWhenEqual(pred)` (also through a pair of the same extension from one type), select with a U arm to the other arm, and a store of U into a function argument is deleted. Every other use sees U := 0 (the T4 refinement). `remui x, 0` stays 0 (measured). A store of U into a local / global is not deleted (no mem2reg at that point): later loads of that element in its block (also inside loops / ifs there, up to the first possible write) read U, and the stored value becomes 0. The rules and the greedy canonicalization alternate until neither changes the IR. Rewrites are recorded in the module attribute `hls.vitis_fold` (counts per rule and the first 256 records with locations) and printed by `--bram-debug` (`--- vitis-fold: N rewrites ...`) and `--bram-debug-json` (`vitis_fold`, additive; null without the pass).
  - *`--inline` works on cgeist output:* the llvm dialect (loaded by the `llvm.linkage` attribute of every cgeist function) only promises its inliner interface, and the inliner collects the interface of every loaded dialect, so `--inline` aborted on any cgeist module ("promised by dialect 'llvm' but never implemented"); T4's func extension covered only inputs without llvm attributes. The other registered dialects implement theirs in the dialect (checked: only func and llvm promise one).
- **Why:** VITIS_NOTES.md N13 (new kernel t8_undef_cint: one output slot per C int use of U, 37 slots, read from `db/a.o.1.ll`; the ap_fixed probes probe / probe2 of the route-A study; dg_d_fold_divrem0{,b} of the T4 review). The model needs no knowledge of the source type: Vitis' fixed-point operators sign-extend their operands (an extension of U is 0, so `in + scale` stays `in`), while a C int expression uses U directly (`a + b / 0` is U and its store goes with both arrays, O16). CLAUDE.md priority 1 (route A, track T8 of MEMOS M3).
- **Effect:**
  - *Kernels* (cgeist + `shim/exactclass`, `--inline --canonicalize --sccp --vitis-fold --promote-globals-to-allocas --dead-memory-elimination` + either pipeline + `--bram-maxi-adapters`): dg_d_fold_divrem0 12 -> **6 = Vitis** (a_sdiv, b_sdiv, a_srem, b_srem, a_udiv, b_udiv eliminated; a_urem, a_ctrl, b_ctrl 2 each), dg_d_fold_divrem0b 8 -> **2 = Vitis** (a_cdiv, a_crem, a_cudiv eliminated). t8_undef_cint: 36 of 37 slots as Vitis in SSA form (lit); the C `short` sum out16[1] differs (Vitis narrows the add before folding U and deletes the store; hls-est stores x), and Vitis stores out[20] only when its condition is false (hls-est unconditionally; same arrays live). probe / probe2 on cgeist output: every slot involving U as Vitis (19 slots).
  - *Route A end to end* (`run_hlsest.py --shim exactclass`, prefix `--inline --canonicalize --sccp --vitis-fold --promote-globals-to-allocas --dead-memory-elimination`, `--bram-maxi-adapters`): forgebench attention_op_p1 no split **73 = Vitis per array** (BRAM_attn_input 2, BRAM_1 2, BRAM_weights_v 64, V 2, scores 1, mem1_m_axi 2, mem2_m_axi 0; Q, K, BRAM_weights_q, BRAM_weights_k eliminated_dead); default pipeline 78 (V 8: the split pass banks V, O2; scores 0). Control k_ctrl (head_dim 4, `~/hls-est-work/feas/A/k_ctrl.cpp`; Vitis Q 3 + K 3 = 6): Q and K kept, total 3 no split (Q 1, K 1, scores 1: the three stores of `scores[i][j]` per body keep the QK loop at II 3; with `--convert-affine-to-loopschedule=forward-stores` (O7) II 1 and Q 3 / K 3 RAM_1WNR as Vitis, scores 1 vs Vitis 0 (15-bit narrowing, O18): 7); 1 with the default pipeline (Q / K split 4 ways). k (head_dim 16; Vitis 0): Q and K eliminated, scores 1 (Vitis narrows it to `[64 x i1]`, O18).
  - *Harness* (frozen binary `~/hls-est-work/t8/hls-est.t8a`, runs `~/hls-est-work/t8/runs/`, MLIR from one cgeist pass per shim (`xc_A_nos` / `x_A_nos`, cgeist cbc105e0), every configuration with `--mlir-from` on byte-identical inputs; conv_block_module re-run with 900 s where it hit 120 s under load, `_meta.retry_900s`). Datasets: the subset (89 compared) plus class_ece8893_spring_2025 and accelerators (no Vitis ground truth: ok counts only). Exact / |err| on the subset:

    | shim | pipeline | no prefix | + `--inline --canonicalize --sccp --vitis-fold --dead-memory-elimination` + adapters (B) | + `--promote-globals-to-allocas` too (A, the T8 task prefix) |
    |---|---|---|---|---|
    | exactclass | no split | 45 / 8616 | **49 / 8442** | 49 / 9473 |
    | exactclass | default | 39 / 7791 | 41 / 7543 | 42 / 7771 |
    | exact | no split | 45 / 8713 | 46 / 8717 | 46 / 9026 |
    | exact | default | 39 / 7184 | 40 / 7176 | 42 / 7524 |

    `undef` on vs `undef=false` (the T4 rule): **no design of the 8 datasets changes** in any of the six exactclass / exact runs with a prefix (status and total identical; no harness design has a C int division by a constant 0, and the attention scale is only used through extensions). Newly exact over no prefix (exactclass): no split attention_op_p1 203 -> 73, tiled_attn_p1 203 -> 73, tiled_attn_p2 55 -> 23 (Q / K dead), activation_module / op2 / op3 256 -> 258 and diff_dims_module_small 1152 -> 1154 (adapter); default activation_* / diff_dims_module_small and gpt_transformer_p1 26 -> 28; no longer exact in both: mult_op_p2, mult_op_p3, vec_mtx_p1 2 -> 4 (they matched by cancellation with the missing adapter, O17 items 2-3, M5). A over B (promotion): attention_op_p2 / p3 20 -> 202, vec_mtx_p1 4 -> 242, Llama_GPT_module 29 -> 85 no split (O17); default +attn_breakdown_op1 (cancellation, M5), +mult_op_p3, -gpt_transformer_p1. ok counts: class_ece8893_spring_2025 exactclass 32 / 94, exact 34 / 94 (failures: cgeist errors, `polygeist.subindex` (O25), struct memrefs, 2 cgeist timeouts, 1 hls-est timeout at 900 s); accelerators 0 / 7 under both.
  - *Default and no-split pipelines unchanged* (scalar shim, T7's final runs replayed with the T8 binary on their MLIR, `~/hls-est-work/t8/runs/s_{def,nos}.json`): 112 / 112 designs identical in status and total; default 37 / 89 exact, |err| 27297; no split 43 / 89, 31503. Gates polybench_pragmas bicg 8, gesummv 16, mvt 8, syrk 32 exact in every run (scalar, exact, exactclass; both pipelines; every prefix). `check_debug_invariance.sh`: 38 PASS / 0 FAIL / 4 ERR (the same parse failures) with the default pipeline and with the T8 prefix.

## 2026-10-10 — cgeist: no store-to-load forwarding across intervening writes (O23) (frontend)
- **Commit:** Polygeist 18a550dd (branch `hls-est-vitis-match`, repo root `/mnt/d/HLS`); this entry: the following changelog-only commit in `~/circt`. **Not installed:** `build/bin/cgeist` is still cbc105e0 (T8 was measuring with it); the binary to install is `/home/peter/tmp/o23/cgeist` (= `cgeist.v3`, md5 6edc69e38765…, built from 18a550dd).
- **Files:** Polygeist new `lib/polygeist/Passes/AffineScalRep.cpp` (pass `polygeist-affine-scalrep`, `createPolygeistAffineScalarReplacementPass`), `lib/polygeist/Passes/CMakeLists.txt`, `include/polygeist/Passes/Passes.h`, `tools/cgeist/driver.cc` (the five `--scal-rep` call sites); new tests `tools/cgeist/Test/HLS/regressions/store-forward-loop-carried.c`, `tools/cgeist/Test/Verification/store-forward-loop.c`; OPEN_ISSUES.md O23 (status).
- **What:** root cause of O23 is MLIR's affine scalar replacement (`affine-scalrep`, LLVM 18 `mlir/lib/Dialect/Affine/Utils/Utils.cpp`), not a Polygeist pass. Bisect (`--pm-enable-printing` on the O23 int kernel): the load of `out[0]` disappears in the first `AffineScalarReplacement` of the driver (`driver.cc` line 686), while the loop is still the `scf.while` from LoopRestructure; with `--scal-rep=0` the IR keeps the load. Its intervening-write check (`hasNoInterveningEffect` -> `mayHaveEffect`) uses the affine dependence analysis, which models only affine.for / affine.if and treats every other region op as straight-line code, so a write after the load in the body of an scf.while / scf.for is never seen to reach the load in the next iteration. The same check guards load CSE (same failure: an earlier load of the location reused in the loop) and dead-store elimination. The driver now runs a Polygeist copy of `affineScalarReplace` and its helpers (same Apache-2.0 WITH LLVM-exception licence as Polygeist) with one addition, `mayReachThroughNonAffineOp`: an affine access found between the forwarded store / load and the replaced load counts as an intervening write when it lies, together with the load, inside a non-affine op that does not enclose the store / load providing the value and whose region holding the load may run again (LoopLikeOpInterface, `RegionBranchOpInterface::isRepetitiveRegion`, or an op that does not describe its control flow), or that holds the two in different regions that may reach one another or in different blocks of one region. Exception: scalar accesses of one identity-layout memref whose index operands are defined outside the op and whose access functions differ by a non-zero constant (`o[1] = o[0] + a[i]` in a while loop still forwards `o[0]`). An op that encloses the store too re-executes it before the load, so it is not a path. Plain C semantics; no HLS-specific lowering changes, no new option (`--scal-rep=0` still disables the pass).
- **Why:** O23 (T7): forgebench mult_op_module_dot / mult_op_p3 / testing_impl lose their accumulations. Pre-existing, not from this session: the O23 kernel is wrong with `cgeist.orig-hls-preserve-name`, `cgeist.prev-8b07bf43`, `cgeist.prev-e5b6bbc9`, `cgeist.tmpc2c5b6a` (June) and `bin/cgeist` (cbc105e0), with and without `--raise-scf-to-affine`, and right in all of them with `--scal-rep=0` (session scratchpad `o23/old1.sh`).
- **Effect:**
  - *Probes, differential* (cgeist MLIR lowered to LLVM and run against the natively compiled source, every output buffer with guard zones, over runtime inputs; exact shim; session scratchpad `o23/probes/`, 38 kernels in 5 files, plus `o23/alias_drv.cpp`, which calls the two-pointer kernels with `q == p`, `p + 1`, `p - 1`): new 38/38 right and 6/6 alias calls right; cbc105e0 wrong on row_acc_var, row_while, alias_inloop, while_affine_inner, row_var_f, sym_offset, sym_two, and aborts on `p1_accum.cpp` (12 kernels; `IRMapping::lookup` assertion on acc_break, the early-exit loop, after the wrong forwarding), right on the rest (all 7 controls, all 6 alias calls). With `--scal-rep=0`, cbc105e0 is right on all of them. IR new vs cbc105e0: the controls (p4_ctrl: store then load, a loop / while that writes other elements, same-iteration forwarding, a load after a loop) are identical, i.e. forwarding still happens there.
