## 2026-10-10 — hls-est: identical modulo scheduling problems are solved once (`reuse-schedules`) (T7 d, part 2 of 3)
- **Commit:** 500079055 (branch `hls-est-vitis-match`); this hash line: the T7 changelog-hash commit at the end of the series
- **Files:** lib/Dialect/Resource/Passes/AffineToLoopScheduleModified.cpp (`scheduleSignature`, `CachedSchedule`, `scheduleCache`, `solveSchedulingProblem`; options `reuse-schedules` (default on) and `verify-schedule-reuse` (default off); statistics `schedules-solved`, `schedules-reused`, `schedule-reuse-mismatches`); new lit test test/hls-est/Passes/schedule_reuse.mlir
- **What:** before solving a loop's `ModuloProblem`, `--convert-affine-to-loopschedule` builds its signature: per operation in problem order its op name, linked operator type (numbered by first use) and latency, linked resource types (numbered by first use) and limits, and its dependences (source position, auxiliary flag, operand indices, distance), plus the anchor's position. If an earlier loop of the same function had an equal signature, the loop gets that loop's II and start times (checked with `ModuloProblem::verify`) and `hls.scheduler` says `<scheduler> (reused from <loc>)`. Both schedulers (`ModuloSimplexScheduler`, `FastModuloScheduler`) read nothing else, compare operator / resource types only for identity, and iterate operations and dependences in problem order (ties broken by that order), so a reused schedule is the schedule a new solve gives. `verify-schedule-reuse=true` solves the reused loops anyway, keeps the new schedule and warns on any difference.
- **Why:** task T7 (d): the user-unrolled copies of a pipelined loop (diff_orders_*, mult_op_p1, mlp, conv_B / conv_C, diff_dims_module_large) are scheduled once per copy, and with part 1 in place the remaining time of diff_orders_module (default pipeline) was the simplex solves of its 417 innermost loops. Keyed on the scheduling problem rather than the IR text: copies whose texts differ only in an outer-index constant can still share a problem.
- **Effect:** loops solved / reused (default pipeline; no split where different): conv_B and conv_C 5 / 8; diff_orders_p1 and mult_op_p1 3 / 137; diff_orders_p2 / p3 3 / 273; mlp 7 / 290; diff_orders_module 139 / 278 (no split 4 / 413); diff_dims_module_large 114 / 714 (4 / 824); conv_block_module 23 / 71 (21 / 73). Whole subset (the 91 designs that finish): 2778 of 3470 pipelined loops reused (default), 3054 of 3470 (no split). Exactness: on all 91 designs that finish, the IR after the full pipeline is identical with `reuse-schedules=false` and with `verify-schedule-reuse=true` (apart from the `hls.scheduler` note), with 0 mismatch warnings, in both pipelines (`t7_ir.py`, `~/hls-est-work/t7/ir/`). Time, part 1 -> parts 1 + 2 (default pipeline, same conditions as in part 1): conv_B 21 -> 16 s, conv_C 21 -> 16 s, diff_dims_module_large 60 -> 15 s, diff_orders_module 109 -> 40 s, diff_orders_p1 26 -> 11 s, diff_orders_p2 11 -> 6 s, diff_orders_p3 11 -> 6 s, mlp 52 -> 25 s, mult_op_p1 26 -> 10 s, conv_block_module 105 -> 64 s.
- **Reverted?:** no

## 2026-10-10 — hls-est: the memory dependence analysis examines only the pairs inside one innermost loop (T7 d, part 1 of 3)
- **Commit:** 843459412 (branch `hls-est-vitis-match`); this hash line: the T7 changelog-hash commit at the end of the series
- **Files:** include/circt/Analysis/DependenceAnalysis.h (`MemoryDependenceAnalysis::Scope`, `Counters`, `getCounters`, `InnermostLoopDependenceAnalysis`), lib/Analysis/DependenceAnalysis.cpp (scoped constructor, `hasNoNestedLoop`, counters), lib/Dialect/Resource/Passes/CyclicSchedulingAnalysisModified.cpp, lib/Dialect/Resource/Passes/AffineToLoopScheduleModified.cpp (`runOnOperation`; statistics `dependence-pairs`, `dependence-tests`, `dependences`; explicit copy constructor for the Statistic members); new lit test test/hls-est/Passes/dependence_scope.mlir; OPEN_ISSUES.md O1 (cost paragraph)
- **What:** `InnermostLoopDependenceAnalysis` groups the accesses by their innermost enclosing `affine.for`, keeps the loops without a nested loop and runs the memoized dependence test only on the pairs inside one such loop, at its depth D. That is exactly the depth-D part of the function-wide analysis (a pair needs D common loops, i.e. the same innermost loop) and the only part a modulo schedule of an innermost loop can use (the analysis assumes enclosing loops run sequentially; `analyzeForOp` uses the last dependence component). `--convert-affine-to-loopschedule` and `CyclicSchedulingAnalysis` use it instead of the function-wide `MemoryDependenceAnalysis` (still used by the upstream pass).
- **Why:** task T7 (d). Are the user-unrolled copies identical? At the affine level after `--memory-banking-bram` (`t7_copies.py`, text of each innermost loop with SSA names canonicalised; `~/hls-est-work/t7/copies/`) mostly yes: diff_orders_p1 / p2 / p3 / mult_op_p1 have 4-5 distinct texts among 140-276 innermost loops, mlp 11 of 297, diff_dims_module_large 13 of 828 without split; with split, diff_orders_module has 144 of 417 (copy k indexes a bank with `%outer + k`) and diff_dims_module_large 284 of 828. But the time was not in scheduling: `--mlir-timing` on conv_B (default pipeline, pre-T7 code) gave 637 of 641 s in `MemoryDependenceAnalysis` (1680 accesses of 8 memrefs in one outer loop, 1,451,720 same-memref pairs at each of 4 depths, 2.1M tests), and its results never reach a scheduler (O1: the lowering re-keys a copy, `CyclicSchedulingAnalysis` reads the cached instance keyed by the erased affine ops). So no schedule can change; the restriction keeps what a future O1 fix needs.
- **Effect:** conv_B 5.8M pairs / 2.1M tests -> 170,368 pairs / 73,600 tests (`--mlir-pass-statistics`). With this part alone each of the 9 designs (and conv_block_module, default pipeline) finishes (wall time, default / no split, 20 processes on 32 cores; `~/hls-est-work/t7/timetab/`): conv_B 21 / 21 s, conv_C 21 / 23 s, diff_dims_module_large 60 / 33 s, diff_orders_module 109 / 53 s, diff_orders_p1 26 / 28 s, diff_orders_p2 11 / 11 s, diff_orders_p3 11 / 11 s, mlp 52 / 52 s, mult_op_p1 26 / 26 s, conv_block_module 105 / 31 s; the slowest, diff_orders_module (default), still spends most of its time in the simplex solves of its 417 innermost loops (parts 2 and 3). The pre-T7 binary on the 9 designs with a 90 min limit (both pipelines, 18 processes): 6 runs (conv_B and conv_C in both pipelines, one each of diff_dims_module_large and diff_orders_module) ended within 15 min; the other 12 were still running after 27 min at 4 GB RSS each and growing, and were stopped before the machine (54 GB) ran out of memory; the previous attempt's measurement of conv_B (no split, function-wide analysis) was 654 s, 545 BRAM. Estimates: no change on any design that finished before (see the T7 d3 entry for the harness runs; IR identical with part 2 / part 3 on and off).
- **Reverted?:** no

## 2026-10-10 — hls-est: pipeline stages run up to a late terminator (forgebench mult_op_p3 abort) (T7 c)
- **Commit:** 6538b104d (branch `hls-est-vitis-match`); this hash line: the T7 changelog-hash commit at the end of the series
- **Files:** lib/Dialect/Resource/Passes/AffineToLoopScheduleModified.cpp (`createLoopSchedulePipeline`); new lit test test/hls-est/Passes/pipeline_yield_late.mlir; OPEN_ISSUES.md O23 (new), O12 (item 3)
- **What:** `createLoopSchedulePipeline` created a stage for every start time up to the last op's, but registers the values the loop terminator yields up to the terminator's start time. When the terminator starts more than one cycle after the last op (a multi-cycle op whose result nothing uses still holds it back), the forwarding stages in between were missing, the register chain of a yielded value ended early and the terminator lookup aborted (`IRMapping::lookup ... expected 'from' to be contained within the map`). The stages now run up to the terminator's start time - 1.
- **Why:** forgebench mult_op_p3 aborted in both pipelines. Chain: cgeist lowers `output[0] = 0; for (i < 64) output[0] += A[i] * B[i];` (unroll 16) to a store of `A[i] * B[i] + 0.0` per iteration (OPEN_ISSUES O23: a pre-existing frontend wrong-code bug, not modelled here); `--auto-pipeline-unroll` promotes the 16 stores of `acc` to an iter_arg that yields only the last copy's sum; the other 15 fmul / fadd results are unused, but the last fmul (start 8, latency 4) holds the terminator to cycle 12 while the yielded value (the identity select of `materializeYieldedValues`) starts at 10, and nothing starts at 11.
- **Effect** (harness subset (`run_hlsest.py --datasets polybench,polybench_pragmas,forgebench,machsuite,spector_hls,vitis_examples -j 8 --timeout 120`, scalar shim, the frozen MLIR of the round-4 harness run via `--mlir-from /home/peter/tmp/r4/h_final.json`; before = `~/hls-est-work/t7/hls-est.base` (circt 8e65ec485 code = HEAD before T7), after = `~/hls-est-work/t7/hls-est.r3` (this series' final code before comment-only edits); runs `~/hls-est-work/t7/runs3/{base,new}_{def,nos}.json`)): forgebench/mult_op_p3 hls-est abort -> 2 = Vitis 2 in both pipelines (counted as newly exact: default 36 -> 37, no split 42 -> 43), but by cancellation, not per array: hls-est prices `BRAM_1` / `BRAM_2` (64 x f32 under the scalar shim) at 1 BRAM each, Vitis has them at 0 (64 x 16 bits) and its 2 are the `mem1` m_axi adapter, which the default pipeline does not model (`--bram-maxi-adapters`, N1). IR after the full pipeline, pre-T7 binary vs T7 a+b+c (`hls.scheduler` removed; `t7_ir.py`, `~/hls-est-work/t7/ir/abc_{def,nos}/`), both pipelines: identical on 80 of the 91 designs that finish after T7; different only spector_hls/histogram (T7 a); mult_op_p3 aborted before; the 9 designs that time out before were not compared here (see the T7 d entries). So no other design has a terminator later than its last op + 1. OPEN_ISSUES O12 item 3 (conv_A/B/C aborting with the same `IRMapping::lookup` assertion when run without the top-selection prefix): no longer reproduces (conv_A 193 with the pre-T7 and the T7 binary; conv_B 545, conv_C 641 with the T7 binary, the same totals as with the prefix).
- **Reverted?:** no

## 2026-10-10 — cgeist: round 4 review fixes (narrow constants by representative; floordiv / mod only for non-negative dividends, at every width) (frontend)
- **Commit:** Polygeist cbc105e0 (branch `hls-est-vitis-match`, repo root `/mnt/d/HLS`); this entry: the following changelog-only commit in `~/circt`
- **Files:** Polygeist `lib/polygeist/Passes/AffineCFG.cpp` (new `arithRange`, `constantOperand`, `constantRepresentative`; changed: `IntRangeOracle::compute` for add / sub / mul, `exactArith`, the `AffineApplyNormalizer` operand loop, header comment); new tests `tools/cgeist/Test/HLS/regressions/{narrow-const-representative,divrem-negative-dividend}.c`; this file (two corrections in the 763b0046 entry below)
- **What:** the review of 763b0046 (D1–D3), fixed as plain C semantics (no Vitis modelling, no change to the HLS lowering).
  1. D1 (new in 763b0046; a lost raising, not wrong code) MLIR folds `subi x, c` into `addi x, -c`, and `exactArith` / the normalizer read the constant with the signedness of the result: the i8 -5 of `uint8_t k = i; k -= 5` read unsigned is 251, k + 251 never fits [0, 255], so a provably in-range narrow decrement became a `memref.load` (`for (uint8_t i = 5; i < 100; i++) { k = i; k -= 5; A[k] }`, the countdown `for (uint8_t i = n; i > 0; i--) { k = i; k -= 1; O[k] = A[k] }`). An iN constant stands for every integer congruent to it modulo 2^N, and the iN add / sub / mul gives the same bits with any of them. The proof now takes the first of the constant as read, that minus 2^N and that plus 2^N for which the exact result lies in the range of the iN as read (`constantRepresentative`; any two that fit give the same value), and the normalizer emits that value as the affine constant (`A[iv - 5]`, `A[-iv + n]`, int8 `k += 128` over i in [-128, -101] -> `A[iv + 128]`). The range oracle uses the same representative.
  2. D2 (older, wrong code) `exactArith` returned before the div / rem sign check for every op of at least 32 bits and for index, so an int, long or index `divsi` / `remsi` / `divui` / `remui` became floordiv / mod for any dividend: `for (int i = 0; i < 20; i++) A[(i - 5) / 2 + 10]` read A[7] at i = 0 (C: A[8]); `int8_t i = -100..99; A[(i % 16) + 16]` and `unsigned w = s & 63; A[(w - i) % 100]` read wrong elements; `if ((i - 3) % 2 == 1)` also held for i = 0 and 2; `A[i / -2 + 60]` hit the affine flattener's "RHS constant has to be positive" assertion (cgeist abort). floordiv / mod equal C's truncating / and % only for a non-negative dividend and a positive divisor; that is rounding, not overflow, so the check now applies at every width, index included, independently of `-affine-cfg-assume-nowrap-width`. So that dividends such as `(i * 64 + j) / 4` under a runtime bound keep their proof, the range oracle reads a wide add / sub / mul read signed as the non-wrapping value of its raised expression (the assumption under which it is raised without a proof). Dividends the interval oracle cannot bound on i32 values (`(i - s) / 2` over `i = s .. s + 19`, `(n - i) / 2`) now keep C's `divsi` instead of the floordiv expansion; in both binaries such an index is a `memref.load` either way (Polygeist expands an index floordiv into arith), so no access changes kind.
  3. D3 unchanged, Peter's call (open question 1 of the 763b0046 report): an unsigned 32-bit add / sub / mul is still raised without a proof (default `-affine-cfg-assume-nowrap-width=32`). The verifier's D3 reproducer p6g (`A[(w - i) % 100]`) is a remainder, so D2 now gives the C result for it; what is left is a 32-bit unsigned add / sub / mul whose raised value leaves [0, 2^32) while C's wrapped value is in bounds.
- **Why:** verifier report on 763b0046: D1 `p1o_ok`, `p1n`; D2 `p6a`, `p6b`; D3 `p6g` (session scratchpad `v4/probes/`); new probes `r6/probes/{d1d2,d1d2b,d3_extra}.cpp`. An interrupted earlier attempt had left the D1 / D2 edits uncommitted in `AffineCFG.cpp`; they were reviewed and kept, with only formatting and comment changes before the build.
- **Effect:**
  - Probes, differential (cgeist MLIR lowered to LLVM and run against the natively compiled source, every output buffer with guard zones, over runtime inputs; the `v4/` reproducers and probes + `r6/probes/`), new `bin/cgeist` (cbc105e0) vs `cgeist.prev-763b0046`: new 246 kernels right, 0 wrong; prev wrong on p6a, p6b, p6g and q2_rem, q2_div, q2_u32, d2_neg1, d4_cond, d8_neg_rt, d9_u32div, and aborts on `d3_extra.cpp` (`i / -2`). f4_exit, n1_exact, n2_countdown (intentional undefined behaviour / infinite loops) behave the same in both. IR: 24 of 29 probe files identical; the differences are exactly the D1 kernels (p1o_ok, p1n, q1_ok, q1_rt, q1_u16, r1_u8_add255, r2_u8_twice, r3_u16_add, r4_bit7, r5_cond, r7_s8_add128*, e4_u7_sub, e5_u16_dec_rt, e6_u8_dec2_rt: `memref.load` -> affine) and the D2 kernels (C's div / rem kept). The 84 sources of earlier rounds (`verify/`, `probes/`, `verify2/`, `verify3/`, `fix4/`, `r4/probes/`): 74 IR-identical, 10 fail in both (pre-existing).
  - Lit, private lit trees pointing at each binary (the other `build/bin` tools shared) and a private HOME: `tools/cgeist/Test/HLS --filter-out hlsfactory` new 90 passed / 2 failed / 3 unsupported, prev 88 / 4 / 3. The 2 failures in both are the pre-existing array_partition/lowering.c and pipeline/lowering.c; prev also fails the 2 new tests. The 3 unsupported tests (consumer/hls-est-banking.c, consumer/hls-est-pipeline.c, polybench/hls-est-e2e.c: no hls-est under the private HOME) fail the same way with both binaries when `HLS_EST` points at hls-est (hls-est rejects cgeist's dlti data-layout entries); these are 3 of the 5 failures counted in the 763b0046 entry. `tools/cgeist/Test/Verification` 134 passed / 8 expectedly failed with both.
  - Harness `run_hlsest.py --datasets polybench,polybench_pragmas,machsuite,spector_hls,vitis_examples,forgebench -j 8 --force-cgeist` (scalar shim, `common.DEFAULT_PIPELINE`), one snapshot of the harness code (circt c6e42c3b3) in private copies with private HOMEs (`/home/peter/tmp/r6/h_*`), the frozen hls-est of the 763b0046 entry (`/home/peter/tmp/r4/hls-est`): new vs control `cgeist.prev-763b0046`: **cgeist output identical on all 112 designs**; new ok 80 / hls-est timeout 10 / cgeist error 12 / hls-est error 6 / cgeist crash 4; the control run hit the 120 s limit under load on 3 more (forgebench conv_block_op1, conv_block_op3, diff_dims_p3; identical IR). The new run equals the 763b0046 entry's final run (`/home/peter/tmp/r4/h_final.json`) in status, total and IR on all 112. `--shim exactclass` on forgebench: identical IR on all 45; ok 31 / timeout 10 / cgeist error 4 after re-running the 2 designs that timed out under load in the new run (conv_block_op1 3825, conv_block_op3 4813, as in the control). So the corpus has no index div / rem with a possibly negative dividend and no narrow decrement that 763b0046 had stopped raising; no BRAM total changes.
  - Build: objects via ninja; link against the ext4 copy of the llvm-project static libs (`/home/peter/tmp/ra2-llvmlib/lib`, mtimes / sizes checked) to `/home/peter/tmp/r6/cgeist` (~40 min under load); installed by copying to `bin/cgeist.r6tmp` + `cmp`, renaming the busy binary to `bin/cgeist.prev-763b0046` and the copy into place, `ninja -t restat bin/cgeist` (`ninja -n cgeist`: no work); also `/home/peter/tmp/cgeist-link/cgeist`. `bin/cgeist.orig-hls-preserve-name` and the other `cgeist.prev-*` untouched.
- **Reverted?:** no. `-affine-cfg-assume-nowrap-width` default unchanged (32, D3).

## 2026-10-10 — hls-est: T6 review fixes (`--bram-seq-dual-port` follows memory order through other arrays; RAM_2P for two reads; N12 loop clause unconfirmed)
- **Commit:** 5756bd6fc (branch `hls-est-vitis-match`); this hash line: the following changelog-only commit
- **Files:** lib/Dialect/Resource/Passes/BRAMAnalysis.cpp (new `storageKey`, `MemTouch`, `memTouches`, `mayConflict`, `inExclusiveArms`, `hoistedArmsOf`; `findSameStatePair` tracks the memory effects of every op of the stretch; `selectStorageKind`: two live ports are RAM_2P unless two writes share a cycle; header comment and `--bram-seq-dual-port` description), test/hls-est/Passes/seq_scope_ports.mlir (`@mem_order`, `@mem_order_straight`, `@sibling_loops`; RAM_2P checks), test/hls-est/Passes/seq_scope_ports_ir.mlir (`@through_other`, `@through_other_disjoint`, `@war_chain`, `@unrelated_store`, `@call_between`; RAM_2P checks), test/hls-est/Passes/access_cse.mlir (RAM_2P check), tools/hls-est-validate/VITIS_NOTES.md (N12: loop clause, memory order, RAM type, exceptions, hls-est line), OPEN_ISSUES.md (O21 widened to under-estimates, O3), MEMOS.md (M6 re-measured), this file
- **What:** the three defects of the review of 3ceac5ba4 / 9389b61bc.
  1. (D1) `findSameStatePair` followed dependences through SSA operands and ordered only accesses of the bank being priced, so `x = a[i]; b[j] = x; y = b[k]; a[y]` gave two independent loads of `a`. Every op of the stretch now contributes its memory effects (MemoryEffectOpInterface; an op without it, a call, or an effect on no memref value may write any storage; an if contributes through its arms). A later op whose effect may conflict with an earlier one (one of them writes; the same storage: views followed to their source, one key per alloca / alloc / global / function argument, anything else may be any storage; two plain loads / stores of one memref at provably different `base + c` addresses do not conflict; not in the two arms of one if) inherits the earlier op's dependences (read after write, write after read, write after write). Only ops that depend on an access of the bank are kept as candidates, so unrelated memory traffic costs nothing. Accesses of the bank among themselves keep the existing rule.
  2. (D3) `selectStorageKind` mapped every two-port demand except 1R+1W to RAM_T2P. Vitis binds ram_2p for two reads (or a read and a write) per state and ram_t2p only for two writes; RAM_T2P now needs two writes per cycle. Both kinds are x18 per port, so no BRAM count changes; the debug `ram type` / JSON `ram_type` / report `kind=` change on those arrays, also in pipelined scopes and with the option off.
  3. (D2) N12 said an inner loop between two accesses separates them; true only where the later access feeds something ordered after the loop (sp_split_loop, rv_sib3), false in rv_sib2. Documentation only: N12's loop clause marked unconfirmed, O21 widened to the under-estimate, `@sibling_loops` pins the model's 1 with the Vitis 2 in the comment. The stretch rule is unchanged (a model needs the parent's list schedule, O21).
- **Why:** review reproducers `~/hls-est-work/exp-src/t6rev/rv_*.cpp`, Vitis td=off runs `~/hls-est-work/experiments/t6rev_<k>/<hash>/` (`sched_ports.py`): rv_memdep (outer-loop body) and rv_memdep2 (function body) load `a` in two states, `a` ram_1p 1 (rv_memdep2 total 2); rv_sib2 `a peak 2R0W` in ST_3, ram_2p 2; rv_sib3 two states, 1; rv_ctrldep 2R in one state, ram_2p 2. RAM type: N12 kernels sp_far / sp_8ld / sp_2ld (two reads) ram_2p, sp_2st (two writes) ram_t2p; in the corpus (`results/vitis.json` `bind_nodes` optype) the arrays hls-est prices at two reads and at most one write bind ram_2p on 17 / 16 and ram_t2p on 9 / 2 (default / no split, option on; the ram_t2p ones are arrays whose writes per state hls-est does not see the same way, VITIS_NOTES.md N12 "RAM type").
- **Effect:**
  - *Kernels* (no-split pipeline, `~/hls-est-work/t6fix/ktab_nos.txt`; the default pipeline gives the same table): rv_memdep `a` 2 -> **1** (= Vitis), rv_memdep2 total 3 -> **2** (= Vitis, `a` 1 + `b` 1) with the option; every seqports kernel unchanged (24 / 33 `a` = Vitis with the option). On the 24 kernels whose region hls-est keeps sequential: 20 / 24 = Vitis with the option (18 / 24 before the fix), 13 / 24 without.
  - *Harness subset* (110 designs with a Vitis total, `-j 8 --timeout 120`, MLIR of the T5 runs / `t4/x_full_def` / `t4rev/rxc_dme_def` via `--mlir-from`, frozen binary `~/hls-est-work/t6fix/hls-est.fix1`, built from this commit's code before the last comment / option-description edits (the final build gives the same kernel table and lit results), runs `~/hls-est-work/t6fix/runs/`; before = `~/hls-est-work/t6rev/runs/head_*` and `~/hls-est-work/t6/runs/{x2,c2}seq*` from 9389b61bc): totals, statuses and per-array BRAM / status identical in all 11 runs, apart from forgebench/conv_block_module, which hit the 120 s limit under load in two of the earlier runs and now finishes with the value it gave alone then (13736 scalar no split off, 6871 exact no split on). Scalar shim: default 35 exact / 18328 |err| (option off) and 37 / 18334 (on); no split 41 / 29227 and 44 / 29230; no split + `forward-stores` 44 / 29654. Exact shim (option on): default 40 / 3048, no split 47 / 5301, + `forward-stores` 47 / 5489. Exactclass + M5 front end (option on): default 43 / 2635, no split 52 / 6036, + `forward-stores` 55 / 6026. So no corpus design has a memory order through a second array inside a sequential stretch that the old rule missed. RAM type labels change from RAM_T2P to RAM_2P on 80 (default) / 107 (no split) memrefs with the option off and 95 / 129 with it. Agreement of the label with the Vitis optype, over the arrays that hls-est prices with two live ports and Vitis binds to a 2-port type: default off 10 / 20 -> 10 / 20 (4 gained, 4 lost: forgebench Llama_GPT_module / llama_transformer_p2 `Q` / `K`, which Vitis binds ram_t2p while hls-est sees one write per cycle), no split off 28 / 33 -> 30 / 33, default on 10 / 29 -> 19 / 29, no split on 28 / 43 -> 40 / 43. Regression gates polybench_pragmas bicg 8, gesummv 16, mvt 8, syrk 32 exact in all 11 runs. Summed hls-est time over the designs: no increase (497 s -> 381 s default + option, 496 s -> 357 s no split + option; the review's runs ran under more load).
  - *test/hls-est/Estimation* (42 inputs, default pipeline, option off and on): 70 of 84 outputs byte-identical, 14 differ only in RAM_T2P -> RAM_2P labels; totals and exit codes identical.
  - *Lit* `test/hls-est`: 89 tests, 60 passed, 1 expectedly failed, 24 unresolved, 4 failed (the pre-existing forgebench CHECK-BRAM ones); the new D1 checks (`@through_other`, `@war_chain`, `@call_between`, `@mem_order`, `@mem_order_straight`) fail on 9389b61bc. `check_debug_invariance.sh`: 38 PASS / 0 FAIL / 4 ERR with and without the option.
- **Reverted?:** no. `DEFAULT_PIPELINE`, lit RUN-line pipelines and every option default unchanged; the D3 relabelling also shows in the default pipeline's debug output and report `kind=` lines (no estimate depends on it). Adopting the option is still MEMOS.md M6.

## 2026-10-10 — cgeist: no wrapping-blind affine promotion of narrow integer arithmetic, own-counter shortcut, exact cast chains, loop exit values, float compound (frontend)
- **Commit:** Polygeist 763b0046 (branch `hls-est-vitis-match`, repo root `/mnt/d/HLS`); this entry: the following changelog-only commit in `~/circt`
- **Files:** Polygeist `lib/polygeist/Passes/AffineCFG.cpp` (new: hidden option `-affine-cfg-assume-nowrap-width` (default 32), `IntRangeOracle`, `peelExactCasts`, `operandsUnsigned`, `exactArith`, `constantRead`, `isValidIndexAs`, `roundTripKeepsValue`; changed: `legalCondition`, `AffineApplyNormalizer` (operand loop, `indexInput`, leaf branch), `fully2ComposeAffineMapAndOperands` / `fully2ComposeIntegerSetAndOperands` (stop on no change), `CanonicalizeIndexCast(UI)`, `isValidIndex`), `lib/polygeist/Passes/CanonicalizeFor.cpp` (`ForOpInductionReplacement::{castOf, boundCastOf, ownCastOf, chooseInitCast}`, the exit value, `ceilDivWide`), `tools/cgeist/Lib/clang-mlir.cc` (`emitIntCompound` -> `emitScalarCompound`, floating LHS of `+= -= *= /=`, narrow construction only for `+=` / `-=`); new tests `tools/cgeist/Test/HLS/regressions/{narrow-arith-wrap,narrow-counter-shared-start,narrow-cast-chain,counter-exit-value,float-compound-double}.c`, `tools/cgeist/Test/Verification/{loop-exit-value,float-compound-assign}.c`; this file (the 8490cbbb entry corrected in place)
- **What:** the five findings of the review of 8490cbbb, fixed as C semantics (no Vitis modelling, no change to the HLS lowering).
  1. F1 (AffineCFG, root cause) The normalizer stripped every `index_cast` / `index_castui` / `extui` / `extsi` above a map operand and turned an AddI / SubI / MulI of any width into a non-wrapping affine expression; the `trunci` of the old `-= *=` path had stopped it, 8490cbbb's narrow construction exposed it (`uint8_t k = 10; k -= i; A[k]` -> `A[-iv + 10]`, `int8_t k = i; k *= 3; A[k + 128]` -> `A[3 iv + 128]`, the older `k = 250; k += i` -> `A[iv - 6]`, the exact shim's `ap_uint<7> k = 5; k -= i` -> `A[-iv + 5]`). Now a cast is stripped only when it keeps the value (`peelExactCasts`): an extension read with its own signedness, an `extsi` read unsigned only for a provably non-negative input, a truncation index -> iN only when the index provably fits the iN as read. An integer add / sub / mul narrower than `-affine-cfg-assume-nowrap-width` (default 32, C's int) becomes an affine expression only when its exact result provably lies in the range of its type as read (`exactArith`; a narrow div / rem only when it equals the affine floordiv / mod: unsigned, or a non-negative signed dividend, positive divisor); its operands enter the map through an `index_cast` / `index_castui` with the signedness the proof used, its constants read the same way (an unsigned `_BitInt(7)` 64 is 64, the old code read it as -64). *(Corrected: a decrement by a constant, which MLIR folds into an add of -c, was read as 2^N - c and never proved, so a provably in-range narrow decrement was no longer raised; fixed in the round 4 review fixes entry above, D1.)* Ranges (`IntRangeOracle`): constants, extensions, affine.for / scf.for induction variables from their bounds, sums / products, and index arithmetic as a linear form bounded through the loop bounds (the countdown counter `n - (iv - 1)` with ub `n + 1` lies in [1, n]). `isValidIndex` (`isValidIndexAs`) accepts exactly what the normalizer raises, `legalCondition` likewise, and the compose loops stop when a round changes nothing; everything else stays a `memref.load` / `memref.store` of the iN value. The round-trip folds index -> iN -> index (`CanonicalizeIndexCast`, `CanonicalizeIndexCastUI`) need the index to fit iN as the outer cast reads it. Integers of at least 32 bits are raised as before (signed overflow is UB; the IR cannot tell unsigned int arithmetic apart). *(Corrected: that reasoning covers add / sub / mul only; div / rem of 32 bits and more, and of index, were still raised to floordiv / mod without the sign check, wrong for a negative dividend; fixed in the round 4 review fixes entry above, D2.)* clang-mlir.cc: the narrow construction stays for `+=` / `-=` only (needed: a narrow counter's `i += 2` / `i -= 2` stays an iN add that CanonicalizeFor raises; value-identical, and AffineCFG now reads the iN op exactly); `*= &= |= ^=` compute in the computation type again (no consumer needs them narrow).
  2. F2 (CanonicalizeFor `chooseInitCast`) the own-signedness shortcut took the cast of any narrow counter whose start was the SSA value a bound was cast from and skipped the range check (`for (uint8_t i = 0, j = 0; i < 10; i++, j += 30) O[j] = i` -> `O[30 iv]`, `j--` -> `O[-iv]`, shared runtime start -> `O[30 iv - 29 s]`). `ownCastOf` takes it only for the loop's own counter or one with its values: lower bound = cast of its start and it steps with the loop (`sameValue`), or, counting down, upper bound = that cast + 1 and it steps by minus the loop step. Other counters go through the range check (and stay iter_args when it fails). Counters as wide as int keep the old choice.
  3. F3 (AffineCFG + CanonicalizeFor) `for (int8_t i = -8; i < 8; i++) O[i + 8] = A[(uint16_t)i]`: `index_castui(extsi(index_cast(iv -> i8)))` was stripped to iv (`A[iv]`, C reads `A[65528..65535]`); the `extsi` read unsigned now needs i >= 0 (F1). A narrow counter passed to a call is read as the callee reads it once inlined, after `chooseInitCast` looked at its users, and the upstream `index_cast(index_cast)` fold does not check ranges: a call user now counts as both views (a `static inline int rd(int A[256], uint8_t k)` called with the int8_t counter gave `A[iv]`; a uint8_t counter over 120..135 passed to an `int8_t` parameter gave `A[iv + 128]`).
  4. F4 (`ForOpInductionReplacement`) the exit value was `init + (ub - lb)` (step = increment) or `init + divui(ub - lb, step) * inc`: wrong for a step that does not divide ub - lb (`uint8_t i < 10; i += 3` stored 10, C 12; int likewise) and for a loop that does not run (`uint8_t i; for (i = s; i < 100; i++); *O = i` stored 100, C stores s for s > 100; `int i < n` stored n for n < 0). It is now `init + max(0, ceildivsi(ub - lb, step)) * inc`, without the `maxsi` when ub >= lb is provable (`indexRange`) and without `ceildivsi` for step 1; `chooseInitCast`'s exit range uses the same trip count (and no longer gives up on loops that may not run).
  5. F5 (clang-mlir.cc) `float f; f += d` (double d) truncated d to float before the add (double rounding). A floating LHS of `+= -= *= /=` goes through the computation-type path (`emitScalarCompound`): `extf`, the op in double, `truncf`; `float += float` and `float += int` (computation type float) are unchanged.
- **Why:** verifier findings F1-F5 on 8490cbbb; reproducers in the session scratchpad `verify3/` (`n5_narrow_ops`, `n11_apsub`, `n3_mixed_pipe`, `n6_two`, `n9_chain`, `n10_mem`, `n7_exit`, `n1_exact`, `n4_dbl`) and new probes `r4/probes/` (`f1_ops`, `f2_two`, `f3_casts`, `f3b_calls`, `f4_exit`, `f5_float`).
- **Effect:**
  - Probes, new `bin/cgeist` vs `bin/cgeist.prev-8490cbbb` (83 sources: `verify/`, `probes/`, `verify2/`, `verify3/`, `fix4/`, `r4/probes/`; the same 9 fail in both, pre-existing): 66 identical. Changed, all to the C values: F1 n5 `w_sub` / `w_add` / `w_sub_c`, n11 `ap_sub`, f1 `a_sub` / `a_add_wrap` / `a_bit7` / `a_u16` -> `memref.load` of the `index_castui` of the iN op (were affine `-iv + 10`, `iv - 6`, `iv - 1`, `-iv + 5`); f1 `a_bit7_ok` `iv + 64` (was `iv - 64`); f1 `a_sym` (`uint8_t k = n; k += 1`) symbol `index_castui(n + 1 : i8)` (was `index_cast(n) + 1`, signed); f1 `a_rem_s` keeps `remsi i8` (was the affine mod, 0..4 for negative i); `*= &= |= ^=` on narrow LHSs back to the computation type (n5 `w_mul`, n11 `s8_mul`, f1 `a_mul` / `a_mul_ok` / `a_s8` / `a_s8_ok` / `a_and` / `a_xor`, e2_compound, probe_compound, probe_compound_uchar, v3_compound; the in-range `a_mul_ok` / `a_s8_ok` are no longer affine, as before 8490cbbb). F2 n3 `m_two` / `m_two_rt` / `m_two_down`, n6 `t_shared_rt_down`, f2 `b_down_two` -> iter_arg (were `O[30 iv]`, `O[30 iv - 29 s]`, `O[-iv]`, `O[-iv + 2 s]`, `O[iv + 99]`); `b_down_rt` / n2 `c_u8_n` (own countdown from a runtime n) still `-iv + n + 1`. F3 n9 `s_chain`, f3 `c_chain` -> `memref.load` of `index_castui(extsi i8 -> i16)`; n10 `mem_call`, f3 `c_call_s2u` / `c_call_u2s` / f3b `g_u_s` -> iter_arg (were `A[iv]` / `A[iv + 128]`); f3b `g_ext` (external callee) iter_arg too (conservative); `c_call_ok`, `g_u_u` still affine. F4 n7 / f4 `z_rt` -> `s + max(100 - s, 0)`, `z_step3` / `d_step3_*` 12 (were 100 / 10); f4 `d_rt_int` `max(n, 0)`, `d_rt_step` `max(ceil(n / 4), 0) * 4` (were n); `d_unsigned_n` unchanged (n, no max). F5 f5 `e_add/sub/mul/div` and the accumulator loop: `extf`, op in f64, `truncf` (were `truncf` of d, op in f32); `e_half` in f32; `e_same`, `e_int`, `e_dbl`, n4_dbl unchanged.
  - Lit (installed binary): `tools/cgeist/Test/HLS --filter-out hlsfactory` 88 passed / 5 failed (83 + the 5 new tests; the same 5 pre-existing failures: array_partition/lowering.c, consumer/hls-est-banking.c, consumer/hls-est-pipeline.c, pipeline/lowering.c, polybench/hls-est-e2e.c); `tools/cgeist/Test/Verification` 134 passed / 8 expectedly failed (132 + 2 new). Every new test fails with `cgeist.prev-8490cbbb`.
  - Harness `run_hlsest.py --datasets polybench,polybench_pragmas,machsuite,spector_hls,vitis_examples,forgebench -j 8 --force-cgeist` (scalar shim, `common.DEFAULT_PIPELINE`), private harness copies and HOME (`/home/peter/tmp/r4/h_*`), one frozen hls-est (`/home/peter/tmp/r4/hls-est` = `build/clion/bin/hls-est` of 06:12, circt f959c57dc + 3 uncommitted files) for all runs: new vs control (`cgeist.prev-8490cbbb`): 112 designs, ok 80 / hls-est timeout 10 / cgeist error 12 / hls-est error 6 / cgeist crash 4 in both, **every status, total and cgeist output identical**. `--shim exactclass` (cgeist_flags: `--scalar-wrapper-abi`) on forgebench: 45 designs, ok 31 / timeout 10 / cgeist error 4 in both, all identical. So the corpus has no narrow `*= &= |= ^=`, no narrow index arithmetic or cast chain that is not provably exact, no shared-start narrow counter, no non-dividing-step or possibly-zero-trip counter used after its loop and no `float op= double`. The installed binary (rebuilt after a cosmetic `/=` cleanup) gives the same output on all 83 probes, and a scalar-shim rerun with it (`h_final`) is again identical to the control on all 112 designs.
  - Strict width experiment (`-affine-cfg-assume-nowrap-width=64`, i.e. i32 arithmetic needs a range proof too; not the default): 93 / 112 identical, 19 designs' cgeist output changes (forgebench attention / attn_breakdown / conv / gpt / llama / tiled_attn / diff_dims_module_small, spector_hls dct / fir): index arithmetic on `int` function arguments (`h * 4 + j`) becomes `memref.load`; all 112 statuses and totals unchanged. Left at 32 (C: signed int overflow is undefined; only unsigned 32-bit wraps are assumed away); requiring the proof for int too, or marking signed arithmetic in the frontend instead, is Peter's call.
  - Build: objects via ninja, link against the ext4 copy of the llvm-project static libs (`/home/peter/tmp/ra2-llvmlib/lib`, mtimes / sizes checked) to `/home/peter/tmp/r4/cgeist`, copied to `/home/peter/tmp/cgeist-link/cgeist`; installed by renaming the busy binary to `bin/cgeist.prev-8490cbbb`, copy, `cmp`, `ninja -t restat bin/cgeist`; `bin/cgeist.orig-hls-preserve-name` and the other `cgeist.prev-*` untouched.
- **Reverted?:** no. Replaced within the change: (a) a first range oracle had intervals only; the own countdown from a runtime start (`for (uint8_t i = n; i > 0; i--)`, `n - (iv - 1)`) lost its affine index, so index arithmetic is bounded as a linear form through the loop bounds; (b) inspecting the callee's reads of a call argument (instead of "both views") was dropped: when the caller is canonicalised the callee's parameter still goes through an alloca (no reads visible), and it reads another function's IR from a function pass.

## 2026-10-10 — hls-est: two ports per state in sequential scopes (`--bram-seq-dual-port`, opt-in); `forward-stores` re-measured (T6)
- **Commit:** 3ceac5ba4 (code, tests, notes and this entry; branch `hls-est-vitis-match`); this hash line: the following changelog-only commit
- **Files:** lib/Dialect/Resource/Passes/BRAMAnalysis.cpp (`SeqScopeIndex`, `splitConstantOffset`, `compareAddresses`, `findSameStatePair`, `sequentialScopeDemand`; `scopeDemand` / `computePortDemand` / `recordAccesses` / `estimateBRAM` take the index; option `--bram-seq-dual-port`; debug text `[same state ...]` and JSON `port_demand.per_scope[].same_state`), lib/Dialect/Resource/Passes/AffineToLoopScheduleModified.cpp (`hoistIfBodies` records `hls.if_arms`; forward-stores comment), include/circt/Dialect/Resource/Passes/LoopPragmas.h (`kIfArmsAttr`), include/circt/Conversion/Passes.td (forward-stores description); new lit tests test/hls-est/Passes/{seq_scope_ports,seq_scope_ports_ir}.mlir; test/hls-est/Passes/access_forwarding.mlir (comment); tools/hls-est-validate/sched_ports.py (new), VITIS_NOTES.md (N12 new; N4, N5 updated), OPEN_ISSUES.md (O3, O7 rewritten; O20, O21 new), MEMOS.md (M6 new), this file
- **What:**
  1. *Sequential scopes* (`--bram-seq-dual-port`, a `--bram-analysis` cl option, default **off**): a sequential scope (`ii == 0`: a function body or a loop that is not pipelined) is no longer one access per cycle. Its accesses to a bank are split into straight-line stretches (the scope's block up to the next loop / pipeline / other region op; scf.if / affine.if arms belong to the stretch); within a stretch, two accesses with no SSA path between them and no memory order (a store against an access whose address is not provably different: `base + c` vs `base + c'` with `c != c'` is different) share a state; the two arms of one if never pair; two loads of one address count once; at most two per state. One such pair makes the scope's demand 2 (2R -> RAM_T2P, 1R+1W -> RAM_2P, 2W -> RAM_T2P; x18 columns). Never replicated (only pipelined loops replicate, unchanged).
  2. *If arms through the hoist:* `--convert-affine-to-loopschedule` moves every if body in front of its if (`hoistIfBodies`); it now tags each memory access it moves with `hls.if_arms = [if id, arm, ...]` so that the sequential rule still sees the two arms as exclusive. Nothing else reads the attribute; the IR printed by the pass gains it on those ops (no lit test checks against it).
  3. *Tool:* `sched_ports.py <run dir>` counts, per module and array, the accesses that issue in each state of `db/*.verbose.sched.rpt` (the bind reports' MemPortTyVec does not list an array in the module that allocates it).
  4. *forward-stores* (O7) re-measured on top of item 1; stays off (see Effect and MEMOS.md M6). Only its documentation changed.
- **Why:** VITIS_NOTES.md N12: 33 Vitis kernels (`~/hls-est-work/exp-src/seqports{,2,3,4}/`, runs `~/hls-est-work/experiments/t6_sp_*/`, td=off): two independent loads / a load and a disjoint store / two disjoint stores in one block of a non-pipelined loop or function body share a state and make the array ram_2p / ram_t2p (sp_2ld, sp_o_8ld, sp_straight, sp_far, sp_o_ldst, sp_2st ...: 2 BRAM for 64x32); a data dependence, a may-alias store, an inner loop between them, or the two arms of one if keep them apart (sp_dd, sp_rmw, sp_stld_alias, sp_split_loop, sp_o_ifarms ...: 1). Corpus: the hoisted invariant reads of polybench atax `buff_x`, bicg `buff_p`, gesummv `buff_x`, mvt `buff_y1` / `buff_y2` issue two per state (`sched_ports.py`: 32 states of 2R), ram_2p 2 each (OPEN_ISSUES O3).
- **Effect** (harness subset, 110 designs with a Vitis total, `-j 8 --timeout 120`; MLIR of the T5 runs via `--mlir-from`; frozen binary `~/hls-est-work/t6/bin/hls-est.seq2`, built from this commit's code (only comments and the forward-stores description changed afterwards); runs `~/hls-est-work/t6/runs/`; "exact" / "exactclass" = the same harness on the cgeist outputs of `t4/x_full_def` / `t4rev/rxc_dme_def`, the latter with the M5 front end `--vitis-fold --canonicalize --sccp --dead-memory-elimination` ... `--bram-maxi-adapters`):
  - *Option off (what the default pipeline computes):* identical to the base binary 009ad6529 on all 112 designs in both pipelines: totals, statuses and every per-array record of the debug JSON (`s2base_*` vs `base_*`). Estimation inputs: identical totals and exit codes.
  - *Option on, exact matches / total abs error* (base -> option): scalar shim, default pipeline 35 -> **37**, 18328 -> 18334; scalar, no split 41 -> **44**, 29227 -> 29230; exact shim, default 38 -> 40, 3047 -> 3048; exact, no split 44 -> 47, 5308 -> 5301 (forgebench/conv_block_module hit the 120 s limit under load in the suite run; alone 6871, unchanged); exactclass + M5 front end, default 41 -> 43, 2634 -> 2635; no split 49 -> 52, 6043 -> 6036. Newly exact: polybench mvt 12 -> 14 (both pipelines), gesummv 134 -> 135 (default), atax 11 -> 13 and bicg 12 -> 14 (no split). Farther: atax 68 -> 70, bicg 69 -> 71 (default pipeline; the same arrays become right, the 64-bank buff_A split dominates, O2); scalar shim only, forgebench conv_block_module / op2 / op3 +5 / +2 / +3: `localWeights` RAM_1P -> RAM_T2P, which is Vitis' ram_2p, but the scalar shim's f32 needs two x18 columns where Vitis' 16-bit element needs one. No design that matched stops matching. Per array, every changed polybench array is Vitis-exact (atax `buff_x`, `buff_y_out`; bicg `buff_p`, `buff_s_out`; gesummv `buff_x`; mvt `buff_y1`, `buff_y2`: 1 -> 2 = Vitis); atax `buff_y_out` / bicg `buff_s_out` get there through the promoted loads of the unrolled-j decision, Vitis through its flattened II-1 pipeline (O3 item 3; with `float-loads-perfect=true` the totals stay 13 / 14 for Vitis' reason). Regression gate polybench_pragmas bicg 8, gesummv 16, mvt 8, syrk 32 exact in all 12 runs. `--bram-analysis` time on conv_block_op1 0.21 s with or without the option.
  - *forward-stores on top of the option:* no total changes in the default pipeline on any shim. No split: scalar 44 -> 44, 29230 -> 29654 (attention Q / K replicated: right copy count 3 where Vitis keeps them, but f32 columns and Q / K that Vitis deletes); exact 47 -> 47, 5301 -> 5489; exactclass + M5 front end 52 -> **55**, 6036 -> 6026 (attention_op_p2 / p3, gpt_transformer_p1 newly exact: Q / K 3 copies = Vitis RAM_1WNR; Llama_GPT_module 29 -> 33 farther, Vitis 30). Not adopted: the default pipeline's numbers do not move, and on the shipped front end it only adds error (working-loop rule 4); MEMOS.md M6 proposes it together with the M5 front end.
  - *Kernels* (N12): on the 19 kernels whose region hls-est also keeps sequential, array `a` = Vitis on 16 with the option, 10 without (`~/hls-est-work/t6/ktab2.txt`); the 3 misses are over-estimates (O21). The 14 `pipeline off` kernels are pipelined by hls-est (O20).
  - *Lit* `test/hls-est`: 89 tests, 60 passed (58 + the 2 new files), 1 expectedly failed, 24 unresolved, 4 failed (the pre-existing forgebench CHECK-BRAM ones). `check_debug_invariance.sh` with the option in the pipeline: 38 PASS / 0 FAIL / 4 ERR (as without).
- **Reverted?:** no. Defaults unchanged (`DEFAULT_PIPELINE`, lit RUN lines, `forward-stores` default): adopting `--bram-seq-dual-port` is MEMOS.md M6. The task text asked for the note as "N9"; N9-N11 were taken, so it is N12.

## 2026-10-10 — hls-est: T5 review fixes (one constant-index definition for td=off rule (b) and memory banking; rule (b) needs an affine access)
- **Commit:** 7c261b1d0 (branch `hls-est-vitis-match`); this hash line: the following changelog-only commit
- **Files:** include/circt/Dialect/Resource/Passes/ConstantIndex.h (new: `getConstantIndexValue`, `foldConstantIndexOperands`), lib/Dialect/Resource/Passes/InferVitisSplitArray.cpp (`constantDimsOfAffine`, `constantDimsOfIndices`, `ConstantIndexInfo::affineAccesses`, `AutomaticPartition::unbankable`, `decideAutomaticPartition`, `applyAutomaticPartitions`, statistic `arrays-constant-index-unbanked`), lib/Dialect/Resource/Passes/MemoryBankModified.cpp (`computeMemrefBankAccess`, `BankAffineLoadPattern`, `BankAffineStorePattern`); test/hls-est/Passes/split_array_td_off_banking.mlir (new), banking_constant_operand.mlir (`@single_trip_and_cast`), split_array_td_off.mlir (`@memref_only`, REMARK run); tools/hls-est-validate/{OPEN_ISSUES.md O2, VITIS_NOTES.md N7 hls-est line}, this file (T5 entry corrected in place, D3)
- **What:**
  - (D1) `--infer-vitis-split-array` td=off rule (b) counted as constant an index cast of a constant and the counter of a loop that runs exactly once, but `--memory-banking-bram` only resolved `arith.constant` operands (folded into the map) and stepped counters. An access through such an index to a dimension rule (b) had split therefore read all f banks and muxed them (f loads + f-1 `arith.select`), and every bank was charged a port. Both passes now use one definition (`ConstantIndex.h`): banking substitutes those operands into the affine access map before it decides the bank (exact at run time; a second `canonicalizeMapAndOperands` drops the operands that no longer appear), and a memref.load / memref.store index goes through the same helper.
  - (D2) Rule (b) now needs at least one affine access to the array: banking (`collectMemRefs`) only splits an array that has one, so an array reached only through memref.load / memref.store got a spec that was then priced as one memory ("spec present, not banked"). Such an array now gets no spec; the decision is counted (`arrays-constant-index-unbanked`), printed under `-debug-only=infer-vitis-split-array` and reported as a remark with `emit-remarks=true`. Rules (a) and (c) need no banking and are unchanged.
  - (D3) the T5 entry said "175 debug records" as `registers` in the td=off run; a complete run has 317, equal to the pass statistic (the 175 came from a run in which designs timed out). Corrected in place.
- **Why:** review of bb6498998 (D1-D3); reproducers `~/hls-est-work/t5review/edge/{e1_singletrip,e1b_literal,e3_indexcast,e2_memrefonly}.mlir` (copies and results in `~/hls-est-work/t5fix/edge/`). Vitis side unchanged from N7: [HLS 214-449] partitions a dimension that only constants index, and the front end has folded single-trip loops and casts of constants by then.
- **Effect:**
  - *Reproducers, td=off:* e1_singletrip 9 -> 8 and e3_indexcast 9 -> 8, equal to the literal-index control e1b_literal (8): bank 0 goes RAM_T2P -> RAM_1P. e2_memrefonly 2 -> 2, now without a partition spec. The other edge files (e4-e7, c1-c3) are unchanged. The banking half also acts under the default `td=auto` pipeline: e1 / e3 5 -> 4 there (the port-pressure cyclic-4 split of A, read through the single-trip counter / the cast, now reads one bank). No corpus input has such an index (below), so no default-pipeline result changes.
  - *Harness subset* (scalar shim, `-j 8 --timeout 120`, the T5 runs' MLIR via `--mlir-from ~/hls-est-work/t5/runs/base_def.json`, runs `~/hls-est-work/t5fix/runs/`; before = the review's runs `~/hls-est-work/t5review/runs/` with the byte-identical pre-fix binary, and T5's `new_tdoff_flp`): under default, no split, td=off and td=off + `float-loads-perfect=true`, 0 of 112 designs change total or status, and the per-array debug records (name, shape, status, BRAM, RAM type, partition kind / factor / dim / source / bank) are identical on every design that has them (80 / 81 / 81). On the 79 designs all eight runs compare: default 35 exact, |err| 18328; no split 41, 20630; td=off 41, 20622; td=off + flp 41, 20514 (before = after). Gates polybench_pragmas bicg 8, gesummv 16, mvt 8, syrk 32 exact in all four. td=off: 317 `registers` records, 256 constant-index banks (atax, bicg, gesummv buff_A + buff_B at 64 banks each), no constant-index spec left unbanked before or after.
  - *test/hls-est/Estimation* (42 inputs): stdout and exit code byte-identical before / after under the default, no-split and td=off pipelines.
  - *Kernels* (cached MLIR: the 198 of `~/hls-est-work/t5/kern`, the 140 of `~/hls-est-work/t5review/kern`): no total changes under default / no split / td=off / td=off + flp (td=off exact 149 / 198 and 130 / 140, as before).
  - Lit test/hls-est: 58 passed (57 + 1 new file), 1 expectedly failed, 24 unresolved, 4 failed (the pre-existing CHECK-BRAM ones); every new check fails on the pre-fix binary. `check_debug_invariance.sh` with the td=off pipeline: 38 PASS / 0 FAIL / 4 ERR (as before).
- **Reverted?:** no. `DEFAULT_PIPELINE`, lit RUN-line pipelines and pass option defaults unchanged.

## 2026-10-10 — cgeist: `+=` through the compound path, no raising of wrapping narrow loops, unsigned bounds, mixed-sign counters (frontend)
- **Commit:** Polygeist 8490cbbb (branch `hls-est-vitis-match`, repo root `/mnt/d/HLS`); this entry: the following changelog-only commit in `~/circt`
- **Files:** Polygeist `tools/cgeist/Lib/clang-mlir.cc`, `lib/polygeist/Passes/CanonicalizeFor.cpp`, `tools/cgeist/Test/HLS/regressions/narrow-counter-wrap.c` (new), `tools/cgeist/Test/Verification/fscanf.c` (one CHECK line)
- **What:** the four defects of the review of e5b6bbc9, fixed generically (C semantics; no Vitis modelling).
  1. D1 `+=` on an integer LHS goes through `emitIntCompound` like the other compound assignments (the memref / LLVM-pointer `p += n` cases and the floating LHS keep their paths): `int *p; *p += 1.5` is `sitofp / addf / fptosi` (it aborted in `cast<IntegerType>`), `unsigned` `uitofp / fptoui`, `bool *b; *b += 1` stores `(b + 1) != 0` (`cmpi ne` + `extui`; it stored 2). In `emitIntCompound`, `+= -= *= &= |= ^=` with an integer computation type wider than an integer non-bool LHS are built in the LHS type on the truncated RHS (the low bits of these ops depend only on the operands' low bits), so `+=` with an integer RHS emits exactly what it did before and a narrow counter's `i += 2` stays an iN add that CanonicalizeFor recognises; `-= *= &= |= ^=` on a narrow LHS lose their `extui` / `trunci` pair. **Correction (Polygeist 763b0046):** the narrow op has the same low bits, but AffineCFG then raised a narrow add / sub / mul as a non-wrapping affine expression (the `trunci` had stopped it), so `uint8_t k = 10; k -= i; A[k]` became `A[-iv + 10]` (F1 of the next review); since 763b0046 only `+=` / `-=` are built narrow, and AffineCFG raises narrow arithmetic only when it provably does not wrap.
  2. D2 `WhileToForHelper::counterStaysInRange`: a counter narrower than int (unsigned: wraps modulo 2^W; signed: `++` / `+=` is computed in int and converted back) is raised only when it provably stays in its range as the test reads it ([0, 2^W) through extui or an unsigned compare, the signed range through extsi or a signed compare, [0, 2^(W-1)) through extsi read unsigned): counting up by s with `i < B` (`i <= B`) the largest accepted value plus s, counting down by d with `i > B` (`i >= B`) the smallest accepted value minus d, exact for a constant start (last = start + k*s), else with the largest / smallest value the bound's definition allows (constant, extui / extsi of a narrower value, else the whole type). A runtime step is not provable. So `ap_uint<4> i < 16`, `i <= 15`, `uint8_t i = 250; i < 300`, `uint16_t i < 70000; i += 1000`, `uint8_t i >= 0`, `uint8_t i < 255; i += 2`, `int8_t i < 200` stay `scf.while`, and so does `for (uint8_t i = 0; i < n; i++)` with an `int n` (never ends for n > 255; assuming n <= 255 is Peter's call, not made here). The review's bound "ub <= 2^W for `<`" is off by one (`ap_uint<4> i < 16` has ub = 2^4 and never ends); the rule used is last + s <= 2^W - 1. A counter as wide as int is assumed not to wrap, as before: signed overflow is UB, and the IR cannot tell `unsigned j < n; j += 2` from `int jj < unsigned row_end; jj += 4` (spector_hls/spmv, `cmpi ult` on i32 either way). `prepareFor` adds the `+ 1` of an inclusive bound / a countdown's start in index after the cast: `for (uint8_t i = 255; i > 0; i--)` gets ub 256 (it was `(uint8_t)(255 + 1)` = 0, zero iterations).
  3. D3 `prepareFor`: the counter's start and step are cast with the counter's signedness, the comparison's bound with the predicate's (`ult/ule/ugt/uge` -> `index_castui`): `ap_uint<8> i < ap_uint<8> len` gives `affine.for 0 to index_castui(len)` (len >= 128 gave 0 iterations). Verification/fscanf.c expected the signed cast of `unsigned i < (int)n`'s bound; its CHECK line now expects `index_castui` (C converts n to unsigned).
  4. D4 `ForOpInductionReplacement::chooseInitCast`: a narrow counter iter_arg is replaced by `index_cast(X -> iN)` only when one cast of its start keeps X in the range of every view the counter is read with (extui / index_castui: [0, 2^N), extsi / index_cast: signed), since AffineCFG's cast stripping and the round-trip folds read those back as X: when every view is the counter's own signedness (the cast MoveWhileToFor made the bounds with; X is then the counter's value, kept in range by D2), or when the range of X from the bounds, steps and start (constants, casts of narrower values) lies in every view. Otherwise the counter stays an iter_arg, whose iN arithmetic is exact. A counter as wide as int takes its own signedness when the bounds show it, else the users' view, as before. AffineCFG is unchanged: every `index_cast(index -> iN)` the replacement creates now holds values its extensions read back exactly. **Correction (763b0046):** not for a second counter that shares the start value (the own-signedness shortcut skipped its range check, F2), nor for reads the counter gets later through a cast chain or an inlined callee (F3).
- **Why:** verifier of e5b6bbc9, probes in the session scratchpad `verify2/` (`r01..r08_add`, `q*_bool`, `p1_u8_down`, `p2_u16_step`, `p3_ap4`, `p5_crash` c_mix, `p6_apbound` k8_len) plus `fix4/e1_edges.cpp` (37 loop shapes) and `fix4/e2_compound.cpp` (18 compound assignments).
- **Effect:**
  - Probes, new `bin/cgeist` vs `bin/cgeist.prev-28236352` (66 sources: `verify/`, `probes/`, `verify2/`, `fix4/`): abort -> ok: r01 (`int += 1.5`: sitofp/addf/fptosi), r02 (`uchar += 0.5`: uitofp/addf/fptoui i8), r03 (`unsigned`: fptoui), r04 / q02 (`bool += 0.5`: `cmpf une` + extui), p4_bool, e2_compound (`bool += 255` -> `(b + 255) != 0` in i32). q05 / r07 / r08 (`bool += 1` / `+= true`): extui, addi i32, `cmpi ne`, extui i1 -> i8 (was `addi i8`, storing 2). p1: d_u8_ge0 (`i >= 0`) and d_u8_lt300 -> `scf.while` (were `affine.for 0 to 11` / `250 to 300`). p2: s_u16_wrap -> while (was `0 to 70000 step 1000`), s_u16_stepvar (runtime step) -> while (was raised), s_u16_rev2 (`i -= 2`, 62..2) now raised (`affine.for 1 to 63 step 2`, index 62 - 2*floordiv(iv - 1, 2) as a memref.load; was while), s_u16_rev2b stays while. p3: a4_lt16, a4_le15 and a4_ltn (`int n`) -> while (were `0 to 16` / `0 to n`), a4_ltk bound `index_castui` (was `index_cast`). p5 c_mix: `affine.for 200 to 210 iter_args(i8)`, store index `extui(i) - 200` = 0..9 (was `%arg2 - 456` = -256..-247). p6 k8_len: `index_castui len`. v12 n_u8_bound (`uint8_t i < int n`) -> while (the documented choice); every other v12 / v10 loop still raises; v10 l_int_sizet_bound, l_int_unsigned_bound, l_sizet_sizet_bound: bound `index_castui` (was `index_cast`). Narrowing only, same values: v3_compound (`&= |= ^= -= *=` on `ap_uint<7>` in i7, `short -= int` in i16), probe_compound (`*p *= 3` `muli i9`, `*s -= 2` `addi i9 -2`), probe_compound_uchar (`muli i8`). **Correction (763b0046):** the same values in the IR, but not once AffineCFG raised the narrow add / sub / mul to a non-wrapping affine index (F1); `*= &= |= ^=` are back to the computation type. Against the stored e5b6bbc9 outputs: verify/fix3 11 identical + v10 / v12 / v3 as above, probes/fix3 11 identical + the 2 compound probes, verify2/out 13 identical + the 8 above; the 9 sources that failed before fail the same way. e1_edges: `uint8_t i = 255; i > 0` -> `1 to 256`; `int8_t i < 100` raised, `< 200` and `< int n` while, `< int8_t n` raised; `uint8_t i < 255; i += 3` raised, `+= 2` while; `(int8_t)i` over 200..209 only -> `O[iv - 156]` (44..53); over 120..135 -> iter_arg (prev gave `O[iv + 128]` = 248..263); `int` / `unsigned` / `unsigned long` counters with runtime bounds raised as before.
  - Lit: `tools/cgeist/Test/HLS --filter-out hlsfactory` 83 passed / 5 failed (82 + the new narrow-counter-wrap.c; the same five pre-existing failures); `tools/cgeist/Test/Verification` 132 passed / 8 expectedly failed (fscanf.c failed on the D3 cast before its CHECK line was updated). The new test fails (aborts) with the previous binary.
  - Harness `run_hlsest.py --datasets forgebench,vitis_examples,spector_hls,machsuite --shim exact -j 8 --timeout 120 --force-cgeist`, `common.DEFAULT_PIPELINE`, private harness copies and HOME (`/home/peter/tmp/fix4/h_{new,ctl}`), one frozen hls-est copy (`/home/peter/tmp/fix4/hls-est` = `build/clion/bin/hls-est` of 03:46, circt 6ad4853a5) for both: new `/tmp/cg3.json`, control (`bin/cgeist.prev-28236352`) `/tmp/cg3-control.json`. 96 designs, ok 64 / hls-est timeout 10 / cgeist error 12 / hls-est error 6 / cgeist crash 4 in both; **every status and total identical**. cgeist output differs in 2 designs: spector_hls/spmv (`row_start` / `row_end` are `unsigned int`, the loop `int jj < row_end`: both bounds `index_castui` instead of `index_cast`, D3; BRAM 0 = 0) and vitis_examples/12_basic_examples_vhls_accs_w_asserts (`i <= xlimit`: the bound's `index_cast` is created after the `+ 1` moved to index, same map; hls-est error `unregistered dialect` in both). No corpus design has a wrapping narrow loop, a mixed-view narrow counter or an integer `+=` with a floating RHS.
  - Build: `CanonicalizeFor.cpp.o`, `libMLIRPolygeistTransforms.a`, `driver.cc.o` via ninja (68 s); link with `-o /home/peter/tmp/cgeist-link/cgeist` against the ext4 copy of the llvm-project static libs (`/home/peter/tmp/ra2-llvmlib/lib`, sizes checked; 227 s); installed by renaming the busy binary to `bin/cgeist.prev-28236352`, copy, `cmp`, `ninja -t restat bin/cgeist`; `bin/cgeist.orig-hls-preserve-name` untouched.
- **Reverted?:** no. Replaced within the change: (a) the D2 check also covered unsigned 32/64-bit counters; it turned spector_hls/spmv's `int jj < unsigned row_end; jj += 4` into a while loop (the IR cannot tell it from an unsigned counter), so counters as wide as int keep the no-wrap assumption; (b) a first D4 version took the users' view whenever the bounds did not show the counter's signedness; it replaced `(int8_t)i` over 120..135 once the bound casts had been folded to constants (`O[iv + 128]`), so the range check was added.

## 2026-10-10 — Route A review fixes: cgeist wide-integer constants and wrapper corner cases, exactclass `double -> ap_int` and signed 1-bit types, shim checks through cgeist
- **Commit:** Polygeist 28236352 (branch `hls-est-vitis-match`, "cgeist: route A review fixes"); `~/circt`: the commit "hls-est-validate: route A review fixes (exactclass shim, check_shim_cgeist)" that carries this entry
- **Files:** Polygeist `lib/polygeist/Passes/CanonicalizeFor.cpp` (`SubToAdd`), `tools/cgeist/Lib/{clang-mlir.cc,clang-mlir.h}`, `tools/cgeist/Test/HLS/wrapper/{wrapper_derived,wrapper_static_local,wrapper_valueinit}.cpp` (new), `wrapper_not.cpp`, `tools/cgeist/Test/HLS/regressions/wide-int-sub-const.c` (new); `~/circt` `tools/hls-est-validate/shim/exactclass/{ap_int.h,ap_fixed.h,hls_math.h,README.md}`, `check_shim.py`, `check_shim_cgeist.py` (new), `VITIS_NOTES.md` (N10, N11), `OPEN_ISSUES.md` (O19), `MEMOS.md` (M3), this file (corrections marked in the route A entry below)
- **What:** fixes for every defect of the review of Polygeist 8b07bf43 / circt c733e425e.
  1. *D1, cgeist, all modes (plain C-semantics fix):* Polygeist's canonicaliser pattern `SubToAdd` rewrote `x - C` as `x + (-C)` with `-C` built from the `int64_t` value and zero-extended, so on integers wider than 64 bits `x - 1` was `x + (2^64 - 1)`. It now negates the APInt in the constant's width (same constants as before up to 64 bits). The `_BitInt` arithmetic of the exactclass shim hit it (`from_raw` works in W2+W+3 bits, `range()` masks in W+1 bits): negative ties under AP_RND_INF / AP_RND_MIN_INF / AP_RND_CONV and negative values under AP_TRN_ZERO were one LSB off in the MLIR, `ap_int<80> x - 3`, `--ap_int<70>`, `ap_uint<128>::range`, `ap_uint<512>::range` were wrong.
  2. *D2, shim:* `ap_int<W>(double)` now reproduces Vitis' `ap_int_base(double)`, which keeps the shifted mantissa in an `ap_int_base<25, true>` for 2^24 <= |d| < 2^52 (VITIS_NOTES N10, confirmed with csynth); `ap_int<W>(float)` stays exact (`float_to_int`), as in Vitis.
  3. *D3, cgeist (option):* a class that is not a wrapper but derives from one through a wrapper chain (`E : D : B`) no longer asserts in `GetAddressOfBaseClass`; the identity starts at the first wrapper on the path, in both cast directions.
  4. *D4, cgeist (option):* a static local with constant initialisation gets clang's evaluated value as the dense initial value of its global and no run-time initialisation (no guard flag, no stores), so `static const ap_uint<12> lt[4] = {...}` is a ROM; a run-time array initialiser or init list is stored through the global's static shape (the dynamic-shape view made `ValueCategory::store` assert, e.g. for the shim's `const T&` constructors); a constructor call keeps the dynamic-shape view as its `this`.
  5. *D5, cgeist (option):* value-initialisation of a wrapper subobject (`W2() : F() {}`, member `b()`) stores one zero into that object (a loop per array dimension of the constructed type, `count` for `new T[n]()`), not a loop over `memref.dim` of `this`.
  6. *D6, cgeist (option):* a record is a wrapper only if it has its scalar's size and alignment (`struct AW { alignas(16) int v; }` keeps the default `!llvm.struct<(i32)>` lowering).
  7. *D7, shim:* the signed 1-bit types (`ap_int<1>`, `ap_fixed<1,I>`) keep their bit in an `unsigned _BitInt(1)` (an `i1` in the MLIR, as wide as in Vitis) and every value read goes through `rval()`, which sign-extends it. The `_BitInt(2)` storage had also given `ap_int<1> x = 1` the value 1 instead of -1.
  8. *R3:* the `to_int()` comments in `ap_fixed.h` / `ap_int.h` say truncation toward zero (the code and Vitis truncate).
  9. *Checks:* `check_shim_cgeist.py` (new) compiles a targeted list (wide-integer constants, rounding-mode ties, the 25-bit `double -> ap_int` path, signed 1-bit types) and seeded random expressions three ways - host clang++ against the shim, cgeist (harness flags + `cgeist_flags`, `-emit-llvm`, inputs as run-time arguments and as constants cgeist folds), g++ against the Vitis headers - and compares the raw bits; `check_shim.py` alone only ever compiled the shim with the host compiler. `check_shim.py` gained signed 1-bit and 40-bit types, large doubles for `ap_int` and the D2/D7 table rows, and two known, reported-not-failed categories: `divcsim` (VITIS_NOTES N11: Vitis' C-simulation division wraps min / -1 LSB in `_W1` bits, csynth and the shim do not) and `sqrttbl` (`hls::sqrt`'s lookup-table branches compare against constants that wrap in small types, e.g. `ap_fixed<8,4>(7.9375)` gives 0 in Vitis and 2.8125 in the shim; the header is "All Rights Reserved", not ported). R1 and R2 of the review are corrections of the implementation report; R1 also corrects the route A entry below (marked in place).
- **Why:** the review findings, each reproduced before the fix (`~/hls-est-work/rvA/{iso,bx,probes}`, `~/hls-est-work/rfx/{iso,rvcases}`); Vitis evidence: csynth kernels `rfx_d2k_dbl2int` (D2: `a.o.3.ll` stores 0 for `ap_int<32>(1073741824.0)`, -1 for `ap_uint<40>(4294967295.0)`) and `rfx_divk_overflow` (N11: `store i32 128` for `ap_fixed<8,8>(-128) / ap_fixed<8,8>(-1)`, g++ -128), `~/hls-est-work/experiments/`.
- **Effect:**
  - *Bit-exactness through cgeist* (`check_shim_cgeist.py`, installed binary, seeds 11/12/13/2026, `--n 60`): 412 expressions x 3 paths, **0 mismatches** (one `hls::exp` expression 1 LSB off on all three paths, the documented deviation). Negative controls (seed 11, `--n 30`, 73 expressions): the previous cgeist (8b07bf43) with the new shim gives 9 run-time and 7 folded-constant mismatches (D1); the new cgeist with the previous shim gives 15 x 3 (D2, D7). `check_shim.py` (host): `--seed 11 --n 40` 67 131 values, `--seed 12 --n 150` 71 311 values, 0 mismatches; 2 known (`divcsim` 1, `sqrttbl` 1 of 19 table-branch values); exp 1 LSB on 2 / 3 values.
  - *Option-off identity* (pre-route-A binary `bin/cgeist.prev-e5b6bbc9` vs the new cgeist, the same normalisation as the route A entry): every cgeist RUN line of `tools/cgeist/Test/HLS` (958 invocations, 858 files, 11.4 MB): 957 identical, the one difference is the new D1 test `regressions/wide-int-sub-const.c`; harness, all 783 designs: `--shim exact` 1067 invocations identical (682 MLIR files, 7.1 MB), `--shim scalar` 1067 identical (682, 7.1 MB). So D1 changes no option-off output of the corpus. These runs used the first link of this change; the installed binary differs from it only inside the `--scalar-wrapper-abi` static-local code (item 4), and the lit identity run repeated with the installed binary gives the same result (957 of 958 RUN lines identical once the run-to-run order of the `#pragma HLS` warnings is allowed for: `bind_op/bind_op.c -DERRORS` printed its two warnings in the other order, and three runs of each binary show both orders with the same lines).
  - *Lit* (`--filter HLS/ --filter-out hlsfactory`): 82 passed / 5 failed (the 5 pre-existing failures); the previous binary fails the 5 new or extended tests too (77 / 10).
  - *Harness, exactclass* (7 datasets, 208 designs, `-j 6 --timeout 120`, hls-est pinned md5 4e2680bd as in the route A entry, private MLIR caches; control = previous cgeist 8b07bf43 + previous shim c733e425e under the same conditions): no BRAM total changed - on the 58 designs compare.py compares in both runs, exact 32 = 32 and sum |err| 1750 = 1750. Status: forgebench conv_block_op1 (3825) and diff_dims_p3 (192) are ok in the new run and hls-est timeouts (120 s) in the control (load, as R1); class gabriel_lopez_lugo_lab3 and zihan_qi_lab2 reach hls-est (`invalid memref element type`) instead of the 600 s cgeist timeout - gabriel_lab3's `static data_t C_local[N * K] = {}` is now a zero-initialised global instead of a guarded run-time initialisation (previous binary: still running after 900 s on that file alone); zihan_qi_lab2 reached hls-est in the route A run too (timing). New run: ok 92, compared 60, exact 32, sum |err| 1984 (incl. the two newly ok forgebench designs).
  - *Attention mirror:* `feas/A/k.cpp` / `k_ctrl.cpp` with the new shim: rc 0, `memref<8x16xi16>` / `memref<8x8xi16>`, no `llvm.struct`, no `memref2pointer`; `k.cpp` keeps `arith.divsi %c4194304_i29, %c0_i29` (track T8).
  - *Not fixed (pre-existing, filed as O19):* array / `T (*)[8]` / struct-array arguments passed on to pointer parameters, `sizeof` of a record with an aligned member (option on or off), and two option-off-only asserts (static local array initialiser with cleanups, `new F[n]()`).
  - *Build:* objects with ninja; link = ninja's command with `-o` on ext4 and the llvm-project archives from the ext4 copy `/home/peter/tmp/ra2-llvmlib` (sizes checked against the originals), ~15 min under load; installed: busy binary renamed `bin/cgeist.prev-8b07bf43`, copy + `cmp`, `ninja -t restat bin/cgeist`; `cgeist.orig-hls-preserve-name` untouched.
- **Reverted?:** no. Replaced within the change: a first version of item 4 also passed the static-shape view of the global as `this` to a static local's run-time constructor; the harness run showed two class designs (`static hls::stream<...> s("name")`) failing with `'func.call' op operand type mismatch` (memref<1xi32> vs memref<?xi32>); the constructor now gets the dynamic-shape view (lit case `stream` in `wrapper_static_local.cpp`).

## 2026-10-10 — infer-vitis-split-array: td=off automatic partitions (small arrays, constant-index dimensions)
- **Commit:** bb6498998 (branch `hls-est-vitis-match`); this hash line: the following changelog-only commit
- **Files:** lib/Dialect/Resource/Passes/InferVitisSplitArray.cpp (options `td`, `auto-partitions`, `complete-threshold`; `getConstantIndex`, `constantDimsOfAffine`, `analyzeConstantIndices`, `decideAutomaticPartition`, `applyAutomaticPartitions`, `runOnOperation`), include/circt/Dialect/Resource/Interfaces/MemoryDS.h (`kRegistersAttrName` = `hls.registers`, `kRegistersReasonAttrName`), include/circt/Dialect/Resource/Passes/MemrefBankClassification.h + lib/Dialect/Resource/Passes/MemrefBankClassification.cpp (`StorageMode::Registers`), lib/Dialect/Resource/Passes/AffineToLoopScheduleModified.cpp (`linkMemoryResource`), lib/Dialect/Resource/Passes/BRAMAnalysis.cpp (`estimateBRAM`: status `registers`); test/hls-est/Passes/{split_array_td_off,split_array_td_off_bram,split_array_registers_sched}.mlir (new); tools/hls-est-validate/{VITIS_NOTES.md N7, OPEN_ISSUES.md O2, MEMOS.md M1}
- **What:** `--infer-vitis-split-array` gets the option `td=auto|off` (Vitis `syn.array_partition.throughput_driven`). `td=auto` is the default and is the port-pressure inference unchanged. `td=off` infers no port-pressure partition at all (Vitis replicates instead, RAM_1WNR, which `--bram-analysis` already prices) and only the three automatic partitions Vitis still makes: (a) an array of at most `complete-threshold` (4) elements in total, any width or rank -> registers; (c) an array every load/store indexes with constants in every dimension -> registers (scalarised); (b) a dimension every load/store indexes with a constant -> `hls.array_partition = [{kind = "complete", dim = d+1, factor = extent, source = "constant-index"}]`, which memory banking turns into one bank per index (BRAM when >= 1024 bits). "Constant" = an arith constant (also through index casts), an affine expression that folds once constant operands and the counters of single-trip loops are substituted (also through `affine.apply`); the accesses are those left after `--auto-pipeline-unroll`. (b)/(c) need every use of the alloca to be a plain affine/memref load or store (an array that escapes into a call, a cast or a view is left alone); (a) does not look at the accesses. Arrays with a user `array_partition` / `array_reshape` / `bind_storage` / `stream` pragma, function arguments and globals are left alone. "Registers" is the unit attribute `hls.registers` (+ `hls.registers_reason`) on the alloca: `--bram-analysis` reports status `registers`, 0 BRAM, RAM type `none`; the bank classification gives it `StorageMode::Registers` and the scheduler links no port resource to its accesses (no II limit). `auto-partitions=true` applies (a)-(c) under `td=auto` as well, before the port-pressure inference (Vitis does them in both modes); off by default so the default output does not change. Several constant-indexed dimensions on one array are all recorded, but banking applies only the first entry (none in the harness subset: statistic `arrays-constant-index-multi` = 0).
- **Why:** VITIS_NOTES.md N7, `~/hls-est-work/exp-notes/partition.md` R5 + `partition_chk.md` R5 (Vitis 2025.1, td=off): `[HLS 214-421]` fires iff <= 4 elements (small_w32_n4 / small_w8_n4 / small_2d_2x2 vs small_w32_n5 / small_w8_n5 / small_2d_4x4; unconditional: partition_chk_small_n4_nopipe), `[HLS 214-449]` iff every access of the dimension is constant (ci2d_cc_64x8 / 64x16 / 8x64, partition_chk_ci2d_cc_64x64 = 64 banks, no cap; negative controls ci2d_64x8 and partition_chk_ci2d_mixed -> RAM_1WNR 7 copies = 14), all-constant arrays have no memory row and no alloca in `a.o.1.ll` (ci_both_n8..n256), ci_rd_n64 stays one RAM. Corpus evidence: polybench gesummv `hls_compile.log:52-53` `[HLS 214-449] Automatically partitioning array 'buff_A' / 'buff_B' dimension 2 completely based on constant index` (64 + 64 BRAM); replication.md R5 (rep_atax_A_R08 / R64 `log:47-49` 214-449 on buff_A).
- **Effect:**
  - *Default pipeline (td=auto):* unchanged. Harness subset (scalar shim, `-j 8 --timeout 120`, MLIR reused with `--mlir-from`, runs `~/hls-est-work/t5/runs/`): identical total on every design, 35 / 77 exact, |err| 14546 before and after; no-split pipeline identical except two designs that hit the 120 s limit in one run and not in the other under load (forgebench conv_block_module 13736, diff_dims_p3 384); test/hls-est/Estimation (42 inputs): stdout byte-identical under the default and the no-split pipeline; the 198 kernels below: default and no-split totals identical to the pre-T5 binary.
  - *td=off vs no split (harness subset, 77 designs both compare):* exact 41 -> 41, |err| 17232 -> 17224. Changed designs (per array): polybench gesummv 20 -> 134 (Vitis 135: buff_A 8 -> 64 = Vitis 64, buff_B 8 -> 64 = 64, tmp1 / tmp2 1 -> 2 = 2: with the banks lp1 / lp3 run at II 1 instead of 32, as in Vitis (`Final II = 1`), so the `tmp[i] +=` update is two ports in one cycle (RAM_2P); buff_x 1 vs 2 is O3); polybench atax 11 -> 68 and bicg 12 -> 69 (Vitis 13 / 14): buff_A 8 -> 64 banks, because `--auto-pipeline-unroll` unrolls lp4 / lp2 (float invariant load treated as imperfect, O13) where Vitis flattens lp3_lp4 / lp1_lp2 and keeps j a variable. With `--auto-pipeline-unroll=float-loads-perfect=true` (MEMOS M8) atax 12 / bicg 13 under both and only gesummv changes: |err| 17230 -> 17116, exact 41 -> 41. Arrays newly reported as `registers`: 317 debug records in the td=off run, equal to the pass statistic (corrected by the T5 review fixes entry: this line said 175, from a run in which designs timed out; (b) 4 arrays: atax, bicg, gesummv x2), every one already 0 BRAM (mostly unnamed cgeist scalar allocas); none changed a total. Default pipeline vs td=off: 35 -> 41 exact, |err| 14546 -> 17224 (the split pass's partitions cancel part of the forgebench over-estimates; MEMOS M1). Gates polybench_pragmas bicg 8, gesummv 16, mvt 8, syrk 32 exact in all six runs (default, no split, td=off, each with and without float-loads-perfect).
  - *Kernels* (cgeist + scalar shim, Vitis td=off totals from `~/hls-est-work/experiments/*/result.json`; script output `~/hls-est-work/t5/kern_all.{json,txt}`): partition topic 136 kernels exact default 116 / no split 119 / **td=off 126** (|err| 290 / 209 / 179); partition_chk 3 / 3 / **4** of 4. Named: small_w32_n4 0 (registers), small_w32_n5 0 (RAM row, LUTRAM rule), ci2d_cc_64x8 8 (8 x 64x32 @ 1), ci2d_cc_64x16 16, ci_both_n64 0 (registers), ci_rd_n64 not partitioned (one RAM; 1 vs Vitis 2: the two constant reads share a state in Vitis, O3), p2d_cyc4_d1 8 (user pragma, 4 banks), ci2d_64x8 14 (RAM_1WNR, not partitioned). The 10 partition misses left under td=off are outside this rule set: bind_storage ram_1p / ram_s2p at 2 reads (2), ci_dot_n32 / n64, ci1d_fixed_n64, ci_rd_n64 (O3), array_reshape (2), `complete dim=0` (64 vs 0). Replication kernels with T2's inputs and pipeline (`~/hls-est-work/t2/mlir_vitis`, `--debug-unroll`): 13 / 13 = Vitis with and without `td=off` (td=auto: 11 / 13). On plain cgeist output of the 58 replication kernels td=off = no split on exact count (19 / 58, |err| 3306 -> 3142): rep_atax_A_R64 / Ax_R64 / Ax_rmw_R64 / nolp3 now within 1 (64-bank buff_A = Vitis 214-449); rep_atax_A_R08 / Ax_R08 65 / 66 vs Vitis 9 / 11: Vitis keeps only the 8 banks that are read (dead banks; `--memory-banking-bram --dead-memory-elimination` gives 9 / 10); rep_nonflat_R08 / R64 get a 64-bank `tmp` because cgeist hoists lp3 out of lp2 (O5).
  - Lit test/hls-est: 57 passed (54 + 3 new), 1 expectedly failed, 24 unresolved, 4 failed (the pre-existing CHECK-BRAM ones). `check_debug_invariance.sh` with the td=off pipeline: 38 PASS / 0 FAIL / 4 ERR (the pre-existing parse failures).
- **Not adopted / for Peter:** the default stays `td=auto`; `--infer-vitis-split-array=td=off` (with M8's `float-loads-perfect=true`) is the alternative to dropping the pass in MEMOS M1.
- **Reverted?:** no (opt-in)

## 2026-10-10 — hls-est: T4 review fixes (DME on branches, ROM tables without `constant`, m_axi through calls, x/0 folds per operator, harness binary label, dead-chain reasons)
- **Commit:** c6e42c3b3 (branch `hls-est-vitis-match`); this hash line: the following changelog-only commit
- **Files:** lib/Dialect/Resource/Passes/DeadMemoryElimination.cpp (roots, `Liveness::survives` / `closeOverSurvivors`, `eliminateOnce`, `DeathNotes`, `deadChain`), lib/Dialect/Resource/Passes/BRAMAnalysis.cpp (`neverWritten`, ROM condition, `scanArgUses`, `AdapterRecord::unresolvedCall`), lib/Dialect/Resource/Passes/VitisFold.cpp; test/hls-est/Passes/{dme_cfg,bram_rom_initialised_global,maxi_adapter_call}.mlir (new), vitis_fold.mlir, dme_attention_int.mlir; tools/hls-est-validate/{common.py,run_hlsest.py,experiment_pipeline.py,make_sheet.py}, VITIS_NOTES.md N1a, OPEN_ISSUES.md O4 / O16, MEMOS.md M5 addendum; kernels `~/hls-est-work/exp-src/t4fix/{dg_d_fold_divrem0,dg_d_fold_divrem0b,mx_call_rw,mx_call_rw2}.cpp`
- **What:**
  - (D1) `--dead-memory-elimination`: every terminator of a function block (func.return and the cf.br / cf.cond_br / cf.switch of a multi-block body, e.g. an inlined callee with an early return) is a liveness root, so branch conditions and the successor operands that feed block arguments are live. The erase step no longer keeps "a side-effect-free op with a live user" while erasing its operands: after the stand-ins, everything an op that stays still uses is made live with the normal propagation (`closeOverSurvivors`).
  - (D2) `--bram-attribute-globals`: an initialised global is a ROM when it is `constant` *or* none of its get_global sites (or views of them) is written, freed or escapes (cgeist emits `static const T t[] = {...}` as a private global with a dense initial value and no `constant`).
  - (L1) `--bram-maxi-adapters`: an m_axi argument passed to a function defined in the module is followed into the callee's argument; only a call without a body is still assumed to read and write it (debug note and JSON `unresolved_call`).
  - (L2) `--vitis-fold` folds only `divsi / divui / remsi / remui` by a constant 0 (ceildivsi / ceildivui / floordivsi dropped: no C expression produces them with a zero divisor); the header documents the measured Vitis result per operator.
  - (L3) harness: `_meta.hls_est_git` is the repo HEAD only for the repo's own build; an `HLS_EST_BIN` binary is labelled from a `<binary>.git` sidecar or as unknown, and `_meta.hls_est_sha256` records the binary's hash (`common.hls_est_identity`; also in the sheet's run-config tab and in the `experiment_pipeline.py` cache key when `HLS_EST_BIN` is set). New `run_hlsest.py --mlir-from <result json>` reuses an earlier run's MLIR files instead of running cgeist, for comparisons on byte-identical inputs.
