  - (L4) the `eliminated_dead` reason lists the chain of ops: the first load of the array and the def-use path to where its value stops mattering (unused result, store into another dead array, a loop-carried value no live op uses), including the deleted overwritten store that cut it (e.g. Q: `affine.load -> arith.extsi -> ... -> arith.addi [its store to 'scores' at :131 is overwritten by the store at :143 before any read] -> out of affine.for -> stored into 'scores_tmp' (dead)`), and the number of overwritten stores of the array itself.
- **Why:** review of 77e9ca1fa (D1, D2, L1-L4). Vitis evidence: rom2r_int `tbl_ROM_AUTO_1R` 1 BRAM (`/mnt/d/HLS/hls-est-vitis/exp/t4rev_rom2r_int/.../rom2r_int_csynth.rpt`); new kernels (VITIS_NOTES.md N1a): `mx_call_rw` / `mx_call_rw2` (ports used only inside `inline off` callees: mem3 read-only 2, mem2 write-only 0, total 3), `dg_d_fold_divrem0` (int x/0, x%0, unsigned x/0 -> undef: outputs and both arrays removed; unsigned x%0 -> 0; total 6) and `dg_d_fold_divrem0b` (int 7/0, 7%0, 7u/0 -> `store i32 undef` in the frontend; ap_fixed<16,5> variable and constant x/0 -> 0; total 2).
- **Effect:** default pipeline and every opt-in configuration re-measured on the T4 runs' MLIR (`--mlir-from`, runs `~/hls-est-work/t4fix/runs/`, MEMOS.md M5 addendum): no total changes on any design, under the scalar shim (default 35 / 77 exact, |err| 14546; no split 41 / 78, 20600; with-dme 36 / 79 and 41 / 80) and the exactclass shim (fold + DME + adapters: no split 49 / 79, |err| 3765; default 41 / 78, 2404; + all: 49 / 80 and 42 / 79); the 10 design/run pairs that timed out under load were re-run with 900 s and are identical. Gates polybench_pragmas bicg 8, gesummv 16, mvt 8, syrk 32 exact in all eight runs. Kernels: the review's cfg / cfg2 / cfg_inline3 (`null operand found`) now 1 BRAM with the array kept; rom2r_int with `--bram-attribute-globals` 2 -> 1 (= Vitis), rom3r_int 2 -> 1 (Vitis 4: II raised instead of replication, OPEN_ISSUES O4); maxi_call 2 -> 0; mx_call_rw2 3 = Vitis; dg_d_fold_divrem0 12 with fold + DME (Vitis 6, OPEN_ISSUES O16). test/hls-est/Estimation (42 inputs, default pipeline): reports byte-identical to the T4 binary. `check_debug_invariance.sh`: 38 PASS / 0 FAIL / 4 ERR (the same parse failures) with the default and the full T4 pipeline. Lit test/hls-est: 54 passed (51 + 3 new), 1 expectedly failed, 24 unresolved, 4 failed (the pre-existing CHECK-BRAM ones).
- **Reverted?:** no (all behaviour opt-in; `DEFAULT_PIPELINE`, lit RUN-line pipelines and pass defaults unchanged)

## 2026-10-10 — hls-est: m_axi adapter cost (`--bram-maxi-adapters`, off by default) + 14 Vitis kernels
- **Commit:** 77e9ca1fa (code, tests, notes and these four entries; branch `hls-est-vitis-match`); this hash line: the following changelog-only commit
- **Files:** lib/Dialect/Resource/Passes/BRAMAnalysis.cpp (`collectAdapters`, `scanArgUses`, `fifoBlocksNarrow`, adapter records in the text / JSON debug output), test/hls-est/Passes/maxi_adapter.mlir; tools/hls-est-validate/compare.py (Vitis `<bundle>_m_axi` instance rows listed as arrays), VITIS_NOTES.md N1a "Interfaces"; kernels `~/hls-est-work/exp-src/maxi/mx_*.cpp`
- **What:** for every public function, the `hls.interface` arguments with mode = "m_axi" are grouped by bundle (default `gmem`). A bundle with a load gets the read FIFO cost: depth D = max_read_burst_length x num_read_outstanding (defaults 16 x 16), bus W = max(32, element bits), D <= 32 -> 0, else the x18 bands of N8 (ceil(W/18) up to 1024, ceil(W/9) up to 2048, 8 for W = 32 at 4096). A bundle with a store and max_write_burst_length > 16 gets ceil(W/18). Each bundle is a pseudo-memref (kind `m_axi_adapter`, varname `<bundle>_m_axi`) in the debug output, one `m_axi.adapter ...` line on stdout (no `kind=` token, so report parsers skip it) and part of the total. Behind the plain cl flag `--bram-maxi-adapters` (Peter decides defaults; MEMOS.md M5).
- **Why:** dead_globals.md R8 (12 kernels) plus 14 new single-variable kernels (VITIS_NOTES.md N1a, `~/hls-est-work/experiments/mx_*/<hash>/report/<kernel>_csynth.rpt:78`): read depth 2048 -> 4; 40 / 48 / 64 (two factorisations) -> 2; ap_int<16> at burst 256 -> 8; long long at burst 64 -> 4; write bursts 32 / 128 / 256 -> 2; 64-bit write -> 4; num_write_outstanding 32 at burst 16 -> 0 and 1 at burst 64 -> 2; read + write burst 64 on one bundle -> 4; two ports without `bundle=` -> one gmem adapter, 2. Every forgebench top in results/vitis.json has `mem1_m_axi_U` = 2 (45 designs).
- **Effect:** cgeist output of all 27 kernels (`~/hls-est-work/t4/maxi/`): adapter BRAM equal per bundle 27 / 27, design totals equal 27 / 27. Harness subset with the flag alone: scalar shim (`maxi_nos`): exact 39 / 73 -> 39 / 73 on the pre-T4 designs, |err| 4283 -> 4327 (every forgebench top +2, none becomes exact: 2x widths, O6). Exact shim: no split 42 / 73 -> 44 / 73 (|err| 1439 -> 1437, `x_new_nos` -> `x_maxi_nos`), default pipeline 36 / 72 -> 38 / 72 (2066 -> 2062): activation_module / op2 / op3 and diff_dims_module_small newly exact; mult_op_p2 and vec_mtx_p1 no longer (they matched only by cancellation with the missing adapter: two globals Vitis does not keep as memories, OPEN_ISSUES O17 items 2-3). All adapter rows compare.py lists for designs hls-est finishes equal Vitis.
- **Reverted?:** no (opt-in)

## 2026-10-10 — hls-est: globals as locals (`--promote-globals-to-allocas`, `--bram-attribute-globals`, both opt-in)
- **Commit:** 77e9ca1fa (branch `hls-est-vitis-match`)
- **Files:** lib/Dialect/Resource/Passes/PromoteGlobalsToAllocas.cpp (new), include/circt/Dialect/Resource/Passes/VitisCleanup.h (new), lib/Dialect/Resource/Passes/BRAMAnalysis.cpp (`collectAccessSummaries` over the get_global sites, ROM pricing, `from_global` / `rom` in the JSON), include/circt/Dialect/Resource/Interfaces/MemoryDS.h (`ROM_1P` / `ROM_2P` / `ROM_NP`), test/hls-est/Passes/{promote_globals,bram_attribute_globals}.mlir
- **What:** (1) `--promote-globals-to-allocas`: a non-constant `memref.global` that is uninitialised or all-zero and whose symbol uses are all `memref.get_global` in one function becomes a `memref.alloca` at that function's entry (`polygeist.varname` = symbol, `hls.preserve`, `hls.from_global` = symbol, the global's `hls.*` pragmas copied), so banking, the scheduler's storage modes, replication and DME treat it as a local. (2) `--bram-attribute-globals`: `--bram-analysis` attributes the accesses of the globals that stay globals through their get_global results (port demand, RAM type, replication like an alloca); a constant-initialised global is a ROM: <= 2 reads per cycle -> ROM_1P / ROM_2P at x36 (read-only ports are SDP, N8), n > 2 -> ceil(n/2) copies at x18 (ROM_NP, from the open formula; unconfirmed by a kernel). Without the flag a global is still the unattributed RAM_1P (OPEN_ISSUES O4 updated).
- **Why:** VITIS_NOTES.md N1a R6 (dg_e_globals_static = dg_e_globals_extern = dg_e_locals = 4 BRAM, same cores; a write-only global is removed at a.o.1); N8 ROM ports.
- **Effect:** dg_e_globals_static / extern / dg_e_locals stay 4 = Vitis on cgeist output (`~/hls-est-work/t4/dg/`). Harness subset, no split, scalar shim (`glob_nos`): exact 39 / 73 -> 39 / 73 on the pre-T4 designs, |err| 4283 -> 6112; `--bram-attribute-globals` alone (`attr_nos`): 4283 -> 4313 (globals read and written in one pipelined loop become RAM_2P at x18, 32-bit under the scalar shim). Per array (OPEN_ISSUES O17): weight arrays of callees Vitis keeps as modules get RAM_1WNR copies (attention_op_p2/p3 BRAM_weights_q/k/v 4 -> 60 each under the exact shim, Vitis 4), two-dimension partition pragmas are banked on one dimension (vec_mtx_p1), callee-parameter pragmas are missing after inlining (mult_op_p2). With exact widths the promotion adds one exact design over folds + DME + adapters, by cancellation (attn_breakdown_op1, default pipeline; MEMOS M5).
- **Reverted?:** no (opt-in; adoption waits for O10 / O17, MEMOS M5)

## 2026-10-10 — hls-est: Vitis integer folds (`--vitis-fold`) and dead-memory elimination (`--dead-memory-elimination`), both opt-in
- **Commit:** 77e9ca1fa (branch `hls-est-vitis-match`)
- **Files:** lib/Dialect/Resource/Passes/{VitisFold,DeadMemoryElimination}.cpp (new), lib/Dialect/Resource/Passes/CMakeLists.txt, tools/hls-est/main.cpp (`registerVitisCleanupPasses`), lib/Dialect/Resource/Passes/BRAMAnalysis.cpp (`hls.dead_memrefs` -> debug records with status `eliminated_dead`), test/hls-est/Passes/{vitis_fold,dead_memory_elimination,dme_attention_int}.mlir, tools/hls-est-validate/experiment_pipeline.py (ABLATION rows `with-dme`, `with-dme-no-split`, `with-dme-passes-only-no-split`, `with-canon-sccp-no-split`, `with-maxi-no-split`, `with-globals-no-split`, `with-call-latency-no-split`)
- **What:** `--vitis-fold` rewrites integer `divsi / divui / remsi / remui / ceildivsi / ceildivui / floordivsi` by a constant 0 to the constant 0 and applies all canonicalization patterns and folders in the same greedy rewrite (`canonicalize=false`: only x/0); floats are not folded (IEEE). `--dead-memory-elimination` is a module-wide aggressive DCE over memory objects (alloca / alloc / global with its get_global sites and views): roots are returns, calls, stores to arguments or unknown memory, unknown-effect ops and escaping objects (passed to a call, stored, yielded, a global used by several functions); a live load makes its object live, a live object makes its writers live, loop iter_args and if results are tracked per index (dead ones become pass-throughs / 0); then every non-live op is erased. Before each liveness round it deletes stores to non-escaping objects that a later store with the same access overwrites before any read, in the same block or after the enclosing loops / ifs; loads of the same element that only feed its own stores (a promotable read-modify-write accumulator) do not count as reads. The accesses must be equal, so there is no cross-loop DSE (R2). `hls.preserve` does not keep a dead array; eliminated arrays are recorded in the module attribute `hls.dead_memrefs` and listed by `--bram-debug` / `--bram-debug-json` (status `eliminated_dead`, reason).
- **Why:** VITIS_NOTES.md N1, N1a (dead_globals.md R1 write-only / unreached / dead value / x*0, R2 no cross-loop DSE, R5 `1/0 -> 0` and the attention chain, R6 globals). The overwritten-store rule is what LLVM's LICM promotion + DSE do to `scores[i][j] += q*k; scores[i][j] *= 0` (dg_d_apfixed16 a.o.1.ll:1171 `store i16 0`, no Q/K loads).
- **Effect:** lit `dme_attention_int.mlir` (integer attention, scale = divsi(4194304, 0)): Q, K and their MACs removed, 3 BRAM vs 7 for the non-zero-scale control. dead_globals kernels on cgeist output: 13 / 22 exact with and without the cleanups (cgeist already deletes the C-int dead arrays; the 9 misses are R3/R4 narrowing (O18), dg_d_int3 replication, dg_d_apfixed16 (f32 under the scalar shim, O16) and dg_f_twice_off (O10)). Harness subset: `--vitis-fold --canonicalize --sccp --dead-memory-elimination` alone changes no total under the scalar (`dmeonly_nos`) and exact (`x_dmeonly_nos`) shims. Under the exactclass shim (`xc_dmeonly_nos`): attention_op_p1 203 -> 71, tiled_attn_p1 203 -> 71, tiled_attn_p2 55 -> 21, tiled_attn_module 304 -> 108 (|err| on the pre-T4 designs 1390 -> 1232); with `--bram-maxi-adapters` (`xc_dmemaxi_nos`) exact 42 / 73 -> 47 / 73, |err| 1390 -> 1210 (attention_op_p1 73, tiled_attn_p1 73, tiled_attn_p2 23, activation_module / op2 / op3, diff_dims_module_small newly exact; mult_op_p2, mult_op_p3, vec_mtx_p1 lose their adapter cancellation), default pipeline (`xc_dmemaxi_def`) 36 / 72 -> 39 / 72, 2011 -> 1759. forgebench attention_op_p1: scalar shim 406 -> 408 (only the adapter), `--shim exact` 203 -> 205 (f16: 1/sqrt(16) = 0.25 - nothing to fold, Q and K live), `exactclass` shim (integer ap_fixed, `--scalar-wrapper-abi`, c733e425e): 203 -> 73 = Vitis per array (Q, K, BRAM_weights_q, BRAM_weights_k eliminated; BRAM_attn_input 2, BRAM_weights_v 64, BRAM_1 2, V 2, scores 1, mem1 adapter 2). dg_d_apfixed16 under exactclass + the cleanups: 2 = Vitis (V 1, scores 1; Q, K eliminated; it needed the O9 fallback of the robustness entry). tiled_attn_module stays off (108 + 2 vs 270): Vitis keeps four arrays filled by non-inlined `load_*` calls as 0R0W memories (N1a, O16).
- **Reverted?:** no (opt-in). Replaced within the change: an overwritten-store rule that treated every read inside the loop as blocking (it missed the memory-carried accumulator of the exactclass output), and a chain test that let a yielded value stay inside the accumulator chain (lit `@rmw_yield_escapes`).

## 2026-10-10 — hls-est: robustness (pointer element types, scf.if hoisting with results, region captures in the schedule, calls in loops, `--inline`)
- **Commit:** 77e9ca1fa (branch `hls-est-vitis-match`)
- **Files:** lib/Dialect/Resource/Passes/MemrefBankClassification.cpp (`enumerateBanks`), lib/Dialect/Resource/Passes/BRAMAnalysis.cpp (status `excluded_type`, index width), lib/Dialect/Resource/Passes/AffineToLoopScheduleModified.cpp (`hoistIfBodies`, `yieldOpLegalityCallback`, `anchorRegionCaptures`, user ancestor in the stage plumbing, `--loopschedule-call-latency`), tools/hls-est/{main.cpp,CMakeLists.txt} (func inliner extension), lib/Scheduling/SimplexSchedulers.cpp, tools/hls-est-validate/common.py (`HLS_EST_BIN` override), test/hls-est/Passes/{robust_inputs,simplex_fallback}.mlir
- **What:** (a) a `memref<memref<?xi32>>` (C pointer variable) or other non-int/float element no longer asserts in the bank classification (skipped) and is reported by `--bram-analysis` as `excluded_type` with the reason; index elements are 64-bit words. (b) The scf.if hoisting ran as a pattern inside the dialect conversion; it now runs after it (same result), and an affine.yield is converted to scf.yield only when its immediate parent is an scf.if. (c) The cause of sobel's "null operand found": the yield of a hoisted scf.if uses values that are not operands of the if, so the scheduler could start the if before, or in another stage than, those values; the problem now gets an auxiliary dependence from each captured value's producer to the region op, and the stage plumbing registers a value up to the stage of the region op that uses it. (d) `--loopschedule-call-latency=N` (default -1 = old behaviour: the loop stays sequential) gives a call without memref operands an operator type of latency N. (e) `--inline` works (func inliner extension registered; it aborted before). (f) OPEN_ISSUES O9: `ModuloSimplexScheduler::scheduleOperation` / `updateMargins` (lib/Scheduling/SimplexSchedulers.cpp) return a failure instead of asserting when an II bump leaves an infeasible tableau, and `--convert-affine-to-loopschedule` then falls back to the greedy scheduler. Non-affine loops inside a function were already sequential scopes since T3; lit `robust_inputs.mlir` pins it.
- **Why:** harness failures: forgebench conv_block_module / conv_block_op1-3 / conv_module (`isIntOrFloat` assertion in `enumerateBanks`), spector_hls sobel_filter_x / sobel_filter_y and Estimation spectorhls/sobel_filter_y (null operand), the O9 simplex assertion (viterbi under the exact shims, and post-DME IR under exactclass).
- **Effect:** default pipeline (scalar shim): exact 33 / 72 -> 35 / 77, identical on the 72 designs compared before (|err| 5023 = 5023); newly compared: sobel_filter_x 0 and sobel_filter_y 0 (= Vitis), conv_block_op2 8901, conv_block_op3 9622, conv_module 1769 (Vitis 4796 / 4931 / 1042); conv_block_module and conv_block_op1 now hit the 120 s limit instead of asserting. No split: 39 / 73 -> 41 / 79, identical on the old designs except diff_dims_p3, which hit the 120 s limit under load in this run (384 in every other T4 run); conv_block_module 13736, conv_block_op1 7265, conv_block_op2 11717, conv_block_op3 9094, conv_module 2937 (Vitis 5139 / 3897 / 4796 / 4931 / 1042: 2x widths, O6). Simplex fallback: no scalar-shim design reached the assertion; under `--shim exact` and exactclass machsuite viterbi is now 128 = Vitis (lit `simplex_fallback.mlir`, the unchanged exact-shim cgeist output). `--loopschedule-call-latency=1` (`call_nos`): no total changes. test/hls-est/Estimation (42 inputs, base pipeline): 41 reports byte-identical, sobel_filter_y error -> 0. `check_debug_invariance.sh`: 38 PASS, 0 FAIL, 4 ERR (was 37 / 0 / 5), also with the T4 pipeline. Lit test/hls-est: 51 passed (43 + 8 new), 1 expectedly failed, 24 unresolved, 4 failed (all pre-existing CHECK-BRAM). Regression gates polybench_pragmas bicg 8, gesummv 16, mvt 8, syrk 32 exact in all 25 T4 runs.
- **Reverted?:** no

## 2026-10-10 — Route A, opt-in: cgeist `--scalar-wrapper-abi` + harness shim `exactclass` (frontend + harness)
- **Commit:** Polygeist 8b07bf43 (branch `hls-est-vitis-match`, repo root `/mnt/d/HLS`): cgeist + lit tests; `~/circt`: the commit "hls-est-validate: exactclass shim (opt-in), shim-specific cgeist flags" that carries this entry
- **Files:** Polygeist `tools/cgeist/Lib/{clang-mlir.cc,clang-mlir.h,CGCall.cc,CGStmt.cc}` (+437/-29), `tools/cgeist/Test/HLS/wrapper/` (10 tests + `Inputs/mini_fixed.h`); `~/circt` `tools/hls-est-validate/run_hlsest.py`, `check_shim.py` (new), `shim/exactclass/` (new: `ap_int.h`, `ap_fixed.h`, `hls_math.h`, `hls_half.h`, `cgeist_flags`, `README.md`, stand-ins copied from `shim/exact`), `OPEN_ISSUES.md` (O15), `MEMOS.md` (M3)
- **What:**
  1. *cgeist, opt-in* (`--scalar-wrapper-abi`, default **off**; design `~/hls-est-work/feas/A/design.md` section 4). A record whose only data member, through a chain of single-base classes without members of their own, is one scalar (integer, `_BitInt`, enum, floating; not a bit-field/reference; no union/lambda/polymorphic/virtual base/packed/aligned) is lowered to that scalar: `ap_fixed<16,5>` over `ap_fixed_base { _BitInt(16) V; }` is `i16`, `this`/references `memref<?xi16>`, arrays `memref<8x16xi16>`. Structural rule, no names. Under the option: `getScalarWrapperField` (memoised) + `getMLIRType` hook; `CommonFieldLookup` identity; base/derived casts identity without null check; `VisitConstructCommon` trivial copy/move = load+store, value-init = zero store, one `scf.for` per dimension of a constant array of class objects (the old code passed the whole sub-array as `this`: the "operand type mismatch" of 84 HLSFactory designs); trivial copy-assignment = load+store; clang constant evaluation of const variables (`static const int F = W - I` reads as a constant) and of global initialisers (dense initial values); explicit width conversion of integer call arguments / return values; `__builtin_bit_cast` between equal-width scalars; the C-math name lowering only for floating arguments (so `hls::exp(ap_fixed)` stays a call). Every changed path is gated: with the option off the previous code runs.
  2. *Harness:* `run_hlsest.py` appends the flags in `<shim dir>/cgeist_flags` (if the file exists) to the cgeist command and adds them to the MLIR cache key only when present (keys of `exact`/`scalar`/`polygeist` unchanged: checked on 40 designs x 3 shims). `shim/exactclass/cgeist_flags` = `--scalar-wrapper-abi`.
  3. *Shim `exactclass`* (harness-local; replacing Polygeist's `Test/HLS/Inputs/vitis` is Peter's call): `ap_int_base<W,S>` / `ap_fixed_base<W,I,S,Q,O,N>` whose single member is `_BitInt(W)` / `unsigned _BitInt(W)`, `= default` ctors, Vitis `RType` result widths, all Q modes and `AP_WRAP`/`AP_SAT`/`AP_SAT_ZERO`/`AP_SAT_SYM`, bit-exact int/float/double conversions (IEEE mantissa decode via `__builtin_bit_cast`, floor for `AP_TRN`), value-returning `range()`/`operator()`/`operator[]`/`bit()`/`(a, b)`, `hls::sqrt` (negative -> 0, round-half-up) and the other `hls::` functions over `ap_fixed`, `ap_ufixed`, `ap_int`, `ap_uint` and `ap_fixed_base`. `hls::exp` is floor(exp)·2^F with Vitis' saturation, not the Vitis table algorithm: `etc/hls_exp_apfixed.h` is "All Rights Reserved" in the install and absent from the Apache-2.0 `HLS_arbitrary_Precision_Types` repository (checked: that repo has only the ap_int/ap_fixed headers). Headers from the interrupted route-A attempt (`~/hls-est-work/routeA/shim/exactclass`), reviewed; comments corrected (`ap_int<1>` is stored as `_BitInt(2)`; exp differs by one LSB in either direction).
- **Why:** CLAUDE.md step 2.1/2.2 (exact `ap_fixed` constants reach MLIR as integers) and MEMOS M3 (route A, opt-in). Evidence for the semantics: `result_read_headers.md` sections 1-2 (g++ + Vitis headers, Vitis csynth `a.o.3.ll`).
- **Effect:**
  - *Option-off identity* (control = previous binary `bin/cgeist.prev-e5b6bbc9` = `cgeist.pre-routeA-193707`, new = Polygeist 8b07bf43): every cgeist RUN line of `tools/cgeist/Test/HLS` (957 invocations in 857 files incl. the 776 hlsfactory `.test`s; 11.4 MB of output) identical; the cgeist command of all 783 harness designs with `--shim exact` (1067 invocations, 682 MLIR files, 7.1 MB) and `--shim scalar` (1071 invocations, 682 MLIR files, 7.0 MB): same exit code, stdout and MLIR, stderr equal after masking crash stack frames, assertion line numbers in clang-mlir.cc (they move with the inserted code) and the order of `#pragma HLS` warnings (that order differs between two runs of the *same* binary, shown on Ray-tracing). Normalised in all comparisons: order of `dlti.dl_spec` entries and heap addresses (both vary run to run). 10 scalar-shim invocations first differed only because the control binary timed out (600 s, `/mnt/d` I/O contention); re-run: identical.
  - *Lit* (`--filter HLS/ --filter-out hlsfactory`): 78 passed / 5 failed = the 68 existing + 10 new wrapper tests passing, the same 5 pre-existing failures (array_partition/lowering, consumer/hls-est-banking, consumer/hls-est-pipeline, pipeline/lowering, polybench/hls-est-e2e). Control binary: 68 + same 5 (the wrapper tests need the option).
  - *Bit-exactness* (`check_shim.py`, clang++ 18 + shim vs g++ + `/mnt/a/Vitis/2025.1/Vitis/include`): `--seed 11 --n 40` 67 113 values, `--seed 12 --n 150` 71 293 values (incl. 65 536 exhaustive `ap_fixed<16,5>` sqrt, 57 design-table constants): **0 mismatches**; `hls::exp` 1 of 240 / 10 of 900 differ by one LSB (documented deviation). *(Corrected by the route A review fixes entry: this compiled the shim with the host clang++ only. Through cgeist the MLIR was not bit-exact - Polygeist's `SubToAdd` on integers wider than 64 bits - and the shim itself differed for `ap_int` from a double >= 2^24 and for the signed 1-bit types.)*
  - *cgeist on the attention kernel*: `k.cpp` / `k_ctrl.cpp` and forgebench `attention_op_p1` with `--scalar-wrapper-abi` + `exactclass`: rc 0, `memref<8x16xi16>` / `memref<8x256xi16>` / `memref<256x256xi16>`, 0 `llvm.struct`, 0 `memref2pointer`, integer `extsi/shli/muli/shrsi/trunci/divsi` arithmetic (f64 only inside the `hls::sqrt`/`hls::exp` bodies, folded for constants); `k.cpp` keeps `arith.divsi %c4194304_i128, %c0_i128` for `1.0/sqrt((data_t)16)` (the Vitis-undef fold is track T8). Current hls-est on them: k 1 BRAM (Vitis 0), k_ctrl 1 (Vitis 6).
  - *Harness* (7 datasets forgebench, soda, machsuite, spector_hls, vitis_examples, accelerators, class_ece8893_spring_2025 = 208 designs; `-j 8 --timeout 120 --force-cgeist`, default pipeline, hls-est pinned to a copy of `build/clion/bin/hls-est` md5 4e2680bd (circt 6d7d92178); `/tmp/routeA.json` vs `/tmp/routeA-exact.json`): ok 95 (`exact`) -> 92 (`exactclass`); *(corrected by the route A review fixes entry: about 97 -> 92, see the next correction)* compare.py exact 31 -> **32** (forgebench mult_op_p3 2 = 2; it crashed hls-est with `exact`), 60 designs compared on each side; on the 57 compared by both, exact 31 = 31, sum |err| 1911 -> 1750 (Llama_GPT_module 0 -> 31 (Vitis 30), gpt_transformer_p1 0 -> 26 (28), conv_module 757 -> 1141 (1042), conv_block_op2 4452 -> 5220 (4796); element widths are 16 bits in both shims, so these move with the schedule of the integer op mix, not diagnosed per array). Newly ok: forgebench conv_block_op1 (3825, Vitis 3897), diff_dims_p3 (192, 354), mult_op_p3, class anirudh_tulasi_lab2. *(Corrected by the route A review fixes entry: conv_block_op1 and diff_dims_p3 are not route-A effects. Their `exact` control run hit the 120 s hls-est limit under load; unloaded, `--shim exact` gives the same 3825 and 192.)* No longer ok: forgebench attn_breakdown_module / op1 / op2 (11 / 9 / 11, Vitis 17 / 10 / 17 -> `ModuloSimplexScheduler` assertion `succeeded(solved)`, the O9 class), class adithi_upadhya_lab2 (banking-factor assertion), lab2_hanchen and matrix_methods..._attention (hls-est timeout 120 s), sahith_lab2_2025 (clang: `cond ? ap_fixed<16,5> : ap_fixed<32,8>` is ambiguous; g++ with the real Vitis headers rejects it too; no Vitis ground truth). cgeist status: crash 16 -> 5 (soda's 11 crashes become clean clang errors), 23 designs (14 with ground truth) stop at an assignable range/bit select (O15; all 23 failed with `exact` too), 3 class designs that failed with `exact` now reach Polygeist's canonicalizer and time out at 600 s (`AffineBufferElimination` in `PolygeistCanonicalize`).
  - *Build:* objects with ninja; link = ninja's command with `-o` on ext4 and the 316 llvm-project archives read from an ext4 copy (`/home/peter/tmp/ra2-llvmlib`, 17 GB, copied once in 264 s): **32 s** instead of 16-66 min (the unmodified command reading the archives from `/mnt/d` was started for comparison and killed after 66 min under I/O contention). Installed: busy binary renamed `bin/cgeist.prev-e5b6bbc9`, copy + `cmp`, `ninja -t restat bin/cgeist`; `cgeist.orig-hls-preserve-name` untouched.
- **Not adopted / for Peter:** the option stays off by default and the harness default shim stays `scalar`; making `exactclass` (and `--scalar-wrapper-abi`) the default, or replacing Polygeist's `Inputs/vitis`, is MEMOS M3. Assignable range references (O15) are the main coverage gap.
- **Reverted?:** no

## 2026-10-09 — hls-est-validate: N8 and visibility-tool review fixes
- **Commit:** dbb6b9550 (scripts, notes, CSVs and this entry; branch `hls-est-vitis-match`); this hash line: the following changelog-only commit
- **Files:** tools/hls-est-validate/formula_reconcile.py, align_arrays.py, vitis_trace.py, rtl_memories.py, VITIS_NOTES.md (N8; the N4 pointer line), CHANGELOG.md (this entry, the missing 3a0c04cc8 entry below, the re-synthesis count of the b93c11751 entry), results/formula_reconcile.csv, results/formula_reconcile_summary.csv
- **What:** the verifier findings on 3a0c04cc8 / b93c11751 / 6c272536e (F1-F4, T1-T5). No hls-est code or estimate changes.
  1. *F1 vector merge.* `formula_reconcile.py` merges the `<MemPortTyVec>` vectors of every module a memory is passed to: it follows the `call ... @<callee>, <args>` lines of `db/<module>.verbose.sched.rpt` from the owner module and maps argument k to port k of `db/<callee>.adb.xml` (`RunGraph`; callee names legalised: `.` -> `_`, runs of `_` -> one, trailing `_` + `s`). The old rule (owner + modules named `<owner>_*`, by array name, else every module) is kept only for run dirs without sched reports. New CSV column `owner_ops` (load/store operations of the owner's own schedule on the array) for every row the vector misses, and a miss listing with a category.
  2. *F2* N8 "Code path" is marked unconfirmed, with the reason (fr_t2p_bram_w32_d512 has per-module vectors [0 0] / [1 1]; MemoryQuerier also gives 2 for [0 0]; the DoubleKey querier doubles only for {RW,RW} on top of a closed-table value; neither observed).
  3. *F3* N8 headline with exact counts: 10 experiment misses with the binder's port types (the (d) rows), 6 + 35 with Vitis's own vectors (8 + 34 before F1); "confirmed: 24682 rows" removed.
  4. *F4* this changelog gets the missing 3a0c04cc8 entry; N8 records the licence finding (CoreQuerier.cpp: only Xilinx/AMD copyright lines, no licence text; llvm/LICENSE.TXT is NCSA; formula_reconcile.py mirrors the source and is not to be ported to hls-est C++ without Peter's decision, MEMOS.md M4).
  5. *T1* `align_arrays.report_groups` groups `* Memory:` rows by the row's own variable (BindNode VARIABLE, else the instance name) and folds a row into another array only when it is a real partition bank of a source array (`known_root` against the a.g.ld.5.gdce allocas / globals / top arguments); vitis_parse's `array` grouping is used only when there is no source stage. `rtl_groups` uses the same rule.
  6. *T2* failed Vitis runs: `first_divergence` reports `stage a.o.N missing (vitis failed: <note>)` instead of "deleted at a.o.N" when the stage file is not in the cache dir, "alive through a.o.3; no memory table to compare" / "top argument (no memory table ...)" when there is no memory table; Vitis BRAM cells are `?` (unknown) instead of `-`/0; the header, the reconcile line (`Vitis BRAM: no number (...)`), `vitis_trace.py --summary` (status line, no `*` marks, "Vitis BRAM unknown" heading, loop/memory table lines) and the bundle README print the status and note. A run that timed out after csynth (forgebench/mlp) keeps its tables and is labelled `[vitis timeout: 3600s; the memory tables exist]`.
  7. *T3* `rtl_memories.find_verilog_dir` says `not in results: ...` for an unknown key and `Vitis failed: ...` for a design whose ground-truth run has no numbers, `no RTL kept: ...` otherwise; `--make-rtl` only re-synthesizes in the last case. `matches_ground_truth` needs a ground truth with numbers, the same top (csynth.xml TopModelName), the same memory table and, when both memory tables are empty, equal csynth BRAM/DSP/FF/LUT totals; it no longer accepts any work dir with an empty table for a key with no entry.
  8. *T4* `align_arrays.bank_base` strips every trailing bank index (`BIAS_1_0_7` -> `BIAS_1` when `BIAS_1` is a source array, via `known_root`; otherwise the longest base another name shares, `sibling_base`); `assign_groups` pass 3 tries every split.
  9. *T5* the b93c11751 entry said 2 ad-hoc re-syntheses; it was 3 (bicg too). Corrected in place.
- **Why:** verifier findings. F1 evidence: conv_block_op1 `localInput_U` (maxpool_tiled) has vectors `maxpool_kernel` [1 1 1 1 3...] and `maxpool_tiled_Pipeline_VITIS_LOOP_632_4_...` [0 3 ...]; the call `call void @maxpool_kernel, i16 %localInput, i16 %localOutput` is in `db/maxpool_tiled.verbose.sched.rpt`. T1 evidence: activation_op1 `FM_buffer_1_U` / `FM_buffer_2_U` are two globals in a.g.ld.5.gdce but vitis_parse grouped them as banks 1 / 2 of `FM_buffer`.
- **Effect:** no estimate changes.
  - `formula_reconcile.py` (8 s; 702 run dirs, 0 calls with an argument/port count mismatch): results/vitis.json N6 23000, open 23003 of 23003 (unchanged); open(vec) 17300/17308 -> **22615/22621** (conv_block_op1 / conv_block_module `localInput_U` 64 -> 192 = Vitis; 5314 rows newly have a vector, all exact; mult_op_module_dot `input_U` lost a wrong all-modules vector); experiments (now 1698 rows) N6 1675, open 1688, open(vec) 1641/1676; legacy corpus reproduction unchanged (13294 / 97). 1WnR copies == n_ports - 1: 214/216 -> 216/216. SDP cross-check: 13379 of 13421 agree; the 42 others are 39 incomplete vectors and 3 ROMs. All 41 vector misses have owner_ops > 0.
  - Report grouping (`report_groups` old vs new on the 99 designs with memory rows, `cmp_groups2.py` in the session scratchpad): 33 change. 14 had real arrays folded into a fake base and are now separate: forgebench activation_module, activation_op1/op2/op3, conv_block_module, conv_block_op1/op2/op3, conv_module (`FM_buffer_N`), Llama_GPT_module, gpt_transformer_p1, llama_transformer_p2 (`BRAM_N`, `BRAM_MLP_N`, `*_norm_weights_N`, `FF_weights_N`), accelerators GP_selection_kernel_fixed_dataset / _float_dataset (`sigma_12`, `sigma_22`, `mu_2`). 19 had multi-index banks as separate report-only rows and now fold into their source array: flowgnn gin, pna; forgebench conv_A/B/C, diff_dims_p1-p3, diff_orders_p1-p3, mlp (2052 rows -> 12 groups), mult_op_p1/p2, testing_impl, testing_unroll, vec_mtx_module, vec_mtx_p1/p2.
  - `align()` on all 143 results keys: 0 errors; the csynth BRAM total of all 131 ok designs still reconciles exactly (memory rows + adapters + FIFO channels); no row says "deleted at" a stage whose file is missing (failed runs: 66 rows `stage a.o.1 missing (vitis failed: ...)`, 50 top-argument rows "no memory table to compare").
  - The six designs of the review: activation_op1 `FM_buffer_1` / `FM_buffer_2` 64 BRAM each (before: a report-only `FM_buffer` 128 plus two "no memory instance" rows), `FM_buffer_3` 48; conv_block_op1 `FM_buffer_1` / `FM_buffer_2` 1024 each; flowgnn/dgn every array `stage a.o.1 missing (vitis failed: ... Impl=uram ...)` with Vitis BRAM `?`, rtl_memories `Vitis failed: ...`; rosetta/BNN__cpp__accel "no arrays to show: IR stages kept none; no Vitis memory table (vitis failed: 'hls_video.h' file not found ...)"; forgebench/mlp (`vitis timeout`, tables kept) `BIAS_1` / `INPUT_BATCH` / `ITM_BATCH` / `RELU_BATCH` 256 BRAM each (before: 2052 report-only bank rows); polybench/gesummv unchanged (RTL `/mnt/d/HLS/hls-est-vitis/exp/gesummv`, memory table and top equal the ground truth). rtl_memories on a key not in results: `not in results: ...`; `--make-rtl` on a failed or unknown key does not start Vitis.
- **Reverted?:** no. Replaced within this change: a first F1 version that scanned every call site per row (quadratic in call arguments; killed after 4 min) and one that decoded every adb.xml whole (15 min under machine load; it hit the 900 s limit while writing --csv-all); the final version indexes call arguments and decodes only the `<ports>` slice.

## 2026-10-09 — hls-est-validate: open-source BRAM formula reconciliation (N8)
- **Commit:** 3a0c04cc8 (branch `hls-est-vitis-match`). This entry was missing; it is added by dbb6b9550 (review fixes, entry above).
- **Files:** tools/hls-est-validate/formula_reconcile.py, bind_portvec.py (new), results/formula_reconcile.csv, results/formula_reconcile_summary.csv (new), VITIS_NOTES.md (N8; one-line pointers in N4, N5, N6)
- **What:** `formula_reconcile.py` transcribes (reads, does not copy) `MemoryQuerier::queryBRAM` / `isSDPMode` / `oldBramImplNum` from hls-llvm-project tag 2025.1 (6ad94cf7d252) and evaluates it next to the measured N4/N5/N6 model on every memory row of results/vitis.json, the experiments' result.json files and the legacy 13391-row corpus (results/baseline/vitis-tdoff.json; its 13294 / 97 split is reproduced). `bind_portvec.py` collects Vitis's `<MemPortTyVec>` vectors from `db/*.verbose.bind.rpt` to feed the transcription. 18 new kernels (`~/hls-est-work/exp-src/formula`, runs `~/hls-est-work/experiments/fr_*`) decide the disagreements: no x9 stacking above 4096 words, {R,R} ROM ports are SDP (x36), n-port ROMs cost ceil(n/2) x18, explicitly bound ram_2p/ram_t2p with {R,W} or one used port are SDP. No hls-est code or estimate changes.
- **Why:** CLAUDE.md Step 1b (find what hls-est does not model); the open-source querier is Vitis's own BRAM formula, so every measured rule can be checked against it row by row.
- **Effect** (as committed): vitis.json N6 23000/23003, open 23003/23003, open(vec) 17300/17308; experiments (1679 rows then) N6 1656, open 1669, open(vec) 1639/1673. Vitis cost: 18 kernel runs. The review (entry above) corrected: the vector merge (callees dropped), the "settled by measurement" code-path claim (unconfirmed), the "confirmed: 24682 rows" headline (10 + 42 misses), and added the licence note.
- **Reverted?:** no

## 2026-10-09 — hls-est-validate: rtl_memories, align_arrays, vitis_trace visibility tools
- **Commit:** b93c11751 (scripts, N9 and this entry; branch `hls-est-vitis-match`); this hash line: the following changelog-only commit
- **Files:** tools/hls-est-validate/rtl_memories.py, align_arrays.py, vitis_trace.py (new); VITIS_NOTES.md (N9)
- **What:** stdlib-only debugging tools; hls-est and every estimate are unchanged.
  1. `rtl_memories.py <dir | ds/bench> [--all] [--json F] [--make-rtl]` reads `syn/verilog`. For each physical memory it lists core, ram/rom_style, width, depth/address bits, port groups (`RW+15R`), the RAM_1WNR storage copies with the port that writes and reads each one, the init file, and how often the parent module is instantiated. It joins the csynth memory table and compares the total. A results key only uses a leftover work dir whose memory table equals the ground truth. `--make-rtl` re-synthesizes once with `vitis_adhoc.py --keep`, because the harvest keeps no RTL.
  2. `align_arrays.py <ds/bench> [--brief] [--run-id ID]` prints one row per array across `a.g.ld.5.gdce` / `a.o.1` / `a.o.2` / `a.o.3` / scheduler allocas (`<module>.verbose.rpt`) / report / RTL / the hls-est debug JSON, with the first stage where the array diverges (deleted, partitioned into N, merged back, width W->W', ROM, RAM_1WNR copies, LUTRAM, interface). It keeps function clones apart from partition banks and matches arrays one to one. It also reconciles the csynth BRAM total as memory rows + adapters + FIFO channels. By default it reads the newest hls-est run whose passes include the default pipeline.
  3. `vitis_trace.py <ds/bench>... [--summary]` writes a bundle to `~/hls-est-work/traces/<ds>__<bench>/` containing the align table, the Vitis log decisions next to hls-est `pipelines[]`, the loop and memory tables, the hls-est debug summary, the RTL summary and trace.json. `--summary` prints at most 60 lines.
- **Why:** CLAUDE.md Step 1b and Step 3 need per-array evidence from every Vitis stage, because totals hide errors that cancel out. The previous attempt's partial scripts (interrupted at 19:40) were reviewed and finished here.
- **Effect:** no estimate changes.
  - On forgebench/attention_op_p1, polybench/gesummv and machsuite/fft_transpose, the RTL memory tables equal the ground truth (8 / 132 / 12 rows).
  - `align()` runs on all 131 ok designs in results/vitis.json with 0 errors and reconciles every csynth BRAM total exactly.
  - New Vitis facts are recorded in VITIS_NOTES N9: IR banks merged back before scheduling in fft_transpose and k3mm, the RAM_1WNR copy/port structure in the RTL, LUTRAM ROMs bound by Vitis math-header pragmas, and BRAM held by adapters and FIFO channels.
  - Vitis cost: 3 ad-hoc re-syntheses (`/mnt/d/HLS/hls-est-vitis/exp/{gesummv,fft_transpose,bicg}`) to get RTL (corrected by the review-fix entry above; this line said 2 and left out bicg).
- **Reverted?:** no

## 2026-10-09 — hls-est: T3 pipeline decision review fixes
- **Commit:** 547677b2d (code, tests, notes and this entry; branch `hls-est-vitis-match`); this hash line: the following changelog-only commit
- **Files:** lib/Dialect/Resource/Passes/AutoPipelineUnroll.cpp, include/circt/Dialect/Resource/Passes/LoopPragmas.h, lib/Dialect/Resource/Passes/AffineToLoopScheduleModified.cpp, lib/Dialect/Resource/Passes/BRAMAnalysis.cpp; new lit tests test/hls-est/Passes/{auto_pipeline_float_loads,auto_pipeline_partial_epilogue,auto_pipeline_recursion,bram_debug_pipelines}.mlir; updated test/hls-est/Passes/{auto_pipeline_inner_unrolled,auto_pipeline_inner65_flattened,auto_pipeline_multilevel,auto_pipeline_perfect_nest,auto_pipeline_siblings,auto_pipeline_threshold,auto_pipeline_user_pragma}.mlir (the new `hls.auto_unrolled` record); tools/hls-est-validate/check_pipeline_unroll.py (`pipeline_unroll_float` kernels, `--apu-opts`), VITIS_NOTES.md (N3), OPEN_ISSUES.md (O3 corrected, O13, O14), MEMOS.md (M8), CHANGELOG.md (T3 entry corrected in place)
- **What:** the four findings of the review of 22d473858 / 815b755f4.
  1. *D1, floating-point invariant loads.* New option `--auto-pipeline-unroll=float-loads-perfect` (default **off**; Peter decides defaults): `LicmModel::movableOutOf` does not hoist a read-only invariant load of a float element at decision time, and `isPerfectPair` treats such a load already sitting in the outer body (cgeist's own LICM, or the source) as if it were in the inner loop (`isSinkableFloatLoad`; not when the inner loop writes that memory). Promotion of an accumulator still makes a nest imperfect for float elements. The notes now call the LICM criterion confirmed for integer elements only and list atax lp3/lp4 and bicg lp1/lp2 as decision mismatches of the default (O13), not O3.
  2. *D2, recursion.* `unrollInside` seeds `visitedCallees` with the function that owns the pipelined loop and skips that loop and its ancestors, so a call back into it (direct or mutual recursion) can no longer collect and erase the pipelined loop (use-after-erase: `SingleBlock::getBody` / "invalid region index" assertions on the review's pB_calls / pB_mutual); `Forest::build` keeps a callee's function on the call stack while it builds the subtree of a loop reached through a call (otherwise a recursive callee reached from a caller is re-collected without end).
  3. *D3, partial-unroll epilogue.* `applyPartialUnroll`: the loop carries a temporary marker for the duration of `loopUnrollByFactor`; afterwards the main loop (step x factor) gets `hls.unroll_partial` and the cleanup clone (original step) is unrolled completely into straight-line code (Vitis has no epilogue loop, UG1399 `skip_exit_check`), so no second partially-unrolled pipeline appears. No op is touched after MLIR erased it (a single-iteration main loop is replaced by its body: trip 6 / factor 4), and the inner loop of a promoted single-iteration cleanup is not mistaken for the cleanup loop (trip 9 / factor 4). The single-iteration main loop losing its loop is O14.
  4. *Minor.* Debug record: every loop `--auto-pipeline-unroll` unrolls for a pipeline is listed on it as `hls.auto_unrolled = [{factor, loc}]`; `coalescePerfectNests` and `createLoopSchedulePipeline` carry it (with `hls.pipeline`, `hls.flattened_parents`, `hls.unroll_partial`) to the `loopschedule.pipeline` op (whose printer prints no discardable attributes); `--bram-debug` prints a `--- pipelines` section and the JSON gets a `pipelines` array (scope_loc, func, ii, trip_count, source, flattened_parents, unroll_partial, unrolled[]); additive, `version` stays 2. The T3 entry's duplicated "Commit:" parenthetical is fixed and its Effect attribution corrected in place.
- **Why:** review of 22d473858. D1 evidence (VITIS_NOTES.md N3 "Floating-point invariant loads"): 18 kernels in `~/hls-est-work/exp-src/pipeline_unroll_float`, Vitis runs under `~/hls-est-work/experiments/<kernel>/<hash>/` - flattened with a float invariant load: rv_st_hoist_f_m64_n64, rv_rmw_hoist_f_m8_n64 / _m64_n64, pf_fmul_m8_n64, pf_fadd_m8_n64, pf_fmac_m8_n32, pf_srchoist_m8_n64, pm_floatarr_iop_m8_n64; unrolled with an int one: rv_st_hoist_m64_n64, rv_rmw_hoist_m8_n64 / _m64_n64, pi_add_m8_n64, pi_mul3_m8_n64, pl_mul_m8_n64, pi_srchoist_m8_n64, pm_intarr_fop_m8_n64, pf_twoload_m8_n64 (float + int load); pf_memred_m8_n16 (promoted float accumulator) unrolled. D2/D3: the review's probes `~/hls-est-work/rv/probes/pB_{calls,mutual}.mlir`, `pA_partial.mlir`, plus `~/hls-est-work/t3fix3/pU_partial_{single,nested}.mlir`.
- **Effect** (runs and logs in `~/hls-est-work/t3r/`; before = the review's HEAD runs `~/hls-est-work/rv/harness/{nosplit,canon}.json`, same cgeist outputs, compared with `~/hls-est-work/t3/cmp_runs.py`):
  - *Default options:* harness subset **identical per design** to HEAD under both pipelines: without `--infer-vitis-split-array` 39/73 exact, |err| 4283; default pipeline 33/73, |err| 5053; statuses identical (74 ok, 9 timeouts, 6 crashes). Regression gate polybench_pragmas bicg 8, gesummv 16, mvt 8, syrk 32 exact.
  - *`float-loads-perfect`* (proposal M8, not adopted): BRAM changes only in polybench atax (no split 11 -> 12, default pipeline 68 -> 69; Vitis 13) and bicg (12 -> 13, 69 -> 70; Vitis 14), so without split 39/73 exact, |err| 4283 -> 4281, default pipeline 33/73, |err| 5053 -> 5055. The harness files say 39/72 (4251) and 33/72 (5025) because forgebench diff_dims_p3 (and conv_block_module in the no-split run) hit the 120 s limit while other workflows loaded the machine (load average 40-50 on 32 cores); run alone without the limit, diff_dims_p3 gives 384 with and without the option (146 s / 152 s) and conv_block_module the same pre-existing `getIntOrFloatBitWidth` assertion. Decisions change in 7 of 96 subset designs (decision pass only, remark sets compared): atax, bicg, polybench_pragmas/bicg (still 8 = Vitis), machsuite gemm_blocked, and three ap_fixed designs under the scalar shim (forgebench Llama_GPT_module, gpt_transformer_p1, llama_transformer_p2; totals unchanged). Gates exact.
  - *Decisions* (`check_pipeline_unroll.py`, now 87 kernels with the 18 float ones, 86 compared - pm_floatarr_iop_m8_n64 does not parse: `memref<1x!llvm.struct<(f32)>>` from the union): default 74/86 agree, BRAM totals equal 58/86; with `--apu-opts float-loads-perfect` 81/86 and 64/86 (pf_fadd / fmac / fmul / srchoist 4 -> 2, rv_rmw_hoist_f_m8_n64 25 -> 3, _m64_n64 98 -> 11, all = Vitis). The 5 remaining disagreements are the known O10 (4) and O8 (1). Tables: `~/hls-est-work/t3r/decisions_{default,float}.md`.
  - *Probes:* pB_calls, pB_mutual, pB_norec, pA_partial, pU_partial_single, pU_partial_nested all exit 0 through the full no-split pipeline (pB_calls / pB_mutual aborted at HEAD; pU_partial_single crashed an intermediate version of this fix).
  - *Lit* `test/hls-est`: 72 tests, 43 passed (39 + the 4 new), 1 expectedly failed, 24 unresolved, 4 failed (all pre-existing).
  - *Debug invariance:* `check_debug_invariance.sh` (default pipeline, 42 Estimation inputs): 37 PASS, 0 FAIL, 5 ERR (identical tool failures with and without the flags: chstone sha, flowgnn gin / pna, spectorhls matrix_multiplication / sobel_filter_y); the `diff` RUN lines of bram_debug_json.mlir and bram_debug_pipelines.mlir pass.
- **Reverted?:** no. Replaced within this change: (a) an intermediate D3 fix that set the marker after `loopUnrollByFactor` and took the next `affine.for` as the cleanup loop - it crashed on trip 6 / factor 4 and unrolled the inner loop of a promoted single-iteration cleanup; (b) an intermediate D1 fix with the float rule on by default (no-split |err| 4281 / default pipeline 5055, `~/hls-est-work/t3fix2/`), moved behind the option under the 2026-10-09 rules.

## 2026-10-09 — cgeist: unsigned float compound back-conversion, bool compound assignment, loops with zero-extended counters raise to affine (frontend)
- **Commit:** Polygeist e5b6bbc9 (branch `hls-est-vitis-match`, repo root `/mnt/d/HLS`); this entry: the following changelog-only commit in `~/circt`
- **Files:** Polygeist `tools/cgeist/Lib/clang-mlir.cc`, `lib/polygeist/Passes/CanonicalizeFor.cpp`
- **What:** the three defects of the review of 8017846b, plus the crash they uncovered.
  1. D1 `emitIntCompound`: the back-conversion of a floating computation type to an integer LHS uses the LHS type's signedness (`convert(v, to, vSigned, toSigned)`, `toSigned` decides fptosi vs fptoui), so `unsigned *u; *u *= 1.5` is `fptoui` (C semantics; fptosi is poison for values >= 2^31). Signed LHS unchanged.
  2. D2 a bool LHS (`bool *b; *b |= 2; *b *= 0.5`) stores the result tested against zero (`cmpi ne, 0` / `cmpf une, 0.0`, then `extui i1 -> i8`), as `CK_IntegralToBoolean` / `CK_FloatingToBoolean` do, instead of `trunci` / `fptosi`.
  3. D3 `CanonicalizeFor`: `WhileToForHelper::computeLegality` looks through `arith.extui` like `arith.extsi` (zero-extension is monotone, so the bound test on the extended value is the same test on the counter); `prepareFor` casts an unsigned counter's start and step with `index_castui` (a bound in the comparison's wider type keeps `index_cast`); `ForOpInductionReplacement` casts a counter's init with the signedness its uses show (`castToIndexLike`: only `index_castui`/`extui` users -> `index_castui`, the rule of AffineCFG's `castOperandToIndex`), so the `index -> iN -> index` round trip folds to the unsigned value; `MoveWhileToFor` and `MoveSideEffectFreeWhile` accept `index_castui` where they accepted `index_cast`. AffineCFG.cpp already treated the ui variants everywhere (8017846b); `handle()`'s `index_cast` of compare operands sits behind `valueCmp(GE, 0)` and was left alone.
  4. Pre-existing crash: `for (int8_t i = 0; i < 64; i++) O[i] = A[i * 64]` aborted with the old binary too (`RewriterBase::eraseOp: expected that op has no uses`, PatternMatch.cpp:326, from `MoveWhileToFor`): when the body widens the counter, the counter's extension is passed from the before region through `scf.condition`, and `MoveWhileToFor` seeded a for iter_arg with that before-region value, then erased the while. `MoveSideEffectFreeWhile` now also recomputes such a cast (`index_cast`, `index_castui`, `extsi`, `extui`) in the body, cloning it when the comparison still uses it; `MoveWhileToFor` refuses a condition argument that is neither a before-block argument nor defined before the loop; `MoveWhileAndDown` rebuilds the extension on its new counter. Every uint8_t/uint16_t/ap_uint<N> counter loop whose body uses the counter in arithmetic (machsuite/viterbi: `s*N_TOKENS`, `prev*N_STATES`) has this shape.
- **Why:** verifier of 8017846b (session scratchpad `verify/v11_fpcompound.cpp`, `v12_narrow_loops.cpp`, `v10_loops.cpp`): `*u *= 1.5` gave `fptosi`, `*b |= 2` gave `trunci`, and the (correct) `extui` promotion of narrow unsigned counters left `for (uint8_t i = 0; i < 64; i++)` as `scf.while` (a regression against the mis-signed `extsi` form, which `computeLegality` looked through); hls-est 57627d6c rejected the `scf.condition` (machsuite/viterbi). Crash reproducers: scratchpad `fix3/r_c.c` (uint8_t), `r_cs.c` (int8_t, crashes the old binary), `r_init.c` (viterbi's three loop shapes). No Vitis modelling change: the frontend says what the C code says.
- **Effect:**
  - v11: `fptoui` for the `unsigned`, `unsigned char`, `ap_uint<8>` and `unsigned long` LHS; `*i *= 1.5` still `fptosi`; `*b |= 2` -> `cmpi ne %22, %c0_i32`, `extui i1 -> i8` (the observed store is the same `i1` zero-extended to i32); `*b *= 0.5` -> `cmpf une %28, %cst_0`, `extui`; the float LHS unchanged. v3_compound: the two bool lines and `*c /= 0.5` (-> `fptoui`) change, nothing else.
  - v12: every loop raises: `n_uchar`, `n_ushort`, `n_u16` -> `affine.for %arg2 = 0 to 64` with `affine.load/store %arg[%arg2]`; `n_u8_bound` -> `affine.for 0 to %0`, `%0 = index_cast %arg2 : i32 to index`; `n_u8_nested` -> two nested `affine.for 0 to 8` with `affine.load %arg0[%arg3, %arg2]` / `affine.store %arg1[%arg2, %arg3]`; `n_u8_noidx` -> the loop folds to `A[0] * 64`; `n_u8_pipe` -> `affine.for 0 to 64 {...} {hls.pipeline = [{II = 1 ...}]}`; `n_schar`, `n_short` unchanged. v10: `l_u8` -> `affine.for 0 to 64`; the other 12 functions identical to the stored output. v8_apcounter (`ap_uint<5>`, pipeline pragma), probe_ap_loop (`ap_uint<9>`, 300 iterations, pipeline) and probe_index (`ap_uint<9>` reduction, `iter_args`) raise. Every other `verify/` and `probes/` source produces its stored `.new.mlir` exactly (module attribute line excluded; v13_misc's histogram loop unchanged); the sources that failed before fail the same way.
  - Lit: `tools/cgeist/Test/HLS --filter-out hlsfactory` 68 passed / 5 failed (the same five pre-existing), `tools/cgeist/Test/Verification` 132 passed / 8 expectedly failed.
  - Harness `run_hlsest.py --datasets forgebench,vitis_examples,spector_hls,machsuite,soda --shim exact -j 8 --timeout 120 --force-cgeist`, base pipeline, `HOME=/home/peter` (`/tmp/cg2.json`, hls-est 815b755f4): 107 designs, ok 70 / hls-est error 10 / cgeist error 12 / cgeist crash 15. Control = previous binary `bin/cgeist.prev-8017846b` with the same hls-est, private work root `/home/peter/tmp/fix3ctl` (`/tmp/cg2-control.json`): **every status and total identical** (107/107). Against `/tmp/d1d4-new.json` (previous binary, hls-est 57627d6c) 9 designs change status, all reproduced by the control, i.e. hls-est-side: machsuite/spmv_crs, stencil2D, spector_hls/sobel_filter_x, sobel_filter_y, vitis_examples/20_...resolved_array_access_bottleneck -> ok 0; histogram -> ok 8; normals -> ok 4; viterbi `hls-est error: unsupported scf.condition` -> ok 64 (hls-est now accepts the while form; with the new binary viterbi has 0 `scf.while` / 7 `affine.for`, total 64 either way). soda (11 designs, new in the set): `cgeist crash` with both binaries (clang errors on `ap_uint<256>` member calls, `.range()` / `operator()`, which the exact shim's `_BitInt` cannot provide, then an abort after the diagnostics). aes_table / aes_tableless: `cgeist error: func.call operand type mismatch` as before.

## 2026-10-09 — hls-est: Vitis automatic pipeline/unroll/flatten decision (pipeline_loops=64)
- **Commit:** 22d473858 (code, tests, notes and this entry; branch `hls-est-vitis-match`); this hash line: 815b755f4 (changelog only)
- **Files:** lib/Dialect/Resource/Passes/AutoPipelineUnroll.cpp (rewritten), include/circt/Dialect/Resource/Passes/LoopPragmas.h (new: attribute names, `getFlattenedRoot`), lib/Dialect/Resource/Passes/AffineToLoopScheduleModified.cpp, lib/Dialect/Resource/Passes/InferVitisSplitArray.cpp; new lit tests test/hls-est/Passes/{auto_pipeline_inner_unrolled,auto_pipeline_inner65_flattened,auto_pipeline_perfect_nest,auto_pipeline_multilevel,auto_pipeline_siblings,auto_pipeline_threshold,auto_pipeline_user_pragma,flattened_parents_hoist,replication_rec_ii}.mlir; updated expectations test/hls-est/Estimation/polybench/{gemm,bicg}.mlir; tools/hls-est-validate/check_pipeline_unroll.py (new), experiment_pipeline.py (`threshold=` -> `pipeline-loops=`), VITIS_NOTES.md (N3, N4), OPEN_ISSUES.md (O3 update, O8-O11), CHANGELOG.md
- **What:**
  1. *`--auto-pipeline-unroll` is Vitis' algorithm* (VITIS_NOTES.md N3; now a module pass; option `pipeline-loops=T`, default 64, 0 disables the automatic decision while user pragmas still apply; `emit-remarks` prints one remark per decision). Per function: (0) user `hls.unroll` applied first (complete, or by factor); (1) perfect-nest collapse - a loop whose body holds one nested loop and no memory op outside it forms one loop with the product trip count; perfectness is judged with an LICM/promotion model (`LicmModel::effectiveLoop`): a load at an inner-invariant address from a memory the inner loop never writes is hoisted, a memory touched only at inner-invariant, pairwise-disjoint addresses with a store is register-promoted, both make the nest imperfect, register-only code never does; a dynamic inner loop never joins a chain (Vitis' semi-perfect rule); (2) top-down feasibility: a (collapsed) loop P is pipelined iff every loop inside it (callee loops through `func.call` included) has a constant trip count, no user `hls.pipeline`, no partial user unroll, and every nesting path's product is <= T; everything inside P is then unrolled innermost-first after `hoistInvariantOps` and `promoteInvariantAccesses` (one `acc[i]` load and store instead of 64 of each) and, with `promote-pipelined` (default on), the invariant locations of P itself are promoted (the `acc[0..7] +=` of an unrolled body become iter_args recurrences the scheduler sees); a loop with no loop inside is pipelined whatever its trip count (dynamic ones too); if P fails, each child is decided alone; (3) flattening: a non-pipelined loop with exactly one child loop (directly in its body, no call boundary) and constant bounds, whose child ends up pipelined, adds itself to the child's `hls.flattened_parents = N` (recursively upward; perfect-chain members count too). Tags: `hls.pipeline = [{II = 0 : i32, source = "auto"}]` on the pipelined loop; a user `hls.pipeline` is kept as written and honoured (pipelined whatever T, everything inside unrolled, nothing above it auto-pipelined); `hls.pipeline_off` is never auto-pipelined; a consumed partial `hls.unroll` becomes `hls.unroll_partial = F` and blocks every enclosing auto pipeline (Vitis ground truth of forgebench/diff_dims_p1, diff_orders_p1, mult_op_p1 and spector_hls/fir: the partially unrolled loops - and every copy a parent's unroll makes of them - are the pipelined ones, their parents are not).
  2. *Consumers of `hls.flattened_parents`* (`getFlattenedRoot`): `--convert-affine-to-loopschedule` `hoistInvariantLoads` treats values defined inside the N flattened ancestors as loop-variant and a write in their bodies as a write of the pipeline; `coalescePerfectNests` carries the tags of the erased innermost loop to the coalesced loop and discounts the band's own levels; `--infer-vitis-split-array` counts such loads (`analyzeAddress`, `isWrittenInFlattenedParents`).
  3. *Conversion robustness* for loops the old pass used to unroll away: an `affine.for` holding an scf loop is not "innermost" (it stays sequential control: machsuite/spmv_crs); a body with an op the scheduler has no operator type for (a `nanf` / `tanh` call) is left as a sequential loop with a warning instead of failing the pass (spector_hls/normals, forgebench/Llama_GPT_module, gpt_transformer_p1); a perfect band whose inner loop has results is not coalesced (`affine::coalesceLoops` erased it with live uses: the three `pu_red3b` kernels); every yielded value goes through an identity `arith.select %true, %v, %v` so the pipeline terminator always consumes a stage result (a loop yielding its own iter_arg - vitis_examples/20 - or an fadd accumulator, which the stage plumbing only found when the terminator happened to be scheduled at the op's completion).
  4. *Tried and reverted within this entry:* sizing RAM_1WNR copies for `max(recurrence II, ceil((R+W)/17))` instead of the final II (with an `hls.rec_mii` attribute from the scheduler). It explains pu_impst_m8_n16 / pu_red3c_l4_m4_n4 / pipeline_unroll_chk_4lvl (15 copies at a final II of 8 / 2) but pu_st_hoist_m8_n16 refutes it (same 16 reads, same II 8 from 16 stores, no copies in Vitis; the rule gave 32 vs 4). The replication formula is unchanged (T2); the conflict is OPEN_ISSUES O11 / VITIS_NOTES N4.
- **Why:** VITIS_NOTES.md N3 (pipeline_unroll.md + pipeline_unroll_chk.md, 64 kernels: R1 threshold, R2 collapse / top-down / flatten, the sharpened perfectness test), the corpus loop tables for the partial-unroll rule (`results/vitis.json` loops of diff_dims_p1: `VITIS_LOOP_138_5` pipe yes x96, `136_4` / `134_3` pipe no, no 214-291 "implied" unroll; fir: `VITIS_LOOP_53_3` pipe no with every inner loop <= 64 trips after the user unroll of 122). The old pass unrolled innermost loops with trip > 32 (inverted threshold, one level, no flatten, no pragmas) and hid the conversion limits of item 3 by unrolling the loops that expose them. spector_hls/fir shows why promotion out of the pipelined loop matters: Vitis' `VITIS_LOOP_120_9` has II 6 from the `firReal[l] +=` recurrence and one RAM_1WNR copy of `ai_a0` / `ai_b0` (2 BRAM each, total 4); without `promote-pipelined` the recurrence runs through memory, which the scheduler does not see (O1), the loop is port-bound at II 1 and each array gets 7 copies (28).
- **Effect:**
  - *Decisions* (`check_pipeline_unroll.py`, 69 pipeline_unroll(+_chk) kernels, cgeist scalar shim, loops matched to source lines by order): 64/69 agree with the Vitis log (214-376 / 214-186 / 203-541 sets). The others: the 4 `pu_call_*` kernels (cgeist inlines the `inline off` callee, OPEN_ISSUES O10; hls-est's decision on the inlined nest follows the same rules) and pu_prefix_m8_n16 (O8). Canonical pipeline without `--infer-vitis-split-array` on the same kernels, BRAM total = Vitis: 49/69; the differences are the simplex scheduler's II heuristic (pu_red_m8_n64 and 7 more: II 5 instead of Vitis' 4 -> 12 copies, 25 vs 32; `fast-threshold=0` gives 31), O11 (pu_impst / red3c / 4lvl: 4 / 14 / 14 vs 30) and O3 (`lb` 1 vs 2 in the sequential parent).
  - *Harness subset* (`run_hlsest.py --datasets polybench,polybench_pragmas,forgebench,machsuite,spector_hls,vitis_examples -j 8 --timeout 120`; before = HEAD 798e705e4 shipped defaults, `/tmp/rf_{nosplit,canon}_nofwd.json` of the T2-review entry, copied to `~/hls-est-work/t3/harness/before_*.json`; after = `after2_*.json` there; `~/hls-est-work/t3/cmp_runs.py` compares designs with the same cgeist output). **Without `--infer-vitis-split-array`: exact 34/74 -> 39/73, |err| 8371 -> 4283.** Newly exact: polybench gemm 32 -> 256, k2mm 56 -> 504, k3mm 56 -> 728, syr2k 56 -> 392, syrk 32 -> 256, polybench_pragmas k2mm 56 -> 184 and syr2k 56 -> 280, machsuite stencil2D crash -> 0; closer: polybench_pragmas k3mm 56 -> 296 (312), fft_transpose 8 -> 12 (28), tiled_attn_module 608 -> 606 (270); **no longer exact: polybench atax 13 -> 11, bicg 14 -> 12, mvt 14 -> 12** - per array they are now right where they were wrong (atax tmp1 1 = Vitis, was 2) and wrong by O3 (buff_x, buff_p, buff_y1 / buff_y2: Vitis 2 each, hls-est 1) and - *corrected by the T3 review fixes entry* - by a decision error, not O3, for atax buff_y_out and bicg buff_s_out (Vitis flattens atax lp3/lp4 and bicg lp1/lp2 because their invariant load is float, VITIS_NOTES.md N3; OPEN_ISSUES O13), the cancellation the T2-review entry described; `promote-pipelined=false` gives 12 / 13 / 12, so the loss comes from the inner-loop promotion the decision itself needs. Regression gate (polybench_pragmas, fresh Vitis): bicg 8 =, gesummv 16 =, mvt 8 =, syrk 32 = unchanged. Status: ok 76 -> 72; recovered: forgebench Llama_GPT_module and gpt_transformer_p1 (unsupported op -> a number), conv_A (crash -> 193), stencil2D (crash -> 0); new `hls-est timeout 120s` (9): forgebench diff_dims_module_large, diff_orders_module / p1 / p2 / p3, mlp, mult_op_p1, conv_B, conv_C - the user-unroll copies (x96 / x128 pipelined loops per design, as in Vitis' loop tables) multiply the scheduler's work (diff_orders_p1 and mult_op_p1 do not finish in 15 min either); none of them matched before; new crash: forgebench mult_op_p3 (2 -> crash); fft_transpose reaches the simplex assertion under the canonical pipeline (O9). **Canonical pipeline (with `--infer-vitis-split-array`): exact 31/73 -> 33/73, |err| 8825 -> 5053** (polybench_pragmas syr2k and stencil2D newly exact, mvt 14 -> 12 as above; the gemm family moves with the split pass's port-pressure partitions, O2).
  - *Lit* `test/hls-est`: 68 tests, 39 passed (30 + the 9 new), 1 expectedly failed (memory_recurrence_xfail), 24 unresolved (no RUN line, pre-existing), 4 failed (the pre-existing forgebench CHECK-BRAM tests). `Estimation/polybench/gemm.mlir` now expects 256 (= Vitis; the old 32 was the old model) and `bicg.mlir` 12 (model output; Vitis 14, O3).
  - *Debug invariance*: `bram_debug_json.mlir`'s `diff` RUN line passes; no new debug fields.
- **Not adopted / for Peter:** the default pipeline is unchanged (CLAUDE.md); the numbers above say the no-split pipeline is the better model (OPEN_ISSUES O2). O3 is now the whole error of mvt and half of atax / bicg (the other half is O13, *corrected by the T3 review fixes entry*) and the next mechanism to model; O11 (replication at II > 1) needs one more kernel pair before any rule is built.
- **Reverted?:** no (item 4 was reverted before the commit)

## 2026-10-09 — cgeist: unsigned index casts, compound-assignment widths, APInt constant folds, typedef signedness (frontend)
- **Commit:** Polygeist 8017846b (branch `hls-est-vitis-match`, repo root `/mnt/d/HLS`); this entry: the following changelog-only commit in `~/circt`
- **Files:** Polygeist `tools/cgeist/Lib/clang-mlir.cc`, `tools/cgeist/Lib/clang-mlir.h`, `lib/polygeist/Passes/AffineCFG.cpp`
- **What:** the four defects of the review of d279a433 (`_Float16`/`_BitInt` lowering), fixed generically.
  1. D1 `castToIndex(loc, val, isUnsigned)`: an index whose C type is unsigned (`unsigned char`, `size_t`, `ap_uint<W>` = `unsigned _BitInt(W)`) is cast with `arith.index_castui`, a constant one is read with `getZExtValue`; the array subscript and the two pointer-add call sites pass the signedness of the index expression. `AffineCFG.cpp` treats `index_castui` like `index_cast` (`isValidSymbolInt`, `isValidIndex`, `legalCondition`, the normalizer's cast look-through), folds `index_castui(index_cast x)` / `index_cast(index_castui x)` -> `x` on matching types and `index_castui(constant)` to the zero-extended index constant (`CanonicalizeIndexCastUI`, registered next to `CanonicalizeIndexCast`), and when it re-casts a stripped non-index map operand it emits `index_castui` for a value that only ever reaches index/wider types through `index_castui`/`extui` (`castOperandToIndex`); the "reuse an existing cast that is a valid symbol" step keeps the cast kind (`recordIndexCastOperand`).
  2. D2 compound assignments `-= *= /= %= <<= >>= &= |= ^=` on an integer LHS go through `emitIntCompound`: both operands are converted to clang's computation type (`CompoundAssignOperator::getComputationLHSType()`, each with the sign of its own type), the op is built there with the computation type's sign, and the result is truncated/extended (fptosi/fptoui for a floating computation type) back to the LHS element type before the store. `+=` and the floating-LHS paths are unchanged; same-width cases (`int`, `unsigned`, `long`, `_BitInt(40) += 1`) emit exactly what they did.
  3. D3 `VisitIntegerLiteral`/`VisitConstantExpr` build the `IntegerAttr` from the APInt (`zextOrTrunc`/`extOrTrunc` to the type width), the `BO_Add` constant fold adds APInts (same type only), `castToIndex` and `CanonicalizeIndexCast` check `getSignificantBits()`/`getActiveBits()` before extracting a 64-bit value, `getConstantIndex` takes `int64_t`.
  4. D4 `isSignedIntegerQualType(qt)` is `!qt->isUnsignedIntegerType()` (canonical type: typedefs, alias templates, `_BitInt`, bool, enums with an unsigned underlying type).
- **Why:** review probes (session scratchpad `probes/`): `ap_uint<8> j = 200; O[0] = A[j]` gave `affine.load %A[-56]`; `unsigned char q; q *= 3` and `unsigned _BitInt(9) *p; *p *= 3` gave `'arith.muli' op requires the same type for all operands`; `(unsigned _BitInt(65))0xFFFFFFFFFFFFFFFFULL + 1` aborted in `APInt::getSExtValue`; `uint32_t a / b` gave `divsi`/`extsi`/`cmpi slt`. No Vitis modelling change: the frontend now says what the C code says (Vitis reads an unsigned index as unsigned).
- **Effect:**
  - Probes (new binary): `probe_ap_loop` `arith.index_castui %arg2 : i9 to index` in the loop body and `affine.load %arg0[200]` (was `[-56]`); `probe_uchar_idx` `index_castui %0 : i8 to index`; `probe_idx_const` `affine.load %arg0[200]`; `probe_index` `[200]`, `affine.load %arg1[symbol(%3)]` with `%3 = index_castui ... i8 to index`; `probe_compound` `*p *= 3` -> `extui i9->i32, muli i32, trunci`, `*p /= 3` -> `extui, divsi i32, trunci`, `*r += 1` -> `addi i40`, `*s <<= 1` -> `shli i9` (computation type is the `_BitInt`, rhs truncated), `*s -= 2` -> `extui, addi -2 : i32, trunci`; `probe_compound_uchar` `extui i8->i32, muli, trunci`; `probe_lit_add` folds to `arith.constant -18446744073709551616 : i65` (the 65-bit pattern of 2^64); `probe_typedef_sign` `divui` x3, `extui`, `cmpi ult`; `review_types.cpp` output identical to the pre-change reference. Unchanged and out of scope: `probe_vec2*` still abort in `ValueCategory::getValue` (vector_size types), `probe_bf16`/`probe_lit_rest`/`probe_wb` are clang diagnostics.
  - Lit `tools/cgeist/Test/HLS --filter-out hlsfactory`: 68 passed / 5 failed before and after, the same five (array_partition/lowering.c, pipeline/lowering.c, consumer/hls-est-banking.c, consumer/hls-est-pipeline.c, polybench/hls-est-e2e.c: dlti strip / polygeist-opt). `tools/cgeist/Test/Verification` with the new binary: 132 passed, 8 expectedly failed, 0 failed.
  - Harness `run_hlsest.py --datasets forgebench,vitis_examples,spector_hls,machsuite --shim exact -j 8 --timeout 120 --force-cgeist`, base pipeline, in a private work root (`HOME=/home/peter/tmp/d1d4/home*`, so the shared `~/hls-est-work/mlir` cache was not touched): 96 designs, ok 63 -> 62. Control = the d279a433 binary (`/home/peter/tmp/d1d4/cgeist.old`) with the same hls-est binary (built 16:41:29): exactly 4 designs change, all statuses, no `ok` total changes (`/tmp/d1d4-control2.json` vs `/tmp/d1d4-new.json`):
    - machsuite/aes_table, aes_tableless: `cgeist crash` (abort in `RewriterBase::eraseOp`, PatternMatch.cpp:326, while canonicalizing the ill-typed uint8_t `^= <<= >>=` ops of D2) -> `cgeist error: 'func.call' op operand type mismatch memref<?xi8> vs memref<16xi8>` (a pre-existing frontend limitation that is now reached).
    - machsuite/viterbi: `cgeist crash` (same abort) -> cgeist ok, `hls-est error: unsupported operation scf.condition`: its uint8_t loop counters compare after promotion (`extui` + `cmpi slt`), so `CanonicalizeFor` leaves five `scf.while` (pre-existing; `probe_ap_loop` shows the same with both binaries).
    - spector_hls/histogram: ok 8 -> `hls-est error: unsupported operation arith.index_castui (i8) -> index`. The `unsigned char` data now indexes the histogram with `index_castui` (bytes 128..255 were negative indices before); hls-est whitelists only `arith.index_cast` (`lib/Dialect/Resource/Passes/AffineToLoopScheduleModified.cpp:1534`, `lib/Dialect/LoopSchedule/LoopScheduleOps.cpp:124`, `lib/Conversion/AffineToLoopSchedule/AffineToLoopSchedule.cpp:308`). Adding `arith::IndexCastUIOp` beside `IndexCastOp` there is the fix; not done in this change (those files carry uncommitted work from the parallel hls-est track).
    - Versus `results/baseline/hlsest-base-exact.json` (hls-est d2a4d3a5c): the same 4 plus 26 forgebench totals (activation_* 196 -> 256, conv_A/B/C 62/159/195 -> 97/273/321, diff_dims_* 960 -> 1152, ...) that the old-cgeist control shows as well — the hls-est storage-model commits since the baseline, not the frontend. No design with a typedef'd unsigned type changed its total.
  - Build: objects via `ninja` (driver.cc.o + the 4 dependents of clang-mlir.h + libMLIRPolygeistTransforms.a), link with the output on ext4 (`/home/peter/tmp/cgeist-link/link_cmd_d1d4.sh`, 8 min), installed by renaming the busy old binary aside and copying (1.5 min), `ninja -t restat bin/cgeist`. `build/bin/cgeist.orig-hls-preserve-name` untouched.
- **Reverted?:** no

## 2026-10-09 — hls-est: T2 storage review fixes (alias-aware hoist, redundant-access cleanup, classification tidy-up, D3/D4 notes)
- **Commit:** 798e705e4 (code, tests, notes and this entry; branch `hls-est-vitis-match`); this hash line: the following changelog-only commit
- **Files:** lib/Dialect/Resource/Passes/AffineToLoopScheduleModified.cpp, include/circt/Conversion/Passes.td, include/circt/Dialect/Resource/Passes/MemrefBankClassification.h, lib/Dialect/Resource/Passes/MemrefBankClassification.cpp, lib/Dialect/Resource/Passes/BRAMAnalysis.cpp; new lit tests test/hls-est/Passes/{invariant_load_alias,access_cse,access_forwarding}.mlir; test/hls-est/Passes/lutram_threshold.mlir (comment only); tools/hls-est-validate/VITIS_NOTES.md (N4, N5), OPEN_ISSUES.md (O3 updated, O6, O7), CHANGELOG.md (this entry; D4 correction in the T2 entry below)
- **What:**
  1. *D1, alias-aware invariant-load hoist* (`collectMemrefAliases`, `isPlainMemrefUse`, `isMemrefWrittenInLoop`): the alias set of a memref is itself plus every `ViewLikeOpInterface` result derived from it (memref.cast, subview, reinterpret_cast, ...), transitively; a write or opaque use of any member inside the loop counts as a write of the memref, and a memref that escaped (call, op with regions, terminator, non-view memref result) is never hoisted from. The reviewer's p3 (store through a `memref.cast` of buf, load buf[0]) keeps its load inside the pipeline.
  2. *D2, redundant-access cleanup* (`cleanupRedundantAccesses`; options `cse-loads` default on, `forward-stores` default **off**): per block, in program order, a load of an address loaded before with no intervening may-alias write takes the earlier value (CSE); with `forward-stores`, a load of an address stored before takes the stored value and a store overwritten before any may-alias read is dropped (store-to-load forwarding / DSE). Addresses are compared per dimension with `AffineValueMap::difference` after full composition (every difference 0 -> same, some nonzero constant -> disjoint, else may alias); only allocas, allocs and function arguments that did not escape take part; views, iter_args, get_global results, non-affine or predicated accesses, ops with regions and any other use of the memref are opaque (forget the memref). Runs before the hoist and before every analysis, so the scheduler's port slots, `MemrefBankClassification` and `--bram-analysis` all see the same ops. Surviving ops carry `hls.merged_loads` / `hls.forwarded_loads` / `hls.removed_stores` through the affine lowering; the debug text prints them per access (`(+N same-address load(s) merged into it: CSE)`, `(absorbed N forwarded load(s), M dead store(s))`) and the JSON access objects gain `merged_loads`, `forwarded_loads`, `removed_stores` (additive; `version` stays 2).
  3. *Classification tidy-up* (review minor): dead `BankProfile::copies` / `loopII` removed; `classifyMode(maxStoresPerLoop)` takes the one argument it uses; `profileAccesses` keys on the innermost `LoopLikeOpInterface` parent (the scope notion of `BRAMAnalysis::sequentialScope`) instead of `affine.for` only.
  4. *D3* (notes only): VITIS_NOTES N5 states that the exact-width LUTRAM rule is reachable end-to-end only with `--shim exact`; the harness default scalar shim widens `ap_int<11>` / `<15>` to i16, so lutram_chk_d93_w11 / d64_w15 come out as 1 BRAM instead of Vitis' 0. Whether `exact` becomes the harness default is OPEN_ISSUES O6 (every baseline would be re-measured); nothing in the harness changed.
  5. *D4*: the T2 entry's "lp1 II 32 = Vitis" for atax without split was wrong and is corrected in place below: with the T2 binary the schedule is copy-in II 32, lp1 II 64, lp3_lp4 II 64 (tmp1's 64 loads + 64 stores on 2 slots). With `forward-stores=true` tmp1 collapses to one store per iteration and lp1 reaches II 32 = Vitis (copy-in 32, lp3_lp4 64).
- **Why:** review of 342b39dfc: D1 is a latent miscompile (a store through a view was invisible to the hoist); D2 is VITIS_NOTES N4 itself ("R = loads/iteration ... after unrolling, CSE, LICM") against raw counts - the reviewer's p1 (8 loads of 2 addresses) got 7 copies; D3 and D4 are measured facts.
- **Effect:**
  - Reproducers (base pipeline): p1_dupload buf 56 -> 8 (R 8 -> 2, RAM_T2P; total 64 -> 16; `cse-loads=false` restores 64); p3_alias_hoist: load kept inside the pipeline (buf 1R+1W at II 1, 2 BRAM; before: hoisted). access_forwarding @acc with `forward-stores=true`: 8 loads + 9 stores of acc per iteration -> 1 store (`hls.forwarded_loads = 8, hls.removed_stores = 8`), II 9 -> 4, acc 2 -> 1 BRAM; @mayalias (two stores + one may-alias load) and @disjoint untouched.
  - Harness subset (`run_hlsest.py --datasets polybench,polybench_pragmas,forgebench,machsuite,spector_hls,vitis_examples -j 8 --timeout 120`, before = T2 binary `~/hls-est-work/t2/harness/{after,after_nosplit}.json`; this run `/tmp/rf_{canon,canon_nofwd,nosplit,nosplit_nofwd}.json`). **Shipped defaults (cse-loads on, forward-stores off): canonical 31/73 exact, |err| 8825; without `--infer-vitis-split-array` 34/74, |err| 8371 - every design's total and status identical to T2 (112/112 in both pipelines; status counts ok 75 / 76 unchanged).** So D1 and the CSE change no corpus number: the only designs with view ops fail before `--bram-analysis` (as the review found) and no corpus loop body loads one address twice.
  - `forward-stores=true` (measured, **not adopted**): canonical 30/73, |err| 8819 (mvt 14 -> 12; attention_op_p2/p3 32 -> 31 vs Vitis 24; tiled_attn_module 2344 -> 2338 vs 270); without split 31/74, |err| 8369 (atax 13 -> 12, bicg 14 -> 13, mvt 14 -> 12, gesummv 22 -> 20 vs 135, the same three forgebench). Per array it is Vitis-exact wherever it acts: atax tmp1 2 -> 1 (Vitis 1), bicg buff_q_out 2 -> 1 (Vitis 1), gesummv tmp1 / tmp2 2 -> 1 (Vitis 1); but mvt buff_x1 / buff_x2 go 2 -> 1 where Vitis keeps `ram_2p` = 2 (the hoisted invariant reads are issued on both ports of the sequential body, O3), and atax / bicg lose the error that cancelled O3 (buff_x / buff_p 1 vs Vitis 2). A change that breaks a matching case is not adopted (CLAUDE.md working loop, rule 5): the mechanism stays behind `forward-stores` and OPEN_ISSUES O7 ties its adoption to O3. polybench_pragmas gate identical in both settings (bicg 8, gesummv 16, mvt 8, syrk 32 exact; k2mm / k3mm / syr2k 56).
  - Lit `test/hls-est`: 59 tests, 30 passed (27 + 3 new), 1 expectedly failed (memory_recurrence_xfail), 24 unresolved (no RUN line, pre-existing), 4 failed (pre-existing forgebench CHECK-BRAM). Debug invariance (stdout identical with and without `--bram-debug`) holds on the three new tests.
- **Reverted?:** no (`forward-stores` kept as an option, default off; see O7)
